### [1] [The double sphere solution in the liquid drop model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00108)
*Manuel del Pino,Rupert L. Frank,Monica Musso*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维空间中在体积约束下能量泛函（包含周长项和两两之间的引力/库仑型非局部项）的临界域问题，构造了一个轴对称的、非最小化的临界点，对于足够小的体积V，其解形似两个体积为V/2的球，由一个宽度约V^(4/3)的微小类悬链面颈相连。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于液体滴或引力聚合体等物理模型中的能量极小化问题，作者试图在体积约束下构造非最小化的临界形态（鞍点），以丰富对该变分问题解空间的拓扑与结构理解。

Method: 构造方法基于对称化与扰动分析：在轴对称假设下，选取两个球体作为主导结构，通过Lyapunov-Schmidt约化或渐近匹配方法，在连接处以悬挂型颈（类悬链面结构）进行局部调整，使边界光滑且满足能量泛函的欧拉-拉格朗日方程（即关键域条件），并控制规模参数使V足够小以保证构造的收敛性与正则性。

Result: 成功构造出光滑、嵌入、紧致的轴对称临界面，且该解在足够小的V下不是能量极小元（即非最小化临界点/鞍点）。颈部的典型尺度为V^(4/3)，远小于球的半径尺度V^(1/3)，呈现出细长连接形态。

Conclusion: 证明在该体积约束能量泛函下，除了全局极小（球）之外还存在非平凡的高阶临界点结构，验证了液滴分裂-重组过渡态（类似fission barrier）的存在性，为理解该非局部变分问题的解景观提供了构造性证据。

Abstract: We consider the problem of finding critical domains $Ω\subset{\mathbb R}^3$ for the energy functional
  $$
  \mathcal E (Ω) = {\rm Per}\,(Ω) + \frac 12 \iint_{Ω\times Ω} \frac{dx\,dy}{|x-y|}
  $$
  under the volume constraint $|Ω|=V$.
  We look for smooth, embedded, compact surfaces $\partialΩ$ that solve this problem. We construct an axially symmetric, non-minimizing solution that, for a sufficiently small $V>0$, resembles the union of two balls with volume $V/2$ connected by a tiny, approximately catenoidal neck with a width of the order $V^{\frac 43}$.

</details>


### [2] [The stability conjecture for the Bernoulli problem in dimension four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05669)
*Xavier Fernández-Real,Joaquim Serra*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明在四维欧几里得空间中，Bernoulli单相问题的所有全局经典稳定解都是一维的，即若自由边界非空，则由一条或两条平行超平面构成。这解决了四维Bernoulli问题中的稳定性猜想，并作为推论获得了Hessian估计、自由边界曲率估计及有限Morse指数解的轴对称性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者致力于解决Bernoulli单相问题中关于全局经典稳定解结构的稳定性猜想。该猜想在低维（如n≤3）已被确认，但在更高维空间中解可能呈现更复杂的形态。在四维情形下验证一维性结论，是推进该猜想维度范围的关键步骤，同时有助于建立解的规则性与对称性估计。

Method: 论文采用解析方法证明四维空间中Bernoulli单相问题全局经典稳定解的一维性。核心策略包括：(1) 利用自由边界问题的稳定性条件与几何测度理论建立解的先验估计；(2) 通过精细的Hessian估计和自由边界曲率控制，排除非平面自由边界构型；(3) 结合Morse指数分析与爆破（blow-up）论证，在四维临界情形下闭合证明。

Result: 主要结果是：在R^4中，Bernoulli单相问题的每一个全局经典稳定解都是一维的，若解非平凡，则自由边界为一条或两条平行超平面。作为推论，获得了四维空间中稳定解的全局部Hessian估计与自由边界曲率估计；同时证明了四维空间中有限Morse指数的全局经典解具有轴对称性。

Conclusion: 作者在四维空间中完全解决了Bernoulli单相问题的稳定性猜想，将已知的一维性结论推广至临界维度n=4，并通过稳定性与Morse指数的分析工具导出了强有力的几何正则性推论。该工作为理解Bernoulli自由边界问题在高维空间中的解结构奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: We prove that every global classical stable solution to the one-phase Bernoulli problem in $\mathbb R^4$ is one-dimensional. In particular, if nonempty, its free boundary consists of one or two parallel hyperplanes. This settles the stability conjecture for the Bernoulli problem in dimension four. As consequences, we obtain universal local Hessian and free-boundary curvature estimates for classical stable solutions in $\mathbb R^4$, as well as axial symmetry of global classical solutions with finite Morse index in $\mathbb R^4$.

</details>


### [3] [Global asymptotic stability of KdV-Burgers fronts in a weakly two-dimensional model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05697)
*Jared C. Bronski,Olivia Clifton,Vera Mikyoung Hur*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维通道中非线性色散-耗散PDE（描述涌潮传播）的前锋型解，证明了当通道在横向上足够窄且相对色散参数处于一维稳定性范围内时，一维前锋在弱二维扰动下是全局渐近吸引子，且唯一（至多空间平移）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解二维通道中涌潮（undular bores）前锋解的稳定性，将经典KdVB方程的一维稳定性理论推广至含弱横向运动的二维情形，为实际流体中涌潮传播的数学建模与验证提供理论支持。

Method: 采用能量方法（energy method）的推广，将先前一维框架下针对时间调制扰动解的分析工具扩展到二维，处理横向动力学对稳定性的影响。关键条件包括通道足够窄以及相对色散参数处于一维稳定性范围。

Result: 证明了在窄通道和适当参数范围内，一维前锋解是弱二维扰动下的全局渐近吸引子；该解在空间平移意义下是唯一的。

Conclusion: 这项工作将一维KdVB方程前锋稳定性的理论结果成功推广至弱二维情形，给出了涌潮前锋解在二维通道中稳定性的充分条件，为色散-耗散系统多维度稳定性分析提供了新方法。

Abstract: We study front-type solutions of nonlinear dispersive-dissipative PDEs modeling the propagation of undular bores in a channel in two dimensions. The system extends the Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers (KdVB) equation by incorporating weak transverse motion, and admits the KdVB fronts as one-dimensional solutions. We investigate their stability under general two-dimensional perturbations. We prove that a one-dimensional front is a global asymptotic attractor in the weakly two-dimensional setting when the channel is sufficiently narrow in the transverse direction and the relative dispersion parameter lies in a range for stability to one-dimensional perturbations. Particularly, the front is unique up to spatial translations. The proof extends the energy method for temporally-modulated perturbed solutions, developed previously in the one-dimensional setting, to accommodate the transverse dynamics.

</details>


### [4] [On Decay of the Local Mean Oscillations of the Vorticity Direction in Critical Navier-Stokes Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05720)
*Zoran Grujic*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程中涡量方向的几何PDE，在临界空间奇点情形下（涡量大小以O(|x|^{-2})浓度集中于临界Lorentz空间L^{3/2,∞}），推导出一个传递定理：核心处方向场的bmo_{1/|log r|}正则性（均匀一致到奇异时刻）由内尺度方向场的对数时间模量与物理应变控制。关键发现是应变仅通过其切向分量P_{ξ^⊥}Sξ进入方向方程，当方向为应变张量特征向量时该分量为零——包括承载最大拉伸的特征向量，因此不同于依赖削弱整个涡拉伸项的经典几何正则性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典几何正则性判据建立在削弱完整涡拉伸项的基础上，仅适用于弱拉伸构型。本文旨在突破这一局限，通过隔离和分析涡量方向的PDE（由球面上的调和映射热流加流体输运、交叉扩散和切向应变组成），研究NSE机制能否传播方向场的对数衰减局部平均振荡正则性，从而为强拉伸情形下的有限时间爆裂预防提供新的理论框架。

Method: 将方向场的几何PDE隔离为球面上调和映射热流结合流体输运、交叉扩散与切向应变。关键分析步骤包括：(1) 从粘性交叉扩散中提取O(|x|^{-2})浓度产生的径向向外漂移4ν x/|x|^2；(2) 证明HMHF非线性对量(1/2)|ξ-e|^2无害（在任意半球上构成子解）；(3) 由此推导传递定理，将核心处方向场的bmo_{1/|log r|}正则性归约为内尺度方向场的对数时间模量和物理应变控制。

Result: 建立了传递定理：核心处均匀一致到奇异时间的bmo_{1/|log r|}方向正则性由内尺度方向场的对数模量与物理应变控制。更重要的是发现应变仅通过切向分量P_{ξ^⊥}Sξ进入方向方程，当方向是应变张量的特征向量（包括最大拉伸特征向量）时该耦合自动消失，表明本判据可覆盖经典判据无法处理的强拉伸配置。

Conclusion: 该结果明确区分于经典涡拉伸削弱范式：由于最大拉伸特征向量方向上的应变-方向耦合自动消隐，本文判据不受弱拉伸限制，可为强涡拉伸情形下的NSE爆裂预防提供更一般化的几何框架。

Abstract: We isolate and analyze the geometric PDE governing the evolution of the vorticity direction in the 3D incompressible Navier-Stokes equations (NSE), restricted to the case of a critical spatial point singularity where the vorticity magnitude concentrates as $O(|x|^{-2})$, inhabiting the critical Lorentz space $L^{3/2, \infty}$. The PDE consists of the Harmonic Map Heat Flow (HMHF) into the sphere supplemented with the fluid transport, cross-diffusion and tangential strain. The question is whether the NSE mechanics can propagate logarithmic decay of the local mean oscillations of the direction -- the condition $\xiVec \in \bmo_{1/|\log r|}$ which (in this setting) was shown in the companion paper to prevent finite time blow-up. The key observations are that the $O(|x|^{-2})$ concentration, factored out of the viscous cross-diffusion, generates an outward radial drift $4ν\, x/|x|^2$ at the core and that the HMHF nonlinearity is harmless for the quantity $\frac12|\xiVec - e|^2$, which is a subsolution on any hemisphere. This yields a transfer theorem: the $\bmo_{1/|\log r|}$ regularity of the direction at the core, uniformly up to the singular time, is controlled by a logarithmic modulus in time of the direction at the inner scale, together with the physical strain. Crucially, the strain enters the direction equation only through its tangential component $P_{\xiVec^\perp} S\xiVec$, which vanishes precisely when the direction is an eigenvector of the strain tensor. Since this includes the eigenvector carrying the maximal stretching, the result is in contrast to the classical geometric regularity criteria which are built on depleting the full vortex-stretching term and thus confined to configurations of weak stretching.

</details>


### [5] [A globally defined polyconvex isotropic energy satisfying the true-stress-true-strain monotonicity condition (TSTS-M++)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06026)
*Dongxin Bai,Yunhao Wu,Yong Li,Kai Zhang,Patrizio Neff*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对可压缩各向同性弹性能量函数，首次给出显式构造，使其同时满足多凸性和真实应力-真实应变单调性（TSTS-M++）。作者提出的存储能函数在给定参数范围内满足TSTS-M++，并证明了应力双射性与TSTS-M++具有不同的阈值，在TSTS-M++区间内还证明了有限平面应变环问题的解的唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多凸性是有限弹性变分存在性理论的标准条件，而TSTS-M++要求正的增量柯西应力响应，这两类本构限制相互独立。Wollner、Holzapfel和Neff曾留下一个开放问题：在GL+(3)整个空间上定义的可压缩各向同性能量能否同时满足这两个条件。本文旨在给出明确肯定回答。

Method: 作者构造了参数化存储能函数 W_k(F)，利用指数形式结合‖F‖²、J的倒数与J的组合，分析其多凸性与严格秩一凸性；通过计算柯西应力增量响应确定TSTS-M++成立的精确阈值 k≥1/(8√3)；进一步研究应力映射V→σ(V)的全局双射性与光滑性，并分析对应的临界阈值kB；最后将该结果应用于有限平面应变环的径向平衡唯一性分析。

Result: 构造的存储能函数对任意μ>0和k>0均多凸且严格秩一凸。其柯西应力响应当且仅当k≥1/(8√3)时全局满足TSTS-M++。在此区间内每个对称柯西应力对应唯一的正定拉伸张量。应力双射性的更小尖锐阈值为kB≈0.00827233304：在等号处应力映射为全局同胚但逆映射不可微，在其上为全局C∞微分同胚。当kB≤k<1/(8√3)时应力映射保持全局双射而TSTS-M++在有限应变下失效。在TSTS-M++区间内，外壁无牵引的有限平面应变环的每个指定内半径有唯一的径向平衡，且内压从零到无穷光滑严格递增。

Conclusion: 本文给出了可压缩各向同性弹性中多凸性与TSTS-M++可同时满足的显式反例构造，解决了一直以来的开放问题，并揭示了应力双射性与TSTS-M++限制之间的严格层次关系——前者是后者的必要条件但非充分条件，二者阈值不同。

Abstract: Polyconvexity is a standard ingredient in the variational existence theory of finite elasticity, whereas true-stress-true-strain monotonicity (TSTS-M++) requires a positive incremental Cauchy-stress response. These two constitutive restrictions are independent, and Wollner, Holzapfel and Neff left open whether a compressible isotropic energy defined on the whole of $\mathrm{GL}^{+}(3)$ can satisfy both. We give an explicit affirmative answer. For every $μ>0$ and $k>0$, the stored-energy function $W_k(F)=\fracμ{2k}\bigl[\exp\bigl(k(\lVert F\rVert^2+3J^{-1}+J-7)\bigr)-1\bigr]$, $J=\det F$, is polyconvex and strictly rank-one convex. Its Cauchy-stress response satisfies TSTS-M++ globally if and only if $k\ge 1/(8\sqrt{3})$. In this regime every symmetric Cauchy stress corresponds to a unique positive-definite stretch, while the reference stretch is stress free with positive infinitesimal shear and bulk moduli. Stress bijectivity has the strictly smaller sharp threshold $k_{\mathrm{B}}\approx 0.00827233304$: at equality the stress map is a global homeomorphism with a nondifferentiable inverse, and above it the map is a global $C^{\infty}$ diffeomorphism. Thus, for $k_{\mathrm{B}}\le k<1/(8\sqrt{3})$, the map $V\mapstoσ(V)$ remains globally bijective while TSTS-M++ fails at finite strain. In the TSTS-M++ regime, every prescribed inner radius of a finite plane-strain annulus with a traction-free outer wall has a unique radial equilibrium, and its inner pressure increases smoothly and strictly from zero to infinity.

</details>


### [6] [Global existence and finite-time blow-up for a strongly damped wave--MGT system with fully subcritical logarithmic nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05819)
*Tae Gab Ha*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了全Sobolev次临界范围内带对数源的强阻尼波-摩尔-吉布森-汤普森(MGT)耦合系统,利用增广变量w=v+τv_t推导精确能量耗散恒等式,结合耦合势阱结构,在阱深d_α以下建立了尖锐的动力学二分法:稳定集初始数据解全局存在且指数衰减,不稳定集初始数据解有限时间爆破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在完整的Sobolev次临界范围2<γ<2^*内,对数非线性项不满足低次临界区域中可利用的标准紧性和差分估计,这构成了主要困难,需要发展新的分析工具来处理该系统的全局动力学行为。

Method: 采用Faedo-Galerkin格式结合封闭非线性微分不等式和空间域分割论证建立局部存在性;通过增广变量构造精确能量耗散恒等式,利用耦合势阱结构分析能量;强阻尼提供额外正则性用于证明全次临界范围内的唯一性和延拓原理;利用Levine凹性方法,通过增广形式中的结构抵消吸收不利的MGT残差项。

Result: 在阱深d_α以下获得尖锐动力学二分法:稳定集初始数据对应的解全局存在且指数衰减;不稳定集初始数据对应的解在有限时间内爆破。爆破论证中的结构抵消使得凹性方法在不施加初始速度符号条件下即可闭合。

Conclusion: 通过增广变量表述和精确能量恒等式,成功克服了全次临界范围内对数源带来的紧性和差分估计困难,完整刻画了该强阻尼MGT系统的动力学行为。

Abstract: We study a coupled strongly damped wave--Moore--Gibson--Thompson (MGT) system with logarithmic source $f(u)=|u|^{γ-2}u\ln|u|$ in the full Sobolev-subcritical range $2<γ<2^*:=2n/(n-2)$. The main difficulty is that, in this full range, the logarithmic nonlinearity does not admit the standard compactness and difference estimates available in the lower subcritical regime. Using the augmented variable $w=v+τv_t$, we derive the exact energy-dissipation identity for the strongly damped system and exploit the associated coupled potential-well structure.
  For the local theory, a Faedo--Galerkin scheme combined with a closed nonlinear differential inequality and a spatial domain-splitting argument yields local existence. The strong damping provides the additional regularity needed to prove uniqueness throughout the full subcritical range and to establish a continuation principle. Below the well depth $d_α$, we then obtain a sharp dynamical dichotomy: solutions with initial data in the stable set exist globally and decay exponentially, whereas solutions with initial data in the unstable set blow up in finite time.
  The blow-up argument reveals a structural cancellation specific to the augmented formulation: once the logarithmic contribution is reconstructed through the exact energy identity, the unfavorable MGT residual in Levine's concavity functional is absorbed algebraically. This allows the concavity method to close without imposing any additional sign condition on the initial velocities.

</details>


### [7] [Modified scattering for semiclassical Bose-Fermi mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06143)
*Kieran Cavanagh*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了Vlasov-Hartree系统（玻色-费米混合体系，玻色子用量子力学描述，费米子用经典力学描述，通过库仑力相互作用）。对于小初值，证明系统表现出修正散射：玻色子与费米子子系统分别收敛于自由Schrödinger方程和Vlasov方程的解，但由于库仑相互作用的长程性质，存在耦合的对数修正项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vlasov-Hartree系统混合描述了量子玻色子与经典费米子通过库仑力相互作用的物理模型，理解其长时间渐近行为是数学物理中的核心问题。由于库仑力是长程相互作用，经典散射框架不再适用，需要研究修正散射现象，并建立系统的渐近动力学描述。

Method: 采用混合拉格朗日与Fourier方法。在Vlasov一侧采用纯拉格朗日分析，从而不需要对初始费米子密度求导；玻色子一侧使用Fourier分析。通过这两种方法的结合，推导出解、相关场以及拉格朗日轨迹的渐近动力学的显式描述。

Result: 在小初值条件下，证明了Vlasov-Hartree系统的修正散射性质。玻色子与费米子子系统均收敛到自由演化解，但带有因库仑长程相互作用产生的耦合对数修正。由于Vlasov侧的纯拉格朗日分析，渐近费米子剖面的正则性与初值相同，且收敛发生的空间几乎与初值所在空间相同，无需对初始费米子密度施加导数条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为混合量子-经典系统的长时间渐近行为提供了严格的数学描述，揭示了库仑长程相互作用下修正散射机制，且在正则性要求的放宽和收敛空间的优化方面优于已有结果，展示了拉格朗日方法在Vlasov类系统中处理低正则数据的优越性。

Abstract: The Vlasov-Hartree system models a mixture of bosons and fermions interacting via Coulomb forces in which the bosons are described quantum-mechanically, while the fermions are described classically. Phys. 222(1) (2001), 117--146.] for this Cauchy problem to analyze the entropy solutions and obtain various new fine properties of entropy solutions; these can be summarized in four aspects: (i) Characteristics and initial waves for the Cauchy problem; (ii) Fine local structures of entropy solutions; (iii) Divides and global structures of entropy solutions; (iv) Invariants and asymptotic behaviors of entropy solutions including the asymptotic profile and the corresponding decay rates. Through these results (i)-(iv), we establish the global dynamic patterns of entropy solutions of the Cauchy problem for the $1$-D pressureless Euler system with general initial data $ρ_0$ being locally finite Radon measures and $u_0\in L^\infty_{ρ_0}$.

Abstract: In this note, we revisit a Liouville-type theorem of Chae and Wolf for stationary Navier-Stokes equations in $\mathbb{R}^3$ [J. Differential Equations 261 (2016) 5541-5560]. Abstract: In this paper, we study the low Mach number limit for a compressible two-fluid model with algebraic closure in the $d$-dimensional torus with $d \geq 2$. Moreover, the problem also has a least energy solution at $α=α_0$ provided $α_0> C(n)\max_{\partial Ω} H$, where $C(n)$ is a positive constant depending only on $n$ and $H$ is the mean curvature on $\partialΩ$.

Abstract: We study the initial-boundary value problem for the Short Wave equation on the half-line $x\ge 0$. This leads to different analyticity properties of the associated eigenfunctions and, consequently, to different spectral formulations in the regimes $u(0,t)\le0$ and $u(0,t)\ge0$.

Abstract: This monograph presents a systematic account of several classical and recent developments in the theory of rotating vortex patches, or V-states, for the two-dimensional Euler equations and related active scalar models. Structural properties of the interaction kernel, in particular complete monotonicity and the resulting spectral factorization, yield a common bifurcation framework encompassing the Euler, generalized surface quasi-geostrophic, quasi-geostrophic shallow-water, and related models.

Abstract: DNA replication requires the coordination of origin firing and fork progression to ensure the entire genome is timely duplicated before cell division. Abstract: We develop a constructive method for solving semilinear elliptic equations $Δu(x)=f(x,u(x))$ on locally finite, connected infinite graphs with layered structure. Abstract: We prove global existence of mild solutions to the spatially inhomogeneous Fermi--Dirac BGK equation for arbitrary-size Pauli-admissible initial data with finite mass and kinetic energy, allowing both vacuum and locally saturated zero-temperature states. Abstract: We prove a pair of complementary small-data stability theorems describing the global future and past structure of classical solutions to the isentropic, spherically symmetric $3D$ compressible Euler equations. We allow any equation of state with positive sound speed, except that our shock-formation results do not apply to the Chaplygin gas.
  Our first theorem concerns open sets of smooth Cauchy data that are perturbations of trivial data with vanishing velocity and constant positive density. The perturbed data have finite kinetic energy, and the perturbed density is equal to a positive constant plus a signed ``asymptotically flat'' tail. Our main theorem yields a complete description of the existence and uniqueness of the maximal globally hyperbolic development (MGHD) of the data. The boundary of the MGHD contains hypersurfaces that extend to spatial infinity on which our solutions develop gradient-singularities. This is the first MGHD existence-uniqueness-stability result for shock-forming solutions for any multi-$D$ quasilinear wave system. While prior works have yielded local portions of ``candidate'' MGHDs, neither the existence nor the uniqueness of an MGHD can be inferred from local considerations.
  Our second theorem considers analogous data with the opposite sign of the $1/r$ density tail. We prove global existence throughout spacetime, to both the future and past. The solution's asymptotic behavior towards null infinity is not linear, but rather is distorted by a logarithmic bending of the sound cones away from the flat Minkowski ones, leading to logarithmically enhanced dispersion. This is the first small-data global existence result in the entire spacetime for any $3D$ quasilinear wave system that fails to satisfy the null condition and the weak null condition. The main mechanism of stabilization is a global-in-spacetime rarefaction effect, tied to the $1/r$ tail.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a Fefferman--Stein inequality in terms of area function and non-tangential maximal function associated with the Schrödinger operator $\mathcal{L} = -Δ+ V$ on stratified Lie groups $\mathcal G$, where $Δ$ denotes the sub-Laplacian on $\mathcal G$ and $V$ is a nonnegative locally integrable function. We also provide explicit examples on the round sphere where the condition can be verified explicitly. Abstract: We study a fourth-order variational model for two-dimensional frustrated ferromagnets with competing exchange interactions and an applied magnetic field of strength $H>0$. Abstract: Let $R\subset\mathbb{R}^d$ be a reduced root system, $G$ the associated finite reflection group, and $k\ge0$ a $G$-invariant multiplicity function. We develop a Dunkl analogue of the obstacle/partial-balayage method of Ouyang, Spector, and Stockdale (https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18068) for the Euclidean fractional Laplacian. For $0<s<2$, nonnegative $f\in L^1(ν_k)\cap L^2(ν_k)$, and $λ>0$, we obtain a decomposition \[ f=μ+(-Δ_k)^{s/2}u, \qquad 0\leμ\leλ, \] with $μ=λ$ on $Ω=\{u>0\}$ and \[ λν_k(Ω)\le\|f\|_{L^1(ν_k)}. \] As an application, we prove that the vector Dunkl--Riesz transform $\mathcal R_k=\nabla_k(-Δ_k)^{-1/2}$ is of weak type $(1,1)$ with constant at most $(M_k+2)$, where \[ M_k=\#\{α\in R_+:k(α)>0\}. \] For $G$-invariant functions, the reflection terms vanish and the same argument gives the universal constant $2$. We further establish a dimension-free weak-type $(1,1)$ estimate for the Dunkl--Schrödinger Riesz transform.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏整数规划常具有块结构，子问题通过少量共享变量交互。将连接等式对偶化可得到可分解的Lagrangian松弛，但通常引入对偶间隙。近期工作表明可通过引入共享变量上指数规模的冗余非线性一致性约束族来消除间隙，但显式构建该松弛在计算上不可行，亟需一个不显式构造该指数规模松弛的计算框架。

Method: 将共享变量一致性编码为非线性边表示，并采用列与行生成（CRG）算法：通过定价生成局部整数解（列生成），通过分离生成编码约束（行生成）。在完全编码与精确分离条件下可恢复精确松弛，同时保持各块的独立优化。提出了若干编码族（Generalized, Vertex, Monomial, Reflected）并分析其表达力与约束数量差异。

Result: 证明了Generalized编码族在最坏情况下所需约束数可比其他编码族（Vertex/Monomial/Reflected）少指数级，但某些实例上又可能比单一问题特定编码需要更多约束（指数级别的分离结果）。在分解的稳定集与支配集实例上，CRG显著优于单块（monolithic）模型，覆盖多种树拓扑与耦合强度。更丰富的编码族在计算上不一定更优。

Conclusion: 编码家族的选择是影响分解方法有效性的核心问题：更丰富的编码并不必然带来更好的计算性能，实际问题中应根据实例特征谨慎选择编码策略。

Abstract: A wide range of sparse integer programs admit a block structure in which subproblems interact through a small set of shared variables. Dualizing the linking equalities yields a decomposable Lagrangian relaxation, but generally introduces a duality gap. Recent work shows that this gap can be closed while preserving decomposability by dualizing exponentially large families of redundant nonlinear consistency constraints on the shared variables. We develop a computational framework for exploiting this idea without explicitly constructing the resulting exponentially large relaxation. Our framework combines nonlinear edge encodings of shared-variable consistency with a column-and-row generation (CRG) algorithm that generates local integer solutions by pricing and encoding constraints by separation. With complete encodings and exact separation, the framework recovers the exact relaxation while retaining independent optimization over the blocks. We introduce several encoding families and establish exponential separations among them: the Generalized family can require exponentially fewer constraints than the Vertex, Monomial, or Reflected families, yet can itself require exponentially many constraints on instances for which a single problem-specific encoding suffices. Computational experiments on decomposed stable-set and dominating-set instances show that CRG substantially outperforms a monolithic formulation across a range of tree topologies and coupling strengths. The results also show that richer encoding families need not perform better computationally, highlighting the choice of encoding as a central issue in effective decomposition.

</details>


### [85] [Sparse plus low-rank matrix embedding with applications in cancer radiotherapy optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05695)
*Mojtaba Tefagh,Gourav Jhanwar,Masoud Zarepisheh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏加低秩矩阵嵌入（SLME）问题，旨在为大稠密矩阵构建计算高效的替代表示A≈S+HW，而非传统的稀疏加低秩分解。作者开发了R3-Trust算法，一种单次无参数运行即可逼近Pareto前沿的信任域算法，应用于癌症放疗中的剂量影响矩阵优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的稀疏加低秩分解（如鲁棒PCA）关注恢复底层结构，但在许多应用中（如癌症放疗计划优化），真正的目标是构建一个大稠密矩阵（如剂量影响矩阵）的计算高效替代品，而非可解释的稀疏和低秩分量。经典的恢复导向方法在此嵌入目标下可能产生次优解。

Method: 将SLME形式化为双目标非凸优化问题，在近似误差与下游任务计算成本之间权衡。提出R3-Trust，一种高效的信任域算法，通过单次参数无关的运行来逼近Pareto前沿，前沿上的每个点提供不同准确度与计算成本平衡的稀疏加低秩表示。

Result: 在临床放疗矩阵上的实验表明，现有恢复导向分解方法在嵌入目标下可能是次优的。在合成实例上，SLME和R3-Trust可应用于稀疏加低秩恢复任务，在重建精度和计算时间上均优于最先进的恢复方法。

Conclusion: SLME为矩阵嵌入问题提供了新框架，R3-Trust算法能够在单一运行中高效逼近Pareto前沿，既适用于嵌入目标也适用于恢复目标，展现出广泛的适用性和竞争性的性能。

Abstract: Decomposing a matrix into sparse and low-rank components is central to robust principal component analysis and has broad applications in machine learning, signal processing, and computer vision. Classical formulations seek to recover the underlying sparse and low-rank structure. We instead introduce \emph{sparse-plus-low-rank matrix embedding} (SLME), whose goal is to construct a computationally efficient surrogate for a large dense matrix, without requiring its components to be interpretable. Given $A\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$, SLME approximates $A \approx S+HW$, where $S$ is sparse, $H\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times r}$, $W\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times n}$, and $r\ll \min\{m,n\}$. The resulting matrix-vector product can be evaluated as $Sx+H(Wx)$ in $\mathrm{nnz}(S)+r(m+n)$ operations, rather than the $mn$ operations required by $Ax$. Our primary motivation arises from optimization problems in cancer radiotherapy treatment planning, where a large dense \emph{dose-influence matrix} is a major computational bottleneck.
  We formulate SLME as a bi-objective nonconvex optimization problem that balances approximation error against the computational cost of the downstream tasks. We then develop \emph{R3-Trust}, an efficient trust-region algorithm that approximates the Pareto frontier in a single parameter-free run. Each point on the resulting frontier provides a sparse-plus-low-rank representation with a different balance between accuracy and downstream computational cost. Experiments on clinical radiotherapy matrices show that decompositions obtained from existing recovery-oriented formulations can be suboptimal for the embedding objective. Conversely, experiments on synthetic instances demonstrate that SLME and R3-Trust can also be applied to sparse-plus-low-rank recovery, where they compare favorably with state-of-the-art recovery methods in both reconstruction accuracy and computational time.

</details>


### [86] [Can Acceleration in Gradient-Norm Minimization Be Anytime? Sharp Last-Iterate Limits in Smooth Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05710)
*Pierre Vernimmen,François Glineur*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究平滑凸优化中，当停止时间（迭代次数视界）未知时，梯度范数最小化的加速可行性。作者推翻了一个点态猜想，证明非自适应、视界无关的线性跨度一阶方法不能在每一视界都达到Ω(N^{-1})以下的下界，并证明limsup下界1/2是精确且不可改进的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度范数是不可微目标中衡量驻点性的直接可观测指标。虽然已知存在最优加速方法（如OGM-G）可在任何有限视界内加速梯度范数最小化，但这些方法的系数依赖于视界长度（迭代次数）。作者想知道当停止视界对方法未知时，何种加速是可行的，以解决Diakonikolas & Wang提出的关于视界无关方法的下界猜想。

Method: 通过构造一个在密度为一的视界集合上实现近N^{-2}最后迭代保证的方法来反驳点态猜想；随后证明对所有方法都存在无限多个视界满足Ω(N^{-1})下界，即limsup_N N·G_N(A)≥1/2，该常数1/2由Rotaru等人的视界无关梯度下降调度精确达到；最后证明了两种极端行为不可能由同一方法同时实现，并指出best-so-far输出可实现均匀O(N^{-2})保证。

Result: (1) 推翻了Diakonikolas & Wang的猜想——存在视界无关方法在密度为一的视界集合上实现近N^{-2}的last-iterate保证；(2) 证明limsup_N N·G_N(A)≥1/2对任意方法成立，且常数1/2是最优的，由Rotaru等人精确实现；(3) 证明了这两种行为的不兼容性：在子序列上达到o(N^{-1})保证的方法必有limsup_N N·G_N(A)=∞；(4) best-so-far输出可提供均匀O(N^{-2})保证，与last-iterate输出形成对比。

Conclusion: 视界无关方法在梯度范数最小化中的加速能力比预期更微妙：虽然可以在大多数视界上实现接近加速下界的表现，但在无限多个视界上必然出现慢速退化；且last-iterate与best-so-far输出在可实现性上存在本质差异。

Abstract: In smooth convex optimization, the gradient norm is a directly observable measure of stationarity. Accelerating a first-order method that minimizes the gradient norm is known to be more delicate than accelerating the minimization of function values. Optimal accelerated methods such as OGM-G (Kim & Fessler, 2021) are known to exist for any prescribed finite horizon, but their coefficients depend explicitly on the length of that horizon, i.e. the number of iterations.
  We ask what kind of acceleration is feasible when the stopping horizon is unknown to the method, i.e. for horizon-independent methods. Diakonikolas & Wang (2022) conjectured that an $Ω(N^{-1})$ lower bound on the squared gradient norm holds at every horizon $N$ for any nonadaptive, horizon-independent linear-span first-order method. We disprove this pointwise conjecture by exhibiting a method that achieves near-$N^{-2}$ last-iterate guarantees on a density-one set of horizons. We show instead that an $Ω(N^{-1})$ lower bound must hold for infinitely many horizons. More precisely, if $\mathcal G_N(\mathcal A)$ denotes the bound on the squared gradient norm after $N$ iterations for a method $\mathcal A$, we prove that $\limsup_{N\to\infty}N \mathcal G_N(\mathcal A) \ge 1/2$ for any method $\mathcal A$. This bound is sharp: the constant $1/2$ is exactly attained by the horizon-independent gradient-descent schedule of Rotaru et al. (2026).
  In addition, we show that the above two extreme behaviors cannot be achieved by the same method: any method $\mathcal A$ with an $o(N^{-1})$ guarantee on a subsequence of iterates must satisfy $\limsup_N N \mathcal G_N(\mathcal A)=\infty$. In contrast, best-so-far output admits a uniform $O(N^{-2})$ guarantee.

</details>


### [87] [Convexity from order: a theory of faces and generalized separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05775)
*Matthew S. Scott*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从序理论视角研究凸集与凸锥，将凸集视为正锥的仿射切片。引入取值于任意有序向量空间的"定向映射"（oriented maps）来推广泛函，实现凸集的序理论分离。定向映射的核形成分离仿射平面的格，其底元素为联合支撑子空间（JSS）。在有限维中，双边面约简对应分离仿射平面格中的下降。在仅假设凸性的条件下，证明了两个凸集之差的面是两个面的差、两个凸集交的面是面的交，解决了Weis提出的开放问题5.10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典凸分析中，分离定理依赖超平面和线性泛函，需要紧性、有限维等假设。本文旨在建立一个无需额外假设（仅凸性）的普适框架，利用凸锥与预序的等价性，将凸集作为正锥的仿射切片进行序理论研究，推广分离概念，并解决已有文献中未决的开放问题。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 利用凸锥与向量空间相容预序的对应关系，将凸集序理论化为正锥的仿射切片；(2) 将经典泛函推广为取值于任意有序向量空间的定向映射（oriented maps），用元素序关系 f(D)≤0≤f(C) 来刻画分离；(3) 在一般向量空间中定义两个凸集的联合支撑子空间（JSS），作为交叉线性度 lin(K-G) 的仿射切片；(4) 通过定向映射的字典序积（lexicographic products）形式化双边面约简在分离仿射平面格中的下降机制。

Result: 得到如下结果：(1) 面被刻画为强最小元集合，定向映射能够暴露每个面；(2) 定向仿射映射的分离推广了超平面分离，其核构成分离仿射平面格，底元素为JSS；(3) 在仅假设凸性（不要求紧性、有限维性或暴露性）的条件下，证明了两个凸集之差的面是相应面的差；(4) 证明了两个凸集交集的面是面的交，解决了Weis文献中的开放问题5.10，去除了非空内部核假设；(5) 有限维中双边面约简对应于分离仿射平面格中的下降，并用字典序积予以形式化。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个基于序理论的统一凸分析框架，将凸集的经典几何概念（面、分离、泛函）推广到任意有序向量空间值域下，仅依赖凸性假设即可获得关于差集和交集面的普适结果，解决了开放问题并推广了刚提出的JSS概念到一般向量空间。

Abstract: It is known that any convex cone in a general vector space is the positive cone of a preorder compatible with the vector space, and that any convex set is an affine slice of a convex cone. We therefore treat convex sets order-theoretically, as affine slices of positive cones. We find that faces are sets of strong minima, and we generalize functionals to oriented maps valued in arbitrary ordered vector spaces. Oriented maps expose every face, and an oriented affine map $f$ separates $C$ from $D$ when $f(D) \le 0 \le f(C)$ element-wise. This generalization of hyperplane separation recovers mathematical objects that are "local", including the generated face at a point and the normal cone. The kernels of separating oriented maps form a lattice of separating affine flats whose bottom element is the joint supporting subspace (JSS), recently introduced in finite dimensions for qualification-free convex analysis results. We define the JSS in general vector spaces for two convex sets $C, D$ as the affine slice of the cross-lineality $\mathrm{lin}(K-G)$, where $K,G$ are the homogenization cones of $C, D$. We show that in finite dimensions, bilateral facial reduction corresponds to a descent in the lattice of separating affine flats, which we formalize as lexicographic products of oriented maps. The lexicographic characterization of faces follows as a corollary.
  By making no assumption other than convexity, our approach yields general results: we show that the face of a difference of two convex sets is a difference of faces, dropping assumptions of compactness, finite dimensionality, and exposedness. We also show that any face of an intersection of two convex sets is an intersection of faces, resolving open problem 5.10 by Weis in "A note on faces of convex sets", dropping the assumption of non-empty intrinsic core.

</details>


### [88] [Flatness of the Generic Under-Actuated 6-Degrees-of-Freedom Unmanned Surface Vessel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05785)
*Taylor J. Klotz,Peter J. Vassiliou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决了长期悬而未决的问题：通用欠驱动6自由度无人水面艇（gUSV）是否允许反馈线性化。通过利用Lie对称性，作者为所有水动力参数值构建了动态反馈线性化、显式平坦输出和平坦参数化。通用情形下动态补偿器阶数为6，而环形船体的hovercraft型船舶补偿器阶数为4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管具有环形船体的欠驱动无人水面艇（hovercraft）的平坦性早已为人所知，但通用欠驱动6自由度无人水面艇（gUSV）是否可反馈线性化这一问题长期悬而未决，本文旨在解决这一开放性问题。

Method: 利用gUSV的Lie对称性构造动态反馈线性化，推导出显式的平坦输出和平坦参数化，适用于所有水动力参数值。

Result: 在通用情形下，动态补偿器阶数为6，而hovercraft型船舶（特殊情形）的动态补偿器阶数为4。这一结果表明gUSV确实可反馈线性化，且比特殊情形需要更高阶的补偿器。

Conclusion: 通用欠驱动6自由度无人水面艇可反馈线性化，其Lie对称性是构建所需动态反馈补偿器和显式平坦输出的关键工具。

Abstract: The flatness of the under-actuated unmanned surface vessel with circular hull-shape (``the hovercraft") has long been known. We resolve the long-standing open problem: whether the generic under-actuated 6-degrees-of-freedom (dof) unmanned surface vessel (gUSV) admits a feedback linearization. Lie symmetries of the gUSV are used to construct a dynamic feedback linearization, explicit flat outputs and flat parametrization, for all hydrodynamic parameter values. In this generic case the dynamic compensator has order 6, in contrast to the hovercraft vessel whose dynamic compensator has order 4.

</details>


### [89] [Transformation of Adaptive Multistage Sampling for Solving Finite-Horizon Markov Decision Processes with Unknown Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05856)
*Hyeong Soo Chang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"自适应多阶段rollout（AMR）"的无采样强化学习算法，用于在底层模型未知的有限时域MDP中估计初始状态的最优值函数。AMR将自适应多阶段采样（AMS）转化为无采样形式，通过非平稳策略探索并与实际环境交互，每轮仅回退更新被访问状态及所采取动作。算法被证明是渐近最优的，期望绝对误差趋近于零，且收敛速度取决于从初始状态到达各可达状态的访问次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实强化学习场景中，决策者通常只知道MDP的状态集和动作集，而不清楚底层转移概率和奖励模型。传统的AMS算法虽然有效但依赖模型采样（simulation-based），需要已知模型才能工作。作者希望将AMS的思想转移到一个更通用的无模型（model-free）强化学习框架中，使得仅通过与真实环境的交互（rollout）即可实现渐近最优的值函数估计。

Method: AMR算法将AMS中的自适应多阶段采样过程转化为无采样（sampling-free）形式。在每一轮迭代中，AMR先生成当前探索所需的非平稳策略，然后通过rollout对该策略执行并获取一条完整的状态-动作经验轨迹。随后以非递归方式从轨迹末端向前回溯，仅对轨迹中访问过的状态及实际采取的动作做值函数更新，避免了穷举式更新，提升了计算效率。

Result: 理论分析表明AMR是渐近最优的，即期望绝对误差序列收敛到零。收敛速率与从初始状态下在每一阶段到达各可达状态的访问次数密切相关，从而将AMS的理论结果推广到了强化学习（无模型）设定中。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地将基于模型的AMS算法转化为适用于无模型强化学习场景的AMR算法，在仅知状态集和动作集的前提下实现了有限时域MDP的渐近最优值估计，为模型已知的采样算法如何迁移到真实交互场景提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: This work provides a learning approach to solving finite-horizon Markov decision processes (MDPs) when the underlying model of a given finite MDP is unknown to the decision maker. We transform the adaptive multistage sampling (AMS) algorithm into a sampling-free algorithm, called "adaptive multistage rollout (AMR)," for estimating the optimal value at an initial state when only the state set and the action set are known. AMR emulates the backward induction as in AMS but in a reinforcement learning (RL) setting. At each iteration, AMR generates a non-stationary policy to be used for exploration and rolls out the policy in order to obtain a single trajectory of experiences and traces it backwards in a non-recursive way while doing relevant updates only at visited states and for actions taken at the visited states. We show that AMR is asymptotically optimal such that the sequence of the expected absolute errors approaches zero and its convergence rate depends on the number of visits to each reachable state at each stage from the initial state, essentially transforming the result of AMS into the RL setting.

</details>


### [90] [Endpoint Asymptotics of Optimal Stabilization Prefactors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05863)
*Changqin Quan,Gengsheng Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性控制系统中最优稳定化预因子Ĉ(δ)的端点渐近行为。通过引入可控性指数μ（可控情形）和不可控部分Jordan块最大尺寸q（不可控情形）这两个结构不变量，证明了Ĉ(δ)在δ趋向可达衰减率范围端点时的渐近阶分别满足Ĉ(δ)≍δ^{μ-1}（δ→+∞）和Ĉ(δ)≍(δ*−δ)^{-(q−1)}（δ↑δ*），从而揭示了系统结构不变量与最优稳定化代价之间的精确对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于稳定可镇定的有限维线性控制系统，需要理解最优状态反馈增益的性能极限，特别是当要求的衰减率δ逼近可达范围的端点时，最优稳定化预因子（即状态转移矩阵范数放大的最坏情况度量）将如何发散或增长。这种端点渐近行为的刻画有助于深入理解线性系统镇定问题的本质困难与结构限制。

Method: 文章采用数学渐近分析的方法。针对可控系统，利用可控性指数μ刻画渐近阶Ĉ(δ)≍δ^{μ-1}；针对可镇定但不可控系统，利用不可控部分中与谱边界Re λ=−δ*相关的最大Jordan块尺寸q，推导出Ĉ(δ)≍(δ*−δ)^{−(q−1)}。通过这两种情形的对比，作者建立了端点渐近阶与对应结构不变量之间的双向对应关系。

Result: 主要结果为：(1) 当(A,B)可控且μ为最大可控性指数时，Ĉ(δ)≍δ^{μ−1}（δ→+∞）；(2) 当(A,B)可镇定但不可控时，Ĉ(δ)≍(δ*−δ)^{−(q−1)}（δ↑δ*），其中δ*为可达衰减率的上确界，q为不可控部分中与谱边界相关的最大Jordan块尺寸。两个情形的渐近阶分别由μ和q唯一决定，且渐近阶可反推出对应结构不变量。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了最优稳定化预因子在可达衰减率范围端点的渐近行为：在可控情形下表现为δ的幂次增长（指数由可控性指数决定），在非可控情形下表现为(δ*−δ)的幂次发散（指数由不可控部分的Jordan结构决定）。该结果揭示了线性系统镇定的结构障碍如何精确反映在最优性能指标的端点渐近阶中，为后续更复杂系统（如无穷维系统）的相关研究提供了有限维基准。

Abstract: Consider the stabilizable finite-dimensional linear control system $\dot x=Ax+Bu$. For a prescribed decay rate $δ>0$, we define the optimal stabilization prefactor $$ \hat{C}(δ) := \inf_{K\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}} \sup_{t\ge0}e^{δt}\|e^{(A+BK)t}\|. $$ We determine its asymptotic behavior as $δ$ approaches the right endpoint of the achievable decay-rate range. If $(A,B)$ is controllable and $μ$ is its largest controllability index, then $\hat{C}(δ)\asympδ^{μ-1}$ as $δ\to+\infty$; whereas if $(A,B)$ is stabilizable but not controllable, then $\hat{C}(δ)\asymp(δ_*-δ)^{-(q-1)}$ as $δ\uparrowδ_*$, where $δ_*$ is the supremal achievable decay rate and $q$ is the largest size of a Jordan block of the uncontrollable part associated with the spectral boundary $\operatorname{Re}λ=-δ_*$. Thus, in both cases, the endpoint asymptotic order of $\hat{C}(δ)$ is determined by the corresponding structural invariant---$μ$ in the controllable case and $q$ in the noncontrollable case---and conversely this order recovers that invariant.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数规划（MIP）是应用优化的基石。近年来，快速找到高质量原始（primal）解的需求日益增长，例如NVIDIA cuOpt求解器的开发以及新的MIPFEAS基准测试（600秒严格时间限制，评估求解器快速找到高质量原始解的能力）都反映了这一趋势。因此需要开发能够快速找到强原始解的并行化求解方案，并高效共享求解过程中的信息。

Method: 提出一种MIP组合portfolio并行化方案，核心在于设计高效的工作进程间信息交换机制。提供了两种实现方式：一种直接集成到开源MIP求解器SCIP中；另一种为外部独立实现，命名为ReXi（基于portfolio并行化原理，在多个求解实例间交换边界和解信息）。

Result: ReXi在MIPFEAS基准测试中是目前最快的非商业求解器，SCIP内嵌实现紧随其后。此外，两个实现的新版本在MIPFEAS基准测试上均大幅优于各自的先前版本。

Conclusion: 所提出的MIP portfolio并行化方案及其两种实现（SCIP内嵌版和外部ReXi版）在MIPFEAS基准上均表现出色，验证了高效信息交换策略对MIP并行求解质量的重要价值。

Abstract: Mixed-integer programming (MIP) is a cornerstone in applied optimization, both in industry and academia. Recently, there has been increased attention to finding strong primal solutions quickly. This is reflected, for example, in the development of the NVIDIA cuOpt solver and, most recently, in the new MIPFEAS benchmark, which has a tight time limit of 600 seconds and evaluates solvers based on how quickly they find high-quality primal solutions. This article introduces a MIP portfolio parallelization scheme, focusing on efficiently exchanging information between its workers. We present two implementations of this scheme: one built directly into the open-source MIP solver SCIP, and an external one, which we call ReXi. ReXi is currently the fastest non-commercial solver in the MIPFEAS benchmark, followed by the SCIP-integrated implementation. Moreover, we present new versions of both implementations that considerably outperform their predecessors on the MIPFEAS benchmark.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机极小极大优化的理论研究大多依赖于随机梯度的有界方差假设。在重尾噪声下（随机梯度仅具有有限p阶矩），通常认为梯度裁剪或归一化是保证收敛所必需的。本文旨在重新审视重尾噪声下的随机极小极大优化，探究是否可以不依赖梯度裁剪实现收敛。

Result: 证明了vanilla SGDA在无任何更新规则修改的情况下即可在重尾噪声下收敛，建立了此类设置下的首个收敛保证；提出的Stoc-TRGDAM和Stoc-TRGDmax算法无需梯度裁剪即可达到目标精度的最优依赖。

Conclusion: 本文挑战了"重尾噪声下必需梯度裁剪"的传统认知，表明vanilla SGDA在无正则化问题中即可直接收敛；对于正则化极小极大问题，通过设计新的裁剪自由算法同样可以实现最优收敛，为重尾噪声下的随机优化提供了更全面的理论保障。

Abstract: Stochastic min-max optimization has attracted increasing attention due to its applications in modern machine learning, while existing theoretical studies mainly rely on the bounded variance assumption for stochastic gradients. Under heavy-tailed noise, where stochastic gradients only possess a finite $p$-th moment for $p\in(1,2]$, gradient clipping or normalization is commonly believed to be necessary to guarantee convergence. In this work, we revisit stochastic min-max optimization under heavy-tailed noise and provide a comprehensive theoretical study of stochastic gradient descent ascent (SGDA). We first show that vanilla SGDA, without any modification to its update rule, can converge under heavy-tailed noise in both nonconvex-strongly-concave (NC-SC) and nonconvex-concave (NC-C) settings, establishing the first convergence guarantees for SGDA in these regimes. Beyond unregularized problems, we further investigate regularized stochastic min-max optimization, where directly incorporating gradient normalization into proximal updates is nontrivial due to the incompatibility between normalization and proximal structures. We overcome this difficulty by developing new clipping-free algorithms, i.e., Stoc-TRGDAM and Stoc-TRGDmax, and they both can achieve the optimal dependence on the target accuracy without using gradient clipping.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不定二次规划问题广泛存在于工程、经济和管理科学等领域，是一类重要的非凸优化问题。传统的数值算法在处理大规模或病态问题时可能面临挑战，而动力系统方法提供了一种替代的求解途径，因此值得深入研究。

Method: 作者构建了一个用于求解带线性约束的不定二次规划问题的动力系统，并通过分析该系统的动力学性质来研究其收敛行为。具体包括：证明系统轨迹收敛到问题的KKT点，推导轨迹与问题最优解之间的距离估计，以及证明目标函数沿轨迹的单调下降性。

Result: 论文证明了系统生成的轨迹能够收敛到不定二次规划问题的KKT点，并给出了轨迹与解之间的误差界估计。同时，证明了目标函数值沿着轨迹单调递减。通过一个说明性示例和数值测试展示了该方法的良好行为与性能。

Conclusion: 动力系统方法能够有效求解带线性约束的不定二次规划问题，具有收敛性保证和较好的数值性能，为处理此类非凸优化问题提供了一种可行的替代途径。

Abstract: This paper studies a dynamical system approach for solving indefinite quadratic programming problems subject to linear constraints. We investigate the convergence of the trajectory generated by the system to a Karush-Kuhn-Tucker point of the quadratic programs. In addition, we derive an estimate for the distance between the trajectory and a solution of the problem. We further prove that the objective value is decreasing along the trajectory. An illustrative example and a numerical test are presented to demonstrate the behavior and performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [94] [Low-rank matrix recovery landscapes beyond RIP with application to rank-one measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06146)
*Andrew D. McRae*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过低秩因子化LASSO（核范数正则化最小二乘）的非凸全局景观进行低秩矩阵恢复的问题。传统的景观保证依赖于测量算子具有受限等距性质（RIP），这在许多实际应用中不成立（特别是测量矩阵本身低秩时）。作者提出了一种更弱的等距条件：仅要求算子在全秩低秩矩阵的低秩切空间上满足上等距性质。以此为基础，作者将结果应用于随机秩一线性测量下的矩阵恢复问题，通过高概率浓度界证明了具有统计近最优样本复杂度和恢复误差的新型景观保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低秩矩阵恢复的非凸景观分析通常假设测量算子满足受限等距性质（RIP），即算子在所有低秩矩阵上近似等距。然而在许多实际应用（如秩一线性测量）中，当单个测量矩阵本身是低秩时，上等距常数往往很差，这一假设不切实际，限制了理论结果的适用性。

Method: 作者放宽了传统RIP条件，提出仅需在低秩真实矩阵所对应的线性低秩切空间上满足上等距性质（而非对所有低秩矩阵），即可保证因子化低秩LASSO的非凸景观是良性的（无不良局部最优）。之后利用随机秩一测量算子的高概率浓度界，将该景观保证应用于具体问题中。

Result: 证明了在弱化的等距条件下，低秩因子化矩阵LASSO的全局景观仍然良性；并将其用于随机秩一测量恢复问题，获得了具有统计近最优样本复杂度与恢复误差的新景观保证。

Conclusion: 该工作放宽了非凸低秩恢复景观分析中RIP条件的限制，使理论保证能够覆盖测量算子本身为低秩（如秩一测量）等更广泛的实际场景，同时仍保持近最优的统计效率。

Abstract: We study the problem of low-rank matrix recovery from linear measurements via the global nonconvex landscape of a low-rank factored formulation of the matrix LASSO (nuclear-norm--regularized least-squares). If the landscape is benign, that is, has no bad local optima, then practical and scalable algorithms can compute good statistical estimates. Previous state-of-the-art landscape guarantees have typically assumed that the linear measurement operator has the restricted isometry property, that is, the operator is approximately an isometry over all low-rank matrices. This is an unrealistic assumption for many applications; in particular, when the individual measurement matrices are themselves low-rank, we typically have poor upper isometry constants. To overcome this, we establish new guarantees of a benign landscape under a weaker isometry condition: rather than requiring upper isometry over all low-rank matrices, we only require it over the linear low-rank tangent space to the low-rank ground truth matrix. To illustrate the utility of this result, we apply it to the problem of matrix recovery from random rank-one linear measurements; via high-probability concentration bounds on the random measurement operator, we prove a novel landscape guarantee with statistically near-optimal sample complexity and recovery error.

</details>


### [95] [Fast PAC Global Optimization via Restarted Langevin: Exploration, Exploitation, and Degenerate Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06196)
*Ioannis Kontoyiannis,Sean Meyn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑（可能非凸）函数全局优化的计算代价，比较了经典模拟退火、固定温度Langevin扩散、并行重启Langevin以及Langevin-梯度混合方案在(ε,δ)-PAC性能要求下的运行时间。主要发现是将探索与利用分离的Langevin-梯度方案能显著降低模拟时间，其总模拟时间近似为N·exp{EL/(Nη)}+O(log(1/ε))，实现了全局探索与所需精度的解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非凸优化问题，现有随机方法（如模拟退火和Langevin扩散）在满足PAC保证时所需的模拟时间通常随精度和置信度指数增长，代价高昂。作者旨在设计新算法以显著降低计算代价，特别是将全局探索与局部利用分离以克服能量势垒的瓶颈。

Method: 在连续时间框架下分析四种方法：(1)经典模拟退火；(2)固定温度Langevin扩散；(3)并行重启Langevin（多个独立固定温度Langevin轨迹并行运行）；(4)Langevin-梯度混合方案（用随机动力学进行全局探索，用梯度流进行局部利用），其中"最佳状态"变体保留迄今为止最好的状态。理论分析采用对数精度下的低温和非低温尺度分析，并通过六驼峰函数和Rastrigin函数进行数值实验验证。

Result: 前两种方法（模拟退火和固定温度Langevin）的模拟时间在低温区随L/ε指数增长。并行重启Langevin在δ→0时给出C₃=L^{1+o(1)}/ε的模拟时间。最佳改进来自Langevin-梯度混合方案，其总模拟时间约N·exp{EL/(Nη)}+O(log(1/ε))，其中探索成本与精度ε对数解耦。超越对数精度的分析揭示了维度依赖的前置因子。

Conclusion: 分离探索与利用是降低非凸全局优化计算代价的关键策略。与单纯依赖模拟退火或Langevin扩散相比，Langevin-梯度混合方案能有效解耦全局探索（与能量势垒相关）和局部精度（与ε相关），为高精度全局优化提供了理论上更优的方案。实验中较暖的探索温度以及谱信息对理解探索时间具有实用价值。

Abstract: We study the computational effort required for global optimization of a smooth, possibly nonconvex objective $Γ:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$. An algorithm satisfies the $(\varepsilon,δ)$-PAC performance requirement if its output $\widehat X$ obeys $\mathbb{P}\{Γ(\widehat X)-Γ^\star>\varepsilon\}\leqδ$. Algorithm design and analysis are in continuous time. We compare classical simulated annealing and fixed-temperature Langevin diffusion with two approaches introduced and analyzed here: parallel-restart Langevin and a Langevin--gradient scheme using stochastic dynamics for global exploration and gradient flow for local exploitation.
  Let $L=\log(1/δ)$ and let $E_*$ denote the dominant energy barrier. At logarithmic precision in the low-temperature regime, the first two approaches require simulation time exponential in $L/\varepsilon$. For parallel fixed-temperature Langevin, an appropriate number of independent trials gives $ C_3=L^{1+o(1)}/\varepsilon$ as $δ\downarrow0$, for each fixed $\varepsilon>0$.
  The most substantial improvement comes from separating exploration from exploitation. If $η$ is the attraction margin of a target region containing the global minimizers, a sufficient low-temperature estimate for total simulation time in the best-state Langevin--gradient variant is $ C_4^{(c)}\approx N\exp\{EL/(Nη)\} +O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$, with $E>E_*$. Thus global exploration is decoupled from the requested accuracy. Analysis beyond logarithmic precision reveals dimension-dependent prefactors, while experiments on the six-hump camel and Rastrigin objectives illustrate the benefits of warmer exploration and the usefulness of spectral information for understanding exploration time.

</details>


### [96] [Algorithmic Optimality Guarantees for Nonsmooth $H_\infty$ Output-Feedback Policy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06197)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间全阶动态输出反馈H∞策略搜索问题（一个非凸非光滑问题），证明在扩展凸提升的精确恒等截面上，ε-平稳性可导出O(ε)-次优性，进而为非光滑策略搜索方法提供收敛率保证；同时利用扩展凸提升的等价性构建了非严格可行性二分法算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接策略搜索是强化学习和连续控制的核心范式，但在鲁棒输出反馈设置下缺乏严格的理论保证。H∞问题是规范基准，因为它同时捕捉扰动衰减与鲁棒性，并暴露策略空间优化的非光滑几何结构。作者希望解决Guo和Hu [2022]提出的有限时间最优性差距问题，并在更一般的动态输出反馈H∞策略搜索场景中进行研究。

Method: 作者首先在扩展凸提升的精确恒等切片上证明了ε-平稳性implies O(ε)-次优性。然后利用扩展凸提升提供的值等价性，构造了非严格可行性二分法方法，并辅以一个最终严格可行性恢复步骤，以获得显式的ε-最优镇定控制器。

Result: 在精确紧致切片上建立了ε-平稳性与O(ε)-次优性之间的联系，由此导出非光滑策略搜索方法的收敛率保证；提出的二分法算法能够产出显式的ε-最优镇定控制器。

Conclusion: 该工作为非光滑H∞策略搜索的先前定性最优性理论提供了定量和算法层面的加强，回应了动态输出反馈H∞策略搜索中有限时间最优性差距的开放问题。

Abstract: We study continuous-time full-order dynamic output-feedback $H_\infty$ policy search, a nonconvex and nonsmooth problem. Direct policy search is a central paradigm in reinforcement learning and continuous control, but rigorous guarantees remain scarce in robust output-feedback settings. The $H_\infty$ problem is a canonical benchmark because it captures disturbance attenuation and robustness while exposing the hard nonsmooth geometry of policy-space optimization. We prove that on the exact identity-gauge slice of the extended convex lift, $\varepsilon$-stationarity yields $O(\varepsilon)$-suboptimality on compact exact slices, which in turn yields convergence-rate guarantees for nonsmooth policy-search methods. This result addresses the finite-time optimality-gap question raised by Guo and Hu [2022] in the more general dynamic output-feedback $H_\infty$ policy-search setting. We further use the established value equivalence supplied by extended convex lifting to formulate a nonstrict-feasibility bisection method with one final strict-feasibility recovery step, yielding an explicit $\varepsilon$-optimal stabilizing controller. These results provide a quantitative and algorithmic strengthening of prior qualitative optimality theory for nonsmooth $H_\infty$ policy search.

</details>


### [97] [Weak Convexity and Proximal Bundle Methods for Nonsmooth Policy Optimization in Robust Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06202)
*Yuto Watanabe,Feng-Yi Liao,Yang Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散时间鲁棒H∞控制中静态输出反馈的策略优化问题，首次提出了具有确定性非渐近复杂度保证的可行性保持算法。该方法基于H∞代价函数的弱凸性和状态反馈情形下的弱Polyak–Łojasiewicz不等式，开发了近端束方法，复杂度为O(max{η⁻⁴, ε⁻²})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒H∞控制中的静态输出反馈策略优化自然归结为非光滑、非凸的优化问题，现有方法缺乏严格的复杂度保证，且难以保证迭代过程中反馈增益始终维持系统的稳定性。

Method: 首先建立H∞代价函数的结构性质：在每个子水平集的凸子集上证明代价是弱凸的；对状态反馈情形进一步建立弱Polyak–Łojasiewicz不等式，确保每个驻点都是全局最优。基于这些性质，开发近端束方法（proximal bundle method），该方法仅需函数值和次梯度信息，可视为近端点法的可实现近似。

Result: 证明了所有迭代点始终保持系统稳定性，并建立了寻找(η, ε)-驻点的确定性非渐近复杂度界O(max{η⁻⁴, ε⁻²})，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为H∞策略优化提供了具备确定性非渐近复杂度保证的可行性保持算法，填补了该领域理论分析上的空白，并为非光滑非凸鲁棒控制优化开辟了新的分析路径。

Abstract: We study policy optimization for discrete-time robust $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ control with static output-feedback, and present the first feasibility-preserving algorithm with a deterministic, non-asymptotic complexity guarantee. This problem naturally leads to a nonsmooth and nonconvex optimization over the set of stabilizing feedback gains. We first establish several structural properties of the $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ cost. In particular, we show that the cost is weakly convex on every convex subset of a sublevel set. For the state-feedback case, we further establish a weak Polyak--Łojasiewicz inequality, which ensures that every stationary point is globally optimal. Building on these properties, we develop a proximal bundle method for $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ policy optimization. The proposed method can be viewed as an implementable approximation of the proximal point method and uses only function value and subgradient information. We show that all iterates remain stabilizing and establish a deterministic non-asymptotic complexity bound of $\mathcal{O}(\max\{η^{-4},ε^{-2}\})$ for finding an $(η,ε)$-stationary point. Numerical experiments illustrate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [98] [Quantitative comparison of closed- and open-loop linear-quadratic $N$-player differential games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06233)
*Asaf Cohen,Jiamin Jian*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在有限时域随机线性二次N人博弈中，比较了闭环与开环纳什均衡。通过引入块对角参考博弈及邻近扰动博弈，证明两信息结构下均衡状态与控制过程在参考博弈中完全一致，并给出弱扰动下的可解性、稳定性及定量界，证明当扰动随人数快速衰减时两者渐近等价。比较在有限玩家层面直接进行，无需可交换性或平均场极限假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多人随机线性二次博弈中，闭环与开环信息结构下的均衡通常由不同的Riccati系统刻画，二者关系缺乏系统理解。弄清二者差异与联系对于博弈论、控制理论与经济建模具有重要意义。

Method: 构造块对角参考博弈与其邻近扰动博弈，分别在闭环和开环信息结构下求解；对比两种信息结构导致的Riccati系统，证明参考博弈中二者诱导的均衡状态与控制过程完全一致；利用弱扰动分析证明可解性与稳定性，推导均衡间的定量误差界。

Result: 参考博弈中闭环与开环均衡状态和控制过程完全一致；在状态成本弱扰动下博弈仍可解且稳定，并获得均衡间定量界；当扰动随玩家数快速衰减时，闭环与开环均衡渐近等价。结果在有限玩家层面直接成立，无需可交换性或平均场极限。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了有限人数随机线性二次博弈中闭环与开环纳什均衡之间等价性的充分条件与定量刻画，为在大规模博弈中利用计算上更简便的开环化解释或逼近闭环均衡提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We compare closed-loop and open-loop Nash equilibria in a finite-horizon stochastic linear-quadratic $N$-player game with decoupled state dynamics and interaction through the state costs. We introduce a block-diagonal reference game and a nearby perturbed game, and study each under both information structures. The closed-loop and open-loop problems lead to different Riccati systems, but for the reference game their induced equilibrium state and control processes coincide exactly. We then prove solvability and stability under weak perturbations of the state costs, yielding quantitative bounds between the corresponding equilibria. In particular, when the perturbation decreases sufficiently fast with the population size, the closed-loop and open-loop equilibria of the perturbed game become asymptotically equivalent. The comparison is carried out directly at the finite-player level, without requiring exchangeability or a mean-field limit.

</details>


### [99] [Isolated calmness of regularized linear inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06238)
*Tran T. A. Nghia,Huy N. Pham,Nghia V. Vo,Khoa V. H. Vu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了凸正则化线性逆问题中解映射的孤立平静性（isolated calmness）性质，建立了基于切锥的刻画条件。对于凸分段线性二次正则子，孤立平静性与解唯一性等价，提供了简便的验证方法；对于分析稀疏正则化，该条件可通过线性规划验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸正则化线性逆问题广泛应用于信号处理、图像重建和统计学习等领域，理解其解映射的稳定性/灵敏度性质对于算法设计和误差分析至关重要。孤立平静性是变分分析中刻画解映射稳定性的重要概念，但目前对此类问题缺乏系统的刻画条件，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文采用变分分析的工具，主要是通过切锥（tangent cone）来刻画孤立平静性性质。具体而言：1) 建立孤立平静性的切锥特征刻画定理；2) 针对凸分段线性二次正则子，利用其结构将孤立平静性与解唯一性建立等价关系；3) 对于分析稀疏正则化（如变换域中的l1范数），将所建立的条件进一步转化为线性规划可验证的形式。

Result: 得到了孤立平静性性质的切锥刻画条件；证明了对于凸分段线性二次正则子，孤立平静性等价于解的唯一性；并为分析稀疏正则化提供了基于线性规划的验证方法。

Conclusion: 本文为凸正则化线性逆问题的孤立平静性提供了完整的理论刻画和实用的验证工具，特别是将抽象的变分分析条件化简为容易实施的计算步骤（如线性规划），大大增强了理论的可用性。

Abstract: This paper studies the isolated calmness property of solution mappings arising from convex regularized linear inverse problems. We mainly establish a tangent-cone characterization of this property. For convex piecewise linear-quadratic regularizers, the isolated calmness coincides with the solution uniqueness, leading to simple verification procedures. In particular, for analysis sparsity regularization, our condition can be verified by linear programming.

</details>


### [100] [Regularized coordinate minimization for nonconvex composite optimization with application to quantized image compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06260)
*Daniela Lupu,George T. Samoila,Adina M. Florea,Ion Necoara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种正则化循环坐标最小化方法，用于求解非凸复合优化问题（目标函数为一个二次连续可微项与一个简单可分离项之和）。作者分析了该方法的收敛行为，并证明其可高效应用于量化矩阵分解问题（如无损/有损图像压缩）。在KODAK和CLIC 2024数据集上，该方法在低比特率下显著优于JPEG，且在分类任务（AlexNet、ResNet50、MobileNetV2）评估中，浮点与整数表示没有明显精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸复合优化问题广泛存在，现有坐标下降方法虽各有优点，但缺乏统一的收敛性分析框架。同时，量化矩阵分解在图像压缩等实际应用中具有重要作用，需要一种既有理论保障又计算高效的求解算法。

Method: 提出一种正则化的循环坐标最小化（regularized cyclic coordinate minimization）方法。目标函数形式为平滑非凸项加简单可分项。作者对算法的收敛率进行理论分析，给出达到一阶最优性准则和客观残差（objective residual）的收敛速率取决于不同问题假设。随后将该框架应用于量化矩阵分解问题，解决有损图像压缩中的瓶颈。

Result: 在KODAK与CLIC 2024图像数据集上，该方法在低比特率下显著优于JPEG，节省了每像素比特数且无明显质量退化；在高比特率下与JPEG性能相当。此外，在ImageNet分类任务中分别使用AlexNet、ResNet50和MobileNetV2（浮点与整数表示）评估原始和量化压缩图像，两种数值表示间无明显的精度下降。

Conclusion: 该算法在理论收敛性上具有保证，且在图像压缩和分类等真实数据任务中比现有成熟方法更具灵活性和效率，展示了其在低比特率图像压缩等领域的实用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a regularized cyclic coordinate minimization method for solving nonconvex composite optimization problems having the objective function formed as the sum of two terms, one is twice continuously differentiable and the second term is simple and separable. We analyze the convergence behaviour of our coordinate minimization method, in particular we provide convergence rates to a first-order optimality criterion and objective residual depending on the assumptions on the problem. Then, we show that our algorithmic framework can be efficiently applied for solving quantized matrix factorization problems that arise in e.g., lossy image compression. More specifically, on KODAK and CLIC 2024 datasets, our method notably outperforms JPEG at low bit rates, achieving savings in bits per pixel without excessive degradation and remains comparable at higher bit rates. Moreover, we evaluate both the original and quantized compressed images in a classification task using 3 well-known convolutional networks AlexNet, ResNet50 and MobileNetV2 based on floating point and integer arithmetic representations. Remarkably, when comparing the two numerical representations of the convolutional networks, there is no substantially accuracy degradation on ImageNet dataset. The numerical results on image compression and classification using real data show the flexibility and efficiency of our algorithm compared to well-established methods from the literature.

</details>


### [101] [Riccati Stability Without Auxiliary Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06275)
*Jeffrey Humpherys*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出了Riccati稳定性的新刻画，通过预解族的标量不变量（协方差半径）实现，该不变量避免引入额外矩阵，介于逐点谱半径与小增益水平之间，并关联了算子空间谱半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erik Verriest在2004年提出了一个开放问题：如何在不引入额外矩阵的情况下刻画Riccati稳定性。本文旨在解决这一公开问题。

Method: 引入预解族的标量不变量（协方差半径），通过至多n^2+1个频率-方向对的协方差平衡来定义；利用有限维分离论证证明该协方差半径等于预解族的最优公共椭球范数；结合严格有界实引理导出Riccati稳定性的充要条件。

Result: 得到了仅涉及第一矩阵Hurwitz性质和一个标量不等式的Riccati稳定性充要条件。该不变量对单个矩阵退化为普通谱半径，位于逐点谱半径与无标度小增益水平之间，且与Shalit和Shamovich的算子空间谱半径在自然预解函数空间上一致。

Conclusion: 本文通过协方差半径这一标量不变量完整解决了Verriest提出的Riccati稳定性刻画问题，为控制理论中的Riccati稳定性提供了简洁而深刻的几何与算子理论视角。

Abstract: In the 2004 collection \emph{Unsolved Problems in Mathematical Systems and Control Theory}, Erik Verriest posed the problem of characterizing Riccati stability ``without invoking additional matrices.'' We give such a characterization through a scalar invariant of the resolvent family. The invariant is defined by covariance balances using at most $n^2+1$ frequency--direction pairs. A finite-dimensional separation argument shows that this covariance radius equals the optimal common ellipsoidal norm of the resolvent family. Combined with the strict bounded real lemma, this yields a necessary and sufficient condition for Riccati stability involving only the Hurwitz property of the first matrix and a single scalar inequality. The invariant reduces to the ordinary spectral radius for one matrix, lies between the pointwise spectral-radius and unscaled small-gain levels of the resolvent family, and coincides with the operator-space spectral radius of Shalit and Shamovich for the natural resolvent function space.

</details>


### [102] [1.73-Optimal Echelon Stock (R,nQ) Policies in Two-Stage Stochastic Serial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06318)
*Ming Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有单位泊松需求、两级连续盘点序列库存系统中的经典(R,nQ)策略。作者提出了一个保持设置的成本分配下界，证明了在经典(R,nQ)策略类内，对于全部非负参数空间，infimum成本至多为最优成本的1.73倍，并且边界情形下该比值退化为1，在K_2=0时改进为5/3。数值实验显示实际比例中位数为1.007，最坏情况1.6004。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典两级序列库存系统中的(R,nQ)策略虽提供简单操作规则，但现有均匀保证要么限制提前期或诱导批量大小，要么依赖于特定原语，未能回答整数比例受限类是否能在完整原语空间上提供任何均匀保证的问题。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者开发了一种保持设置的（setup-preserving）成本分配下界方法，通过与速率平衡（rate-balanced）的可允许对照类比较来分析(R,nQ)策略的成本性能。分析涵盖精确整数批量、两种设置约定（每次正发货计费和每个完整批次计费），并在边界情形（lambda=0, p=0, h_2=0, K_2=0）下推导精确比例。

Result: 证明了(R,nQ)策略类的最优成本至多为最优成本的1.73倍（对所有非负参数成立）；边界条件下比值为1，K_2=0时比值为5/3。数值实验在三个广参数网格上最大比值1.098（中位数1.007），对抗性压力测试下为1.6004，且在该点不允许Stage 2发送不完整Q_1批次没有发现成本收益。

Conclusion: 简单的(R,nQ)策略将固定批次实现与完整非负原语空间上的均匀保证结合在一起，表明该经典策略不仅在操作上简单直接，理论上也具有合理的性能保证。

Abstract: We consider a classical two-stage continuous-review serial inventory system with unit-sized Poisson demand of rate lambda, backlog cost rate p, lead times L_1, L_2, echelon holding-cost rates h_1, h_2, and fixed shipment costs K_1, K_2. The classical echelon stock (R,nQ) policy offers a simple operating rule: Stage 1 orders a fixed Q_1-lot, Stage 2 orders n >= 1 such lots at a time, and a Stage-1 request waits until a complete lot is available. Existing uniform guarantees restrict lead times or induced lot sizes, and the known all-instance guarantee is primitive-dependent, leaving open whether this restrictive integer-ratio class admits any uniform guarantee over the full primitive space. We develop a setup-preserving cost-allocation lower bound and show that the infimum cost within the classical class is at most 1.73 times the optimal cost over a rate-balanced admissible comparison class, for every lambda, p, L_1, L_2, h_1, h_2, K_1, K_2 >= 0. The result holds for exact integer lot sizes and under both setup conventions, one charging each positive dispatch and the other every complete lot. On the boundaries the guarantee is one, as an equality of infima, when lambda=0, p=0, or h_2=0, and improves to 5/3 when K_2=0. Numerically, across three broad parameter grids, the best-found (R,nQ) policy costs at most 1.098 times the evaluated lower bound, with a median ratio of 1.007; a deliberately adversarial stress test reports 1.6004 under the conservative lot-cost convention. At that point, finite searches under shipment-cost accounting find no benefit from allowing Stage 2 to ship an incomplete Q_1-lot, which is consistent with looseness in the evaluated lower bound. The simple (R,nQ) policy, therefore, combines a straightforward fixed-lot implementation with a uniform guarantee over the full nonnegative primitive space.

</details>


### [103] [Averaged proximal reflected gradient method for monotone variational inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06409)
*Xiaokai Chang,Jialin Li,Jun Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对单调变分不等式（MVI）提出一种投影反射梯度（PRG）的平均化变体方法，通过构造新的李雅普诺夫函数建立了收敛理论，改进了黄金比例算法，并引入无需线搜索的全自适应步长策略，在多个数值实验中显著优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Malitsky提出的投影反射梯度（PRG）方法在求解单调变分不等式时十分高效，但现有步长上界因理论分析中的不等式放缩而不够紧致。此外，如何在更一般的MVI设定下进一步提升算法效率并实现自适应步长选择仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 构造PRG方法的平均化变体以处理更一般的单调变分不等式；提出一种新颖的李雅普诺夫函数用于建立收敛性理论；该平均化方法改进了黄金比例算法[Malitsky, Math. Program., 2020]；所采用的步长与经典方法（如Popov外梯度和前向-反射-后向方法）兼容；提出一种无需线搜索的全自适应策略来调整步长，能产生闭式且可能更大的步长。

Result: 数值实验在Nash-Cournot均衡、HpHard和图像重建等问题上表明，所提出的算法在性能上显著优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过平均化PRG方法并结合全自适应步长策略，不仅放宽了步长限制、改进了收敛理论，还在实际应用中大幅提升了求解性能，为单调变分不等式提供了一种更高效的可选举措。

Abstract: Projected reflected gradient (PRG) method proposed by Malitsky is efficient for solving monotone variational inequality (MVI), while the existing upper bound of step size is not tight due to the inequality scaling in the theoretical analysis. In this paper, we construct an averaged variant of PRG method for more general MVI and present a novel Lyapunov function to establish convergent theory. This averaged PRG method provides an improvement of the golden ratio algorithm [Y, Malitsky, Math. Program., 184, 383-410, 2020], and the involved step size is compatible with that for the classical methods, such as Popov's extragradient and forward-reflected-backward methods. Moreover, a fully adaptive strategy without linesearch is presented to adjust step sizes, which generates closed-form and potentially much larger step sizes. Numerical experiments on the Nash-Cournot equilibrium, HpHard, and image reconstruction problems demonstrate that the proposed algorithm significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [104] [Fast Rates and Strong Convergence of Tikhonov-Regularized Mixed-Order Primal-Dual Dynamics for Linearly Constrained Optimization Without Eventual Ball Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06420)
*Hong-lu Li,Yi-bin Xiao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有隐式Hessian阻尼和Tikhonov正则化的混合阶原始-对偶动力系统，用于有限维欧氏空间中的线性约束凸优化问题。在一般Tikhonov正则化系数下证明了原始轨迹强收敛到最小范数解，对偶乘子收敛到兼容KKT乘子，并给出了改进的收敛率估计。对于临界情形ε(t)=c/t²，建立了更尖锐的收敛率o(t⁻²)和o(t⁻¹)，改进了文献中的O(t⁻²)和O(t⁻¹)估计。在无约束极限情形下，回答了Attouch和László提出的强收敛性开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性约束凸优化问题在科学计算与工程中广泛存在。现有的原始-对偶动力学系统在Tikhonov正则化下缺乏对原始轨迹强收敛性的完整刻画，且已有收敛率估计不够尖锐。特别是Attouch和László在2024年提出的临界阻尼情形下的强收敛性开放问题尚未解决，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者构建了一类新的能量函数（energy functions），系统地将原始方程（二阶，含粘性阻尼δ√ε(t)ẋ(t)）与乘子方程（一阶）耦合分析。通过精巧的能量衰减估计与Lyapunov分析技术，在一般Tikhonov正则化系数ε(t)下导出了原始轨迹的强收敛性及各量收敛率，无需任何轨道内外球（inside/outside-ball）条件；对临界参数ε(t)=c/t²进行了专门的精细化分析，并在有限维无约束极限下处理开放问题。

Result: 证明了原始轨迹强收敛到最小范数解，乘子收敛到兼容KKT乘子；Lagrangian间隙、可行性违反和目标残差的收敛率为o(ε(t))，速度范数收敛率为o(√ε(t))。在临界情形ε(t)=c/t²下，建立了更尖锐的收敛率o(t⁻²)（对间隙、可行性违反、目标残差）和o(t⁻¹)（对速度范数），优于文献中相应O(t⁻²)和O(t⁻¹)的衰减估计。此外，在有限维无约束情形下解答了Attouch和László关于临界正则下强收敛性的开放问题。

Conclusion: 该研究为带Tikhonov正则化的混合阶原始-对偶动力系统提供了完整的强收敛性与精确收敛率理论，所构建的能量函数方法避免了以往文献中繁琐的轨道内外条件假设，显著推广了现有结果，并解决了公开的开放问题，对凸优化动力学算法的理论设计具有重要参考意义。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a Tikhonov-regularized mixed-order primal--dual dynamical system with implicit Hessian damping for linearly constrained convex optimization problems in finite-dimensional Euclidean spaces, where the primal equation is second order and incorporates the viscous damping term \(δ\sqrt{\varepsilon(t)}\,\dot x(t)\), whereas the multiplier equation remains first order. By constructing a new class of energy functions, for a general Tikhonov regularization coefficient \(\varepsilon(t)\), we prove the strong convergence of the primal trajectory and derive fast convergence rates under the same parameter assumptions, without imposing any eventual inside/outside-ball condition. More precisely, the primal trajectory converges to the minimum-norm solution, and the multiplier converges to a compatible KKT multiplier, while the convergence rates of the Lagrangian gap, feasibility violation, and objective residual are \(o(\varepsilon(t))\), and the convergence rate of the velocity norm is \(o(\sqrt{\varepsilon(t)})\). For the critical case \(\varepsilon(t)=c/t^2\), in which the damping coefficient \(δ\sqrt{\varepsilon(t)}\) reduces to \(δ\sqrt{c}/t\), we establish the sharper convergence rates \(o(t^{-2})\) for the Lagrangian gap, feasibility violation, and objective residual, together with \(o(t^{-1})\) for the velocity norm, which improve the corresponding \(O(t^{-2})\) and \(O(t^{-1})\) decay estimates obtained in the related literature. Most importantly, when the proposed dynamical system is specialized to the finite-dimensional unconstrained setting, our analysis answers the open question on strong convergence in this critical regime posed by Attouch and László [Math. Methods Oper. Res., 99 (2024), pp.~307--347].

</details>


### [105] [Lie bracket approximations of RMSprop for Extremum Seeking Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06505)
*Victoria Grushkovskaya,Christian Ebenbauer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了受RMSProp动力学启发、基于李括号逼近技术的新型极值搜索算法。作者引入了一种利用二阶李括号的极值搜索方案，使得在李括号系统中能够激励梯度平方项，从而继承经典连续时间RMSProp算法的行为。此外，还提出了两种改进的RMSProp型算法，仅需一阶李括号逼近即可实现，降低了极值搜索控制设计的复杂度。最后通过数值仿真对三种算法进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将RMSProp优化算法的自适应学习率特性引入到极值搜索控制中，同时探索如何利用李括号逼近技术构建能够体现RMSProp动力学行为的极值搜索方案，并降低算法实现的复杂度。

Method: 本文采用Lie bracket逼近技术设计极值搜索算法。首先，利用二阶李括号构造RMSProp型极值搜索方案，以在系统中实现梯度平方项的激励；随后，提出两种改进算法，通过合理的结构设计仅需一阶李括号逼近即可近似RMSProp行为，从而简化控制设计。三种算法通过数值仿真进行比较验证。

Result: 提出了三种RMSProp类极值搜索算法：一种基于二阶李括号的完整方案以及两种基于一阶李括号近似的简化方案，并通过数值仿真对比了三种算法的性能差异。

Conclusion: 本文证明了基于李括号技术可将RMSProp自适应优化机制嵌入到极值搜索控制框架中，同时通过一阶近似方案在保持RMSProp特性的前提下显著降低了实现复杂度，为自适应优化与控制相结合提供了新途径。

Abstract: This paper presents novel extremum seeking algorithms inspired by RMSProp dynamics and based on Lie bracket approximation techniques. To inherit the behavior of the classical continuous-time RMSProp algorithm, we introduce an extremum seeking scheme that exploits second-order Lie brackets, allowing the excitation of squared gradient terms in the resulting Lie bracket system. Moreover, we propose two modified RMSProp-type algorithms that can be implemented using only first-order Lie bracket approximations, thereby reducing the complexity of the extremum seeking control design. The three resulting extremum seeking algorithms are compared through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [106] [Localize, Restart, Accelerate: Stochastic Optimization under Generalized Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06555)
*Darina Dvinskikh,Alexander Gasnikov,Aleksandr Lobanov,Ilgam Latypov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非对称(L0,L1)-广义光滑条件下的随机凸优化问题，提出两阶段加速算法ARC-SG。该算法第一阶段利用广义光滑感知的随机步长降低过大梯度，第二阶段在认证光滑球内通过重启的加速求解器解强凸近质子问题。算法实现了查询级别的认证-局部化构造，在高概率下获得加速优化贡献和光滑子类最优的精度统计依赖，并在L1=0时恢复经典加速随机优化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于机器学习目标函数局域曲率可能随梯度范数增长的现象。在非对称(L0,L1)-广义光滑假设下进行加速很困难：动量可能进入曲率更大的区域，而随机梯度无法可靠地证明无约束轨迹的有效性。已有的随机凸优化结果通常假设光滑模型是全局均匀的，难以刻画这类自适应曲率的目标函数；同时不对称广义光滑模型此前缺乏有效的加速算法与强凸保证。

Method: 提出ARC-SG两阶段加速方法。Phase I采用广义光滑感知的随机步来压缩过大的梯度；Phase II将外层问题转化为强凸近质子问题，并用受限在认证光滑球内的重启加速求解器求解。关键设计是精确近端点不增加梯度范数，从而使得光滑性证书在外层循环中得以传播。该构造是逐查询的认证-局部化，并显式计入广义光滑因子，附带强凸重启扩展。

Result: ARC-SG在高概率下实现加速优化贡献，并获得光滑子类最优的精度统计依赖（仅差对数项与广义光滑因子）。其凸情形精度指数与同期另一项在更宽光滑和仿射方差模型下的公开随机加速结果一致；本工作的区别在于认证几何、显式参数核算和强凸保证。当L1=0时恢复经典加速随机随机率。在具有无界梯度的目标上实验验证了两阶段机制及有限预算优势。

Conclusion: ARC-SG通过在认证的局部几何内受限执行加速优化，克服了不对称广义光滑下随机加速固有的曲率不确定性难题，同时提供了显式的广义光滑因子核算与强凸重启保证，与经典结果及同期独立工作一致并对齐了精度依赖。

Abstract: We study stochastic convex optimization under asymmetric \((L_0,L_1)\)-generalized smoothness, a model motivated by machine-learning objectives whose local curvature may grow with the gradient norm. We assume an unbiased first-order oracle with additive norm-sub-Gaussian noise. Acceleration is difficult in this setting because momentum may enter regions of much larger curvature, while stochastic gradients cannot reliably certify an unrestricted trajectory. We propose \textsf{ARC-SG}, a two-phase accelerated method: Phase~I reduces excessively large gradients using a generalized-smoothness-aware stochastic step, then Phase~II solves strongly convex proximal subproblems by a restarted accelerated solver confined to certified smoothness balls. Exact proximal points do not increase the gradient norm, allowing these certificates to propagate through the outer loop. The contribution is a query-by-query certified-localization construction with explicit generalized-smoothness factors and a strongly convex restart extension. \textsf{ARC-SG} achieves, with high probability, an accelerated optimization contribution and smooth-subclass-optimal statistical dependence on accuracy, up to logarithmic and generalized-smoothness factors. Its convex accuracy exponents agree with a contemporaneous public stochastic-acceleration result under a broader smoothness and affine-variance model; our distinction is the certified geometry, explicit parameter accounting, and strongly convex guarantee. The results recover classical accelerated stochastic rates when \(L_1=0\). Experiments on objectives with unbounded gradients illustrate the two-phase mechanism and its finite-budget advantage.

</details>


### [107] [Stochastic Nonconvex Bilevel Optimization: Improved Rates Without Rare-Visit Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06580)
*Daniel Cortild,Mathias Staudigl,Juan Peypouquet,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究平滑且可能非凸的随机简单双层优化问题，提出对动态障碍梯度下降（DBGD）乘子进行仅分母正则化的简单修改，在无需轨迹依赖的"稀有访问"假设下实现了快速收敛，达到(ε,ε)-平稳性所需的复杂度为O(ε^-2)次迭代、O(ε^-4)上层随机梯度和O(ε^-7)下层随机梯度，改进了现有无假设方法的最优复杂度，并推导了任意时刻（anytime）参数调度方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机扩展动态障碍梯度下降（DBGD）方法存在两难困境：要么需要轨迹依赖且无法验证的"稀有访问"假设以获得快速收敛，要么在去除该假设时付出显著更高的oracle代价。作者希望消除这一不切实际的假设，同时保留快速收敛速率。

Method: 该方法仅对DBGD迭代中的乘子引入分母正则化（denominator-only regularization），这是对原算法的一个简单修改，无需改变整体框架即可消除对轨迹依赖假设的需求。此外，作者还推导了任意时刻（anytime）参数调度方案。

Result: 所提方法实现了(ε,ε)-平稳性，复杂度为O(ε^-2)次迭代、O(ε^-4)次上层随机梯度调用和O(ε^-7)次下层随机梯度调用，优于现有的无假设方法的复杂度界。

Conclusion: 通过简单的分母正则化修改DBGD乘子，即可在不依赖不可验证假设的前提下获得快速收敛，同时实现更优的oracle复杂度，为随机简单双层优化提供了更实用的算法选择。

Abstract: We investigate stochastic simple bilevel optimization with smooth and possibly nonconvex upper- and lower-level objectives. Existing stochastic extensions of dynamic barrier gradient descent (DBGD) either obtain fast convergence under an unverifiable trajectory-dependent ``rare-visit'' assumption, or remove this assumption at a substantially higher oracle cost. We show that a simple denominator-only regularization of the DBGD multiplier eliminates the need for such an assumption while preserving fast convergence rates. Specifically, our method achieves $(\varepsilon, \varepsilon)$-stationarity in $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iterations using $O(\varepsilon^{-4})$ upper-level and $O(\varepsilon^{-7})$ lower-level stochastic gradients, which improves upon the best assumption-free complexities. We additionally derive anytime parameter schedules.

</details>


### [108] [Input-to-State Stabilization of a Coupled ODE-PDE System with Time-Varying Coefficients via Composite Boundary Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06599)
*Yongchun Bi,Jun Zheng,Guchuan Zhu,Jiye Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种复合边界反馈控制律，用于确保具有时变系数的耦合ODE-抛物型PDE系统的输入到状态稳定性（ISS）。通过解析预定义增益函数和时变Volterra核函数避免求解耦合时变抛物-双曲核方程，并利用非二次Lyapunov函数和广义Lyapunov泛函建立系统的L²范数ISS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合ODE-PDE系统广泛存在于实际工程中，而该系统中同时存在时变系数和Dirichlet边界扰动，使得传统反步法需直接求解耦合时变核方程（难度高且计算复杂）。因此需要一种避免求解复杂核方程、同时能处理时变系数与边界扰动双重挑战的鲁棒控制设计方法。

Method: （1）控制器设计：采用解析预定义增益函数与时间依赖的Volterra核函数，显式构造复合边界反馈控制律，绕开对耦合时变抛物-双曲型核方程的直接求解。（2）稳定性分析：在目标系统中，对ODE子系统采用含时变正定矩阵平方根和超线性函数构造的非二次Lyapunov函数；对PDE子系统构造广义Lyapunov泛函，综合建立闭环系统在L²范数意义下的ISS性质。

Result: 成功建立了闭环系统在L²范数下的输入到状态稳定性（ISS），并通过数值仿真验证了所提控制方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为受Dirichlet边界扰动和时变系数影响的耦合ODE-抛物型PDE系统提供了一种无需求解复杂核方程即可实现的鲁棒边界控制策略，所提出的非二次Lyapunov构造方法为该类系统的ISS分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel composite boundary feedback control law that ensures input-to-state stability (ISS) for a coupled ODE-parabolic PDE system with time-varying coefficients in both subsystems. In controller design, we circumvent the need to directly solve coupled time-varying parabolic-hyperbolic kernel equations by employing an analytic pre-defined gain function and a time-varying Volterra kernel function to design the control law explicitly. In stability analysis, to address the simultaneous challenges of Dirichlet boundary disturbances and time-varying coefficients, we employ the square root of a time-varying positive definite matrix and a superlinear function to construct a nonquadratic Lyapunov function for the ODE and a generalized Lyapunov functional for the PDE, respectively, in the target system, thereby establishing the ISS in the $L^2$-norm of the closed-loop system. Numerical simulations are presented to illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed control scheme.

</details>


### [109] [An Exact Dual for Second-Order Cone Programming Using Only Lorentz-Cone Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06757)
*Hao Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文针对二阶锥规划在不满足约束规范条件下可能出现对偶间隙或对偶值不可达的问题，构造了一个精确对偶。该对偶仅使用仿射方程和Lorentz锥乘积中的隶属约束，每个可行对偶点都能给出有效界，且当原始问题可行且有限时，对偶能达到该值。论文还构造了一个仿射二阶锥系统，其可行性恰好等价于给定仿射二阶锥系统的不可行性（包括弱不可行情形）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Pólik和Terlaky提出并在后续工作中反复强调的问题：是否存在仅使用Lorentz锥约束的二阶锥规划的精确对偶。在缺少约束规范条件下，二阶锥规划会存在对偶间隙或对偶值不可达的问题，因此需要构造一种不依赖约束规范条件的精确对偶框架。

Method: 对任意具有实数系数的二阶锥规划，构造了一个使用仿射方程和Lorentz锥乘积隶属约束的精确对偶。该构造对输入系数统一处理，仅使用固定有理常数，且两种公式（精确对偶和不可行性判定系统）均具有多项式规模。

Result: 构造的精确对偶具有以下性质：(1) 每个可行对偶点都提供有效的原始目标值下界；(2) 当原始问题可行且目标函数有限时，对偶能够达到该值。同时构造了仿射二阶锥系统，其可行性与给定系统的不可行性精确对应，包括弱不可行情形。这是关于精确实数数据的公式规模结果，但不蕴含多项式时间可解性或多项式位长的有理证书。

Conclusion: 论文肯定地回答了Pólik和Terlaky提出的问题，证明仅使用Lorentz锥约束即可构造二阶锥规划的精确对偶，且构造具有多项式规模。这为处理缺少约束规范条件的二阶锥规划提供了理论上的精确对偶框架。

Abstract: Without a constraint qualification, second-order cone programs can have a duality gap or an unattained dual value. For every such program with arbitrary real coefficients, we construct an exact dual using only affine equations and memberships in products of Lorentz cones. Every feasible dual point gives a valid bound. Whenever the primal is feasible with finite value, the dual attains that value. This answers a question raised by Pólik and Terlaky and reiterated in subsequent work: whether an exact dual for second-order cone programming can use only Lorentz-cone constraints. The same construction produces an affine second-order cone system that is feasible exactly when a given affine second-order cone system is infeasible, including in the weakly infeasible case. Both formulations have polynomial size and are obtained uniformly from the input coefficients using only fixed rational constants. These are formulation-size results over exact real data; they do not imply polynomial-time solvability or polynomial bounds on the bit length of rational certificates.

</details>


### [110] [Gramian-Informed Framework for Edge Flow Network Analysis Against Nodal Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06897)
*Prasad Vilas Chanekar,Bala Kameshwar Poolla,Jorge Cortés*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了节点输入对网络系统边缘流的一阶影响，通过引入脆弱性矩阵（VM）并基于可控性格拉姆矩阵提供解析表达式，揭示了网络拓扑与动态特性之间的交互作用在决定节点影响力中的关键角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络系统中，节点扰动如何影响边缘流（edge flows）是一个基础但重要的问题。现有方法缺乏系统化的工具来刻画节点输入的"一阶效应"及其在网络中的传播特性，因此需要一种基于可控性格拉姆矩阵的理论框架来量化这种影响。

Method: 作者定义了一种脆弱性矩阵（Vulnerability Matrix, VM），考虑离散及连续时间动力学系统，涵盖脉冲与阶跃两类输入扰动。针对有向线状网络（directed line networks），给出了以边权重、输入持续时间和到节点输入的图距离为参数的闭式解析表达式。数值实验在有向线状网络和随机Erdős-Rényi网络上进行验证。

Result: 数值仿真表明，由VM识别出的关键/有影响力节点，与传统时域性能指标（如H₂和H∞范数）所识别的结果高度吻合，验证了VM作为节点影响力度量工具的有效性。

Conclusion: 脆弱性矩阵作为一种基于Gramian矩阵的解析框架，能够有效刻画节点输入对网络边缘流的一阶影响，且结果与经典的H₂/H∞性能指标高度一致，可用于网络系统的鲁棒性与脆弱性分析。

Abstract: We study the first-order effects of nodal inputs on edge flows of network systems through controllability Gramians. We consider both discrete- and continuous-time dynamics subject to impulse and step input disturbances. To characterize their impact on network behavior, we introduce the notion of a vulnerability matrix (VM) and provide explicit Gramian-based expressions that reveal the critical role played by the interplay between network topology and dynamics. For the class of directed line networks, we particularize these closed-form expressions in terms of edge weights, input duration, and graph distance to the nodal input. Numerical simulations on directed line and random Erdős-Rényi networks show a tight correspondence between influential nodes identified with the VM and conventional time-domain performance metrics, such as $\mathscr{H}_2$ and $\mathscr{H}_{\infty}$.

</details>


### [111] [First-Order Optimization under Uniform Nondegeneracy: Geometry, Computation, and Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06946)
*Hua Su,Lei Zhang,Jin Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出"均匀非退化"（uniform nondegeneracy）概念，将其从经典强凸/强凸-强凹优化中解耦：仅要求曲率与零均匀分离但允许符号可变，无需指定正负分裂。在此设定下仍可建立完整的一阶优化理论：通过一阶割线不等式构造全局双Lipschitz同胚梯度与唯一驻点；配对符号Moreau包络构造光滑标量度标函数，使配对近端下降法达到全局线性收敛与维度无关的一阶oracle复杂度；同时证明在受限域上可计算性可能崩溃（维度灾难），并提出"已验证可行性"（certified feasibility）作为可观察的局部化条件以实现可行延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典强凸最小化及其自然非定号推广（强凸-强凹极小极大问题）同时要求曲率具有定量控制和指定的曲率方向。作者希望解耦这两种作用：仅保留曲率均匀与零分离（符号可正可负、无预定正负划分），探究仅凭此更弱的条件能在多大程度上重建熟悉的优化理论。

Method: 1) 通过一阶割线（secant）不等式给出内在刻画，证明梯度是R^d上的全局bi-Lipschitz同胚，从而存在唯一驻点。2) 配对带符号的Moreau包络，构造光滑标量度标（merit）函数，在零阶与一阶层面恢复缺失的下降几何，由此设计配对近端下降方法。3) 在受限域情形下分析信息复杂度，证明仅假设域内驻点存在可能面临维度灾难（即使有无穷阶oracle）；引入"已验证可行性"作为可观测的局部化条件以突破该信息势垒，实现可行延拓。

Result: 建立了仅基于均匀非退化的完整一阶优化理论：配对近端下降法实现全局线性收敛及维度无关的一阶oracle复杂度；同时揭示了在受限域上该可计算性的边界——无额外可观测条件时出现维度灾难，而"已验证可行性"条件可克服此信息障碍。

Conclusion: 曲率的"方向规定"并非强凸优化理论可计算性结果所必需，均匀非退化（仅要求曲率与零均匀分离）本身即可在全局域上重构一阶方法的线性收敛理论；而在受限域上，问题的可计算性高度依赖可观测的可行性局部化条件。这建立了一个横跨几何、计算与信息的统一一阶优化理论框架。

Abstract: Strongly convex minimization and its natural indefinite extension to strongly convex--strongly concave minimax problems combine quantitative control of curvature with a prescribed curvature orientation. We disentangle these two roles by retaining uniform nondegeneracy alone: curvature remains uniformly separated from zero but may have either sign, with no prescribed positive--negative splitting. Surprisingly, a large part of the familiar theory nevertheless re-emerges. We first derive an intrinsic formulation through first-order secant inequalities, making the gradient on $\mathbb{R}^d$ a global bi-Lipschitz homeomorphism and yielding a unique stationary point. We then pair signed Moreau envelopes to construct a smooth scalar merit that recovers the missing descent geometry at both zeroth and first order, and the resulting paired proximal descent method achieves global linear convergence with dimension-free first-order oracle complexity. Meanwhile, we show that this tractability can break down on restricted domains: merely assuming the existence of a stationary point in the domain may lead to the curse of dimensionality, even with access to an infinite-order oracle. To overcome this information barrier, we introduce certified feasibility, an observable localization condition that enables feasible continuation. Together, these results establish a first-order optimization theory under uniform nondegeneracy that spans geometry, computation, and information.

</details>


### [112] [The Exact Time-Uniform Rate Frontier for Stochastic Gradient Descent on Smooth Convex Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08537)
*Ruijie Li,Kang Chen,Tianyu Wang*

Main category: math.OC
