TL;DR: 本文研究标准随机梯度下降（SGD）算法在光滑凸目标函数上原始迭代的时间均匀收敛性。作者证明，在标准噪声假设下，时间均匀收敛速率可以任意接近√(log n/n)，但永远无法达到该速率。具体地，对于任意满足h(n)=o(√n)的正非递减序列h，形如h(n)/√n的界能同时对所有n成立（概率至少1-α且对问题类一致）当且仅当Σ1/h(2^j)²收敛。充分性通过二元时不变的调度结合加性条件重启不等式证明；必要性适用于所有确定性非负调度，即使对于一维解析光滑凸目标加高斯噪声也成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于SGD收敛性的研究大多关注固定迭代次数下的单次收敛界（即非均匀版本），而未路径一致地（time-uniformly）刻画原始迭代的收敛行为。时间均匀收敛在高概率置信序列、统计检验与在线推断等场景中具有重要价值，因此作者希望精确刻画原始SGD迭代在时间一致意义下的最优收敛速率及其上下界条件。

Method: 作者采用两个部分的技术路线：(1) 充分性证明：提出一个基于二元分段（dyadic）的水平无关调度（horizon-free schedule），并结合一个加性条件重启不等式（additive conditional-restart inequality）来构造同时对所有时间n成立的高概率收敛界；(2) 必要性证明：针对任意确定性非负调度，构造一个一维解析光滑凸目标函数（带高斯噪声）的反例，证明若序列h不满足所给无穷级数收敛条件，则无法达到h(n)/√n量级的时间均匀收敛界。

Result: 作者得到精确的充要条件：时间均匀收敛界h(n)/√n（对所有n同时成立、概率至少1-α、对问题类一致）可达当且仅当Σ_{j=1}^∞ 1/h(2^j)² < ∞。特别是，当取h(n)=√(log n)时级数发散，说明√(log n/n)的速率不可达；但当取h(n)=(log n)^{1/2+ε}等形式时级数收敛，说明任意接近√(log n/n)的速率均可实现。

Conclusion: 论文准确刻画了标准SGD原始迭代在时间均匀意义下的收敛速率极限：最优可达速率接近但严格达不到√(log n/n)。该充要条件为SGD的时间一致置信序列设计提供了理论指导，也为理解其本质收敛极限划定了清晰边界。

Abstract: We study the time-uniform convergence of the raw iterate of standard stochastic gradient descent (SGD) for unconstrained smooth convex objectives. We prove that, under standard noise assumptions, the time-uniform convergence rate gets arbitrarily close to $\sqrt{\log n / n}$ but never reaches it. More specifically, we prove that for every positive, eventually nondecreasing sequence $h$ satisfying $h(n) = o(\sqrt{n})$, a bound of order $h(n)/\sqrt{n}$, holding simultaneously for all $n$ with probability at least $1-α$ and uniformly over the problem class, is achievable if and only if \[
  \sum_{j = 1}^{\infty} \frac{1}{h(2^j)^2} < \infty. \] The constructive sufficiency result follows from a dyadic horizon-free schedule together with an additive conditional-restart inequality. The necessity counterpart applies to every deterministic nonnegative schedule and holds even for a one-dimensional analytic smooth convex objective with Gaussian noise.

</details>


### [113] [Input-to-State Stability Framework for Fully Distributed Primal-Dual Dynamics for Quadratic GNEPs Without Multiplier Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06983)
*Shao-An Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种无需共享乘子的分布式广义纳什均衡（GNEP）求解算法，采用输入-状态稳定性（ISS）框架证明收敛性，支持非变分均衡解并减少通信开销、提升隐私性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNEP在多智能体工程应用中广泛出现，需要分布式算法求解。传统方法需要强制乘子共识（consensus），增加通信负担并牺牲隐私。本文旨在移除乘子共享的需求，从而减少通信开销并提升隐私保护。

Method: 通过避免共享乘子的方式设计分布式算法（消除乘子共识约束），并利用输入-状态稳定性（ISS）框架推导收敛的充分条件。由于不再强制乘子一致，不同初始化可收敛到不同广义纳什均衡，包括非变分均衡。

Result: 在ISS框架下建立了算法的收敛性充分条件；算法无需共享乘子，适用于包括非变分GNEP在内的多解场景，且不同初始化可导致不同的均衡解。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式算法在降低通信成本与增强隐私的同时，仍能保证收敛至广义纳什均衡（含非变分情形），并通过ISS框架提供了可验证的收敛条件。

Abstract: Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems (GNEPs) often arise in multi-agent engineering applications that require distributed algorithms. Unlike traditional approaches that enforce consensus on multipliers, our method removes the need to share multipliers, reducing communication and improving privacy. As a result, different initializations can lead to different GNEs, including non-variational ones. We establish convergence under sufficient conditions using an input-to-state stability (ISS) framework.

</details>


### [114] [Equivalence Between Continuous-Time Risk-Sensitive Control and Rényi Divergence Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07045)
*Shinji Kataoka,Kaoru Teranishi,Yasumasa Fujisaki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了连续时间风险敏感控制问题等价于轨迹路径测度上的Rényi散度最小化问题，将任意风险态度（风险中性、风险厌恶、风险追求）统一纳入概率推断框架，并通过Girsanov定理将风险敏感度显式映射为Rényi散度阶数，推导出受风险偏好缩放且与噪声相关的控制惩罚项，从而在零强迫（风险厌恶）与质量覆盖（风险追求）之间实现平滑插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准KL散度控制（KL control）在将随机最优控制重构为概率推断时规避了HJB方程的计算难题，但本质上是风险中性的；现有minimax扩展也仅局限于风险厌恶设定。为克服这一局限，需要建立能够统一刻画任意风险态度的概率框架。

Method: 基于Girsanov定理，将连续时间非线性系统中的风险敏感控制问题等价地转化为轨迹路径测度上的Rényi散度最小化问题。将风险敏感度参数显式对应到Rényi散度阶数，并推导出一个与噪声相关、由风险偏好缩放的控制惩罚项，从而实现风险态度（风险厌恶到风险追求）的统一建模。

Result: 该等价性框架能够无缝调制分布的尾权重行为：风险厌恶策略对应零强迫（zero-forcing）特性，风险追求策略对应质量覆盖（mass-covering）特性，在两者之间实现平滑插值，覆盖了连续时间非线性系统中任意风险态度的控制问题。

Conclusion: 该研究弥合了随机控制与信息论推断之间的鸿沟，为基于采样的控制算法提供了理论基础，同时为处理任意风险偏好的连续时间控制问题提供了一个统一且可计算的概率框架。

Abstract: In this study, we show that a continuous-time risk-sensitive control problem is equivalent to a Rényi divergence minimization problem over trajectory path measures. Reformulating stochastic optimal control as probabilistic inference via Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence minimization avoids the computational intractability of the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation. However, standard KL control is inherently risk-neutral, and recent minimax extensions remain restricted to risk-averse settings. Our equivalence result resolves this limitation by offering a unified probabilistic framework for arbitrary risk attitudes in continuous-time nonlinear systems. Based on Girsanov theorem, we explicitly map the risk sensitivity to the Rényi divergence order, deriving a noise-dependent control penalty scaled by risk preference. This formulation seamlessly modulates tail-weighting behaviors, interpolating between zero-forcing for risk-averse policies and mass-covering for risk-seeking policies. These findings bridge stochastic control and information-theoretic inference, providing a foundation for sampling-based control algorithms.

</details>


### [115] [A Lower Bound for the Heavy-Ball Method on Smooth Convex Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08656)
*Jianhao Ma,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了经典Heavy-Ball方法即使使用预先设定好的、随水平（horizon）变化的任意参数调度，也无法在一般光滑凸目标上达到Nesterov的O(T^{-2})末尾迭代收敛速率。作者对每个T≥2和任意非负步长及[0,1)范围动量调度，构造了一个凸1-光滑目标，使得最后一次迭代的次优性至少为Ω(T^{-φ} / log T)，其中φ=(1+√5)/2为黄金比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heavy-Ball（动量）方法在实际中广泛使用且经验表现优异，但其是否能像Nesterov加速方法一样在光滑凸问题上保证O(T^{-2})的末尾迭代收敛率一直是一个悬而未决的问题。以往的分析多局限于固定参数或平均迭代，本文希望解答"完全非平稳、依赖水平（horizon）预调参数"是否足以让Heavy-Ball达到Nesterov速率这一根本性问题。

Method: 作者采用反例构造方法。对于任意给定的水平T和任意的预定参数调度（非负步长、动量在[0,1)内），构造一个特定的凸1-光滑目标函数，使得Heavy-Ball方法在该目标上的最后一次迭代满足f(x_T)-f* = Ω(1/(T^α log T))，其中α=(1+√5)/2≈1.618。通过这种"对手式"构造，排除了所有预定（horizon-dependent）参数选择的可能性。

Result: 关键结果为：对于每个T≥2以及每个预定参数调度，都存在一个凸1-光滑目标（初始距离≤1，初始速度为零），使得Heavy-Ball的末尾迭代满足f(x_T)-f* = Ω(T^{-φ}/log T)，其中φ=(1+√5)/2。该下界严格的慢于Nesterov的O(T^{-2})速率，因此给出了明确的否定回答：即使允许完全非平稳且依赖T的参数调度，经典Heavy-Ball也无法在光滑凸类上保证Nesterov速率。

Conclusion: 该否定结果阐明了Heavy-Ball方法在光滑凸优化中的理论局限性：任何预定（即使充分非平稳和horizon-dependent）的参数调度都无法使其获得Nesterov加速速率。这加深了人们对动量方法收敛理论的理解，说明Heavy-Ball与Nesterov加速方法之间在复杂类上存在本质的速率鸿沟。

Abstract: Can the classical Heavy-Ball method, with arbitrary horizon-dependent parameters chosen in advance, achieve Nesterov's $O(T^{-2})$ last-iterate rate on every smooth convex objective? We provide a negative answer. For every horizon $T\ge2$ and every predetermined schedule with nonnegative step sizes and momenta in $[0,1)$, there exists a convex $1$-smooth objective, with initialization distance at most one and zero initial velocity, for which the last iterate of the Heavy-Ball method satisfies \[ f(x_T)-f^\star=Ω\!\left(\frac{1}{T^α\log T}\right), \qquad α=\frac{1+\sqrt5}{2}. \] Thus even fully nonstationary, horizon-dependent tuning cannot give the classical Heavy-Ball method a Nesterov-rate guarantee on the smooth convex class.

</details>


### [116] [Mapping locally nondominated curves in multiobjective topology optimization of compliance and volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07149)
*Tom De Weer,Elke Deckers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了拓扑优化中关于柔度与体积两个目标的非支配集（Pareto前沿），通过简单方法生成具有严格受控拓扑的局部最优解，并用延拓法将其扩展为局部非支配曲线，揭示Pareto前沿的底层数学结构，并分析了两种数值不稳定现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标拓扑优化中常用的标量化方法只能逐点逼近Pareto前沿，掩盖了其潜在的数学结构（即在长度尺度约束下由局部非支配曲线段组成）。本文旨在直接研究这一结构，以获得对拓扑复杂度、对称性及数值稳定性等特征的深层理解。

Method: 采用两步式简单方法：(i) 生成拓扑严格受控的局部最优解；(ii) 利用延拓法将这些点扩展为局部非支配曲线。随后执行两轮数值实验：第一轮应用于若干悬臂梁算例以考察拓扑复杂度与对称性；第二轮研究两种数值不稳定性（解不唯一性与曲线不连续性）。

Result: 第一轮实验提供了关于拓扑复杂性和对称性的新见解，并确认了前人关于连续性、光滑性和凸性的结论。第二轮实验发现：解的不唯一性与优化景观的平坦性相关；曲线的不连续性归因于分支分裂，被归类为亚临界对称破缺音叉分岔的高维类比。

Conclusion: 论文将多目标拓扑优化中的不连续性识别为分支分裂现象，并将其与分岔理论联系，最终为未来研究如何处理多目标优化景观中的这些特征提出了建议。

Abstract: This paper studies the nondominated set for topology optimization of compliance and volume. In the multiobjective topology optimization literature, scalarization is a popular method to approximate this set, which is better known as the Pareto frontier. However, previous work indicates that the pointwise approximation obtained by scalarization obfuscates its underlying mathematical structure: under an imposed length scale, it consists of locally nondominated curve segments. To investigate this structure, the paper uses a simple methodology to (i) generate local optima with a tightly controlled topology and (ii) use continuation to extend these points to locally nondominated curves. The paper then performs two rounds of numerical experiments. The first round applies the methodology to several cantilever examples. This leads to novel insights regarding topological complexity and symmetry and confirms prior conclusions regarding continuity, smoothness and convexity. The second round investigates two numerical instabilities: (i) a lack of uniqueness and (ii) discontinuities of the locally nondominated curves. The former is linked to an observed flatness of the optimization landscape, whereas the latter is attributed to branch splitting and linked to bifurcation theory. Namely, we classify these discontinuities as higher-dimensional analogues of the subcritical, symmetry-breaking pitchfork bifurcation. The paper concludes with suggestions for future research to address these features of the multiobjective optimization landscape.

</details>


### [117] [$\mathrm M$-Eigenpairs of Partially Symmetric Tensors: Exact Reformulation and Perturbation Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07202)
*Zhuolin Du,Hanxin Liu,Yisheng Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对弹性理论中四阶部分对称张量的M-特征对计算问题，构建了提升的四阶张量，将原始M-特征值问题重构为参数化的广义张量特征值问题，建立了两种特征值问题之间的精确对应关系，并推导了最大M-特征值的扰动界，最后通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性理论中需要计算四阶部分对称张量的M-特征对，但现有方法在计算所有实M-特征对时可能不够完备或效率不高，因此需要一种新的计算框架来精确、高效地求解该问题。

Method: 首先构造提升的四阶张量，将原始M-特征值问题在B_{α,β}-归一化条件下重构为参数化的广义张量特征值问题；然后建立两种特征值问题之间的精确对应关系，据此可以计算所有实M-特征对；同时推导最大M-特征值的扰动界，并证明提升重构不引入额外松弛地保持这些界。

Result: 建立了原始M-特征值问题与参数化广义张量特征值问题之间的精确对应关系，为计算所有实M-特征对提供了完整流程；推导了最大M-特征值的扰动界，并证明提升重构能够保持这些界；数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新颖且完备的M-特征对计算方法，能够在保持扰动界的前提下，通过参数化广义张量特征值问题求得所有实M-特征对，为弹性理论中张量特征值问题的求解提供了坚实的理论基础和高效的算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the computation of $M$-eigenpairs of fourth order partially symmetric tensors arising from elasticity theory. First, a lifted fourth order tensor is constructed, and the original $M$-eigenvalue problem is reformulated as a parameterized generalized tensor eigenvalue problem under the $\mathbf B_{α,β}$-normalization. Then, an exact correspondence between the two eigenvalue problems is established, which provides a procedure for computing all real $M$-eigenpairs through the proposed reformulation. Furthermore, perturbation bounds for the largest $M$-eigenvalue are derived, and the lifted reformulation is shown to preserve these bounds without introducing any additional relaxation. Finally, numerical experiments are reported to show the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [118] [New Bounds for Limited Zarankiewicz Numbers from $K_{5t}$ Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07227)
*Hanxin Liu,Yisheng Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一类基于K_{5t}关联二分图与Z_5循环标记的新无穷图族，用于限制增广Zarankiewicz数z_L(m,n)，其相对间隙渐近收敛到2/5，优于此前基于K_{4t}图族的1/4间隙上限，并通过t=1时的完整枚举验证了z_L(10,5)=23的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限增广Zarankiewicz数z_L(m,n)为双二次型的最大SOS秩提供核心组合下界，而此前所有已知的无穷可行图族都依赖于K_{4t}关联图，渐近相对间隙极限仅为1/4。为获得更紧的下界，需要构造新的无穷图族以突破该间隙瓶颈。

Method: 基于K_{5t}关联二分图构造新的无穷图族，采用Z_5循环标记对块分区进行标号；构造有效的非退化块内与块间2-边；推导z_L的二次闭式下界；通过t=1的完整枚举验证精确值；在非退化与广义C_4-free约束下分析块框架下2/5间隙比的可达性。

Result: 证明该新图族的相对间隙渐近收敛到2/5，突破了此前K_{4t}图族的1/4上限；t=1时的完整枚举验证了z_L(10,5)=23的精确值；在非退化和广义C_4-free约束下，2/5被证明是该块框架下可达到的最大渐近比率。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了极值二分图的库，并锐化了双二次型SOS秩的下界；研究展望指出一般K_{kt}构造中的进一步开放问题。

Abstract: The restricted augmented Zarankiewicz number \(z_L(m,n)\) yields core combinatorial lower bounds for the maximal SOS rank of biquadratic forms. All previously known infinite admissible graph families rely on \(K_{4t}\) incidence graphs, attaining an asymptotic relative gap limit of \(1/4\). This work develops a new infinite family built from \(K_{5t}\) incidence bipartite graphs with \(\mathbb{Z}_5\) cyclic labeling for block partitions. We construct valid nondegenerate intra-block and inter-block 2-edges, derive a quadratic closed-form lower bound of \(z_L\), and prove its relative gap converges asymptotically to \(2/5\). Full enumeration for \(t=1\) verifies the exact value \(z_L(10,5)=23\). Under nondegenerate and generalized \(C_4\)-free constraints, the ratio \(2/5\) is shown to be the maximal asymptotic ratio attainable under this block framework. Our results expand the library of extremal bipartite graphs and sharpen lower bounds for biquadratic SOS rank, with further open problems for general \(K_{kt}\) constructions outlined in closing.

</details>


### [119] [Mathematical Programming in Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence: A Unified Taxonomy of Models and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07254)
*Chaosheng Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统性地将数学规划（线性、二次、整数、锥、双层、多目标、逆、分布鲁棒、子模、极小极大优化）与机器学习/人工智能中的各类决策任务（检索上下文选择、Token路由、推理计算分配、结构化预测器拟合、分布偏移防护、多目标权衡）进行统一梳理，并提出数学规划并非“一切学习都是LP或MIP”的强主张，而是连接预测与约束决策的规范化接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献分散在优化、信息检索、推荐、自然语言处理、计算机视觉和学习理论等多个领域，缺乏统一的视角来组织数学规划在ML/AI系统中的各类应用，导致方法与理论难以横向比较与借鉴。

Method: 将多种ML/AI应用归入统一的数学规划范式分类（线形、二次、二元与混合整数、锥、双层、多目标、逆、分布鲁棒、子模、极小极大），采用统一的符号体系规范化各模型，并为每个应用明确输入、决策变量、主公式、结构性质、求解策略与局限；跨范式比较可解性、松弛质量、分解方法、近似保证与可扩展性瓶颈。

Result: 论文提供了一个跨应用、跨范式的系统化梳理与比较框架，展示了不同数学规划范式在ML/AI各任务中的适用性、可解性与性能边界，并指出各自的瓶颈与适用场景。

Conclusion: 数学规划在ML/AI中的核心价值不在于宣称所有学习问题都可归约为LP或MIP，而在于为“预测”与“受约束决策”之间提供一个规范化、可分解、可分析的接口。

Abstract: Mathematical programming provides a common language for many decisions embedded in modern machine-learning (ML) and artificial-intelligence (AI) systems: selecting retrieval context, routing tokens, allocating inference compute, fitting structured predictors, protecting against distribution shift, and balancing competing objectives. However, the relevant literature is fragmented across optimization, information retrieval, recommendation, natural-language processing, computer vision, and learning theory. This paper organizes various applications under common mathematical programming paradigms: linear, quadratic, binary and mixed-integer, conic, bilevel, multi-objective, inverse, distributionally robust, submodular, and min--max optimization. We normalize the models with a mostly unified notation and, for every application, identify inputs, decision variables, a principal formulation, structural properties, solution strategies, and limitations. Across paradigms, we compare tractability, relaxation quality, decomposition, approximation guarantees, and scalability bottlenecks. The paper shows that mathematical programming is most useful not as a claim that all learning is LP or MIP, but as a disciplined interface between predictions and constrained decisions.

</details>


### [120] [CLInstGen: An Instance Generation Framework for Two-Tier City Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07289)
*Julia Lange,Johannes Gückel,Pirmin Fontaine,Timo Gschwind*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出CLInstGen实例生成框架和有史以来第一个双层级城市物流基准库CLLIB。CLLIB基于真实城市地理数据，提供标准化实例集合，解决了该领域缺乏统一基准数据、研究可复现性和可比性差的问题。研究通过两种不同问题场景的计算实验验证了生成器和库的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层级城市物流虽已被广泛研究，但缺乏标准化、最先进的基准数据。现有研究多依赖过时、人工构造或特定案例的实例，导致结果的可复现性、可比性和普适性受限，严重阻碍了研究进展。

Method: 作者首先识别双层级城市物流的一般问题特征，然后提出通用实例生成框架CLInstGen，适用于广泛的问题设定。在此基础上构建了首个基准库CLLIB，基于真实城市地理区域，提供标准化实例集合用于直接基准测试和统计评估。代码全部开源，方便研究者使用、适配和生成定制实例。最后通过两种不同问题设定（双层级战术性服务网络设计和单层级带同时取送货的车辆路径问题）进行计算实验验证通用性。

Result: 计算实验验证了CLInstGen生成器和CLLIB库在两种不同问题设定下的通用性，并提供了CLLIB基准实例的首次数值和管理的见解。

Conclusion: 通过开源CLInstGen和CLLIB，为双层级城市物流研究建立了统一的基准测试标准，提升了研究的可复现性、可比性和泛化能力，有望加速该领域的研究进展。

Abstract: City logistics addresses the design and management of efficient freight transportation in urban areas. Two-tier networks, where goods are moved by different vehicle fleets from distribution centers to customers using intermediate consolidation facilities, are particularly effective. Although studied from routing, location, and network design perspectives, the research progress is slowed by the lack of standardized, state-of-the-art benchmark data. Many studies rely on outdated and artificial or case-specific instances, limiting reproducibility, comparability, and generality of results. To address this gap, we identify general problem characteristics for two-tier city logistics, provide an instance generation framework CLInstGen that is applicable to a wide range of problem settings, and propose the first benchmark library CLLIB. The library and the CLInstGen code are open-source, allowing researchers to easily use, adapt, and generate instances for their specific applications. CLLIB is based on real-world geographies of urban regions and provides a curated collection of standardized instances for direct benchmarking of models and methods as well as statistical evaluation. A computational study with two different problem settings, namely tactical service network design on two tiers and vehicle routing with simultaneous pickups and deliveries on a single tier, confirms the versatility of both the generator and the library, and provides first numerical and managerial insights on the CLLIB benchmark instances.

</details>


### [121] [A Unified Efficient Gradient-Based Heuristic For Box-Constrained Expectation-Related and Risk-Averse Stochastic Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07427)
*Mazen Alamir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的随机优化算法，通过参数化方式统一处理期望、VaR和CVaR等多种随机优化目标。该算法基于确定性优化中的Search & Accelerate算法，通过一系列热启动求解的实例获得候选解及其凸包内的采样解，在离散候选集中依据目标准则的样本近似选择最优解，并通过定制示例验证了算法的有效性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机优化问题中，代价函数依赖于具有已知统计特性的不确定参数向量，但现有方法通常只能处理单一类型的随机目标函数（如期望或风险度量），缺乏一种统一的参数化框架来覆盖从期望型到VaR型再到CVaR型的多种随机优化表述。

Method: 算法利用近期提出的基于梯度的Search & Accelerate搜索算法（原用于确定性优化问题），将其扩展至随机优化场景。通过参数化控制以适配不同随机目标。核心思想是生成一系列热启动求解的问题实例，将这些解与属于其凸包内采样的其他解共同构成候选集合，然后在此离散候选集中依据目标准则的样本近似选择最优解。

Result: 作者通过一个定制的说明性示例展示了算法的相关性与效率，验证了所提算法在应对不同随机目标（期望、VaR、CVaR）时的有效性。

Conclusion: 该算法为统一处理多种随机优化目标提供了一种可行的框架，将确定性优化中的高效搜索策略与随机场景下的样本近似方法相结合，为实践中的随机优化问题提供了新的求解途径。

Abstract: This paper presents a new algorithm addressing the problem of stochastic optimization where the cost function depends on a vector of uncertain parameters with known statistics. The algorithm is parameterized so as to address various stochastic formulations spanning from Expectation-focused to Value-at-Risk (VaR) as well as Conditional-Value-at-Risk (CVaR)-focused formulations. The algorithm leverages a recently proposed gradient-based Search & Accelerate algorithm which is originally dedicated to deterministic optimization problems. The approach is based on a sequence of warm-started solutions of instances of the problem. These solutions together with a samples of other solutions belonging to the convex hull of the first ones constitute the set of admissible candidates. Among this discrete set of candidates, the optimal solution is selected with regards to a sample-based approximation of the targeted criterion. The relevance of the algorithm and its efficiency are discussed and shown using a tailored illustrative example.

</details>


### [122] [On the Asymptotic Switching Density in Time-Optimal Control of Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07431)
*Omri Dalin,Alexander Ovseevich,Michael Margaliot*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究可控线性系统在长时间域[0,T]上时间最优控制的渐近切换密度。当系统矩阵仅含实特征值时，切换次数有界；当含复特征值时，切换次数随T增长。作者刻画了任意谱（含实特征值、复特征值及非平凡Jordan结构）下切换次数的增长规律，并证明若主导模态为复模态，切换次数至少线性增长于T，下界通过平均运动问题和Bohl-Weyl-Wintner公式显式给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅分别处理了实特征值和复特征值情况：实特征值下切换次数一致有界，复特征值下无界且随T增长，但缺乏对任意谱（含混合特征值和Jordan结构）的统一刻画。

Method: 基于Bohl-Weyl-Wintner公式，结合平均运动问题建立切换次数增长的显式下界；对系统矩阵的任意谱（实特征值、复特征值及Jordan块结构）统一分析主导模态对切换密度的贡献。

Result: 证明若主导模态是复模态，切换次数至少随T线性增长，并给出显式下界；在线性化飞机俯仰/高度模型上数值验证，预测的渐近切换率与数值计算的时间最优控制结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 推广了切换次数增长的已知结论至任意特征值谱情形，确立了复主导模态下切换次数线性增长的普适性，并将其与LQ最优控制中的经典结论（高增益下极点随增益移动）联系起来，为切换系统研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the time-optimal control of a controllable linear system on a time horizon [0,T], focusing on the asymptotic switching density for large T. When the system matrix has only real eigenvalues, it is well-known that the number of switches is upper bounded uniformly in T; when it has complex eigenvalues, no such uniform bound exists, and the switching count instead typically grows with T. We characterize this growth for a system matrix with an arbitrary spectrum, allowing simultaneously for real eigenvalues, complex eigenvalues, and a non-trivial Jordan structure. If the dominant mode is complex, the number of switches grows at least linearly in T, with an explicit lower bound expressed via the mean motion problem and the Bohl-Weyl-Wintner formula. We illustrate the theory on a linearized aircraft pitch/altitude model, showing close agreement between the predicted asymptotic switching rate and numerically computed time-optimal controls.

</details>


### [123] [On Minimax Optimality and Uniqueness of Fixed-Step First-Order Methods for Smooth Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07671)
*Benjamin Grimmer,Sunghyeon Jo,Chanwoo Park*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对高维L-光滑凸函数的最小化问题，研究最优固定步长一阶方法的设计。通过代数证明方法，分别确立了OGM、OGM-G和Lemniscate方法在三种不同性能度量下的最优性和唯一性，突破了传统信息论下界只能证明最优性而无法证明唯一性的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对固定步长一阶方法的最优性分析主要依赖信息论下界（information-theoretic bounds），这种方法虽然能证明OGM等算法的最优性，但无法证明其唯一性。本文旨在通过代数归约的方法，为多种性能度量下的最优方法同时建立最优性与唯一性证明，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 本文采用代数归约（algebraic reductions）进行下界论证，而非传统的信息论界限。具体针对三种不同的性能度量分别分析：1）以最终函数值次优性（相对于初始到最优点距离平方）为度量，分析OGM；2）以最终梯度范数平方（相对于初始次优性）为度量，分析OGM-G；3）以最终梯度范数平方（相对于初始到最优点距离平方）为度量，分析Lemniscate方法。通过代数方法为每种情形建立下界并证明对应方法的唯一最优性。

Result: 在三种不同的最坏情况性能度量下，分别证明：OGM在最终函数值次优性度量下最优且唯一；OGM-G在最终梯度范数度量（相对于初始次优性）下最优且唯一；Lemniscate方法在最终梯度范数度量（相对于初始距离平方）下最优且唯一。所有唯一性证明均通过代数归约实现，这是传统方法无法达到的。

Conclusion: 本文通过代数归约方法，为固定步长一阶优化方法的最优性问题提供了更强健的证明框架，不仅确认了OGM、OGM-G和Lemniscate方法各自的领域最优性，还首次确立了其唯一性，深化了对该类优化算法理论极限的理解。

Abstract: This paper considers the design of optimal fixed-step first-order methods for high-dimensional minimization of $L$-smooth convex functions. For optimizing worst-case performance measured via suboptimality of the final function value (relative to the initial squared distance to a minimizer), we provide an algebraic proof of the optimality of the optimized gradient method (OGM) and establish its uniqueness among all fixed-step first-order methods. For the alternative measure of final squared gradient norm (relative to initial suboptimality), we prove the OGM-G method is optimal and uniquely so among fixed-step first-order methods. Finally, for the setting measuring the final squared gradient norm (relative to the initial squared distance to a minimizer), we show the recently proposed Lemniscate method is optimal and uniquely so. Our proofs rely on algebraic reductions for lower bound arguments rather than traditional information-theoretic bounds, which were previously only able to establish OGM's optimality but not uniqueness.

</details>


### [124] [Efficient Optimization on the Euclidean Sphere: Riemannian Gradient Alignment as a Unifying Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07723)
*Puqian Wang,Nikos Zarifis,Jelena Diakonikolas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼梯度对齐（RGA）作为统一的结构性条件，用于解释和改进欧几里得球约束非凸优化中一阶方法的快速收敛性。RGA是一种参数化方向误差界，要求切向量与目标解充分对齐。作者证明了在RGA条件下，黎曼梯度下降（采用递减步长）可实现线性收敛（r=1）或次线性收敛（r>1），并提供了基于导数的充分条件，涵盖多实例学习、高斯半空间、单指标模型、相位恢复、字典学习和（稀疏）PCA等多种学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得球约束优化是非凸约束问题中最基本的类别之一，但关于简单一阶方法何时以及为何能在球面上高效优化，目前理解甚少。现有结果分散且缺乏统一的理论框架，需要一种结构性条件来解释多种问题实例的高效可优化性。

Method: 作者引入RGA（黎曼梯度对齐）作为参数化方向误差界条件，证明在RGA成立时黎曼梯度下降（递减步长）的收敛率，并将结果推广到非梯度性切向量场。主要技术贡献是构造了一族基于导数的充分条件来验证RGA，并在多种学习问题中验证这些条件，包括标准高斯、条件高斯以及更一般的结构化分布。

Result: 当RGA的阶数r=1时黎曼梯度下降呈线性收敛，当r>1时收敛率为O(k^{-1/(2r-2)})。该结果适用于非目标函数梯度的切向量场。作者在多种学习问题中验证了RGA条件，这些目标函数通常非（测地）凸、可能非光滑，甚至来自离散分布，为之前缺乏误差界条件的问题类别建立了可解性，同时统一和推广了多项先前结果。

Conclusion: RGA为球约束非凸优化中一阶方法的快速收敛提供了统一的理论框架，弥合了理论与实际问题之间的鸿沟，为更广泛的学习问题建立了可证明的收敛保证。

Abstract: Euclidean sphere-constrained optimization problems represent perhaps the most basic class of nonconvex-constrained problems for which there exist efficient optimization algorithms for specific problem instances, such as the eigenvalue problems and learning problems arising in the recent literature. However, when and why we can efficiently optimize on the sphere using simple first-order methods is poorly understood. We introduce Riemannian Gradient Alignment (RGA) as a unifying structural condition enabling fast convergence. RGA is a parameterized directional error bound requiring a tangent vector to sufficiently align with a target solution vector. We prove that Riemannian Gradient Descent with diminishing step sizes converges linearly when RGA holds with order $r=1$, and at rate $O(k^{-1/(2r-2)})$ for $r>1$. The result applies even to tangent vector fields that are not gradients of any objective.
  Our main technical contribution is a family of derivative-based sufficient conditions for RGA. We prove these conditions for a range of learning problems, including multi-instance learning/max pooling, Gaussian halfspaces, single-index and generalized linear models, phase retrieval, complete orthogonal dictionary learning, and (sparse) PCA. The provided examples span distributional families that include standard Gaussian, conditionally Gaussian, and more general structured distributions. The provided guarantees cover objectives that are generally not (geodesically) convex, may be nonsmooth, and may arise from discrete distributions. These results establish tractability of problem classes where no prior error bound conditions were known, as well as unify, strengthen, and generalize several prior results.

</details>


### [125] [Exact Decision and a Surjective Stabilizer Atlas for Affine Coprime-Factor Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07733)
*Junkai Qiu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一般有限输入输出维数（n）的正方形proper实有理被控对象的同时内部镇定问题，将被控对象表示为仿射右互质因子段，在显式非退化假设下，通过函数级双重Cayley分解将全局谱割约束与有限极点及无穷远处的重构约束编码为有限维半代数种子。若输入系数来自有效呈现的实闭域，则该种子可通过经典量词消元获得精确的存在性判定（无需枚举控制器McMillan度）；同一编码在不同的有理半径、容许种子及残差酉和有理Schur参数上遍历时，可给出该因子段所有proper实有理公共镇定器的满射图册。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时镇定问题在控制理论中具有重要意义，但三个一般被控对象的同时镇定在有理意义下不可判定。作者试图在一个结构化类（仿射右互质因子段，固定有限维输入输出维数n且有非退化假设）中，找到精确存在性判定与全参数化方案，从而与一般情形的不可判定性结论形成兼容对照。

Method: 采用以下技术路线：(1) 将plant排列为仿射右互质因子段并施加非退化假设；(2) 经经典规范化后，将全局谱割约束以及有限极点与无穷远处的重构约束编码为一个有限维半代数种子；(3) 利用函数级双重Cayley（double-Cayley）分解实现此编码；(4) 对实闭域系数输入，使用经典量词消元作为存在性判定引擎，不枚举控制器McMillan度；(5) 遍历有理半径、容许种子以及残差酉参数和有理Schur参数，构造所有公共镇定器的满射图册。

Result: 在给定结构化类上，获得了无需枚举控制器McMillan度的精确存在性判定，以及所有proper实有理公共镇定器的满射参数化图册。该结果与三个一般plant同时镇定问题的有理不可判定性结论相容，表明此结构化类处于可判定与不可判定之间的清晰边界。

Conclusion: 通过对结构化plant类引入有限维半代数编码与函数级双重Cayley分解，本文在所限制的意义下解决了同时镇定存在性的精确判定及其全参数化问题，结果与一般情形下的无理不可判定性兼容，为该领域提供了新的理论工具与算法框架。

Abstract: We consider simultaneous internal stabilization of square proper real-rational plants of arbitrary but fixed finite input-output dimension $n$, arranged as an affine right-coprime factor segment, under explicit nondegeneracy hypotheses. After a classical normalization, the remaining global spectral-cut constraint and the reconstruction constraints at finite poles and at infinity are encoded, without loss, as a finite-dimensional semialgebraic seed, using a function-level double-Cayley factorization. For input coefficients in an effectively presented real closed field, that seed yields an exact existence decision that does not enumerate controller McMillan degree; the decision engine is classical quantifier elimination. The same encoding, now ranging over rational radii, admissible seeds, and residual unimodular and rational Schur parameters, yields a surjective atlas of all proper real-rational common stabilizers of the segment. The results concern this structured class and are compatible with the rational undecidability of simultaneous stabilization of three general plants.

</details>


### [126] [Submodular Welfare under Routing Coupling: A Hierarchical Decomposition with Perturbation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07767)
*Joan Vendrell Gallart,Nhat-Minh Tang-Nguyen,Alan Kuhnle,Solmaz Kia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究图中的联合子模福利最大化与路由问题，其中智能体在收益递减条件下选择物品并通过具有拥塞依赖成本的网络运输。核心发现是：在给定路由时，目标函数在分配变量上仍保持子模性，据此提出Welfare-based Hierarchical Routing Algorithm (WHIRL)算法，在可解的路由与分配更新间交替迭代，结合模化路由初始化与有界扰动建模，具备有限收敛保证及依赖于与模化路由偏差的近似界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 福利最大化在模成本下可借助拟阵逼近，路由在模成本下退化为最短路问题，但两者的耦合会产生超模交互从而破坏可分离性，需要提出新方法来联合处理这一组合优化难题。

Method: 先证明固定路由时目标在分配变量上仍具子模性，从而支持分解；据此提出WHIRL算法，交替执行可解的路由更新与分配更新。路由通过其模化对应物初始化，超模效应则作为有界扰动建模。算法具有有限收敛性，近似界显式依赖于与模化路由的偏离程度。

Result: 有限收敛保证；近似界显式取决于对模化路由的偏离；数值实验表明路由诱导的耦合对结果有显著影响，WHIRL在解质量与计算开销之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: 通过揭示联合问题中仍保留的子模结构并设计分层交替算法，本文为具有拥塞依赖路由成本的社会福利最大化提供了首个可计算且具理论保证的求解框架。

Abstract: This paper studies joint submodular welfare maximization and routing over graphs, where agents select items under diminishing returns and transport them through a network with congestion-dependent costs. Although welfare maximization admits matroid-based approximations and routing reduces to shortest paths under modular costs, their coupling creates supermodular interactions that break separability. We show that, for fixed routing, the objective remains submodular in the allocation variable, enabling a principled decomposition. Building on this property, we propose the Welfare-based Hierarchical Routing Algorithm (WHIRL), which alternates between tractable routing and allocation updates. Routing is initialized through its modular counterpart, while supermodular effects are modeled as bounded perturbations. The method has finite convergence guarantees and approximation bounds that depend explicitly on the deviation from modular routing. Numerical results illustrate the impact of routing-induced coupling and show that WHIRL achieves a favorable tradeoff between solution quality and computational cost.

</details>


### [127] [The Price of Feasibility: Greedy Approximation Bounds for String Supermodular Optimization over Oracle-Conditioned Greedoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07787)
*Joan Vendrell Gallart,Russell Bent,Solmaz Kia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在物理约束与组合可行性耦合下的单调非递减超模最小化问题，提出基于前瞻口（look-ahead oracle）的条件顺序贪婪算法（Conditioned Sequential Greedy Algorithm），导出了称为"可行性代价"（price of feasibility）的闭式近似界，并以多源辐射状网络重构算法FORWARD为案例验证了该框架的有效性与界紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贪婪算法能高效近似求解组合优化问题，但当可行性将组合结构与全局物理约束耦合时，其保证会减弱。作者旨在扩展贪婪算法理论，使其适用于在图形贪婪拟阵（graphic greedoid）基上、受物理约束（如辐射状拓扑等）作用的单调超模最小化问题。

Method: 本文提出条件顺序贪婪算法，利用前瞻口（look-ahead oracle）识别可扩展为可行基的候选元素；推导基于oracle受限候选集变异性和未观测元素概率校正的闭式近似界（可行性代价，price of feasibility）；以多源辐射状网络重构算法FORWARD作为实例化该框架的案例。

Result: 数值实验表明所导出的近似界具有紧性，并量化了可行性与最优性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究将贪婪算法的近似保证推广到受物理约束耦合的组合问题中，以可行性代价量化近似损失，并在辐射状网络重构中展示实际价值。

Abstract: Greedy algorithms efficiently approximate combinatorial optimization problems, but their guarantees weaken when feasibility couples combinatorial structure with global physical constraints. We study monotone nondecreasing supermodular minimization over the bases of a graphic greedoid under physics-induced constraints. We model physics-informed selection using a look-ahead oracle that identifies candidates extendable to a feasible basis, yielding the Conditioned Sequential Greedy Algorithm. We derive a closed-form approximation bound, which we call the price of feasibility, based on the variability of oracle-restricted candidate sets and a probabilistic correction for unobserved elements. As a case study, we show that FORWARD, an algorithm for multi-source radial network reconfiguration, instantiates this framework. Numerical results demonstrate the tightness of the bound and quantify the feasibility-optimality trade-off.

</details>


### [128] [Boundary null controllability of weakly coupled structurally damped beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07806)
*Julian Edward,Yacouba Simpore*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了对有限个欧拉-伯努利梁系统（可能带有不可对角化的耦合）在任意正时间内实现边界零可控性。通过傅里叶-乔丹约化将问题转化为向量矩量问题，在Fattorini-Hautus条件、全局谱不碰撞及模态转移因子非零等假设下，利用块双正交族构造出H²边界控制。耦合矩阵的几何重数决定最小控制维数，乔丹块长度决定广义矩量阶数。临界情形ρ=2时，通过精确热系统约化在A=A*≥0条件下获得相同结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限个欧拉-伯努利梁系统的边界零可控性，特别是处理非对角化耦合的复杂情形，以及不同参数区域（ρ<2和ρ=2）下的统一可控性结果。

Method: 采用傅里叶-乔丹约化将系统化为向量矩量问题；利用块双正交族构造控制函数；通过Fattorini-Hautus条件与谱非碰撞假设保证可控性；对ρ=2情形采用精确热系统约化技术处理边界输入的微分耦合。

Result: 在任意正时间内建立了H²边界零可控性：ρ<2情形依赖谱与几何条件，ρ=2情形在A=A*≥0下成立；确定了控制维数与耦合矩阵几何重数、矩量阶数与乔丹块长度之间的关系。

Conclusion: 该工作将矩量方法与乔丹标准形理论相结合，系统性解决了不可对角化耦合梁系统的边界可控性难题，为更一般的结构耦合PDE系统提供了分析框架。

Abstract: We prove boundary null controllability in any positive time for a finite system of Euler--Bernoulli beams with possibly non-diagonalizable coupling. For $0<ρ<2$, a Fourier--Jordan reduction yields a vector moment problem with polynomial--exponential modes. Under the Fattorini--Hautus condition, global spectral non-collision, and nonvanishing modal transfer factors, a suitably estimated block-biorthogonal family produces an $H^2$ boundary control. Geometric multiplicities of the coupling matrix determine the minimal control dimension, and Jordan-block lengths determine the degrees of the generalized moments. At $ρ=2$, an exact heat-system reduction gives the same result for $A=A^*\geq0$, despite the time-differential linkage of the reduced boundary inputs.

</details>


### [129] [Configuration-Constrained Tube MPC for Difference-of-Convex Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07809)
*Filippo Badalamenti,Jose A. Borja-Conde,Filiberto Fele,Teodoro Alamo,Daniel Limon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对受约束非线性系统的凸管模型预测控制（tube MPC）方法。该方法的动力系统包含参数不确定性与加性扰动，并允许差分凸（difference-of-convex）分解。通过结合非线性动力学的凸方向界与配置约束的多胞体管，实现鲁棒一步管传播的充分条件验证，并在单一凸规划中联合优化管几何形状与顶点控制律。隐式终端条件保证递推可行性与预测管收敛至目标鲁棒控制不变集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统在参数不确定性和加性扰动下的鲁棒MPC通常面临非凸优化问题，计算复杂度高且难以保证全局最优性。现有的管MPC方法多局限于线性或简单非线性系统，缺乏既能保证鲁棒性又能保持凸优化结构的一般化框架。本文旨在弥合这一空白，为更广泛的非线性系统类别提供计算可处理的鲁棒管方法。

Method: 方法的核心包括：(1) 利用动力系统的差分凸分解结构，构造凸方向界来描述非线性动态；(2) 引入配置约束的多胞体管（configuration-constrained polytopic tubes），其预定义的组合结构允许顶点进行仿射参数化；(3) 构建有限维充分条件来验证鲁棒一步管传播；(4) 在单一凸规划中联合优化管几何与顶点控制律；(5) 通过隐式终端条件实现递推可行性与收敛性保证。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提方法在闭环特性方面的有效性，并展示了管几何灵活性与计算复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该凸管MPC框架成功将鲁棒管设计拓展至含参和加性不确定性的非线性系统，在保持凸规划可解性的同时实现了管几何与控制律的联合优化，并通过隐式终端条件保证了递推可行性与收敛性。

Abstract: This paper develops a convex tube model predictive control formulation for constrained nonlinear systems. We consider dynamics described by a discrete-time state-space model with parametric and additive uncertainty that admits a difference-of-convex decomposition. Convex directional bounds of the nonlinear dynamics are combined with configuration-constrained polytopic tubes, whose predefined combinatorial structure yields an affine parameterization of their vertices. The resulting finite-dimensional sufficient conditions certify robust one-step tube propagation under parametric uncertainty and additive disturbances, while allowing the tube geometry and an associated vertex control law to be optimized jointly in a single convex program. An implicit terminal condition guarantees recursive feasibility and convergence of the predicted tube to a target robust control invariant set. Numerical results illustrate the closed-loop properties and the trade-off between geometric flexibility and computational complexity.

</details>


### [130] [Mean-field quadratic BSDEs and related mean-field portfolio games of controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07909)
*Huilin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类源于均值场投资组合博弈（指数效用）的新型均值场二次倒向随机微分方程（qBSDEs），其生成元含均值项E[Z]^T Z和|E[Z]|^2而非经典路径依赖的Z^T Z项。在终值及其Malliavin导数的L^q可积条件下证明局部适定性，并在Malliavin导数满足额外指数可积条件下证明全局适定性，进而通过该qBSDE理论证明两类均值场博弈全局均衡的存在唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均值场博弈与投资组合优化中的指数效用模型自然产生一类新型二次BSDE，其二次项涉及均值项而非路径项。已有qBSDE理论无法直接处理此类结构，需要建立新的适定性理论，并以此为工具解决均值场博弈均衡的存在唯一性问题。

Method: 针对含E[Z]^T Z与|E[Z]|^2形式二次项的均值场qBSDEs：(1) 在L^q可积性和Malliavin导数可积性假设下证明局部适定性；(2) 引入对Malliavin导数的额外指数可积条件来扩展至全局适定性；(3) 将所建立的qBSDE理论应用于带价格冲击的均值场投资组合博弈和含市场出清条件的有限合约定价模型，证明这两类博弈全局均衡解的存在唯一性。

Result: 建立了含均值二次项的新型均值场qBSDE的局部与全局适定性理论，并成功应用于两类具体均值场博弈模型，得到全局均衡解的存在唯一性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了均值场二次BSDE的理论框架，克服了生成元中均值二次项带来的技术困难，为均值场博弈中的指数效用均衡分析提供了统一的数学工具。

Abstract: We study a new class of mean-field quadratic backward stochastic differential equations (qBSDEs) arising from mean-field portfolio games with exponential utility. Typical examples of such games include a mean-field portfolio game with price impact, and a finite-contract pricing model with market clearing conditions. Generators of these mean-field qBSDEs contain quadratic terms $\mathbb{E}[Z]^{\top} Z$ and $|\mathbb{E}[Z]|^2$, instead of the classical pathwise $Z^\top Z$ term. We prove local well-posedness under $L^q$-integrability assumptions on terminals and their Malliavin derivatives, and global well-posedness under an extra exponential integrability condition on the Malliavin derivatives. Then we show the existence and uniqueness of global equilibria of the above two mean-field games via our qBSDE theory.

</details>


### [131] [Symmetry breaking in the polygonal Szegö-Weinberger inequality as $p\to1^+$: the longest shortest-fence quadrilateral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07929)
*Beniamin Bogosel,Dorin Bucur,Ilaria Fragalà*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多胞形版本的Pólya问题：在给定面积和给定边数的凸多边形中，寻找具有最长"最短栅栏"（即最大内切半径的意义下的最窄方向）的形状。三角形情形平凡地为等边三角形，但四边形情形出现对称破缺：最优四边形不是正方形，而是一个由底角的基本方程唯一确定的特殊等腰梯形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Pólya问题关注在给定面积的凸集合中寻找最长最短栅栏（涉及凸集的内切半径相关量）。本文将其推广到多变边形限制（边数固定的多边形竞争类），研究在该离散/多胞形设定下最优形状的结构，特别关注是否存在对称性破缺现象。

Method: 对三角形情形直接证明等边三角形最优；对四边形情形，将问题约化为特定的等腰梯形，并证明其最优性。证明结合了解析论证与严格的区间算术（interval-arithmetic）计算来验证关键不等式或方程。

Result: 四边形情形下最优形状不是正方形，而是对称性破缺的特定等腰梯形。该梯形由关于其底角的一个基本方程唯一确定（在相似变换和平移旋转意义下），即存在唯一解。

Conclusion: 在多变边形Pólya问题中，三角形保持对称最优（等边三角形），而四边形则发生对称破缺，最优解为唯一确定的等腰梯形而非正方形，显示边数固定的离散约束会改变Pólya问题的最优几何结构。

Abstract: We consider Pólya's problem of finding, among convex sets of prescribed area, the one with the longest shortest fence, in the polygonal setting, namely when the class of competitors is restricted to polygons with a prescribed number of sides. While it is straightforward to show that, among triangles, the optimal shape is the equilateral one, we prove that symmetry breaking occurs in the case of quadrilaterals: the optimal quadrilateral is not the square. More precisely, we identify it as a specific isosceles trapezium, which is uniquely determined, up to homotheties and rigid motions, by an elementary equation for its base angle. The proof combines analytical arguments and rigorous interval-arithmetic computations.

</details>


### [132] [A New Perspective on Clustering: A Mixed-norm Model and its Solution by Progressive Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08002)
*Junyi Liu,Yulin Peng,Yao Xie,Yancheng Yuan,Jong-Shi Pang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在经典K-means和K-medians基础上提出了一种ℓ_{p,q}混合范数聚类模型，并开发了渐进式整数规划（PIP）方法高效求解对应的混合整数规划。数值实验验证了该模型在特定污染场景下的优越性及PIP方法的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典K-means（ℓ₂范数）和K-medians（ℓ₁范数）各自适用不同数据分布和噪声场景，但单一范数在聚类中心和分配规则上无法分别适应不同的稳健性或稀疏性需求。作者希望设计一个统一框架，允许聚类中心更新与簇分配采用不同范数，从而更灵活地应对多样化的数据污染场景（如坐标稀疏的均值偏移污染与稠密的坐标柯西污染）。

Method: 1) 建立ℓ_{p,q}混合范数聚类模型，其中中心更新与簇分配分别在ℓ_p和ℓ_q下进行，并将其建模为带Heaviside复合约束的MIP。2) 提出渐进式整数规划（PIP）方法，通过自适应固定置信分配并求解受限MIP子问题。3) 对q=1的情况，构造差分凸约束的凸内部近似以保证子问题能求得全局最优解。4) 建立局部极小点与受限子问题全局最优解之间的理论关联（strong center-local minimizer），提供局部极小点的实用证书。5) 发展自适应固定集和工作集的构建技术。

Result: 广泛的数值实验表明：ℓ_{2,1}混合范数模型在坐标稀疏、均值平衡污染下表现优异，而ℓ_{1,2}模型在稠密坐标柯西污染下更优。PIP方法能够逃脱交替优化方法（如K-means型迭代）的劣质局部解，获得显著更优的可行聚类，同时在无改进时能保持强的热启动。

Conclusion: 论文提出的ℓ_{p,q}混合范数聚类框架为不同污染场景提供了灵活且可计算的统一建模工具，PIP方法解决了原始MIP不可行的计算瓶颈，并在理论上保证了局部极小点的可验证性。该工作为聚类问题的离散优化求解与理论分析建立了新范式。

Abstract: Extending the classical $K$-means and $K$-medians models, this paper introduces an $\ell_{p,q}$ mixed-norm clustering model where the centroid updates and cluster assignments are under the $\ell_p$ and $\ell_q$ norms, respectively. The model is formulated as a mixed-integer program (MIP) with Heaviside composite constraints that describe the nearest-center assignments. The framework recovers $K$-means and $K$-medians when $p=q=2$ and $p=q=1$, respectively, and yields new models when $p\ne q$. To address the computational challenges, we develop a progressive integer programming (PIP) method that adaptively fixes confident assignments and solves restricted mixed-integer subproblems. For $q=1$, we develop a convex inner approximation of the difference-of-convex constraints in the restricted subproblems, for which a global solution can be computed. Importantly, we establish the connection between the local minimizer and the strong center-local minimizer of the mixed-norm clustering problem and the global optimal solution of the restricted subproblems under certain assumptions. This connection provides a practical certificate of a local minimizer of the nonconvex mixed-norm clustering model. We further develop techniques for constructing adaptive fixing sets and working sets for $q=1$. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the superior performance of the mixed-norm clustering model and the efficiency of PIP for solving the MIP model, which may be intractable otherwise. In particular, the $\ell_{2,1}$ mixed-norm clustering model is effective under coordinate-sparse, mean-balanced contamination, whereas the $\ell_{1,2}$ model is preferred under dense coordinatewise Cauchy contamination. The numerical results also show that PIP can escape poor alternating solutions and obtain substantially better feasible clustering, while preserving strong warm starts when no improvement is found.

</details>


### [133] [Bi-ZOL: Bilevel Zeroth-Order Learning with Nonsmooth Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08021)
*Zhisen Jiang,Saverio Bolognani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究响应预言机（response-oracle）设定下的下层级约束双层优化问题，提出结构引导的零阶方法Bi-ZOL（双层零阶学习）。Bi-ZOL分离双层链式法则结构：保持精确的上层偏导，仅用零阶采样估计响应雅可比矩阵，从而得到与Clarke链式法则次微分更对齐的近似超梯度。文章证明该方法收敛到(δ,ε)-Bi-ZOL Frank-Wolfe稳定点，其偏差对分段C^{1,1}响应为O(δ)，对分段仿射响应在活动胞邻域上可消逝。实验表明Bi-ZOL在激励跟踪问题上优于普通零阶平滑方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典双层优化的响应雅可比矩阵与简化超梯度在下层级模型信息不可用、响应映射局部Lipschitz但可能非光滑的设定下可能不存在。现有零阶方法通常估计完全平滑后的简化超目标梯度，但这种方法忽略了双层结构中的链式法则特性，导致近似不够精确。因此需要一种不需要下层级模型信息、能够在非光滑响应设定下有效找到稳定点的新方法。

Method: Bi-ZOL方法的核心思想是分离双层链式法则结构：保留在查询响应处计算得到的精确上层偏导数，仅使用零阶采样来估计响应雅可比矩阵。这与完全平滑整个超目标梯度不同，得到的近似超梯度与Clarke链式法则次微分更直接地保持一致。论文还建立了Bi-ZOL方向的偏平滑（partial-smoothing）解释，量化了逐点结构偏差，并在一定的局部正则条件下证明对分段C^{1,1}响应的偏差为O(δ)，对分段仿射响应在活动胞邻域上偏差消逝。

Result: 理论上证明了Bi-ZOL在有限时间内收敛到(δ,ε)-Bi-ZOL Frank-Wolfe稳定点。在分段C^{1,1}响应的局部正则条件下，方法的结构偏差为O(δ)；在分段仿射响应上，活动胞邻域内偏差可完全消逝。在基于激励的跟踪问题（incentive-based tracking）的实验上，Bi-ZOL在相同响应预言机预算下取得了更小的稳定间隙和更低的超目标值，优于普通零阶平滑方法。

Conclusion: Bi-ZOL提供了一种在非光滑、不可访问下层级模型信息的响应预言机设定下求解双层优化问题的有效途径。通过结构引导地分离链式法则，仅对雅可比进行零阶采样而非对整个超梯度平滑，使得方向与Clarke次微分更对齐，从而实现更优的收敛性能与更小的结构性偏差。

Abstract: This paper studies lower-level-constrained bilevel optimization in a response-oracle setting, where lower-level model information is unavailable and the induced response mapping is locally Lipschitz but potentially nonsmooth. In this setting, the classical response Jacobian and reduced hypergradient may fail to exist. We propose Bilevel Zeroth-Order Learning (Bi-ZOL), a structure-guided zeroth-order method for finding stationary points of the nonsmooth reduced problem. Instead of estimating the gradient of a fully smoothed reduced hyperobjective, Bi-ZOL separates the bilevel chain-rule structure: it keeps the exact upper-level partial gradients at the queried response and uses zeroth-order sampling only to estimate the response Jacobian. This construction yields an approximate hypergradient that is more directly aligned with the Clarke chain-rule subdifferential. We show that the Bi-ZOL direction admits a partial-smoothing interpretation, quantify its pointwise structural bias, and prove finite-time convergence to a $(δ,ε)$-Bi-ZOL Frank--Wolfe stationary point. The bias is $O(δ)$ for piecewise $C^{1,1}$ responses under local regularity and vanishes for piecewise affine responses on active-cell neighborhoods. Experiments on incentive-based tracking problems show that Bi-ZOL achieves smaller stationarity gaps and lower hyperobjective values than vanilla zeroth-order smoothing under comparable response-oracle budgets.

</details>


### [134] [M-duality in fixed-point problems for general normed spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08151)
*Jongmin Lee,Sangyeop Woo,Ernest K. Ryu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在一般赋范空间中建立了Mann迭代的M-对偶定理，并利用该对偶性推导出新的算法，其最坏情况残差界严格优于此前最优的Halpern迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不动点迭代中，通过减小残差界来求解问题是一类核心方法，Halpern迭代被认为能有效减小残差。近期在凸优化和不动点问题领域发现了新的对偶定理，揭示了互为对偶的算法在收敛行为上的某种对称性。然而，现有对偶定理依赖Lyapunov风格的证明结构，且主要局限于Hilbert空间。作者希望在一般赋范空间中也建立类似的对偶理论，并借此改进现有算法的最坏情况残差界。

Method: 首先在一般赋范空间中，针对带Lipschitz算子的Mann迭代建立了M-对偶定理，仅需对迭代系数施加温和条件。在此基础上，利用该M-对偶性构造新算法，使其最坏情况残差界比Halpern迭代更小。

Result: 成功建立了一般赋范空间中Mann迭代的M-对偶定理，并基于此推导出新的算法，其最坏情况残差界严格优于此前在一般赋范空间中公认的最优方法——Halpern迭代。

Conclusion: 本文将对偶理论从Hilbert空间推广到一般赋范空间，利用M-对偶性打破了Halpern迭代在一般赋范空间中残差界上的最优性，为不动点迭代算法设计提供了新的理论工具和更优的算法方案。

Abstract: Solving fixed-point problems by reducing residual bounds is one of the central approaches in fixed-point iteration, and, in particular, the Halpern iteration is known to effectively reduce the residual. Recently, a new type of duality theorem has been discovered in the convex optimization and fixed-point problem literature and establish a certain symmetry in the convergence behavior of algorithms that are dual to each other. However, these duality theorems rely on a Lyapunov-style proof structure and mostly focus on Hilbert spaces. In this work, we first establish an M-duality theorem for Mann iterations with Lipschitz operators in general normed spaces, assuming only mild conditions on the coefficients of the iterations. Moreover, leveraging this M-duality, we derive new algorithms that attain a strictly smaller worst-case residual bound than that of the Halpern iteration, which was previously known as the state-of-the-art method in general normed spaces.

</details>


### [135] [Risk-Averse Bayesian Games with an Unknown Type Distribution: Bayesian Learning, Equilibrium Analysis, and Finite-Sample Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08274)
*Yuan Tao,Huifu Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一群短视（myopic）玩家在贝叶斯纳什假设下重复博弈，并从观测到的类型剖面中通过贝叶斯学习框架学习参数化的联合类型分布。提出两种模型：风险厌恶贝叶斯学习者的贝叶斯纳什均衡（BNE-RABL）和基于贝叶斯预测分布的BNE（BNE-BPD），前者分别评估认知不确定性和偶然不确定性带来的风险，后者将两类不确定性整合进预测分布并用单一风险度量评估。在适当条件下证明两种均衡的存在性与唯一性，并推导出均衡序列向真实分布下oracle BNE收敛的非渐近收敛速率，且两种模型的策略差异随样本增大而消失。最后应用于价格竞争问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯博弈假设玩家类型的联合分布是共同知识，但这一假设在实际应用中极少成立。本文旨在解决这一核心问题——当分布参数未知、玩家需要通过观测数据学习时，如何构建和分析均衡概念。

Method: 在贝叶斯学习框架内，研究持有异质先验的短视玩家从历史类型观测中学习参数化联合类型分布。提出两种均衡模型：(1) BNE-RABL，将认知不确定性（关于分布参数）与偶然不确定性（竞争对手类型）分开，分别由不同风险度量评估；(2) BNE-BPD，将两类不确定性统一整合进贝叶斯预测分布，通过单一风险度量评估。两种模型均允许玩家具有风险厌恶偏好。

Result: 在适当条件下证明两种均衡的存在性与唯一性；推导出均衡序列随样本量增长向真实类型分布下oracle BNE收敛的非渐近收敛速率；证明BNE-RABL与BNE-BPD之间的策略偏差随样本量增大而趋于零，即两模型互为有效近似。

Conclusion: 两种模型在有限样本下分别从不同角度刻画不确定性，但随博弈重复进行和观测数据增多，它们趋于一致并逼近真实分布下的均衡。模型被应用于价格竞争博弈并数值验证了理论结果。

Abstract: Classical Bayesian games assume that the joint distribution of players' types is common knowledge, an assumption that rarely holds in practical applications. We address this issue by studying a group of myopic players who repeatedly interact under the Bayesian Nash conjecture and learn a parametric joint type distribution from type profiles observed over time within a Bayesian learning framework. Players may hold heterogeneous priors and may be risk averse toward both epistemic uncertainty about the distributional parameter and aleatoric uncertainty in their rivals' types. We propose two models: a Bayesian Nash equilibrium among risk-averse Bayesian learners (BNE-RABL), in which epistemic and aleatoric risks are evaluated separately, and a BNE based on Bayesian predictive distributions (BNE-BPD), in which the two sources of uncertainty are integrated into a Bayesian predictive distribution and evaluated through a single risk measure. Under suitable conditions, we establish the existence and uniqueness of both equilibria, and derive non-asymptotic convergence rates of the equilibrium sequences toward the corresponding oracle BNE under the true type distribution as the game is repeatedly played and more type profiles are observed. These results further show that the discrepancy between the BNE-RABL and BNE-BPD strategies vanishes as the sample size grows, which illustrates that the two models can be regarded as effective approximations to each other. We apply the proposed models to a price competition problem and numerically illustrate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [136] [How to Make the Gradient Mapping Small for Constrained Stochastic Min-Max Problems and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08380)
*Ahmet Alacaoglu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带约束或正则化的凸-凹极小极大优化和随机单调变分不等式问题在梯度映射（前向-后向残差/自然残差）度量下的随机一阶预言复杂度。将梯度映射复杂度从已知最优的Õ(ε^{-4})改进为Õ(ε^{-2})，并进一步在无界方差（Blum-Gladyshev假设）情形下证明同样的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束凸-凹极小极大问题中，梯度映射是衡量次优性的自然推广，但其随机复杂度Õ(ε^{-4})远逊于无约束情形下的近最优Õ(ε^{-2})，存在显著的复杂度间隙，亟需弥合。

Method: 通过改进随机一阶算法的分析，利用前向-后向算子/自然残差的结构性质，在标准无偏预言和方差假设下将复杂度从ε^{-4}降至ε^{-2}，并引入Blum-Gladyshev假设处理无界方差情形以扩展结果。

Result: 对于约束凸-凹极小极大问题，在梯度映射度量下的复杂度达到Õ(ε^{-2})，与无约束情形的近最优复杂度相匹配；在放宽到Blum-Gladyshev方差假设下仍保持该复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了约束与无约束情形在梯度映射复杂度上的差距，证明了约束凸-凹极小极大问题在最坏情形下可达到与无约束情形一致的近最优随机一阶复杂度。

Abstract: We study the stochastic first-order oracle complexity for constrained or regularized convex-concave min-max optimization and stochastic monotone variational inequalities. We focus on the case when suboptimality is measured in terms of the gradient mapping, also known as, forward-backward or natural residual, an optimality notion that generalizes the gradient norm for unconstrained problems. In this setting, under standard unbiased oracle access with now-standard variance assumptions, the best-known complexity for making the norm of the gradient mapping less than $\varepsilon$ is $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-4})$, compared to the near-optimal $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ that is established in the unconstrained case. We bridge this gap to improve the gradient mapping complexity for constrained convex-concave min-max problems to $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$. We then extend to prove the same complexity for problems without the bounded variance, by using the Blum-Gladyshev assumption.

</details>


### [137] [Identification of forward models: a nonparametric approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08440)
*Giulio Fattore,Marco Peruzzo,Giacomo Sartori,Mattia Zorzi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于核的方法用于辨识前向模型的脉冲响应。所得到的估计器构成一个非线性Tikhonov正则化问题，作者证明了该问题解的存在性，从而证明了用高阶MAx模型（带外生输入的自回归滑动平均模型）近似前向模型的合理性。作者还针对边际似然优化来估计核超参数，由于边际似然没有闭式表达式，提出基于Laplace近似的方法进行评估。数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向模型的脉冲响应辨识是系统辨识领域的重要问题。已有的线性核方法在处理某些非线性结构时存在局限，需要一种能够同时保证解存在性且具有实际可操作性的核方法。

Method: 提出新的基于核的估计方法，将辨识问题转化为非线性Tikhonov正则化问题；证明解的存在性以支撑高阶MAX模型近似；利用Laplace近似来处理无闭式解的边际似然，用于估计核超参数。

Result: 证明了非线性Tikhonov正则化问题解的存在性；给出了基于Laplace近似的边际似然评估方法；通过数值实验展示了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该核方法在理论上保证了非线性正则化问题解的存在性，使得采用高阶MAX模型进行前向模型近似具有合法性与合理性，且实验结果支持该方法的实用性。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a new kernel-based method for the identification of the impulse responses of forward models. The resulting estimator leads to a nonlinear Tikhonov regularization problem for which we prove the existence of a solution. The latter result makes legitimate to approximate the forward model through a high-order Moving Average with eXogenous input (MAX) model, i.e. the numerical solution found using this model introduces only a negligible bias in the estimate. The optimization of the marginal likelihood to estimate the kernel hyperparameters is also taken into account. Since there does not exist a closed-form expression for the marginal likelihood, we present an evaluation method that relies on the Laplace approximation of the marginal likelihood. Finally, some numerical experiments are discussed to show the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [138] [Wasserstein Stability and Free Boundaries in Measure-Parameterized Bilevel Obstacle Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08454)
*Kun Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类受障碍约束的变分问题，其约化能量通过下层优化器依赖于概率律。在一致强凸条件下，下层响应为单值Lipschitz映射；在凸性与Poincaré不等式下，上层策略唯一。当约化边际密度具有类型-Lipschitz性质时，策略映射从1-Wasserstein度量到能量空间是Lipschitz连续的。在一维线性障碍子类中，策略导数等于累积驱动力的正部，单位交叉性使重合集为区间；交叉阶数为m时，端点具有W_1^{1/m}模量，奇次幂例子表明该指数是紧的。针对紧集上的经验律，建立了策略与阈值的有限样本率；在横截总体根处，经验阈值渐近线性并满足CLT。正则斑块的局部Hausdorff稳定性由非退化切换函数保证。作者给出了一个二次响应企业的非线性公司税制的解析例示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在研究一类新颖的泛函优化框架，其中目标（能量）函数依赖于一个概率测度，且测度通过下一层变分问题的解进入目标。这类"测度依赖型障碍约束变分问题"广泛出现在经济政策设计、机制设计、最优控制等场景中。该工作试图给出从概率律空间到策略空间映射的完整正则性理论，包括Lipschitz连续性、模量估计及经验测度下的统计推断性质。

Method: （1）利用一致强凸性推导下层跟随者的单值Lipschitz映射；（2）结合凸性与Poincaré不等式建立上层策略的唯一性；（3）通过类型-Lipschitz约化边际假设，在能量空间中建立策略映射关于1-Wasserstein度量的一致性估计；（4）在一维线性障碍情形中，将策略导数表示为累积驱动力的正部分，利用交叉阶数刻画重合集端点的模量；（5）对经验律应用维度依赖型Wasserstein界，建立有限样本收敛率；（6）在横截根处推导经验阈值的影响函数与CLT；（7）借助条件水平集论证和切换函数的非退化性，给出高维正则斑块的局部Hausdorff稳定性结果。

Result: 获得了策略映射的Wasserstein-Lipschitz连续性定理；建立了重合集端点的最优模量（m阶交叉给出W_1^{1/m}界，横截情形恢复Lipschitz）；给出了经验律下策略与阈值的有限样本率和渐近正态性；在高维情形证明了局部Hausdorff稳定性。解析的二次响应解构出一个具有内生零税率区域及Wasserstein稳定阈值的非线性公司税函数。

Conclusion: 本文建立了测度依赖型障碍约束变分问题的统一分析框架，串联了概率律空间的正则性、变分问题的唯一性与稳定性、以及经验推测下的统计极限性质。所得结论不仅涵盖了精确的模量/指数紧性刻画，还提供了纯解析（无需经验校准）的经济学应用示例，展现出该方法在政策设计中的可操作性与理论深度。

Abstract: We study obstacle-constrained variational problems whose reduced energies depend on a probability law through a lower-level optimizer. Uniform strong convexity yields a single-valued Lipschitz follower response, while convexity and a Poincare inequality give a unique upper-level policy. A type-Lipschitz reduced marginal then implies Lipschitz continuity of the policy map from the 1-Wasserstein metric to the energy space. In a one-dimensional linear-obstacle subclass, the policy derivative is the positive part of a cumulative forcing. Single crossing makes the coincidence set an interval. An algebraic crossing of order m gives a $W_1^{1/m}$ modulus for its endpoint; odd-power examples show that this exponent is sharp, while a transversal crossing recovers Lipschitz stability. For empirical laws on compact subsets of $\mathbb{R}^k$, dimension-dependent Wasserstein bounds yield finite-sample rates for policies and thresholds. At a transversal population root, the empirical threshold is asymptotically linear, with an explicit influence function and central limit theorem. For higher-dimensional regular patches, a conditional level-set argument gives local Hausdorff stability when a nondegenerate switching function is available. An explicit quadratic firm response produces a nonlinear corporate-tax schedule with an endogenous zero-tax region and a Wasserstein-stable threshold. The tax illustration is analytic and uses no empirical calibration.

</details>


### [139] [Rank-Adaptive and Linearly Convergent Frank--Wolfe Method over Spectrahedron via Nonconvex Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08522)
*Houduo Qi,Haoning Wang,Liping Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种秩自适应的 Frank--Wolfe (FW) 方法用于谱面体（spectrahedron）上的凸优化，其每次迭代的更新秩满足 r̂_t ≤ r_t ≤ r^*，在二次增长和严格互补条件下经有限次 burn-in 后实现线性收敛。方法结合非凸谱 oracle 与高效秩代理，在无需先验知道最优秩 r^* 的情况下弥合了低秩效率与快速收敛之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对谱面体上凸优化的 FW 方法，一个悬而未决的问题是：当块更新变体的每次迭代更新秩不超过（未知的）最优秩 r^* 时，其能否实现线性收敛。已有块更新和谱 FW 方法通常要求更新秩至少达到 r^*，且往往需要预先知道 r^* 才能获得线性收敛速率。这限制了此类方法在实际低秩问题中的适用性。

Method: 方法包含两个核心设计：(1) 一个非凸谱 oracle，利用单纯形与谱面体之间的几何联系，提供当前迭代点的阈值秩 r_t 以及闭式低秩解，但精确计算 r_t 需要完整特征分解；(2) 引入当前迭代点的"高效秩" r̂_t，作为谱 oracle 的廉价替代，继承其最优性。算法在阈值秩与高效秩之间切换，使得实际 FW 更新使用 r̂_t，保持与标准 FW 相当的单次迭代成本，并最终识别出 r^*。

Result: 理论分析证明在二次增长与严格互补这两个谱 FW 分析中常用条件下，所提秩自适应方法能在有限的 burn-in 后实现线性收敛，且每轮迭代更新秩始终满足 r̂_t ≤ r_t ≤ r^*。数值实验验证了该方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了谱面体上 FW 方法中低秩效率与快速收敛之间的矛盾，首次实现了无需先验最优秩信息、更新秩不超过 r^* 且仍能线性收敛的 FW 变体，填补了该研究方向的理论空白。

Abstract: For Frank--Wolfe (FW) methods for convex optimization over the spectrahedron, it remains open whether a block-update variant can be linearly convergent when the update rank never exceeds the (unknown) optimal rank $r^*$ at each iteration. Existing block and spectral FW methods require an update rank at least $r^*$---and typically prior knowledge of $r^*$---to obtain a linear rate. This paper develops a rank-adaptive FW method whose update ranks satisfy $\widehat{r}_t\le r_t\le r^*$ at every iteration and which converges linearly after a finite burn-in under quadratic growth and strict complementarity, the two conditions commonly used in spectral FW analyses. The method is built on two designs. The first is a nonconvex spectral oracle, motivated by the geometric connection between the simplex and the spectrahedron; it yields a thresholding rank $r_t$ of the current iterate and a closed-form low-rank solution. Computing $r_t$ exactly, however, requires a full eigendecomposition. The second introduces the efficient rank $\widehat{r}_t$ of the current iterate, a cheap surrogate that inherits the optimality properties of the spectral oracle. The algorithm switches between the thresholding rank and the efficient rank so that the actual FW update uses $\widehat{r}_t$, keeps the per-iteration cost comparable to standard FW, and eventually identifies $r^*$. These results close the gap between low-rank efficiency and fast convergence for Frank--Wolfe methods over the spectrahedron. Numerical experiments demonstrate the advantage of the proposed method.

</details>


### [140] [Structured Positive-Definite Optimal Control Synthesis of Closed-Loop Recommendation Systems over Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08567)
*Mariano Simone,Frasca Paolo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究网络化多主题舆论动态中的反馈推荐策略设计，将其建模为无限时域线性二次最优控制问题，通过意见对齐作为参与度代理，并同时惩罚极化、偏离无控制均衡及当前意见、推荐努力等。利用agent-wise表示暴露社交网络互连结构，在阶段成本严格正定假设下，仿射最优控制问题分解为严格凸稳态优化与LQR问题。集中式Riccati控制器作为性能基准，结构化增益通过基于耗散性和H2的LMI代理公式获得，可重叠的证书簇提供可扩展的局部充分条件，稳态簇分解提供精确共识重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体平台需要设计推荐策略来影响用户意见动态，但在多主题网络化舆论环境中，如何平衡用户参与度（意见对齐）与控制极化、偏离等目标之间的权衡仍是一个重要且具挑战性的问题。作者聚焦于利用最优控制理论在社交网络上设计具有结构约束的反馈推荐策略。

Method: 将推荐策略设计建模为无限时域LQ最优控制问题，性能指标同时惩罚极化、偏离无控制均衡、偏离当前意见、推荐努力及邻居间推荐不一致性。采用agent-wise表示揭示网络互连结构；在阶段成本严格正定假设下，将仿射最优控制问题分解为：(1) 严格凸稳态优化（确定最优均衡和仿射控制器偏移），(2) LQR问题（确定静态状态反馈增益）。集中式Riccati控制器作为基准，结构化增益通过耗散性/ H2基LMI代理公式求解，可重叠证书簇提供局部充分条件，稳态簇分解提供共识重新构造。

Result: 通过分解问题，集中式Riccati控制器提供了性能基准，而结构化控制器设计可通过LMI公式实现。可重叠的证书簇产生可扩展的局部充分条件，稳态优化通过簇分解获得精确共识重构。数值实验在社交网络上展示了闭环行为以及性能与控制器局部性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为网络化多主题舆论动态中的反馈推荐策略提供了系统的控制设计框架，能够在追求控制性能的同时兼顾控制器结构（局部性）约束，为实际社交媒体平台中分散化、可扩展的推荐策略部署奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the design of feedback recommendation policies for networked multi-topic opinion dynamics. The design of recommendations is formulated as an infinite-horizon linear-quadratic optimal control problem that favors suggestions aligned with each agent's current opinion, thereby using opinion alignment as a proxy for engagement. At the same time, the performance index penalizes polarization, deviation from the uncontrolled equilibrium of the opinion dynamics, and from the current agents' opinion, recommendation effort, and favors coherence among neighbors' recommendations. An agent-wise representation exposes the interconnection structure of the social network and enables structured controller and certificate synthesis. Under the assumption that the stage cost of the performance index is strictly positive definite, the affine optimal control problem separates into a strictly convex steady-state optimization, which determines the optimal equilibrium and the affine controller offset, and an LQR problem, which yields the static state-feedback gain. The resulting centralized Riccati controller provides a performance benchmark, while structured gains are obtained from dissipativity-based and $\mathcal H_2$-based LMI surrogate formulations. Possibly overlapping certificate clusters yield scalable local sufficient conditions for the structured gain design, while a separate steady-state cluster decomposition provides an exact consensus reformulation of the steady-state optimization. Numerical results on a social network illustrate the closed-loop behavior and the trade-off between performance and controller locality.

</details>


### [141] [McCormick relaxations for PDE-constrained optimization on multi-dimensional domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08614)
*Mariia Pokotylo,Paul Manns*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究利用McCormick不等式计算非凸最优控制问题的近似对偶界。作者将现有理论扩展到含双线性项的多维域椭圆型PDE上，并推广了总变差罚项离散化的假设条件。同时证明了状态变量有效界的存在性，使其能够启用基于优化的界紧化（OBBT）过程来收紧McCormick不等式。由于理论界相当保守，OBBT成为获得尖锐近似对偶界的关键但也是主要计算瓶颈；论文展示了如何对OBBT中产生的一系列LP进行合理的热启动，从而将数值实验成功扩展到比以往更精细的离散化网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸最优控制问题的求解需要紧的近似对偶界，而McCormick不等式提供了一种有效途径；但相关的数值计算负担巨大，其界收紧过程（尤其OBBT）是计算瓶颈。现有理论局限于特定场景，缺乏对多维域椭圆PDE双线性项情形以及离散化灵活性方面的普适性支撑，亟需理论扩展与高效的数值求解策略。

Method: 采用McCormick不等式构造非凸最优控制问题的凸松弛以获得近似对偶界。具体方法包括：(1)对PDE约束中的双线性项进行局部平均处理以缓解数值负担；(2)将相关理论推广至多维域含双线性项的椭圆PDE；(3)推广必要假设以允许总变差罚项离散化的灵活选择；(4)证明状态变量有效界的存在性以支撑OBBT；(5)利用OBBT过程中产生的LP序列的结构相似性进行热启动以加速求解。

Result: 理论上扩展了基于McCormick不等式的近似对偶界方法，将其适用范围从原有情形拓展到多维域含双线性椭圆PDE，并放宽了总变差离散化假设；证明了状态变量有效界的存在以支撑OBBT。数值上，通过LP热启动策略显著加速了OBBT过程，使得实验能够在比先前更细的离散化网格上成功运行，获得了更尖锐的近似对偶界。

Conclusion: 该工作为含双线性项的椭圆PDE约束的非凸最优控制问题提供了一套完整的近似对偶界计算框架，理论上填补了多维域情形的空白并使离散化选择更加灵活，数值上通过LP热启动突破了OBBT的计算瓶颈，实现了更精细网格上的可扩展计算。

Abstract: We are interested in computing approximate dual bounds for nonconvex optimal control problems using McCormick inequalities. To handle the numerical burden concerned with the tightening of these inequalities, we locally average the bilinear term in the PDE constraint. We extend recent theory in this area to elliptic PDEs on multidimensional domains that involve bilinear terms. As an additional result, we also generalize the necessary assumptions to allow for more flexibility in the choice of the discretization for the total variation penalty term from the underlying control problem. Furthermore, we prove the existence of valid bounds on the state variable, which are crucial to enable the optimization-based bound-tightening (OBBT) procedure to tighten the McCormick inequalities.
  Since theoretical bounds on the state variable are rather conservative, OBBT is both crucial and the main computational bottleneck for deriving a sharp approximate dual bound. We show how the sequence of LPs arising during OBBT may sensibly be warmstarted, allowing us to successfully scale the numerical experiments to finer discretizations than what was possible before.

</details>


### [142] [Optimal input design via Frank-Wolfe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08751)
*Fethi Bencherki,Bruce Lee,Nikolai Matni,Anders Rantzer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域线性动力系统的最优输入设计问题，旨在能量预算约束下最小化加权逆协方差（信息）准则。由于因果策略可达的协方差集合虽凸但缺乏显式描述，作者提出用Frank-Wolfe算法求解：每个线性最小化子问题为带预算约束的有限时域LQ问题，可通过Riccati递推和一维二分搜索求解。利用目标函数的光滑性建立O(1/M)的目标值收敛率，利用强凸性得到O(1/√M)的迭代序列收敛率，并将框架推广到未知动力学的系统辨识输入设计和自适应在线LQR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是解决因果策略下最优输入设计的计算难题。虽然可达协方差集合具有凸性，但缺少可操作的显式描述，使得基于投影的优化方法（如投影梯度法）不可行。因此需要一种不依赖投影的优化框架来高效求解该问题。

Method: 采用Frank-Wolfe（条件梯度）算法求解。关键洞察在于：每个线性最小化子问题天然对应一个带能量预算约束的有限时域线性二次（LQ）最优控制问题，可通过Riccati递推配合关于Lagrange乘子的一维二分搜索高效求解。理论分析方面，利用目标函数在可行集上的光滑性证明目标值O(1/M)收敛率，利用强凸性证明迭代点O(1/√M)收敛率。此外还将该框架扩展到未知动力学系统辨识的输入设计以及自适应在线LQR场景。

Result: 建立了Frank-Wolfe方法的目标值O(1/M)收敛率和迭代点O(1/√M)收敛率，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Frank-Wolfe方法为有限时域线性系统的最优输入设计提供了一种无需投影的有效求解途径，并能自然扩展到系统辨识与自适应控制等更广泛的场景，具备理论保证和实际可行性。

Abstract: We study optimal input design over a finite horizon for linear dynamical systems. The goal is to minimize a weighted inverse-covariance (information) criterion subject to an energy budget. The set of covariances achievable by causal policies is convex but lacks a tractable explicit description, ruling out projection-based methods. We show that Frank--Wolfe applies naturally: each linear minimization subproblem is a budget-constrained finite-horizon linear quadratic (LQ) problem, solvable by a Riccati recursion and one-dimensional bisection over a Lagrange multiplier. Using smoothness of the objective over the feasible set, we establish an $\mathcal{O}(1/M)$ convergence rate for the objective value, while strong convexity yields an $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{M})$ rate for the iterates. We further extend the framework to input design for system identification with unknown dynamics and adaptive online LQR, and illustrate the approach numerically.

</details>


### [143] [On the cost of reaching an attainable state for the boundary-controlled 1-D heat equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08842)
*Olivier Glass*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实直线区间上具有零初始条件与Dirichlet边界控制的热方程的"可达成本"问题。给定在时间T>0时可被合适边界控制达到的目标状态，估计到达该状态所需的最小控制的大小（用该状态的适当范数表示），并证明该估计在时间域趋于0时具有某种最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边界控制理论中，对于一个可控系统，不仅需要知道某个状态是否可达，还需要了解到达该状态所需的"成本"（即最小控制能量）。特别是当时间域很小时，控制成本通常会急剧增长，精确刻画这种成本与状态范数之间的关系以及对极小时间行为的估计，是热方程精确可控性研究中的核心问题之一。

Method: 论文在实直线区间上考虑具有零初始条件和Dirichlet边界控制的热方程。对于可达到的目标状态，利用适当的范数（可能与可达集合上的某种对偶或加权范数相关）来估计达成该状态所需的最小边界控制的大小；并通过分析时间趋于0时的渐近行为来建立估计的最优性。

Result: 给出了到达可达到状态所需的最小边界控制大小的估计，该估计以目标状态的适当范数来表示；并且证明该估计在时间水平趋于0的意义下是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文为短时间热方程边界精确可达性问题提供了控制成本的最优估计，揭示了时间趋于0时可达成本与目标状态范数之间的精确依赖关系。

Abstract: We consider the heat equation in an interval of the real line, with null initial condition, and Dirichlet boundary controls. We study the cost of reaching an attainable state at some time $T>0$. Precisely, given a state in the interval that we know is reachable by suitable boundary controls, we estimate the size of the smallest controls that reach it, in terms of an appropriate norm of this state. Our estimate is optimal in some sense as the time horizon goes to $0$.

</details>


### [144] [Equilibria for Time-inconsistent Regular-singular Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08877)
*Yuting Jia,Zongxia Liang,Xiaodong Luo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间下具有非指数贴现的时间不一致混合正则-奇异控制问题，通过博弈论框架定义正则-奇异均衡与温和均衡，建立验证定理，并将其应用于努力与分红问题，得到显式均衡解及数值分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间不一致的控制问题在实际经济与金融模型中广泛存在（如非指数贴现），但涉及混合正则-奇异控制的均衡研究尚属空白。作者旨在建立统一的理论框架，将正则与奇异控制置于同一博弈论视角下，以刻划时间一致的均衡策略。

Method: 采用代际内博弈（intrapersonal game）框架：首先通过"控制法则"将正则与奇异控制统一，提出正则-奇异均衡的新定义并建立充分性验证定理；随后提出对两类控制分别施加扰动的"温和均衡"概念及相应的温和验证定理。在应用层面，通过求解扩展的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB)系统，在混合指数贴现与伪指数贴现函数下解析构造努力与分红的最优阈值策略，并严格证明值函数的凸性及均衡条件。

Result: 在均匀指数贴现和伪指数贴现场景下获得了依赖努力阈值与分红阈值的显式均衡解；严格证明了值函数的凸性并验证了均衡条件；在混合指数贴现下建立了努力与分红阈值的存在性；数值实验揭示了外生参数对阈值及均衡值函数的影响及其经济含义。

Conclusion: 本文为时间不一致的混合正则-奇异控制问题提供了统一且严格的均衡理论与验证工具，并通过努力-分红问题的显式解展示了方法的实用性。数值结果表明参数对均衡策略和值函数有显著影响，对相关经济模型的策略设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: This paper studies a time-inconsistent mixed regular-singular control problem in continuous time with non-exponential discount. We seek time-consistent equilibria in an intrapersonal game framework and propose a novel definition of regular-singular equilibrium where both regular and singular controls are unified via control laws. We establish a verification theorem to provide a sufficient condition of the equilibrium. Furthermore, we propose a novel concept of mild equilibrium for which perturbations are applied separately to the regular and singular control and give the mild verification theorem. We illustrate the applicability of the theory to the effort and dividend problem and obtain an explicit equilibrium depending on the effort and dividend thresholds under a mixture of exponential discount functions and a pseudo-exponential discount function by solving the extended Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman (HJB) system. The convexity of the value function is rigorously established, and the equilibrium conditions are verified. In addition, we establish the existence of the effort and dividend thresholds under a mixture of exponential discount functions. Numerical results reveal the impacts of exogenous parameters on the effort and dividend thresholds and the equilibrium value function, along with their economic implications.

</details>


### [145] [Distributed Linear Programming on GPU Clusters at Extreme Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09108)
*Arnaud Deza,Santanu Dey,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: SHARDLP是一个分布式GPU线性规划求解器，将矩阵和原始-对偶状态从分片的输入到求解输出全程保持分区存储，突破了单节点内存限制。在Google PDLP基准上，9/11实例达到发布标准（优于CPU版本）；8块H200 GPU在9.9分钟内解决了11.85亿变量、63.38亿非零元的大规模LP（较CPU版21.06小时大幅加速）；扩展测试支持高达136.04亿变量和408.07亿非零元，最多覆盖76块GPU和29个计算节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模线性规划问题可能超出单计算节点内存。虽然一阶方法用适合GPU的矩阵-向量积替代了稀疏分解，但求解器的其他阶段仍会引入单节点内存瓶颈（即内存分区问题仍未全程解决）。

Method: 提出SHARDLP框架，实现求解全程（从分片输入到解输出）的矩阵与原始-对偶状态的分布式GPU分区存储。针对列分区求解，采用支持感知（support-aware）的通信机制，跳过对某行无系数存储的GPU，减少不必要通信。

Result: 在Google PDLP基准上，SHARDLP在11个实例中的9个达到发布标准（CPU PDLP研究为8个）。最大基准实例上，8块H200 GPU在9.9分钟内求解含11.85亿变量、63.38亿非零元的LP，远快于CPU实验报道的21.06小时。多节点求解验证达136.04亿变量、408.07亿非零元，验证执行覆盖最多76块GPU、29个节点。在含27.6亿非零元的LP上，支持感知通信削减92.97%的模拟通信量，求解时间提升1.27x-1.52x。

Conclusion: SHARDLP证明了通过全程分区的GPU求解架构可以显著突破大规模LP的单节点内存限制，并在求解时间上较CPU一阶方法取得数量级加速；支持感知通信策略能有效提升列分区求解的通信效率。

Abstract: Large linear programs can exceed the memory of a single compute node. Although first-order methods replace sparse factorizations with GPU-suited matrix-vector products, other solver phases can reintroduce a single-node memory limit. We present SHARDLP, a distributed GPU LP solver that keeps the matrix and primal-dual state partitioned from sharded input through solution output. On the Google PDLP benchmark, SHARDLP reaches the published criterion on nine of eleven instances, compared with eight in the published CPU PDLP study. On the largest benchmark, eight H200 GPUs solve a 1.185-billion-variable, 6.338-billion-nonzero LP in 9.9 minutes; the published CPU experiment reports 21.06 hours on different hardware. Beyond this benchmark, separately checked multi-node solves reach up to 13.604 billion variables and 40.807 billion nonzeros, while validated executions span up to 76 GPUs across 29 compute nodes. For column-partitioned solves, support-aware communication skips GPUs that store no coefficients for a row; on an LP with 2.76 billion nonzeros, it cuts modelled communication by 92.97% and improves solver time by 1.27x-1.52x

</details>


### [146] [Silver Rate Is (Almost) Optimal for Gradient Descent Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09152)
*Yuhan Ye,Kaizhao Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在光滑凸优化中，使用预定非负步长的梯度下降（GD）算法能达到的最佳多项式收敛速度。作者证明了非任意时刻（non-anytime）设定下的Ω(n^{-p_sil-O(√(log log n/log n))})下界，以及任意时刻（anytime）设定下任意无限非负步长调度在无限多个时间水平上误差为Ω(n^{-2p_sil/(1+p_sil)-O(√(log log n/log n))})的下界。结合已有的silver schedule上界和anytime上界，本文确定了两种设定下的最优多项式收敛指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降是优化领域最基础的算法之一，其收敛速度能否通过精心设计的预定非负步长加以加速是一个核心理论问题。此前已有silver schedule给出上界，但缺乏匹配的下界来确认这些收敛指数是否为最优。本文旨在填补这一空白，确定在光滑凸优化中预定非负步长GD算法在非anytime和anytime两种设定下的确切的收敛指数下界。

Method: 作者通过构造对抗性的光滑凸函数（很可能基于silver stepsize的某种对偶或最坏情形构型），并利用信息论或最坏情形分析手段，推导出收敛误差的下界。在非anytime设定中证明了与silver schedule上界匹配的指数下界；在anytime设定中证明了任何无限非负步长调度都至少存在无限多个不良时间水平，其误差以某个特定指数衰减。关键的技术难点在于处理polynomial convergence exponent的精确确定，其中出现了p_sil=log_2(1+√2)这一常数以及√(log log n/log n)阶的修正项。

Result: 本文证明：1) 在非anytime设定下，任何预定非负步长GD的误差下界为Ω(n^{-p_sil-O(√(log log n/log n))})，与[Altschuler and Parrilo, 2025]的silver schedule上界匹配；2) 在anytime设定下，任何无限非负步长调度都必然存在无限多个时间水平使误差达到Ω(n^{-2p_sil/(1+p_sil)-O(√(log log n/log n))})，与[Zhang et al., 2025]的anytime上界匹配。从而在两种设定下都确定了最优多项式收敛指数。

Conclusion: 本文的结果表明：在非anytime设定下，最优多项式收敛指数由p_sil≈1.2716决定；在anytime设定下，最优指数退化为2p_sil/(1+p_sil)≈1.1195。这确定了预定非负步长GD在光滑凸优化中的信息论极限，完整解答了该问题在两设定下的最优收敛速率问题。

Abstract: We study how far gradient descent (GD) can be accelerated by predetermined nonnegative stepsizes in smooth convex optimization. Writing $p_{\mathrm{sil}}=\log_2(1+\sqrt{2})$, we prove an $Ω\left(n^{-p_{\mathrm{sil}}-O(\sqrt{\log\log n/\log n})}\right)$ non-anytime lower bound. In the anytime setting, every infinite nonnegative schedule has infinitely many horizons with error $Ω\left(n^{-\frac{2p_{\mathrm{sil}}}{1+p_{\mathrm{sil}}}-O(\sqrt{\log\log n/\log n})}\right)$. Together with the silver-schedule upper bound [Altschuler and Parrilo, 2025] and the anytime upper bound [Zhang et al., 2025], our results determine the optimal polynomial convergence exponents in both settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [AhaBench: Do Agents Learn from Prior Experience? A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05435)
*Zerui Cheng,Jiawei Xu,Huacan Chai,Jiayang Sun,Pramod Viswanath,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AhaBench是一个用于评估语言智能体在长时程任务中能否从经验中学习并改善后续行为的基准套件，包含三个组件（Aha-Puzzle、Aha-Euler、Aha-Vending），通过三部分评分卡（初始分数、经验后分数、学习提升）来分解评估模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言智能体被期望在长时间跨度上运行（如追问、复用样例、处理工具反馈、适应延迟后果），但大多数现有评估仍然在提示后重置智能体或仅评分单条轨迹的最终状态，无法回答"当固定模型获得有用经验后，其在移除/改变/延迟显式支持的相关评估条件下行为是否改善"这一操作性问题。

Method: AhaBench包含三个组件：Aha-Puzzle测试在解决隐藏状态谜题后无提示探索能力；Aha-Euler将Project-Euler式数学思想转化为生成的教学/留出任务并配备精确验证器；Aha-Vending（受Vending-Bench启发的开源实现）测试模拟售货智能体在处理延迟反馈和运营事件时是否保持盈利。评估采用三部分评分卡：初始分数（Initial Score）衡量起始能力、经验后分数（Post-Experience Score）衡量后续经验结果、学习提升（Learning Lift）为两者之差。

Result: 在8个模型的共同面板上，Claude Opus 4.6在聚合经验后分数上领先（64.3），聚合学习提升为+25.8；Gemini 3.1 Pro紧随其后（63.4）。组件结果揭示：谜题轨迹能提高有支持分数但往往未能转化为无提示探索行为；Aha-Euler完整教学达到78.6%-100.0%而仅答案迁移范围仅0.0%-73.9%；Aha-Vending能区分盈利性事件处理与破产/无订单失败。

Conclusion: 能够良好利用可见支持的模型、能够达到高经验后分数的模型、以及在运行中提升最多的模型并不总是同一批模型——这一分解是AhaBench的主要实证信息。研究团队发布了基准任务、评分规则、验证器、模拟器代码和评估新智能体的接口。

Abstract: Modern language agents are expected to operate over long horizons: they ask follow-up questions, reuse worked examples, handle tool feedback, and adapt to delayed consequences. Most evaluations still reset the agent after a prompt or score only the final state of one trajectory. AhaBench asks a more operational question: when a fixed model receives useful experience, does its later behavior improve under a related evaluation condition where the obvious support has been removed, changed, or delayed? The suite contains three components. Aha-Puzzle tests no-hint exploration after solved hidden-state puzzles; Aha-Euler turns Project-Euler-style mathematical ideas into generated taught/held-out tasks with exact validators; and Aha-Vending, an open-source implementation inspired by Vending-Bench, tests whether a simulated vending agent remains profitable while handling delayed feedback and operational incidents. AhaBench reports a three-part scorecard: Initial Score measures starting competence, Post-Experience Score measures the later empirical outcome, and Learning Lift is their difference. This decomposition is the main empirical message: models that use visible support well, models that reach high post-experience scores, and models that improve most during a run are not always the same. On the common eight-model panel, Claude Opus 4.6 leads aggregate Post-Experience Score at 64.3 and aggregate Learning Lift at +25.8, with Gemini 3.1 Pro close behind at 63.4. The component results explain the split: puzzle traces raise supported scores but often fail to become no-hint exploration behavior; Aha-Euler full teaching reaches 78.6-100.0% while answer-only transfer ranges from 0.0 to 73.9%; and Aha-Vending separates profitable incident handling from bankruptcy and no-order failure. We release benchmark tasks, rubrics, validators, simulator code, and interfaces for evaluating new agents.

</details>


### [148] [When Do Options Help? Policy Necrosis and Redundant Coverage in Option-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05508)
*Bingyun Liu,Yuheng Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过理论和实验解释了Option-Critic算法的性能提升来源。研究发现：终止规则对性能提升没有贡献（其学习结果等同于始终终止）；选项内部策略几乎不进行探索，导致"策略坏死"现象（约五分之三的状态坏死）；额外选项并非改善单个选项，而是降低了所有选项在同一状态失败的概率（从59%降至4%），性能提升正源于这一联合量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Option-Critic宣称选项增多会提升性能，但其标题性结论的来源机制尚不明确。作者希望从机制上解释这一现象，厘清终止规则、选项内部策略的探索以及选项数量各自的实际贡献，从而深入理解分层强化学习中选项学习的真实工作原理。

Method: 结合理论与实验进行分析：(1)数学证明终止规则在最大化合回报的目标下、当其与选项选择策略读取相同值时会在每一步触发，等价于始终终止；在选项探索而测试不探索时可能阻碍探索，产生Ω(T)遗憾；(2)提出"策略坏死"（policy necrosis）概念，给出状态级检测方法，并通过实验统计坏死状态比例；(3)通过恢复探索来修复坏死状态；(4)分析多选项中联合失败概率的影响。

Result: 终止规则学习无贡献；强制每步终止时选项数-性能曲线保持不变。约五分之三的状态在典型选项中处于坏死状态。修复探索后单个选项即可解决问题。额外选项将"所有选项在相同状态全部失败"的概率从59%降至4%，性能提升源于该联合失败概率的降低而非单选项能力增强。

Conclusion: Option-Critic的性能提升并非来自其学习的终止规则或单个选项内在能力的增强，而是源于多选项带来的"风险均摊"效应——降低了所有选项共同失败的可能性。终止规则贡献为零，内部探索的缺失造成策略坏死，恢复探索才是关键，而这可以在单选项下实现。

Abstract: Option-critic learns options: sub-policies together with a learned rule for when each one hands control back. Its headline result is that performance improves as options are added. We explain that result, with theory and experiment. First, the termination rule option-critic learns by maximising return contributes nothing. When the termination test and the policy that picks options read the same values, the test fires at every step, so the learned rule is identical to always terminating. When that policy explores and the test does not, as in option-critic itself, the rule can block the exploration; there are instances where it suffers $Ω(T)$ regret while always terminating holds to $O(\log T)$. Forcing termination at every step leaves the option-count curve intact. Second, the policy inside an option barely explores at all, so a state locks onto the first action that looked good and never updates again. We name this policy necrosis, give a state-level test for it, and find three fifths of states necrotic in a typical option. Restoring exploration repairs those states, and one option then solves the task. Third, extra options improve no option; what falls is the chance that all of them fail in the same state, from $59\\%$ to $4\\%$, and performance follows that joint quantity.

</details>


### [149] [Multi-granularity Adaptive Hypergraph Representation Learning via Granular-ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05574)
*Sen Zhao,Yifan Guan,Jinyuan Ni,Gaojie Xu,Zhang Xu,Xiaoyu Lian,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多粒度超图表示学习框架MGHRL，通过自适应粒度球分裂生成多粒度超边，并用多子网超图网络进行特征捕获与层级集成，在基准数据集上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图表示学习方法多依赖预定义规则来生成超边，忽视了图拓扑结构的多样性以及超边的多粒度特性，导致无法有效且自适应地发现高阶关系并高效处理复杂结构信息。

Method: 提出MGHRL框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 自适应粒度超图生成策略，通过粒度球的适应性分裂在不同粒度层次生成超边，基于图拓扑结构捕获高阶关系；(2) 多粒度超图网络，包含多个子网络，捕获不同粒度超边的特征，并通过层级可逆连接进行特征整合。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验结果表明，MGHRL显著优于各类基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过自适应策略生成多粒度超边并利用层级可逆连接的多子网结构进行特征融合，能够更有效地捕获图的复杂高阶关系，验证了多粒度超图学习方法的有效性。

Abstract: Hypergraph representation learning aims to capture high-order information in graphs by constructing hyperedges that simultaneously connect multiple nodes. These hyperedges adapt to the graph's topological features, facilitating the extraction of high-order relationships at multiple granularities. Most prior work relies on predefined definitions to generate hyperedges, overlooking the diversity in graph topological structures and the multi-granularity characteristics of hyperedges. As a result, this limits their ability to effectively and adaptively discover high-order relationships and efficiently process complex structural information. To address this limitation, we propose a novel framework called \underline{M}ulti-\underline{G}ranularity \underline{H}ypergraph \underline{R}epresentation \underline{L}earning (MGHRL).
