MGHRL introduces an Adaptive Granular Hypergraph Generation strategy, which generates hyperedges at multiple levels of granularity through the adaptive splitting of granular-ball, effectively capturing high-order relationships based on the graph's topological structure. Additionally, we propose a Multi-Granularity Hypergraph Network with multiple sub-networks, capturing features from hyperedges at different granularities and integrating them via hierarchical reversible connections. Experimental results show that MGHRL significantly outperforms baseline models on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [150] [Capsule Lens: Locating and Tracking Concept Geometry in Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05575)
*Yiming Tang,Harshvardhan Saini,Samyak Jha,Huaming Chen,Xufeng Duan,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Capsule Lens框架，用可闭式拟合、可解释参数定义的"胶囊"（capsule）几何形状来表征概念在模型内部表征空间中占据的区域，并在静态和动态两类表征设置下进行验证与分析，揭示不同训练范式下概念几何的动态变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性领域的一个核心问题是理解概念如何在模型内部表征中被编码。现有方法大多将表征映射到更可解释的空间，而并未直接刻画概念在表征空间中的占据方式；已有的各种假设往往缺乏严格验证，且大多聚焦于静态表征。因此需要一种能够直接定位、分析并追踪概念几何形状的框架。

Method: Capsule Lens框架将概念占据的区域与一种简单、可追踪的几何形状——"胶囊"（capsule）进行匹配。该胶囊由若干可解释参数定义，以闭式解对每个概念的几何形状进行拟合，并在留存样本（held-out samples）上进行验证。框架应用于两类设置：静态表征（定位各类模型中的概念几何，通过span和norm曲线揭示关键几何特征）与动态表征（三个案例：CLIP预训练、视觉问答上的RL后训练、数学推理上的RL后训练，追踪表征漂移）。

Result: 三个动态表征案例揭示了性质不同的几何动态：CLIP预训练表现为广泛的网络级重构，而RL后训练则呈现局部化、概念特异性的变化。实验发现了与现有文献一致的结果以及新观察。

Conclusion: Capsule Lens有望成为在静态与动态表征中定位、分析和追踪概念几何的实用工具，为机制可解释性研究提供了新方法。

Abstract: Understanding how concepts are encoded in the internal representations of machine learning models is a central problem in mechanistic interpretability, essential both for the science of deep learning and for the trustworthy deployment of increasingly capable models. Existing approaches to interpret model representations mainly map representations onto more interpretable spaces and do not directly characterize how concepts occupy representation space; various hypotheses have been proposed, but often lack of rigorous validation and largely focus on static representations. In this work, we introduce Capsule Lens, a framework that matches the region a concept occupies with a simple, trackable geometric form, a capsule, defined by several interpretable parameters, fitted in closed form to each concept's geometry and validated on held-out samples. We apply Capsule Lens in two major settings: static and dynamic representations. On static representations, we demonstrate how to locate concept geometry across various models, and how the span and norm curves uncover important geometric characteristics. On dynamic representations, we present three case studies tracking representation drifts induced by distinct training settings, CLIP pretraining, RL post-training on visual question answering, and RL post-training on mathematical reasoning. These analyses reveal qualitatively different geometric dynamics, ranging from broad network-wide restructuring in CLIP pretraining to localized and concept-specific changes in RL post-training. Our results include findings aligned with existing literature as well as novel observations. We believe Capsule Lens stands as a promising tool for locating, analyzing, and tracking concept geometry in both static and dynamic representations.

</details>


### [151] [HB-PVI: A Hierarchical Bayesian Personalization and Value-of-Information Framework for Complex Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05582)
*Hammed A. Olayinka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HB-PVI，一个在人体活动识别中联合建模个性化收益/危害与数据标签经济价值的层次贝叶斯框架。在47人MUSIC-CAR数据集上，所有个性化机制的平均F1增益均极小且部分为负，EVSI决策规则在几乎所有成本-阈值设定下都选择不购买标签、保留群体模型，论证了在健康感知应用中应以价值驱动而非原始精度驱动个性化决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化可以提升活动识别性能，但不同参与者的个性化收益存在异质性，且每个校准标签都有人力与时间获取成本。现有方法往往只关注个性化带来的精度提升，忽略了标签成本及个性化可能带来的伤害（如过拟合或负迁移），缺乏同时考虑个体异质性、四种个性化机制的收益/危害以及标签经济价值的统一决策框架。

Method: HB-PVI由三个核心组件构成：(1) 层次贝叶斯个性化框架，将参与者异质性建模为高阶参数；(2) 四种个性化机制的收益/危害联合建模（adapter、adapter-plus-head、prototype-residual等），使用Student-t层次增益模型刻画跨参与者的增益分布；(3) 单步期望样本信息价值（EVSI）停止规则作为经济决策准则。评估采用防泄漏的设置：留一参与者法（leave-one-participant-out），结合序贯蒙特卡洛方法更新参与者效应。

Result: Adapter个性化在47人数据上产生了很小的正向平均F1增益（1个标签时0.00099，10个标签时增长至0.00198），而adapter-plus-head和prototype-residual方法平均为负。在主要实际获益阈值（Δmin=0.01）和成本设定下，单步EVSI在所有决策状态下均为零，因此策略不购买标签且对所有47名参与者保留群体推断，与"始终停止"策略完全一致（实际等价区间概率为1）。相比固定十次adapter个性化，该方法减少100%标签需求，同时保持后验平均F1损失仅为0.00217（95%可信区间0.00048至0.00389），低于0.005容差的后验概率为0.9992。HB-PVI在216种成本-阈值设定中有199种（以及所有不低于主要标签成本设定）达到效用最优。

Conclusion: 当个性化增益相对于标签获取、计算和潜在伤害成本而言较小时，应采用"群体优先"（population-first）的部署策略。价值信息推理而非原始预测精度，应作为健康感知应用中个性化决策的驱动准则。

Abstract: Personalization can improve activity-recognition performance, but participant-specific gains are heterogeneous, and every additional calibration label has an acquisition cost. This study presents HB-PVI, a hierarchical Bayesian personalization and value-of-information framework jointly modeling participant heterogeneity, the benefit and harm of four personalization mechanisms, and the economic value of an additional label, for the 47-participant MUSIC-CAR complex-activity cohort. A leakage-safe, leave-one-participant-out evaluation combines a sequential-Monte-Carlo participant-effect updater with a Student-$t$ hierarchical gain model and a one-step expected-value-of-sample-information (EVSI) stopping rule. Adapter personalization produced small positive mean F1 gains, growing from 0.00099 at one label to 0.00198 at ten, while adapter-plus-head and prototype-residual personalization were negative on average. Under the primary practical-benefit threshold ($Δ_{\min}=0.01$) and cost setting, one-step EVSI was zero at every decision state, so the policy purchased no labels and retained population inference for all 47 participants, matching always-stop exactly (region-of-practical-equivalence probability $=1$). Relative to fixed ten-shot adapter personalization, this reduced labeling by 100\% while keeping the posterior mean F1 loss at 0.00217 (95\% credible interval, 0.00048 to 0.00389), with posterior probability 0.9992 of remaining below the 0.005 tolerance. HB-PVI was utility-optimal in 199 of 216 cost-threshold settings and in every setting at or above the primary label cost. These results argue for a population-first deployment policy whenever personalization gains are small relative to labeling, computation, and harm costs, and show that value-of-information reasoning, not raw predictive accuracy, should drive personalization decisions in health-sensing applications.

</details>


### [152] [Endogenous Exploration in Reinforcement Learning with Intrinsic Curiosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05650)
*Armando Vieira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种由内在好奇心驱动的强化学习框架，基于液态状态机（LSM）实现，在非平稳、稀疏/延迟/缺失奖励场景中，通过外在与认知动机的联合驱动进行行动选择。核心假设是"中等不连贯水平产生最佳探索"，在LunarLander和BipedalWalker基准上取得与PPO和ICM相当的竞争力表现，并证明该"好奇心窗口"在Active Inference智能体中不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实强化学习场景中奖励往往稀疏、延迟、无信息甚至缺失，环境也可能非平稳，传统基于外部奖励的探索策略难以有效应对。作者假设探索效率与动力学的不连贯程度呈倒U型关系（中等不连贯最优），并希望验证这一假设以及其在LSM上的可实现性。

Method: 构建一个RL框架，行动选择由外部奖励与"认知动机"机制（将智能体偏向结构化探索方向）联合决定；将框架实现于液态状态机（LSM）基底上，测试探索效率与不连贯程度的关系；对比PPO和ICM，并在LunarLanderv2与BipedalWalkerv3上评估；同时对Active Inference智能体进行相同的分析对照。

Result: 在两个标准基准任务上取得与PPO、ICM等成熟深度RL算法相当的性能；证实存在最优的"好奇心窗口"即中等不连贯水平对应最佳探索；对Active Inference智能体的对比分析中未观察到同样的好奇心窗口，表明所提出的动力学表征了一种独特的探索机制。

Conclusion: 内在好奇心与中等程度动力学不连贯相结合能有效驱动探索；LSM作为神经基底可支撑该探索机制；该方法与现有深度RL基线具有竞争力，并揭示了与传统基于信息增益/预测误差的ICM及Active Inference方法不同的探索范式。

Abstract: We propose a reinforcement learning framework in which exploration is driven by intrinsic curiosity, designed for scenarios where environments are non-stationary and rewards are sparse, delayed, uninformative, or absent. In our model, action selection is guided by a combination of external rewards and an epistemic motivation mechanism that biases the agent toward structured exploratory directions. The central hypothesis is that effective exploration emerges at intermediate levels of incoherence, while performance degrades under both overly rigid and overly disordered dynamics. To test this idea, we implement the framework on top of a Liquid State Machine (LSM) substrate and evaluate it on two standard benchmarks: the discrete-action LunarLanderv2 and the continuous-control BipedalWalkerv3. The proposed method achieves competitive performance on both tasks relative to established deep RL algorithms, including Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Intrinsic Curiosity Module (ICM). We further show that the curiosity window is not recovered in Active Inference agents under the same analysis, suggesting that the proposed dynamics capture a distinct exploration regime

</details>


### [153] [Robustness of LLM-Generated SystemVerilog Assertions to Semantics-Preserving RTL Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05658)
*FNU Aditi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受控的变质评估方法，用于检验大语言模型在语义保持的RTL代码变换下生成SystemVerilog断言（SVA）的稳定性。基于VERT数据集构造评测集后，在两种开源代码模型上施加操作数重排、标识符重命名和冗余括号化三种变换，发现9.7%-27.0%原本正确的行为在变换后变得错误，表明仅凭点准确率无法刻画LLM的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成SVA的评估通常只报告单一语法表示下的准确率，这种点准确率无法揭示当同一RTL行为以不同但语义等价的方式书写时，模型的正确输出是否保持稳定。作者认为这种不稳定性可能被聚合准确率掩盖，而硬件验证场景对可靠性要求极高，因此需要系统性量化模型在语义保持变换下的鲁棒性。

Method: 从VERT数据集出发，构建质量过滤后的条件控制池和包含295个赋值行为的40程序分层评估集。选用Qwen2.5-Coder-7B和DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite两个开源代码模型，采用相同评估提示和贪心解码。研究三种语义保持变换：操作数重排、确定性标识符重命名和冗余括号化。在基线和变换准确率之外，度量子条件鲁棒性、不变性失败率及任意翻转率，并使用10,000样本聚类bootstrap在RTL程序层面计算置信区间。人工审查30个正确转错误的样本，对失败模式进行分类。

Result: 在全部六种模型-变换组合下，9.7%-27.0%在原始RTL上正确的行为在语义保持变换后变为错误。特别地，在标识符重命名条件下，DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite的准确率从53.9%提升到63.7%，但同时有19.5%原本正确的行为失败，说明聚合准确率掩盖了显著的不稳定性。人工审查识别出四类失败模式：路径谓词遗漏、分支极性错误、布尔结构破坏和输出契约违反。

Conclusion: 点准确率单独不足以刻画LLM在断言生成任务中的可靠性。语义保持变换会导致显著的正确性翻转，模型中普遍存在稳定性和准确率之间的隐性张力。该研究为AI辅助硬件验证提出了鲁棒性感知评估的必要性，即评估不仅要看平均正确率，还需关注模型对输入表示变化的敏感程度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored for automating SystemVerilog Assertion (SVA) generation, yet most evaluations report correctness on a single syntactic representation of an input. Such point accuracy does not reveal whether a model's correct output is stable when the same RTL behavior is written differently. This paper presents a controlled metamorphic evaluation of LLM-based SVA generation under semantics-preserving RTL transformations. Starting from the VERT dataset, we construct a quality-filtered conditional-control pool and a stratified 40-program evaluation set containing 295 assignment behaviors. We evaluate two open code models, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite, with an identical evaluation prompt and greedy decoding. Three transformations are studied: operand reordering, deterministic identifier renaming, and redundant parenthesization. Beyond baseline and transformed accuracy, we measure conditional robustness, invariance failure, and any-flip rate, with 10,000-sample clustered bootstrap intervals at the RTL-program level. Across all six model-transformation conditions, 9.7%-27.0% of behaviors that were correct on the original RTL become incorrect after a semantics-preserving transformation. Aggregate accuracy can therefore hide substantial instability: under identifier renaming, DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite improves from 53.9% to 63.7% accuracy while 19.5% of its originally correct behaviors fail. Manual review of 30 sampled correct-to-wrong transitions identifies dropped path predicates, branch-polarity errors, Boolean-structure corruption, and output-contract violations. The results show that point accuracy alone is insufficient for characterizing LLM reliability in assertion generation and motivate robustness-aware evaluation for AI-assisted hardware verification.

</details>


### [154] [PAC-Private Autoregressive Generation: Calibrating Noise to Ensemble Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05676)
*Mina Mirzadehsarcheshmeh,Amir Keyvan Khandani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将PAC隐私从分类任务扩展到自回归生成任务。作者构造128个重叠世界（每篇记录出现在64个世界中），为每个世界在冻结的公共模型上训练一个适配器。每个token处，公共模型定义候选集，各世界投票，后验加权分歧决定PAC噪声，一致同意时无需校准噪声。实验表明在WikiText-103与GPT-2-small上，以2^-32的每token预算保留了74%的微调收益，且与PMixED相比在同等成员推断边界下保留了98%的非隐私余量（PMixED最多56%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型经私有文本适配后通常以API形式服务，隐私泄露通过生成输出而非权重暴露发生。私有预测可保护此类发布，但现有方法如PMixED在每次发布时消耗隐私预算，且随时间推移越来越依赖公共模型。PAC隐私根据跨可能秘密的输出变异性校准噪声，在预测稳定时仅需很少噪声，但此前尚未从分类扩展到自回归生成。

Method: 构造m=128个重叠世界，每条记录恰好出现在m/2个世界中；在每个冻结的公共模型上为每个世界训练一个适配器，实现世界即秘密。在每token生成时，公共模型定义候选集，各世界对候选集投票，其后验加权分歧决定PAC噪声注入量；若各世界完全一致则无需校准噪声。证明I(S;Y_1:T)≤I(S;H_T)≤bT。引入耦合解码策略，在保持隐私核算的同时避免贪婪退化，并处理自适应自生成上下文。

Result: 在WikiText-103上用GPT-2-small实验：以每token预算2^-32保留了74%的微调收益；10^6个token后成员推断成功率被限制在51.08%；后验熵估计的泄露约为收费预算的17%。推理隐私并非内容保护——即使成员推断优势为零，记忆化的金丝雀仍以相同速率被生成。与PMixED在相同数据宇宙和测试集上进行匹配成员推断边界对比，在10^2到10^6 token范围内保留了98%的非私密余量，而PMixED最多56%，且无交叉。

Conclusion: 本文成功将PAC隐私扩展到自回归生成任务，提出处理自适应自生成上下文的方法及耦合解码策略。实验证明该方法在极低隐私预算下仍能保持较高的模型效用，且显著优于PMixED。同时指出推理隐私不等同于内容保护——即便成员推断攻击无法区分样本，被记忆的内容仍可能被原样输出，这一发现对推理隐私的安全边界有重要启示。

Abstract: Language models adapted on private text are often served through APIs, so privacy leakage occurs through generated outputs rather than exposed weights. Private prediction protects these releases. Methods such as PMixED incur privacy cost at each release and increasingly rely on the public model over long horizons. PAC privacy instead calibrates noise to output variability across possible secrets, adding less noise when predictions are stable. To our knowledge, PAC-private prediction has not previously been extended from classification to autoregressive generation.
  We construct $m=128$ overlapping worlds from the private corpus, with each record appearing in exactly $m/2$ worlds, and train one adapter per world over a frozen public model. The realized world is the secret. At each token, the public model defines a candidate set, the worlds vote, and their posterior-weighted disagreement determines the PAC noise; unanimity requires no calibration noise. We prove $I(S;Y_{1:T}) \leq I(S;H_T) \leq bT$. Our contributions are extending PAC privacy to autoregressive generation, handling adaptive self-generated contexts, and introducing coupled decoding that preserves privacy accounting while avoiding greedy degeneration.
  On WikiText-103 with GPT-2-small, we retain 74% of the fine-tuning gain at a per-token budget of $2^{-32}$, while membership-inference success is bounded by 51.08% after $10^6$ tokens; posterior-entropy estimates of leakage are roughly 17% of the charged budget. Inference privacy is not content protection: even when membership advantage on a memorized canary is indistinguishable from zero, the canary is emitted at the same rate. Against PMixED under matched membership-inference bounds on the same data universe and test set, we retain 98% of non-private headroom from $10^2$ to $10^6$ tokens, versus at most 56%, with no crossover.

</details>


### [155] [Connecting Score Matching, Maximum Likelihood, and Expectation-Maximization in Mixed Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05688)
*Zhankun Luo,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合线性回归（MLR）中响应变量的方差保持扩散，并重点分析了未知混合权重的统计推断问题。研究将分数匹配的统计保证与固定扩散噪声水平下的损失几何和优化信号相分离，证明了估计量收敛到MLR的真实参数，且其尺度化误差收敛到最大似然估计的高斯极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合线性回归是重要的统计模型，但其参数估计（特别是混合权重未知时）在理论上缺乏统一框架。扩散模型/分数匹配与参数估计之间的联系尚未被系统阐明，需要厘清分数匹配目标的统计性质与其优化几何之间的相互作用。

Method: 通过KL散度将沿扩散路径积分的去噪分数匹配目标与似然函数及终端差异联系起来。在固定扩散噪声水平下，推导了分数匹配损失与交叉熵及EM算子之间的分解关系，进而导出含潜变量方差修正项的EM相关低噪声梯度展开，并在高噪声极限下刻画各向同性协方差情形下的梯度下降行为。

Result: 在温和正则条件和特定终止调度下，估计量收敛到MLR的真实参数，且尺度化误差实现MLE的高斯极限。在高信噪比射线方向上，分数匹配不平衡梯度和潜变量方差项逐点趋于零。数值实验验证了理论发现和统计保证。

Conclusion: 本文为基于扩散的分数匹配方法在混合线性回归参数估计中的有效性提供了严格的理论依据，同时揭示了分数匹配与EM算法之间的内在联系，为该类方法在高维统计推断中的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study variance-preserving diffusion of the response in mixed linear regression (MLR) with unknown mixing weights. Our analysis separates the statistical guarantees of score matching from the loss geometry and optimization signal at a fixed diffusion noise level. The KL divergence links the denoising score matching objective integrated over the diffusion path with the likelihood and a terminal discrepancy. Under mild regularity conditions and terminal schedule, the resulting estimator converges up to the ground truth parameters of MLR, and its scaled error converges to the Gaussian limit of the maximum-likelihood estimator. At a fixed scale of the diffusion noise level, we derive a decomposition linking the score matching loss to cross-entropy and Expectation-Maximization (EM) operators. This decomposition yields an EM-related low-noise gradient expansion with additional correction terms of latent variance. In the high-noise limit, we further characterize gradient descent on this limiting loss under isotropic covariance. Along fixed high signal-to-noise ratio rays, the score matching imbalance gradient and the latent-variance term tend to zero pointwise. Numerical experiments illustrate our theoretical findings and statistical guarantees.

</details>


### [156] [PhysSAE: Mechanistic Interpretability with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07061)
*Nandita N. Patil,Eshwar R. A.,Gajanan V. Honnavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysSAE框架，通过在PINN隐层激活上训练过完备稀疏自编码器（SAE），并通过直接因果干预（在冻结的原始隐状态中减去对齐原子）来解释物理信息神经网络的内部表征。实验发现SAE原子与独立定义的物理可观测量高度对齐（最大|r|=0.951），其因果足迹比PCA/ICA更空间集中，且优于随机对照，证明了PINN能够发展出稀疏、物理结构化的潜在表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在嵌入PDE残差到神经网络训练中取得了成功，但其内部表征仍然不透明——不清楚隐藏层编码了哪些物理特征，也不清楚这些特征是否具有局部化的因果作用。这阻碍了科学机器学习模型的可解释性与可信度。

Method: PhysSAE框架：在PINN倒数第二层激活上训练过完备稀疏自编码器，提取字典原子（dictionary atoms）；通过直接因果干预评估原子作用，方式为在原始冻结隐状态中执行h_cf = h - αz_k d_k（绕过SAE解码器）。在6类PDE族上，使用3个PINN种子和3个SAE种子进行系统性验证，并引入基于置换的零假设检验、与PCA/ICA干预对比，以及随机控制对照。

Result: (i) SAE原子与独立物理可观测量强对齐（最大Pearson |r|=0.951，显著超过置换零分布）；(ii) 顶级对齐原子消融的因果足迹空间集中度是PCA或ICA干预的1.2–4.2倍；(iii) 顶级对齐原子在结构化物理概念因果定位上优于随机对照（ESF_80优势0.04–0.44）；双原子双侧表征优于单原子，概念回归R²提升ΔR²=0.05–0.15，而随机配对则降低最多0.60。

Conclusion: PINN会形成稀疏、物理结构化的潜在表征，可以通过过完备稀疏自编码器事后识别，并通过直接因果干预进行验证。该工作为可解释性驱动的科学机器学习开辟了路径。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) embed PDE residuals into neural network training, but their internal representations remain opaque: it is unknown what physical features their hidden layers encode or whether those features have a localized causal role. We present PhysSAE, a mechanistic interpretability framework that trains overcomplete sparse autoencoders (SAEs) on PINN penultimate-layer activations and evaluates dictionary atoms through direct causal intervention in the original frozen hidden state: $h_{\mathrm{cf}} = h - αz_k d_k$, bypassing the SAE decoder entirely. Across six PDE families, with 3 PINN seeds and 3 SAE seeds each---we show that (i) Our discovered SAE atoms align with independently-defined physical observables (max Pearson $|r|=0.951$, always $\gg$ permutation null), (ii) the causal footprint of top-aligned atom ablation is 1.2--4.2$\times$ more spatially concentrated canonical than PCA or ICA interventions, and (iii) top-aligned atoms outperform matched random controls on causal localization for structured physical concepts (ESF$_{80}$ advantage 0.04-0.44). Two-atom bilateral representations improve concept regression R$^2$ by $ΔR^2\!=\!0.05\text{-}0.15$ over single atoms, while random pairs decrease it by up to 0.60. These results demonstrate that PINNs develop sparse, physically structured latent representations that can be identified and causally interrogated post-hoc, opening a path toward interpretability-aware scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [157] [GraphNOSE: A Graph Transformer in Olfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05694)
*Mrityunjay Sharma,Sarabeshwar Balaji,Valentina Parma,Ritesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphNOSE是一个开源的图Transformer框架，用于从SMILES字符串预测单分子和二元混合物的多标签气味描述符。相比标准GNN基线，其参数量减少6倍，平均AUROC提升4.52%（p<0.01），在分布外（OOD）化合物上达到84%的AUROC，超越当前最优的Open-POM（81%），并通过XAI方法揭示驱动气味预测的关键子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从分子结构预测气味品质是化学信息学中的开放问题。线性模型虽能将分子特征与气味描述符关联，但在泛化到新型化学骨架、极端分子量或复杂气味混合物时常常失败，亟需更具泛化能力和可解释性的预测架构。

Method: 提出GraphNOSE框架，采用基于Transformer的图架构，集成位置编码和结构编码，从SMILES字符串构建分子图，支持单分子和二元混合物的多标签气味预测。

Result: GraphNOSE参数量仅为标准GNN基线的六分之一，平均AUROC提升4.52%（p<0.01）；在分布外化合物上达到84%的AUROC，显著超越当前最优的Open-POM（81%，p<0.001）；通过XAI分析识别出驱动气味预测的关键分子子结构和特征，结果与化学直觉一致。

Conclusion: GraphNOSE是一种可扩展且可解释的气味预测架构，能够很好地泛化到当前感知数据库中代表性不足的结构独特化合物，并有助于识别线性模型实际失效的条件。

Abstract: Predicting olfactory qualities from molecular structure is an open problem in chemoinformatics. Although linear models can link molecular features to odor descriptors, they often fail when extrapolating to novel chemical scaffolds, extreme molecular weights, or complex odor mixtures. To address this, we introduce GraphNOSE, an open-source graph transformer framework that predicts multi-label odor descriptors from simplified molecular-input line-entry system (SMILES) strings for single molecules and binary mixtures. By integrating positional and structural encodings within a transformer-based graph architecture, GraphNOSE achieves strong performance with six times fewer parameters than standard graph neural network (GNN) baseline while consistently outperforming linear models, molecular language model embeddings, molecular fingerprints, and baseline GNNs by an average area under the ROC curve (AUROC) margin of 4.52% (p < 0.01). GraphNOSE achieves an AUROC of 84% on out-of-distribution compounds (OODs). This exceeds the current state-of-the-art GNN for OOD in olfaction (Open-POM: 81%, p < 0.001), and identifies conditions under which linear models empirically fail. Finally, we apply XAI (explainable AI) methods to identify which substructures and molecular features drive odor predictions, yielding insights consistent with chemical intuition and grounded in the model's learned representations. Together, these results establish GraphNOSE as a scalable and interpretable architecture for olfactory prediction that generalizes to structurally distinct compounds underrepresented in current perceptual databases.

</details>


### [158] [PPIM: Pennes Physics-Informed Mamba for Heat-Source-Conditioned 3D Bioheat Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06869)
*Dongyun Lee,Kyungho Yoon,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pennes Physics-Informed Mamba（PPIM），一种用于三维Pennes生物热方程仿真的神经PDE求解器。PPIM在通用PINNMamba时序子序列模型基础上，引入条件热源输入和Pennes感知的状态空间模型衰减初始化。在代表性600秒模拟中，PPIM在多种神经求解器中取得了最低的MAE、相对L1误差和相对L2误差，有效逼近了FDM参考解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维生物热仿真（如微波消融）需要精确预测生物组织中的瞬态温度分布，通常使用Pennes生物热方程建模。尽管已有多种神经PDE求解器，但如何在这些受控仿真条件下实现高精度近似仍具挑战性，现有方法缺乏对热源条件和Pennes方程物理特性的充分感知建模。

Method: PPIM基于时序子序列模型，通过两大改进：(1) 加入条件热源输入以感知局部热源信息；(2) 采用Pennes感知的状态空间模型（SSM）衰减初始化，使模型内嵌Pennes方程中的生物热物理先验。在完全相同的Pennes残差和训练条件下，与空间傅里叶特征PINN和通用PINNMamba进行对比，以显式有限差分法（FDM）解作为数值参考基准。

Result: 在代表性600秒仿真中，PPIM在所有被评估的神经求解器中取得最低的MAE、相对L1误差和相对L2误差。误差图进一步显示PPIM的剩余误差更集中于热源区域附近，而非均匀分布在域中，表明模型在远离热源的区域近似性能更优。

Conclusion: PPIM在受控的3D Pennes生物热仿真中有效逼近了FDM参考的最终温度场，验证了将热源条件感知和Pennes物理先验融入时序状态空间模型的可行性与有效性。源代码已开源。

Abstract: Three-dimensional bioheat simulation aims to predict transient temperature distributions in biological tissue and is commonly modeled using the Pennes bioheat equation, which combines thermal diffusion, perfusion-mediated heat loss, and external heat generation. In this study, we consider a controlled 3D Pennes bioheat simulation under a localized heat-source condition inspired by microwave ablation (MWA). To evaluate neural approximation performance, we compare three neural partial differential equation (PDE) solvers under the same controlled simulation: a spatial Fourier-feature physics-informed neural network (PINN), a generic PINNMamba temporal subsequence model, and Pennes Physics-Informed Mamba (PPIM). PPIM builds on the temporal subsequence model by incorporating conditioned heat-source input and Pennes-aware state-space model (SSM) decay initialization. All three neural models are trained under the same conditions with the same Pennes residual, and an explicit finite-difference method (FDM) solution is used only as the numerical reference. In a representative 600~s run, PPIM achieved the lowest MAE, relative $L_1$ error, and relative $L_2$ error among the evaluated neural solvers. Error maps further showed that the remaining PPIM errors were more concentrated near the heat-source region than across the rest of the domain. These results indicate that PPIM is effective for approximating the FDM reference final temperature field in this controlled simulation. The source code is available at https://github.com/muvYun/PPIM.

</details>


### [159] [Constitutive State-Space Modeling of Path-Dependent Plasticity: A Resolution-Consistent and Parallelizable Computational Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07294)
*Rui Barreira,Taylan Soydan,Francesco Scipione,Miguel A. Bessa,Dirk Mohr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"本构状态空间（CSS）"的模型，将结构化状态空间动力学重构为增量式本构算子。通过将应变增量分解为大小和方向，利用连续时间线性递归的零阶保持离散化，CSS模型在多个多轴路径相关材料模型上表现出与现有方法相当或更优的预测精度，同时具有训练速度快、对路径离散化鲁棒等显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于非线性循环神经网络的数据驱动路径相关塑性本构模型存在两大缺陷：一是顺序状态演化导致无法并行训练，训练效率低下；二是预测结果可能依赖于施加应变路径的离散化方式，导致在粗分辨率下模型误差显著增大。需要一种既能高效并行训练又能保持离散化鲁棒性的新型建模框架。

Method: 文章提出Constitutive State Space (CSS)模型：将应变增量分解为幅值和方向两部分——加载方向驱动潜状态空间系统，而增量幅值进入其连续时间线性递归的零阶保持离散化。这种力学定制构造保证了零增量下的平稳性，大幅降低对应变路径分辨率的敏感性，并保留S5的并行扫描结构以支持长本构历史的并行训练。作者将该架构与最小状态单元（MSC）在四种多轴路径相关材料模型（含各向同性J2塑性、压力敏感泡沫塑性、各向同性-随动联合硬化）上进行对比。

Result: CSS在预测精度上匹配或超越MSC，对塑性不可压缩材料验证损失降低一个数量级。更重要的是，当应变路径离散化显著变化时，CSS仍保持低误差，而MSC在比训练时更粗的分辨率下评估时误差大幅增加。CSS训练速度显著更快，所需应变-应力数据对更少，且学习到的潜状态结构维度与底层物理本构模型维度一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明，力学定制的结构化状态空间动力学可作为一种高效且离散化鲁棒的数据驱动本构建模计算框架，为路径相关材料本构模型的构建提供了新的思路和工具。

Abstract: Data-driven constitutive models for path-dependent plasticity are commonly formulated using nonlinear recurrent neural networks, whose sequential state evolution limits parallel training and whose predictions may depend on the discretization of the applied strain path. We introduce a Constitutive State Space (CSS) model that reformulates structured state-space dynamics as an incremental constitutive operator. The strain increment is decomposed into magnitude and direction: the loading direction drives the latent state-space system, while the increment magnitude enters the zero-order-hold discretization of its continuous-time linear recurrence. This mechanics-tailored construction guarantees stationarity under zero increments, strongly reduces sensitivity to strain-path resolution, and retains the parallel-scan structure of S5 for efficient training on long constitutive histories. The CSS and Minimal State Cell (MSC) architectures are compared for four multiaxial path-dependent material models including isotropic J2 plasticity, pressure-sensitive foam plasticity, and combined isotropic-kinematic hardening. CSS matches or exceeds the prediction accuracy of the MSC, including one order of magnitude lower validation losses for the plastically incompressible materials. Importantly, CSS maintains low errors across large changes in strain-path discretization, whereas the MSC error increases substantially when evaluated at coarser resolutions than used for training. CSS trains substantially faster and requires fewer strain-stress pairs to attain comparable or better accuracy. Analysis of the learned state further reveals latent structure consistent with the dimensionality of the underlying physical constitutive models. These results establish mechanics-tailored structured state-space dynamics as a computational framework for efficient and discretization-robust data-driven constitutive modeling.

</details>


### [160] [Analysis of Respiratory Sinus Arrhythmia with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05698)
*Julian Szymanski,Patryk Orkisz,Higinio Mora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经网络的深度学习方法来估算呼吸频率，利用呼吸性窦性心律不齐（RSA）现象，直接从心电图（ECG）信号预测呼吸波形。研究开发并评估了三种神经网络架构，可自动提取ECG中的相关特征，无需人工预处理，为非侵入性呼吸监测提供稳健可扩展的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 呼吸频率是重要的生理指标，传统非侵入性呼吸监测方法往往需要额外的传感器或复杂的人工预处理。利用ECG信号中蕴含的RSA现象来估算呼吸频率，可以提供一种无需额外硬件、非侵入式的呼吸监测途径。

Method: 采用深度学习模型直接从ECG输入数据预测呼吸波形。开发并评估了三种不同的神经网络架构，这些架构能够自动从ECG信号中提取相关特征，无需人工预处理步骤。

Result: 三种神经网络架构均能自动提取ECG特征并预测呼吸波形，实现了基于ECG的呼吸频率估算，提供了稳健且可扩展的非侵入性呼吸监测方案。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了基于深度学习、利用RSA现象从ECG信号估算呼吸频率的可行性，为医疗健康和可穿戴技术领域的呼吸监测提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: The paper introduces a neural network-based approach for analyzing ECG signals to estimate respiratory rate by leveraging the phe- nomenon of Respiratory Sinus Arrhythmia (RSA). Our method employs a deep learning model trained to predict respiratory waveforms directly from ECG input data. To achieve this, we developed and evaluated three different neural network architectures capable of automatically extract- ing relevant features from ECG signals without the need for manual preprocessing. The proposed approach offers a robust and scalable solu- tion for non-invasive respiratory monitoring, with potential applications in healthcare and wearable technology

</details>


### [161] [Newton Matching for Generative Modeling: A Unified Framework for Fine-Tuning and Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05727)
*Zeyang Li,Yunan Wang,Paolo Giaretta,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Newton Matching，一个统一的微调与采样框架，将生成建模中的微调与采样统一为迭代优化规范模型（canonical models）的过程，并证明其牛顿方向等同于负Fisher-Rao梯度，实现严格的逆向KL下降与全局/局部二次收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式建模中的微调（如RLHF/DPO类方法）与采样（如扩散采样）方法通常依赖孤立的损失函数设计，缺乏统一的数学框架。作者希望建立将二者的目标（π∝με^{τr}）统一处理的迭代优化范式，从"孤立损失"转向"规范模型上的迭代优化"，从而在理论上解释并改进现有方法。

Method: 作者将微调与采样统一表述为目标密度π∝με^{τr}。核心方法包括：(1) 在规范速度场上构建与密度流形微分同胚的流形；(2) 将Fisher-Rao度量与混合联络（mixture connection）传输至该流形，证明逆向KL的Hessian恰等于该度量；(3) 每阶段采用正则化奖励r−(1/τ)log(ρ/μ)产生的切向步，随后进行保持终端密度的规范化（canonicalization），构成精确有限步长的密度刻画（canonical retraction）；(4) 提出协方差与梯度两种形式，各有前向/反向回归对构建方式，获得具有相同总体极小元的样本级切向更新损失，无需重要性采样或全轨迹反向传播。

Result: 理想迭代下证明了严格逆向KL下降（0<η≤τ时）、一般条件下的全局收敛性、以及全步长（η=τ）时的局部二次收敛。同时定义了临界点一致性（critical-point consistency）：切向位移消失当且仅当ρ=π。作者还展示了许多代表性方法可作为该框架的精确实现、临界点一致近似或目标变更变体，支持模块化算法设计。

Conclusion: Newton Matching为生成式模型的强化学习微调与采样提供了统一的几何理论框架与算法设计平台，同时推进了强化学习用于生成模型的理论与算法基础。

Abstract: We develop Newton Matching, a unified framework for fine-tuning and sampling in generative modeling. The target is $π\proptoμe^{τr}$, where $r$ is the reward, $τ>0$ the inverse temperature, and $μ$ denotes the pretrained model's terminal density for fine-tuning or the constant $1$ for sampling. We shift the paradigm from isolated losses to iterative optimization over canonical models: population minimizers of standard conditional matching for terminal densities. Under compatible smooth-realization assumptions, canonical velocities form a manifold diffeomorphic to the density manifold. Transporting the Fisher-Rao metric and mixture connection to this manifold, we show that the reverse-KL Hessian equals the metric, so the Newton direction coincides with the negative Fisher-Rao gradient. At terminal density $ρ$, each stage takes a tangential step generated by the regularized reward $r-\frac1τ\log(ρ/μ)$, followed by terminal-density-preserving canonicalization. This canonical retraction yields an exact finite-stepsize density characterization. For the ideal iteration, we prove strict reverse-KL descent away from the target for $0 < η\le τ$, global convergence under mild conditions, and local quadratic convergence for full steps ($η=τ$). Covariance and gradient forms, each with forward or reverse regression-pair constructions, yield sample-wise tangential-update losses with the same population minimizer, without importance sampling or full-trajectory backpropagation. We develop approximate updates and define critical-point consistency as vanishing tangential displacement if and only if $ρ=π$. We recover representative methods as exact realizations, critical-point-consistent approximations, or objective-altering variants, enabling modular algorithm design. Our work advances the theory and algorithms of reinforcement learning for generative models.

</details>


### [162] [Local gradient neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07752)
*Baiming Zhang,Jinsong Tang,Ying Xu,Lihua Chen,Shiying Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出局部梯度神经算子（LGNO），一种轻量级、可解释的神经算子方法，基于非线性梯度离散化先验和MLP卷积层学习平移不变的局部卷积核，用于典型力学问题中的场时域演化预测和源识别。该方法通过零一致模板分解将系数学习与场重建分离，并通过网络折叠共享等效分量以减少参数量，在多种PDE基准上展现出精度、参数效率和滚动稳定性的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统场时域预测与源识别依赖对控制偏微分方程的深入理解；而现有基于深度学习的全局神经算子通常需要大规模训练数据和大量可学习参数，且可解释性差、泛化能力有限。作者旨在提出一种轻量、可解释且具备良好泛化能力的替代方案。

Method: LGNO以非线性梯度离散化中的先验知识为基础，利用多层感知机卷积层学习类似于离散差分模板的平移不变局部卷积核。采用零一致模板分解（zero consistent stencil factorization）将系数学习与场重建解耦以增强可解释性；对于具有对称性的问题，通过网络折叠共享等价组件以降低参数数量。

Result: 在覆盖线性和非线性、静态与动态、低维与高维的广泛PDE基准任务上评估，LGNO在保持精度的同时展现出参数效率和滚动稳定性，并成功应用于扩散、流动和量子现象等典型力学问题。

Conclusion: LGNO作为局部梯度神经算子，提供了一种结合物理先验与深度学习的数据驱动范式，在可解释性、参数效率和预测稳定性上优于现有全局神经算子，并对多种力学问题具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Field temporal prediction and source identification constitute canonical problems in dynamical systems. Conventional approaches to these problems depend on a thorough understanding of the governing partial differential equations (PDEs). Recently, deep learning, as represented by neural operators, has provided a data-driven paradigm for addressing such tasks. However, most existing global neural operators for PDEs require large training datasets and many learnable parameters, with limited interpretability and generalization. We propose the local gradient neural operator (LGNO) as a lightweight and interpretable alternative for field temporal evolution prediction and source identification in typical mechanical problems. The method builds on priors from nonlinear gradient discretization and uses multilayer perceptron convolutional layers to learn translation-invariant local kernels that resemble discrete stencils. A zero consistent stencil factorization separates coefficient learning from field reconstruction, rendering the learned operators more transparent. For problems with symmetries, network folding shares equivalent components and reduces parameter counts. We evaluate the method on PDE benchmarks covering linear and nonlinear, static and dynamic, and low and high dimensional cases. Results show that LGNO maintains accuracy, parameter efficiency, and rollout stability across these tasks, and further exhibits wide applicability to mechanical problems including diffusion, flow, and quantum phenomena.

</details>


### [163] [Latent-MoE: Domain-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for PDEs with Multi-Regime Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07814)
*Hanwen Wang,Paris Perdikaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在物理规律随空间变化的PDE上表现不佳的问题，提出Latent-MoE架构，利用混合专家（MoE）与紧支撑路由核实现局部化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准坐标网络的NTK具有平移不变性（实际为平移可变性），导致大坐标幅度的训练点对远处预测产生不成比例的影响，造成长距离耦合和训练期间的梯度冲突，使PINNs在处理跨域物理规律变化的PDE时性能严重下降。

Method: (1) 分析并证明具有中心化、紧支撑路由器的MoE架构能产生均匀带状（uniformly banded）NTK，其核回归权重随距离指数衰减，从而实现学习局部化；(2) 提出Latent-MoE架构，在共享主干网络中交错插入领域感知的MoE块；(3) 与FB-PINNs/X-PINNs的刚性分区（域和参数同时硬划分、独立更新）不同，Latent-MoE通过共享主干保持跨区域的能力流动，同时保留领域感知路由的局部化优势。

Result: 在标准同质物理基准上，Latent-MoE与既有基线相当；在多阶段时变物理基准上，相较于全局模型和刚性域分解方法，其性能提升超过一个数量级，且训练过程中梯度冲突显著减少。

Conclusion: 通过MoE的紧支撑路由实现的局部化NTK是提升复杂异构物理PDE求解能力的关键机制，Latent-MoE在保持共享表示能力的同时兼顾了局部学习的优势。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) struggle on PDEs whose governing physics varies across the domain. We trace this to a structural property of standard coordinate networks: their neural tangent kernel (NTK) is translation-variant and lets training points of large coordinate magnitude disproportionately influence predictions elsewhere, producing long-range coupling and gradient conflict during training. We show analytically and empirically that mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures with centered, compact-support routers yield a uniformly banded NTK whose kernel-regression weights decay exponentially with distance, localizing the learning. Building on this, we propose \emph{Latent-MoE}, which interleaves domain-aware MoE blocks within a shared backbone. Unlike FB-PINNs or X-PINNs, which rigidly partition both the domain and the parameters so that the parameters on different subdomains are updated independently, Latent-MoE is designed to preserve the localization benefit of domain-aware routing while allowing capacity to flow across regions through the shared backbone. On standard homogeneous-physics benchmarks Latent-MoE is competitive with established baselines; on benchmarks with multi-stage time-variable physics, where global models and rigid domain decompositions both fall into spurious solutions, it improves over them by more than an order of magnitude, with markedly reduced gradient conflict during training.

</details>


### [164] [A Multi-Source Ensemble Approach to Candidate Generation for Alternative Vacation Rental Property Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05748)
*Syed Mohammed Arshad Zaidi,Eric Rincon,Shayan Hassantabar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对度假租赁平台中替代房源候选项生成（CG）方法进行了全面研究，比较了协同过滤、浅层嵌入和图神经网络（GNN）方法。在拥有200多万活跃房源的平台上实验表明，结合基于物品的协同过滤与GNN检索的混合架构，相比最强基线在Recall@300上提升14.8%。同时发现CG阶段的收益并不总能清晰传导到下游排序阶段，存在"召回-转化"差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度假租赁平台的替代房源推荐对用户体验至关重要，但其候选生成面临异构库存、地理约束、快速可用性变化和长尾分布等独特挑战，现有研究缺乏对这些场景下CG方法的系统性比较。

Method: 在超过200万活跃房源的平台上，系统比较了协同过滤、浅层嵌入（Hotel2Vec）和GNN等CG方法，并提出结合基于物品的协同过滤与GNN检索的混合架构，利用两种方法的互补优势。

Result: 混合架构在Recall@300上比最强基线提升14.8%；GNN嵌入单独相比浅层Hotel2Vec嵌入在K值范围内有48-68%的相对召回提升；更强的候选池能提升下游排序质量，但CG与排序器训练的耦合使效果归因复杂化。

Conclusion: CG阶段协同过滤与GNN的互补性可显著提升候选生成质量，但CG增益到下游排序的传导存在"召回-转化差距"，部署新检索方法时需要谨慎评估端到端效果，而非仅关注召回指标。

Abstract: Alternative property recommendations play a critical role in vacation rental marketplaces, helping users discover relevant options when viewing a specific listing. However, generating high-quality candidate alternatives presents unique challenges: heterogeneous inventory, geographic constraints, rapid availability changes, and long-tail property distributions. We present a comprehensive study of candidate generation (CG) approaches for vacation rental alternatives, comparing collaborative filtering, shallow embeddings, and graph neural network (GNN) methods.
  Our experiments on a large-scale vacation rental platform (over 2M active properties) show that a hybrid architecture combining item-based collaborative filtering with GNN-based retrieval improves Recall@300 by 14.8% over the strongest baseline, by leveraging the complementary strengths of the two sources: collaborative filtering excels at early recall for properties with rich interaction history, while GNNs discover diverse, non-obvious alternatives and handle cold-start scenarios more effectively. As a component result, GNN-based embeddings alone substantially outperform shallow Hotel2Vec embeddings (48-68% relative recall improvement across K), motivating their inclusion in the ensemble.
  Crucially, we examine how CG-stage gains carry through to the downstream ranking stage, and find that a stronger candidate pool yields higher downstream ranking quality, though attributing this effect cleanly is complicated by the coupling between candidate generation and ranker training. This recall-conversion gap is an important consideration for practitioners deploying new retrieval methods in two-stage recommendation systems.

</details>


### [165] [Data Scout: Targeted Web Crawling for Domain-Specific Pretraining Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05766)
*Chirag Garg,Eelaaf Zahid,Farhan Ahmed,Jay Pankaj Gala,Eric Butler,Heiko Ludwig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Data Scout提出了一种逆向的数据构建思路：不再从大规模网页存档（如CommonCrawl）中过滤语料，而是由LLM将主题扩展为分类体系与搜索查询，引导定向爬取，并以子域名级抽样+用户提供的分类器（探针）筛选语料。该方法在数学领域以FineMath分类器为探针，获得21.9%的高质量数学内容，比过滤方法高70倍，且63.2%的页面不在CommonCrawl中；用1.9B token对Llama-3.2-3B继续预训练可在GSM8k上匹敌FineMath语料。由于探针是唯一的领域特定组件，该方法可推广到任何拥有此类分类器的领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流方法通过过滤大型网络存档（如CommonCrawl）构建领域预训练语料，这对热门领域有效，但对专业/小众领域失效——相关内容稀疏且难以被流行度驱动的爬虫覆盖。作者希望解决专门领域语料稀缺的问题，提出一种不依赖存档过滤、而是直接开展定向爬取的替代范式。

Method: Data Scout逆转过波过程：不使用现有存档，而是发起定向爬取。具体流程为：(1) 用LLM将根主题扩展为一个分类体系（taxonomy）并生成数千条搜索查询；(2) 将查询返回的URL（种子）按子域名分组；(3) 使用用户提供的分类器（probe）对每个子域名的小样本进行筛查，按子域名级别接收。该方法的理论基础是：相关性在子域名层面有清晰边界——在数学领域，同一子域名内页面相关性是兄弟子域名的21倍。

Result: 以FineMath分类器为探针时，21.9%的爬取页面是高质数学内容，比从可比网络样本过滤得到的0.31%高出70倍，爬取浪费的算力显著减少。更重要的是不仅效率提升：63.2%的这些页面完全不在CommonCrawl存档中，却同样适用于训练。对Llama-3.2-3B在1.9B Data Scout token上继续预训练，在GSM8k基准上可与FineMath语料达到同等性能。

Conclusion: Data Scout证明了定向爬取+子域名级筛选是构建专门领域预训练语料的有效替代路径，可显著提高语料采集效率并获得存档之外的独特数据。由于领域特定组件仅在于用户提供的探针分类器，该方法原则上可推广到任何具有此类分类器的领域，具有较强通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: The dominant approach to building domain-specific pretraining corpora is to filter large web archives such as CommonCrawl. This works well for popular domains but breaks down for specialized ones, where relevant content is sparse and often beyond the reach of popularity-driven crawlers. We present Data Scout, which inverts this: instead of filtering an archive, it directs a targeted crawl. An LLM expands a root topic into a taxonomy and thousands of search queries; the returned URLs (seeds) are grouped by subdomain and screened with a user-supplied classifier (the probe), admitting each subdomain on the basis of a small sample. This works because relevance has a sharp boundary at the subdomain level: in mathematics, a page is 21x more likely to be relevant than one on a sibling subdomain. With the FineMath classifier as the probe, 21.9% of crawled pages are high-quality math content, 70x the 0.31% rate from filtering a comparable web sample, so the crawl wastes far less effort. But the payoff is not just efficiency: 63.2% of these pages are missing from CommonCrawl altogether, yet just as useful for training. Continued pretraining of Llama-3.2-3B on 1.9B Data Scout tokens matches FineMath corpus on GSM8k. Because the probe is the only domain-specific component, Data Scout can in principle apply to any domain with such a classifier.

</details>


### [166] [Learning Metamaterial Eigenmodes with Wavelet-Encoded Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08102)
*Han Zhang,Alexander Ogren,Cynthia Rudin,Johann Guilleminot,L. Catherine Brinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了傅里叶神经算子（FNO）结合基于小波的输入编码可以同时学习并预测弹性波方程（声波在任意超材料几何中传播的变形模态）的多个特征模态，解决了传统算子学习框架假设唯一输入-输出映射而难以处理特征值问题（需同时求解特征参数和多个有效特征模态）的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征值问题要求同时求解特征参数和多个可能有效的特征模态，而标准的算子学习方法假设输入-输出映射唯一，因此难以直接应用于此类问题。需要一种能够处理多模态映射的神经网络架构与输入编码方案。

Method: 将傅里叶神经算子（FNO）与小波基输入编码相结合，利用小波编码的双重空间-频谱结构与FNO的谱处理能力的匹配性，实现连续值几何和二元值几何上的确定性模态选择。

Result: 该方法在单个模型中可预测弹性波方程的多重特征模态；相比在消费级CPU上运行的有限元分析，代理模型将超材料设计仿真阶段加速三个数量级，同时保持高保真度。研究还发现预测精度随几何不连续性的变化而有所不同。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅为超材料设计中的特征模态预测提供了高效的神经代理模型，还揭示了小波编码与谱神经算子结构之间的契合机制，为其他基于谱神经算子的多模态PDE求解器的输入编码设计提供了更广泛的指导意义。

Abstract: Machine learning surrogates based on neural operators have shown broad applicability in solving forward PDE problems. However, eigenvalue problems, in which an eigenparameter and one of several valid eigenmodes must be simultaneously solved, remain difficult because standard operator learning formulations assume a unique input-output map. This work demonstrates that Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs), combined with wavelet-based encodings of PDE inputs, can learn and predict multiple eigenmodes of the elastic wave equation, corresponding to deformation modes of acoustic waves propagating through arbitrary metamaterial geometries. We provide a mechanistic explanation and experimental evidence for why wavelet encodings are well matched to the dual spatial-spectral structure of the FNO, enabling deterministic mode selection on both continuous-valued and binary-valued geometries within a single model, and for why prediction accuracy varies with geometric discontinuities. For metamaterial design, the resulting surrogate accelerates the simulation stage of the design cycle by three orders of magnitude relative to finite element analysis on a consumer-grade CPU, while preserving high fidelity. These results also carry broader implications for designing input encodings in other multi-mode PDE solvers based on spectral neural operators.

</details>


### [167] [RAPTOR: Role-Aware Private Training for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05770)
*Duc Dm,Khai Le-Duc,Nguyen Do,Minh Son Hoang,Florent Draye,Thai Hoang,Hoang Phuong Dam,Jiarui Liu,Chris Ngo,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Anh Le Duc Tran,Nhat Do Minh,Minh Ngoc Le,My T. Thai,Ran Xu,Silvio Savarese,Mona Diab,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin,Huy L. Nguyen,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAPTOR提出一种角色感知的私有训练框架，用于差分隐私下的稀疏MoE模型微调。它针对三个失败模式（全局裁剪抑制专家梯度、批次级归一化稀释稀疏专家更新、固定隐私噪声降低低负荷专家的信噪比），通过交替优化共享层与专家层，采用专家特定裁剪与噪声、公开期望所有者分母及不依赖私有计数的更新调度。理论上证明了(ε,δ)-差分隐私性质，并给出了偏差-方差分解以指导隐私无关的层选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私微调方法将稀疏MoE模型当作单个稠密块处理，忽略了共享层看到所有数据而专家只看到路由记录这一关键差异，导致三个明确的失败模式：全局裁剪抑制专家梯度、批次级归一化稀释稀疏专家更新、固定隐私噪声降低低负荷专家的信噪比。论文旨在解决这些被忽视的问题。

Method: 提出RAPTOR框架，核心在于：(1) 交替优化共享层与专家层参数；(2) 使用专家特定裁剪与噪声；(3) 引入公开的期望所有者分母替代私有实现计数；(4) 设计与计数无关的更新调度以避免依赖私有路由计数。理论上通过"每个记录恰好被分配给一个所有者专家"的并行组合论证隐私代价，并对公开分母估计器进行偏差-方差分解分析。

Result: 在Switch Transformer和OLMoE的GLUE任务微调以及DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny上的实验显示，RAPTOR在多个隐私预算(ε)下均优于标准DP基线，且在最严格的隐私预算下通常取得最大性能增益。

Conclusion: 通过专门针对MoE架构中共享层与专家层角色差异设计私有训练策略，RAPTOR能在不增加隐私代价的前提下有效缓解稀疏专家的梯度抑制与噪声稀释问题，在紧隐私预算下优势尤为显著。同时提供了公开的隐私无关层选择规则，增强了框架的实用性与可部署性。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) fine-tuning methods treat sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models as a single dense block, ignoring that shared layers see all data while experts only see routed records. We identify and formally characterize three resulting failure modes: global clipping suppresses expert gradients, batch-level normalization dilutes sparse expert updates, and fixed privacy noise degrades signal-to-noise ratio on low-load experts. We introduce RAPTOR - a Role-Aware Private Training framework, which alternates shared and expert optimization and targets each failure directly, using expert-specific clipping and noise together with a public expected-owner denominator and a count-independent update schedule that avoids conditioning on private, realized expert counts. We prove the resulting mechanism satisfies $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP: because each record is assigned to exactly one owner expert, per-expert mechanisms within a layer compose in parallel, so updating all $E$ experts costs no more, in privacy terms, than updating one, with shared and expert streams composing sequentially across training. We further derive a bias-variance decomposition of the public-denominator estimator showing its bias grows predictably with routing imbalance, yielding a privacy-free rule for selecting which layer to protect from routing entropy measured on a small public corpus. Experiments on Switch Transformer and OLMoE fine-tuning across GLUE tasks, and on DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny, show consistent gains over standard DP baselines across several privacy levels ($\varepsilon$), with the largest margins typically at the tightest budgets. Code and models are publicly available: https://github.com/leduckhai/RAPTOR

</details>


### [168] [Multi-Level-Set-Based Physics-Driven Neural Network to Solve 3-D Inverse Scattering Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08594)
*Yutong Du,Zicheng Liu,Bo Qi,Yali Zong,Peixian Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于水平集的物理驱动神经网络求解器（LSPDNN），用于三维电磁逆散射成像。该方法利用多坐标依赖的神经水平集组件对未知目标进行分段均匀表示，通过软联合多材料模型分别描述目标支撑域和材料分布，并引入模型一致的TV正则化和自适应损失平衡策略，实现了边界清晰、材料均匀、背景伪影显著减少的高质量重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于体素对比度重构的逆散射方法存在边界模糊和重构伪影问题，无法充分利用实际散射体具有分段均匀性的先验信息，导致重构质量受限。

Method: 提出多材料神经水平集参数化方法：1）采用软联合多材料模型，以多个水平集组件的并集描述目标整体支撑域；2）局部对比度由归一化组件权重和可学习的复介电常数候选值共同确定；3）在材料区域指示符上施加模型一致的TV正则化而非直接加在对比度上；4）引入自适应损失平衡策略以减少对手动正则化权重的依赖；5）对每次测量实例，通过最小化物理一致的目标函数优化神经水平集参数和材料候选值。

Result: 数值与实验结果表明，LSPDNN能够重构出边界清晰、材料区域更均匀、背景伪影大幅减少的散射体。在不规则形状、紧密排列物体、多材料以及含测量噪声的挑战性三维逆散射场景中均表现出优势。

Conclusion: 神经水平集参数化方法在三维电磁逆散射中展现出显著优势，能够有效利用散射体的分段均匀先验，在复杂场景下实现高质量重构。

Abstract: This paper proposes a level-set-based physics-driven neural network solver (LSPDNN) for 3-D electromagnetic inverse scattering. To mitigate boundary blurring and reconstruction artifacts in voxel-wise contrast reconstruction, the proposed solver exploits the piecewise homogeneity of practical scatterers by representing unknown targets with multiple coordinate-dependent neural level-set components. Specifically, a soft-union multi-material model is proposed to separately describe the object support and material distribution. The global support is formed by the union of multiple level-set components, while the local contrast is determined by normalized component weights and learnable complex permittivity candidates. In addition, a model-consistent total variation (TV) regularization is imposed on the material-region indicators, rather than directly on the reconstructed contrast, to suppress fragmented material assignments without excessively smoothing material interfaces. An adaptive loss balancing strategy is further introduced to reduce the dependence on manually selected regularization weights. For each measurement instance, the neural level-set parameters and material candidates are optimized by minimizing a physics-consistent objective function. Numerical and experimental results demonstrate that LSPDNN can reconstruct scatterers with clear boundaries, more uniform material regions, and substantially reduced background artifacts. The results highlight the advantage of the neural level-set parameterization in challenging 3-D inverse scattering cases involving irregular shapes, closely spaced objects, multiple materials, and measurement noise.

</details>


### [169] [Nonlinear elliptic homogenization with the parametric Deep Ritz method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05778)
*Conor Rowan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用参数化Deep Ritz方法来求解非线性均匀化中出现的参数化胞元问题，利用神经网络同时离散空间与宏观状态变量，从而得到连续可微的胞元响应表示，显著加速宏观尺度的求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非线性椭圆均匀化问题中，均匀化本构关系依赖于宏观状态及其梯度，需要求解参数化的胞元问题。传统方案（如FE²）虽可行但计算代价高，且求解宏观问题时需要胞元问题解的可微表示（用于Newton迭代），因此需要一种高效且可微的参数化胞元求解方法。

Method: 利用胞元问题的变分结构，采用Deep Ritz方法求解参数化胞元问题。将胞元响应对空间坐标和宏观状态的依赖同时用神经网络离散，通过强边界条件施加方式满足边界约束，通过参数化Deep Ritz方法在宏观状态范围内同时求解胞元问题。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法准确、高效，能够提供关于宏观状态及其梯度的连续可微胞元响应表示；与传统的FE²方案相比，参数化胞元响应表示能够显著加速宏观尺度的求解过程。

Conclusion: 参数化Deep Ritz方法是非线性均匀化中求解胞元问题的有效工具，提供了可微且连续的表示，在加速宏观尺度计算方面优于传统FE²方法。

Abstract: Elliptic homogenization is used to determine coarse-grained properties of materials with features on small scales. When these small scale features have rapid, periodic fluctuations, the solution field corresponding to a homogenized constitutive relation closely resembles the true solution based on the heterogeneous material. This homogenized behavior of the material is computed from a cell problem, where a cell is defined to be one period of the fluctuating material. In the context of linear elliptic partial differential equations, the homogenized constitutive relation is defined simply by a constant coefficient tensor, but for nonlinear problems, the homogenized response depends on the macroscopic state and/or its gradient, thus requiring solutions to parametric cell problems. When computing a numerical solution with the homogenized constitutive relation, it is useful to have a differentiable representation of the solution to the cell problem, as derivatives of the homogenized constitutive relation are required in Newton iterations for the macroscopic state field. In this work, we use the Deep Ritz method to solve the parametric cell problems that arise from nonlinear homogenization. First, we exploit the variational structure of the cell problem, then we discretize the dependence of the cell response on both space and the macroscopic state with a neural network. Enforcing boundary conditions on the cell response strongly, we next use the parametric Deep Ritz method to simultaneously solve the cell problem over a range of macroscopic states. We show that this method is accurate, efficient, and offers a continuous and differentiable representation of the cell response over the macroscopic state and gradient. We then show that our parametric representation of the cell response significantly expedites macroscale solutions when compared to a traditional $\text{FE}^2$ scheme.

</details>


### [170] [Online Learning with LLM Experts from Limited Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05820)
*Wang Wei,Soumyabrata Pal,Koyel Mukherjee,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Branislav Kveton,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线环境中带有限反馈的大语言模型（LLM）专家路由自适应问题，将其建模为带d个提示特征的K臂老虎机问题，提出了在完整信息和老虎机设置下分别达到Õ(dT/√m)和Õ(dT√(K/m))遗憾界的算法，并通过实验验证了从有限反馈中高效学习高质量路由策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际中，将不同提示路由到不同的LLM专家（如不同规模或不同类型的模型）以最大化响应质量至关重要，但由于反馈获取成本高且在线交互具有不确定性，需要一种能在有限反馈预算下自适应学习最优路由策略的方法。

Method: 将提示路由问题形式化为具有K个动作（专家）和d个提示特征的上下文老虎机问题，时间范围T轮，受到反馈预算m（m≪T）的限制。分别设计了完整信息设置和老虎机（bandit）设置下的策略选择与奖励观察算法来最小化遗憾。

Result: 在完整信息设置下达到Õ(dT/√m)的遗憾界，在老虎机设置下达到Õ(dT√(K/m))的遗憾界。实验表明该方法能在多种LLM上从有限反馈中高效学到高质量路由策略。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法能够从有限的反馈预算中有效地进行LLM专家路由，在理论上给出了遗憾上界保证，在实践上验证了跨多样LLM的高效学习能力，为大规模LLM路由问题提供了一种可行的在线学习框架。

Abstract: We study adaptive routing of prompts to large language model (LLM) experts to maximize response quality in an online setting with limited feedback. We formulate it as a bandit problem with $K$ actions that represent experts and $d$ features that encode prompts, over a horizon of $T$ rounds. We propose algorithms that strategically select and observe rewards to minimize regret. In the full-information setting, we achieve a regret of $\tilde{O}(d T / \sqrt{m})$, while in the bandit setting we achieve $\tilde{O}(d T \sqrt{K / m})$, where $m \ll T$ is a budget on feedback. Our experiments show that we efficiently learn high-quality routing strategies across diverse LLMs from limited feedback.

</details>


### [171] [Selective Posterior Margin Regularization for Forward-Corrected Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05859)
*Zexing Zhang,Jichao Li,Tianyang Lei,XiongYi Lu,Yang Kewei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性后验边界正则化（SPMR），在不改变Forward目标函数的前提下，利用干净类别的反向后验分布与注释之间的不一致性，对干净分类器施加基于后验间隔的梯度化正则项，从而缓解类条件标签噪声下的记忆效应，在五个基准上将Forward提升2.5-7.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在类条件标签噪声下，Forward纠正通过将转移矩阵嵌入似然中来处理噪声，但有限样本下的网络仍可能记忆损坏标签。反向后验中若领先类别与注释不同，说明模型和转移矩阵对该注释提供了反证，但各候选类别往往几乎不分上下。现有方法缺乏一个可区分对待不同置信度冲突的机制。

Method: SPMR保留Forward目标函数，将后验冲突转化为对干净分类器的分级更新。具体做法为：(1) 选取反向后验中的领先类别；(2) 用分离后的后验前二类的间隔缩放一个detached逐对间隔；(3) 对弥散性冲突赋予很小的权重。间隔因子分解为转移调整后的逐对间隔与前两类后验质量的乘积，活跃间隔沿局部最小范数logit方向扩大所选逐对间隔。

Result: 在五个已知转移基准上，SPMR相比完整训练长度的Forward提升2.5-7.0个百分点；相比加入Mixup和早停的Forward仍高出0.7-2.5个百分点。消融实验证明后验空间系数、转移调整目标和逐对操作各自带来独立收益。该设计可迁移到估计转移矩阵、人工标注、架构变化及更强的Forward方案。

Conclusion: SPMR通过利用Forward纠正内部已有的潜在类别证据来实现正则化，而无须将每个后验冲突都提升为修正标签。其机制在有限样本下有效抑制了噪声记忆现象，且在不改变模型与损失结构的条件下即可与多种训练方案兼容。

Abstract: Learning with class-conditional label noise often relies on a transition model from latent clean classes to observed annotations. Forward correction embeds this transition in the likelihood, yet finite-sample networks may still memorize corrupted labels. The corrected likelihood also induces a reverse posterior over the clean classes that could explain each annotation. When its leading class differs from the annotation, the model and transition matrix provide evidence against that annotation, but the leading alternatives can remain nearly tied. We introduce Selective Posterior Margin Regularization (SPMR), which preserves the Forward objective and converts this disagreement into a graded update on the clean classifier. SPMR selects the leading reverse-posterior class, scales a detached pairwise margin by the separation between the two leading posterior classes, and assigns correspondingly little influence to diffuse conflicts. The gap factorizes into transition- adjusted pairwise separation and the posterior mass carried by the leading pair. The active margin follows the locally minimum-norm logit direction that enlarges the selected pairwise margin. Across five known-transition benchmarks, SPMR improves full-length Forward by 2.5-7.0 percentage points and remains 0.7-2.5 percentage points above Forward with Mixup and early stopping. Matched interventions support distinct gains from the posterior-space coefficient, transition-adjusted target, and pairwise action. The same design transfers to estimated transitions, human annotations, architectural changes, and stronger Forward recipes. The formulation uses latent-class evidence already available inside Forward correction without promoting every posterior conflict to a corrected label.

</details>


### [172] [Generalizing HVAC Control With Domain Randomized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05822)
*Pablo Boitel,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NOMAD-RL，一种通用强化学习HVAC控制器，通过基于物理信息归一化流的自适应域随机化方案进行在线元适应，使策略能在异构热区之间跨建筑迁移。在单区和多区评估中，NOMAD-RL优于PID和无域随机化的RL基线，性能接近调优完善的MPC，尤其在多区场景下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模部署先进的HVAC控制器仍然困难，因为其性能通常依赖准确的建筑模型或需针对每个站点重新调参。需要一个通用且非侵入式（基于温度设定点接口）的控制器，能够在异构热区之间高效迁移而不需要繁重的站点定制。

Method: NOMAD-RL（Neural Online Meta-Adaptation for Dynamics）通过通用非侵入式温控接口运转，基于区域测量和预测作用于温度设定点，利用循环策略支持部分可观测条件下的在线适应。核心贡献是基于物理信息归一化流（normalizing flows）的自适应域随机化方案，用以建模热区参数的相关且多模态分布，同时保持物理合理性和可控性，形成渐进自适应训练课程以改善跨建筑迁移。

Result: 在单区与多区设定下，NOMAD-RL一致优于恒定设定点PID控制器和无域随机化的RL基线，性能逼近经过良好调优的MPC，尤其是在更具挑战性的多区场景下优势更加明显。

Conclusion: 自适应、物理信息驱动的域随机化方法对鲁棒且可迁移的HVAC控制具有巨大潜力，为大规模部署先进控制器提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: Deploying advanced HVAC (Heating, Ventilation and Air Conditioning) controllers at scale remains difficult because performance often depends on accurate building models or per-site retuning. We propose NOMAD-RL (Neural Online Meta-Adaptation for Dynamics), a general-purpose Reinforcement Learning (RL) controller designed to transfer across heterogeneous thermal zones through a universal, non-invasive thermostat interface. The controller acts on temperature setpoints from zone measurements and forecasts, while a recurrent policy supports online adaptation under partial observability.
  Our main contribution is an adaptive domain randomization scheme based on physics-informed normalizing flows, which models correlated and multimodal distributions of thermal-zone parameters while maintaining physical plausibility and controllability. This produces a realistic and progressively adaptive training curriculum that improves transfer across buildings. We evaluate NOMAD-RL against a constant-setpoint PID controller, RL without domain randomization, and MPC in single- and multi-zone settings. NOMAD-RL consistently outperforms the PID and non-randomized RL baselines, and approaches the performance of a well-tuned MPC, especially in the more challenging multi-zone case. These results highlight the potential of adaptive, physics-informed domain randomization for robust and transferable HVAC control.

</details>


### [173] [On-the-go Forgetting without Explicit Unlearning via ERASE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05966)
*Kushal Chakrabarti,Mayank Baranwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ERASE（Erasure via Reconstructive Adversarial Signal Editing），一种无需修改模型权重的推理时遗忘框架，通过结构化、类别条件化的输入扰动实现选择性遗忘，仅需删除私人数据的可观测影响即可完成数据移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘（unlearning）方法依赖训练后的权重自适应或知识蒸馏，存在内存开销重复、泛化性能下降和可扩展性受限等缺陷。作者旨在消除对重训练、微调和模型副本的依赖，实现兼顾效率与保真度的推理时遗忘。

Method: ERASE 通过重构性对抗信号编辑，向输入注入结构化、类别条件化的扰动，在推理阶段诱导选择性遗忘指定子类，而不触碰模型权重。作者严格刻画了 ERASE 在温和正则假设下实现指定子类功能遗忘的充分条件，同时保证同一超类下其他子类的预测不受影响。

Result: 在多种架构与基准数据集上，ERASE 在遗忘效果、计算效率与保留保真度之间的平衡优于当前的基于遗忘的方法，保持最优观测效果。

Conclusion: ERASE 将数据移除重新构想为"无需遗忘的忘记"，建立了具有可扩展性且符合法规要求的持续隐私学习路径，为推理时遗忘提供了原则性理论基础。

Abstract: Existing unlearning approaches typically rely on post hoc weight adaptation or distillation, leading to duplicated memory costs, degraded generalization, and limited scalability. In this work, we introduce ERASE, Erasure via Reconstructive Adversarial Signal Editing, a framework for on-the-go forgetting that suppresses the observable influence of private data without modifying model weights. ERASE leverages structured, class-conditioned input perturbations to induce selective forgetting during inference, eliminating the need for retraining, fine-tuning, or model copies. We rigorously characterize sufficient conditions when ERASE provably achieves functional forgetting of designated subclasses while preserving predictions across other subclasses within the same superclass. This analysis offers a principled foundation for inference-time forgetting under mild regularity assumptions. Across diverse architectures and benchmark datasets, ERASE maintains the best observed balance between forgetting efficacy, computational efficiency, and retention fidelity over recent unlearning-based methods. By reimagining data removal as forgetting without unlearning, our work establishes a scalable, regulation-aligned pathway for continual, privacy-conscious learning.

</details>


### [174] [Scaling Optimal Classification Trees via Adaptive Feature and Sample Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05826)
*Jiancheng Tu,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于STreeD的联合特征与样本空间约简框架：Weighted STreeD通过合并投影后的重复记录为加权代表来降低样本相关计算，Adaptive STreeD通过迭代精化有界候选特征集并重建加权表示来求解缩减后的优化问题。实验表明Weighted STreeD相比标准STreeD最高提速121.41倍，Adaptive STreeD在深度2-4匹配比较中缩短运行时间，并在更大深度下仍能返回可行树，预测性能在相同计算预算下与最优分类树基线相当甚至更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于动态规划的最优分类树方法（如STreeD）在特征数量和训练样本增加时计算代价急剧上升，难以扩展到更大规模的实际应用场景。需要对特征空间和样本空间同时进行约简以提升可扩展性。

Method: 提出两类方法：(1) Weighted STreeD——将数据投影到固定候选特征集后合并重复记录为加权代表，减少样本维度计算且不改变固定候选优化问题；(2) Adaptive STreeD——迭代精化有界候选特征集，保留当前最优树的特征，重建加权表示并求解缩减后的问题。Weighted STreeD的解对当前候选集是最优的，外层特征搜索仍为全特征空间上的启发式。

Result: 在五个数据集上，Weighted STreeD相对标准STreeD最高加速121.41倍。Adaptive STreeD在深度2至4的匹配比较中缩短了运行时间，在更大深度下仍返回可行树，而全特征方法受限于时间或内存。相同计算预算下预测性能与最优分类树基线相当，部分比较中更高。

Conclusion: 联合特征与样本空间约简能够有效将基于动态规划的最优分类树学习扩展到更大规模的实例，为解决此类方法在实际应用中的计算瓶颈提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Dynamic programming for optimal classification trees becomes computationally expensive as the numbers of features and training samples increase. We develop a joint feature- and sample-space reduction framework based on STreeD. Weighted STreeD merges duplicate records created after projection onto a fixed candidate set into weighted representatives. This reduces sample-dependent computation without changing the fixed-candidate optimization problem. Adaptive STreeD repeatedly refines a bounded candidate set, retains features used by the incumbent tree, rebuilds the weighted representation, and solves the resulting reduced problems. Each certified Weighted STreeD solution is optimal for its current candidate set, while the outer feature search remains heuristic over the full feature space. Experiments on five data sets show that Weighted STreeD achieves speedups of up to 121.41 times over standard STreeD. Adaptive STreeD reduces runtime in matched comparisons at depths 2 to 4 and continues to return feasible trees at greater depths where full-feature methods are limited by time or memory. Under the same computational budget, its predictive performance remains comparable to the evaluated optimal classification tree baselines and is higher in some comparisons. These results show how joint feature- and sample-space reduction can scale dynamic-programming-based optimal-tree learning to more demanding instances.

</details>


### [175] [SAFEGuard: Detect Optimization-Based Jailbreak Attacks Through Harmful Semantic Analysis and Fluency Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05850)
*Quoc Viet Vo,Trung Le,Damith C. Ranasinghe,Ehsan Abbasnejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一检测框架SAFEGuard，通过混合流畅度度量（跨层分布距离+困惑度）和梯度匹配的恶意语义分析，有效检测各类基于优化的对抗性越狱攻击，显著优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM越狱防御方法大多只能检测特定类型的攻击，难以有效应对广泛的基于优化的越狱机制，这些机制可能产生高度流畅优化的提示或有害语义混淆的提示，亟需统一的检测框架。

Method: SAFEGuard采用混合检测策略：(1)基于跨层分布距离和困惑度的流畅度测量，捕捉保持高度流畅的恶意提示；(2)通过梯度匹配分析有害语义，识别语义混淆的越狱提示。核心观察是高流畅度提示的恶意意图接近有害提示，而语义混淆提示常注入乱码序列。

Result: 在多种基于优化的越狱攻击上，SAFEGuard一致优于最先进的基线方法，在准确率上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: SAFEGuard展示了针对不断演进的越狱攻击的有效性和鲁棒性，为LLM安全部署提供了统一的检测方案。

Abstract: Despite the significant efforts devoted to aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values and ensuring safe deployment, recent work has revealed that LLMs remain vulnerable to adversarial jailbreak attacks that can bypass safety guardrails and elicit harmful responses. Many defense methods are proposed to detect jailbreaks but they are limited in their effectiveness to counter wide-range optimization-based jailbreak mechanisms that can yield highly fluency-optimized or harmful semantic obfuscated prompts. To tackle this challenge, we propose a unified detection framework SAFEGuard which incorporates a hybrid fluency measurement based on cross-layer distribution distance and perplexity, and the analysis of harmful semantics through gradient matching. Our method is grounded in a paramount observation: high fluency prompts maintain their malicious intention close to harmful prompts while harmful semantic obfuscated prompts often inject gibberish token sequences. Our evaluation demonstrates that SAFEGuard consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and achieves significant improvement in accuracy across different optimization-based jailbreaks. This underscores the effectiveness of SAFEGuard against evolving jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [176] [Stability and Generalization of Straight-Through Estimators for Training Two-Layer Quantized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06430)
*Yiming Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从统计学习理论（SLT）视角研究二值激活网络的身份直通估计器（STE）训练算法。通过将零初始化的样本级STE递归等价地表示为凸潜在损失上的随机次梯度下降，作者推导出精确的距离恒等式和近似非膨胀性，进而获得显式的ℓ₂平均模型稳定性与泛化界，以及O(n^{-1/2})的过量诱导风险率和在边际可分条件下最优阶的O(R²/(γ²n))期望过量误分类误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：不连续的STE训练规则产生的估计器，其统计泛化是否能由算法稳定性来解释。二值激活网络中梯度不连续（几乎处处为零）给传统泛化分析带来困难，作者希望从稳定性视角建立理论保证。

Method: 在饱和输出机制下，将零初始化的样本级STE递归精确转化为凸潜在损失(-yu^⊤x)_+上的随机次梯度下降；推导两个耦合更新的精确距离恒等式；证明公共样本映射的近似非膨胀性（仅在潜在边际跨零时存在二次缺陷）；将稳定性从潜在向量等距传递到完整第一层矩阵；结合优化界得到显式泛化保证。在边际可分条件下通过补充论证给出随机单次STE迭代的期望过量误分类误差界和多数投票界。

Result: 获得了显式的ℓ₂平均模型稳定性与泛化界；在T=n²时得到O(n^{-1/2})率的过量诱导风险保证；在边际可分条件下得到最优阶O(R²/(γ²n))的期望过量误分类误差界及对应的多数投票界。

Conclusion: 算法稳定性确实能够解释不连续STE训练规则的统计泛化行为。通过潜在损失的凸表示，STE训练在饱和输出机制下享有与随机次梯度下降相似的稳定性结构，从而获得匹配最优统计率的泛化保证。这一结果在理论与实际之间弥合了不连续二值网络训练的理论分析鸿沟。

Abstract: We study the identity straight-through estimator (STE) for training a two-layer binary-activation network with hinge loss from the perspective of Statistical Learning Theory (SLT). Our central question is whether algorithmic stability can explain the statistical generalization of the estimator produced by the discontinuous STE training rule. In the saturated-output regime, the zero-initialized samplewise STE recursion is exactly the stochastic subgradient descent on the convex latent loss $(-yu^\top x)_+$. This representation makes a stability analysis possible. We derive an exact distance identity for two coupled updates and prove approximate non-expansiveness of the common-example map, with a quadratic defect only when the two latent margins straddle zero. We then obtain explicit $\ell_2$ on-average model-stability and generalization bounds, transferring stability isometrically from the latent vector to the full first-layer matrix. Combining stability with a standard optimization bound yields an explicit excess induced-risk guarantee and the rate $O(n^{-1/2})$ when $T=n^2$. Under margin separability, a complementary argument gives the optimal-order $O(R^2/(γ^2n))$ expected excess misclassification error for a randomized one-pass STE iterate and a corresponding majority-vote bound.

</details>


### [177] [Deep Barycentric Regression for Optimal Transport Map Estimation and its Statistical Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06598)
*Kunwoong Kim,Insung Kong,Yongdai Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BROT（Barycentric Regression for OT）方法，通过先计算无正则化OT计划，再用深度神经网络对诱导的质心目标进行最小二乘回归拟合，同时实现minimax最优收敛率和实用参数化估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OT映射估计器存在理论与实践之间的鸿沟：理论估计器能达到minimax最优收敛率但通常是非参数化、实现复杂且推断成本高；实用估计器是参数化且可扩展的，但其统计保证研究不足，且min-max对抗式训练目标对优化算法敏感。

Method: BROT采用两步法：第一步计算无正则化的OT计划，第二步将深度神经网络（DNN）通过最小二乘回归拟合到由OT计划诱导的质心（barycentric）目标上。

Result: 在标准正则条件下，证明当真实OT映射为Lipschitz时BROT的DNN估计器达到minimax收敛率。合成数据集和图像数据集的数值实验表明BROT在映射精度、目标分布匹配和传输成本上优于现有方法。在单细胞扰动预测和无监督域适应两个下游任务中表现出更强的任务性能。

Conclusion: BROT通过将OT计划估计与DNN回归简单结合，在理论最优性和实际可扩展性之间实现了统一，为OT映射估计算法提供了兼具统计保证与实用性的新方案。

Abstract: The optimal transport (OT) map provides a geometric transformation for aligning probability distributions and has become a useful tool in machine learning. However, existing estimators of the OT map still exhibit a gap between sharp statistical guarantees and practical parametric estimation based on stable training objectives. Theoretical estimators achieve minimax optimal convergence rates, but they are typically nonparametric and can incur demanding implementation design or inference costs. Practical estimators are parametric and scalable, but their statistical guarantees remain underexplored, and their min-max, adversarial-like training objectives can be sensitive to optimization algorithms. We propose BROT (Barycentric Regression for OT), a simple two-step method that first computes the unregularized OT plan and then fits a deep neural network (DNN) to the induced barycentric targets by least-squares regression. Under standard regularity conditions, we prove that the DNN estimator of BROT attains the minimax convergence rate, when the ground-truth OT map is Lipschitz. Numerical studies on synthetic datasets and an image dataset show that BROT provides accurate map estimates, strong target distribution matching, and competitive transport costs, compared to existing estimation methods. Experiments on two downstream tasks, single-cell perturbation prediction and unsupervised domain adaptation, further suggest that the accurate estimation of BROT can translate into stronger task performance.

</details>


### [178] [Beyond Arbitrary Geometry: Topology Generalization In neural PDE Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05860)
*Peiyao Chen,Zhouyuan Xu,Jianguo Nie,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Hodge热流视角引入TopoBox-3D基准，系统研究神经算子跨域拓扑泛化问题，发现任意网格兼容的神经算子并非真正的拓扑通用，其性能退化主要由谱特征（而非调和维数变化）驱动，并揭示显式拓扑先验并非总能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然神经算子常被认为对任意网格具有几何通用性，但未见的域拓扑变化会同时改变PDE算子的不变子空间和衰减子空间。作者旨在区分"几何泛化"与"拓扑泛化"，弄清拓扑作为独立于几何的泛化轴的作用机制及其在Hodge谱上的影响分布。

Method: 作者利用Hodge热流作为受控观测视角，构造TopoBox-3D基准数据集，其中通过隧道和空洞变化Betti数支撑，同时利用精确的Hodge分解将调和核与正谱分离。在六个架构、45个模型-任务拓扑分布外（OOD）组合上系统评测，并设计受控探针检验显式关联性和调和坐标对核识别精度的影响。

Result: 隐式推断拓扑的模型在37/45个拓扑OOD单元格中遭受额外的匹配退化；但调和维数变化的场景并未平均受到更强的惩罚。主要困难来自谱方向：初始Rayleigh商是最稳定的误差预测因子，谱展宽对边和面余链增加额外信息。受控探针显示显式关联性坐标并不产生最佳核识别精度，但TNO在全部六个具有非平凡调和支撑的任务中排名混合输入非调和精度第一。

Conclusion: 拓扑是与任意几何兼容性之外的一条独立泛化轴，其影响贯穿整个Hodge谱而非局限于调和核。全局低频结构先验可能有助于组织快速衰减互补分量的预测，为神经算子跨拓扑及几何泛化提供新视角。

Abstract: Neural operators that accept arbitrary meshes are often treated as geometry-general, but unseen domain topology changes both the invariant and decaying subspaces of a PDE operator. We use Hodge heat flow as a controlled lens on this distinction and introduce TopoBox-3D, where tunnels and cavities vary Betti support while the exact Hodge decomposition separates the harmonic kernel from the positive spectrum. Across six architectures, models that infer topology implicitly suffer excess matched degradation in 37 of 45 model--task topology-OOD cells, yet cases that change harmonic dimension are not more strongly penalized on average. The dominant difficulty is instead spectral: the initial Rayleigh quotient is the most stable predictor of error, and spectral broadening adds information for edge and face cochains. Most strikingly, controlled probes show that explicit incidence and harmonic coordinates do not yield the best kernel-identity accuracy; nevertheless, TNO ranks first in mixed-input nonharmonic accuracy on all six tasks with nontrivial harmonic support. Together, these results establish topology as a distinct generalization axis beyond arbitrary-geometry compatibility and show that its influence extends across the Hodge spectrum rather than remaining confined to the harmonic kernel. More broadly, they suggest that global, low-frequency structural priors may help organize predictions in the faster-decaying complementary component, offering a new perspective on how neural operators may generalize across topology as well as geometry.

</details>


### [179] [Efficient Learning and Symmetry Discovery under Exact Invariances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07031)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Behrooz Tahmasebi,Patrick Jaillet,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了学习问题中确切群不变性的两个核心挑战：一是提出首个统一适用于有限与无限群的多项式时间算法，运行时间与数据维度和样本规模呈多项式关系且与群无关，并具强泛化保证；二是在对称性未知的发现场景中，针对有限群的子群格，证明可多项式时间内从数据中识别确切对称性并用于学习，样本复杂度匹配已知对称性的极小极大最优水平。分析基于随机Cayley图与扩张图理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群不变性学习是科学与几何学习问题的核心，但其计算基础仍不清晰。此前仅对已知的有限群给出了确切不变性的多项式时间算法，无限群和未知对称性情形悬而未决，亟需从计算角度解释几何机器学习中不变与等变方法为何有效。

Method: 针对已知群情形，设计统一适用于有限与无限群的多项式时间学习算法，运行时间仅依赖于数据维度与样本量而与群无关；针对未知对称性情形，聚焦有限群的子群格，结合随机Cayley图与扩张图理论的分析工具，从数据中恢复底层对称性并完成回归学习。

Result: (1) 首次给出对有限与无限群统一适用的确切群不变性多项式时间算法，运行时间为数据维度与样本规模的多项式且与群无关，具备强泛化保证；(2) 在对称性发现场景中，算法可多项式时间内确证恢复底层对称性，样本复杂度匹配已知对称性设置下的极小极大最优水平。

Conclusion: 本文从计算复杂度角度为群不变学习的可行性提供了正面答案，部分回应了文献中的开放问题，所引入的随机Cayley图与扩张图工具也可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: Learning with group invariances is central to many scientific and geometric learning problems, yet its computational foundations remain poorly understood. Even for classical supervised regression settings, it has been unclear whether one can efficiently compute a regression function that is exactly invariant to a given group action. Recent work showed that exact invariance can be enforced in polynomial time when the underlying group is finite and known, but left open the cases of infinite groups and unknown symmetries. In this paper, we resolve both challenges. First, we present the first polynomial-time algorithm for learning with exact group invariances that applies uniformly to finite and infinite groups. The runtime is polynomial in the data dimension and sample size, and independent of the group, while achieving strong generalization guarantees. This provides a computational explanation for the empirical success of invariant and equivariant methods in geometric machine learning and partially answers a recent open question in the literature. Second, we study learning in the symmetry discovery setting, where the invariance group is unknown. Focusing on the subgroup lattice of a finite group, we show that exact symmetries can be identified from data and exploited for learning in polynomial time. For regression over finite-dimensional feature spaces, our algorithm provably recovers the underlying symmetry, matches the minimax-optimal sample complexity of the known-symmetry setting, and runs in time polynomial in the data dimension and sample size. Our analysis relies on tools from random Cayley graphs and expander theory, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [180] [Budgeted Task-Aware Acquisition of Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05862)
*Zihe Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Scout，一个轻量级框架，用于在只部分观测到动态图变化时，根据下游任务价值决定刷新哪些过期的图信息，以在有限的观测预算下最大化任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图中的底层网络变化往往只能被部分观测到，而获取完整的最新图信息会带来观测与计算成本，在资源受限情况下不切实际。因此需要一个任务感知的预算分配策略，决定优先刷新哪些过期的图信息以服务下游任务。

Method: Scout是一个轻量级框架，从维护的图和观测历史中学习查询每个节点的任务价值，从而决定哪些陈旧图信息需要刷新。它采用任务效用监督（而非结构变化监督）来指导采集决策。

Result: 在21个基准-预算组合中的19个取得最高平均下游性能；在16个真实世界设置中有13个优于结构变化监督方法。任务匹配的采集相比任务不匹配的采集，在链路预测AUC上提升0.012-0.016，在节点分类准确率上提升0.064-0.09。

Conclusion: 有用的图观测取决于下游任务本身，通过直接从下游效用学习，可以在有限的观测预算下实现更有效的资源分配。

Abstract: Learning on dynamic graphs is difficult when changes in the underlying network are only partially observed. Acquiring current graph information incurs observation and computational costs, making complete updates impractical under limited resources. This paper focuses on budgeted task-aware acquisition on dynamic networks, where a model needs to decide which stale graph information to refresh for a downstream task. We propose Scout, a lightweight framework that learns the task value of querying each node from the maintained graph and observation history. Our evaluation covers one synthetic and four real-world dynamic networks, two downstream tasks, nine acquisition baselines, and several query budgets. Scout achieves the highest mean downstream performance in 19 of the 21 benchmark-budget settings. Task-utility supervision also outperforms structural-change supervision in 13 of the 16 real-world settings. On the same dynamic network, task-matched acquisition improves link-prediction AUC by 0.012-0.016 and node-classification accuracy by 0.064-0.09 over task-mismatched acquisition. These results show that useful graph observations depend on the downstream task and that limited observation budgets can be allocated more effectively by learning directly from downstream utility.

</details>


### [181] [Dense Structural Compression of Transformers via Gauge-Correct Channel Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07264)
*Jed A. Duersch,Naïm Es-Sebbani,Nathanaël Haas,Zied Bouraoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GaugeLasso方法，通过通道级惩罚将稠密张量的整条切变置零以实现物理移除，在训练中自适应压缩transformer结构，在不损失性能的前提下实现最高255倍的推理计算压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的推理能耗由token的密集矩阵乘法主导，包含FMA操作和内存流量。现有的稀疏化或剪枝方法或破坏张量密度导致GPU吞吐下降，或在训练后进行但无法达到最优结构。作者希望从第一性原理出发，在训练过程中自适应调整结构复杂度，最大化单位计算下的推理效用，同时保持稠密张量以维持高GPU吞吐。

Method: 核心方法为GaugeLasso：对每个通道施加可加的对称群套索惩罚，通过均衡分析发现，直接的通道范数惩罚会被规范自由度（gauge freedom）干扰而失去稳定性。GaugeLasso在网络收敛到规范平衡（gauge balance）时恢复产品范数的单调函数，实现了逐通道校准，能够正确抑制在推理效用（单位计算下）表现不佳的通道。该方法在训练过程中施加持续压力，使网络重组为深度相关的结构剖面，将完整张量切片归零后物理移除，保持张量稠密性。

Result: 在F_31上的多项式长除法任务中，计算量压缩148至255倍且保持完全准确；在字符级语言建模中，压缩后的模型在相同FMA下优于手工设计的基线；在掩码自编码中，压缩试验揭示了哪些轴过度配置、哪些轴饱和，指导了更好的第二版设计。压缩随训练推进单调加速训练。事后剪枝无法达到相同结构，证明持续压力是发现高效模型的关键。重新训练已发现的结构在统计任务上恢复基线质量，但在精确算法任务上失败。

Conclusion: GaugeLasso通过在训练中施加持续的结构压力，能够发现比原架构小得多且更高效的结构，同时在推理时保持稠密张量以保证GPU吞吐。这一发现表明训练中自适应压缩优于事后剪枝，但压缩后的结构在精确算法任务上无法通过重新训练恢复，说明压缩发现的模型权重与结构之间存在紧密耦合。

Abstract: Inference energy per token drives the cost and carbon footprint of deployed transformers. It is dominated by dense matrix products that incur fused multiply-accumulate (FMA) operations and memory traffic. To reduce these computations while retaining dense tensors for high GPU throughput, we develop a methodology from first principles to adapt structural complexity during training to maximize inference utility per unit compute. Channel penalties drive entire tensor slices to zero to enable physical removal while preserving density and the network function.
  The natural approach, penalizing the norm of operator components acting through each channel, is provably destabilized by gauge freedom. We resolve this pathology with GaugeLasso: additive symmetric group-lasso penalties that recover a monotone function of product-norms when the network converges to gauge balance. Our equilibrium analysis enables per-channel calibration to correctly suppress slices that under-perform in inference utility per unit compute.
  Under adaptive pressure, the network reorganizes into depth-dependent structural profiles that can be far smaller than the architecture required to learn the task. On polynomial long division over $\mathbb{F}_{31}$, compute compresses from 148 to 255 times with perfect accuracy. On character-level language modeling, compressed models outperform the hand-designed baseline at equal FMA. On masked autoencoding, a compression trial exposes which axes were over-provisioned and which saturated, guiding a better second design. Compaction also accelerates training monotonically as the model progresses. Post-hoc pruning with the same utility ranking cannot reach these structures, showing that sustained pressure is central to discovery of efficient models.
