Retraining a discovered architecture recovers baseline quality on our statistical tasks, but fails on our exact algorithmic task.

</details>


### [182] [A budget-dependent crossover between coverage- and response-based training-set selection for machine-learned interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05877)
*Jia Bi,Alin-Marin Elena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过预算分辨（budget-resolved）对比实验，系统研究了机器学习原子间势训练集的选择策略：在保留数据规模和预测精度之间建立联系，比较"结构覆盖"与"响应引导"（基于模型分歧）两种选择准则。在GAP-20 Carbon和pooled revised MD17数据集上重训练MACE模型，发现覆盖策略在小预算（1%和5%）下误差更小，但响应引导（witness）策略在20%预算下反超覆盖策略，直接留出集力误差降低0.46-5.89%，平均力误差减少0.164-0.167 meV/Å。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择紧凑的训练集需要在保留结构多样性和针对模型分歧的构型之间权衡，而最佳选择可能取决于保留数据量的大小，仅在单一训练集规模下比较不足以得出结论。作者旨在建立选择准则与预测精度之间的预算分辨关系，验证何时基于模型分歧的响应引导压缩能优于结构覆盖。

Method: 在GAP-20 Carbon和pooled revised MD17数据集上，对MACE模型进行重训练，采用预算分辨的比较框架。对比两个选择准则：(1)结构覆盖（coverage），由覆盖训练模型提供；(2)响应引导选择器（witness），针对覆盖训练模型与全数据参考模型之间的分歧进行目标选择，作为一种追溯性响应见证测试，检验模型分歧在压缩已标注池中的价值。在1%、5%和20%等不同保留预算下评估，并辅以学习相似性保持分析、冻结模型误差选择等补充实验，以及8个配对训练种子的统计验证。

Result: 在5%预算下，覆盖策略在两个数据集的四个力端点上的绝对偏差小于随机采样。Witness策略在1%和5%时偏差大于覆盖，但在20%时排序反转：witness选择的模型直接留出力误差比覆盖低0.46-5.89%，且8个配对种子区间全部支持witness；其中6个误差低于全数据参考。平均力误差降低0.164-0.167 meV/Å，尾部和遮蔽端点的增益更大。补充分析表明学习相似度保持覆盖排序，而冻结模型误差选择的误差高于嵌入覆盖。

Conclusion: 保留数据预算是原子训练集选择中的决定性变量：结构覆盖在小预算下更优，而响应引导（模型分歧驱动的选择）在大预算下能够超越结构覆盖。这为何时响应引导压缩优于结构覆盖提供了直接检验并为预算依赖的训练集选择策略提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Selecting compact training sets for machine-learned interatomic potentials requires deciding whether to preserve structural diversity or target configurations on which models disagree. The better choice can depend on how much data is retained, making a comparison at one training-set size insufficient. Here we link selection criteria to prediction accuracy through a budget-resolved comparison of retrained MACE models on GAP-20 Carbon and pooled revised MD17. Structural coverage is compared with a response-guided selector that targets disagreement between a coverage-trained model and a full-data reference. This retrospective response witness tests the value of model disagreement for compressing an already labelled pool. At 5\%, coverage gives smaller absolute deviations from the full-data error than random sampling across four force endpoints in both datasets. The witness has larger deviations than coverage at 1\% and 5\%, but the ordering reverses at 20\%. At 20\%, witness-selected models also lower direct held-out force errors by 0.46--5.89\% relative to coverage, with all eight paired training-seed intervals favouring the witness. Six errors fall below the full-data reference. Mean force-error reductions are 0.164--0.167~meV~$\textÅ^{-1}$, with larger gains for tail and masked endpoints. Complementary analyses show that learned similarity preserves the coverage ranking, while selecting by frozen-model error gives higher error than embedding coverage. These findings establish retained-data budget as a deciding variable in atomistic training-set selection and provide a direct test of when response-guided compression improves on structural coverage.

</details>


### [183] [Statistical versus machine learning-based spatial interpolation of post-processed ensemble weather forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07512)
*Mária Lakatos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种统计与机器学习方法（EMOS、分布回归网络、Transformer、图神经网络）对ECMWF 2米温度和10米风速集合预报的统计后处理效果，覆盖德国已观测与未观测站点。研究提出一种海拔感知的线性池（ALP）方法用于温度校准，在未观测站点相比标准线性池有小幅但显著的改进。结果表明后处理普遍优于原始集合预报，但没有任何单一方法在所有变量、站点组和评估指标上全面胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合天气预报的统计后处理能提升预测可靠性，但在缺乏观测数据的站点（未观测位置）生成校准预测仍具挑战性，需要探索更有效的后处理方法。

Method: 在德国地区对ECMWF 2米温度和10米风速预报进行多种方法对比，包括EMOS类方法、分布回归网络、Transformer和图神经网络，在有限及扩展预测因子设置下评估。针对温度，进一步研究线性预报组合，并提出基于海拔感知的线性池（ALP）方法。

Result: 多数设置下后处理改善了原始集合预报；没有任何单一方法在所有变量、站点组和评估指标上均表现最优；所提出的ALP方法在未观测站点上比标准线性池带来小幅但显著的改进。

Conclusion: 不同的后处理方法在不同变量、站点类型和指标下各有优劣，需视具体应用场景选择合适方法；融合海拔信息的线性组合方法（ALP）能有效提升未观测站点的温度校准质量。

Abstract: Statistical post-processing improves ensemble weather forecasts, but generating calibrated predictions at locations without observations remains challenging. This study compares statistical and machine-learning-based methods for post-processing ECMWF 2-m temperature and 10-m wind speed forecasts at observed and unobserved stations in Germany. We consider EMOS-based approaches, distributional regression networks, Transformers, and graph neural networks under both limited and extended predictor settings. For temperature, we also investigate linear forecast combinations and propose an altitude-aware linear pool (ALP). The results show that post-processing improves upon the raw ensemble in most settings, but no single method performs best across all variables, station groups, and evaluation metrics. The proposed ALP provides a small but significant improvement over the standard linear pool at unobserved locations.

</details>


### [184] [Grounded and Faithful P&ID Reasoning: Constraining Vision-Language Models with Recovered Evidence Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05880)
*Prathamesh Gadekar,Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-Grounded Harness（GGH）方法，通过从P&ID图纸中恢复显式图结构（符号、工艺连接、标签），并强制视觉语言模型仅通过七个只读算子查询该图来回答问题，从而避免模型凭空捏造或遗漏工艺连接。在TopoPID-VQA数据集上，该方法将精确匹配准确率从36.7-41.3%（仅图像提示）提升至74.3-76.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P&ID图纸是工艺厂隔离、维护和HAZOP决策的权威依据，而现有视觉语言模型虽然能流畅描述图纸，却常会捏造或遗漏工艺连接，这可能导致隔离或可达性判断错误。由于流畅的模型回答无法被验证是否与图纸线网一致，因此需要一种可核查、基于显式图结构的方法，确保拓扑性声明有据可依。

Method: 作者首先从P&ID图纸中恢复一个显式图，包含图中的符号、符号间的工艺连接以及命名这些符号的标签。然后要求模型仅通过七个只读算子查询该图来回答问题，任何拓扑性声明只有引用支撑它的查询结果时才会被返回，从而将模型的答案锚定在恢复出的图上，而非自由生成。

Result: 在包含3000个拓扑问题的TopoPID-VQA数据集上，GGH将Qwen3-VL-4B、Qwen3-VL-8B和Gemma-4-E4B的精确匹配准确率从仅图像提示下的36.7-41.3%提升至74.3-76.0%。在Digitize-PID数据集上，恢复的图在精确工艺连接上F1为0.742，当符号和标签合并后达0.801。结果表明接地查询在恢复图正确时显著有效，但感知误差仍会在恢复图错误时破坏拓扑问题。

Conclusion: 基于图接地的问答框架能显著提升P&ID拓扑问题的可靠性，因为答案被显式锚定在可核查的恢复图上而非模型自由生成的叙述中。然而方法的上限受制于底层图的恢复精度：剩余误差正对应着恢复图的感知缺失，图恢复质量是进一步改进的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Piping and Instrumentation Diagrams (P&IDs) are the authoritative maps of process plants: isolation, maintenance, and HAZOP decisions depend on what connects to what. Vision-language models describe these sheets fluently, yet they often invent or miss process connections---and an invented or missed link can reverse an isolation or reachability call, so a plant decision cannot trust a fluent answer that was never checked against the linework. We instead recover an explicit graph of the drawing---its symbols, the process connections between them, and the tags that name them---and then require the model to answer only by querying that graph through seven read-only operators, so a topology claim is returned only when it cites the query results that support it. On TopoPID-VQA, a new suite of 3000 topology questions over these sheets, Graph-Grounded Harness (Ours) raises exact match accuracy from 36.7--41.3% under image-only prompting to 74.3--76.0% for Qwen3-VL-4B, Qwen3-VL-8B, and Gemma-4-E4B. It does so on an imperfect substrate: on Digitize-PID dataset the recovered graph scores F1 0.742 on exact process connections, and 0.801 once symbols and tags are pooled in. The residual errors track that gap---grounding pays off where the recovered graph is right, and perception error still breaks topology questions where it is not.

</details>


### [185] [Structured Extrema Errors in Classical Surrogates for Viscous Burgers: A Physics-Consistent Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07952)
*Youssef Oubari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了四种经典机器学习代理模型（RBF核岭回归、线性Ridge、ExtraTrees和随机森林）用于近似一维粘性Burgers方程时间演化的局部误差。研究发现所有模型在预测极大极小值附近形成弯曲的误差分支，核岭回归的误差与二阶空间导数（局部曲率）强相关，表现为预测值与曲率形成的局部两分支折叠结构。针对Burgers方程平流和扩散项的测试表明，KRR和Ridge在中等及高粘度下存在粘性平滑不足的问题，保留了比真实未来状态更多的小尺度结构，而树模型的这一物理解释较弱。基于仅预测量的修正可同时减少单步误差和递归推演误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解机器学习代理模型在逼近偏微分方程时间演化时的局部误差结构及其物理根源，旨在揭示此类替代模型系统性的误差模式，为误差修正提供理论依据。

Method: 作者在相同预测任务上对比四种基于空间网格值输入的回归模型；定义单步残差为真实值减去预测值；检查残差与一阶/二阶空间导数的相关性；分析预测值与曲率的几何折叠关系；对Burgers平流项和扩散项分别进行回归测试、破坏扩散项空间对齐的对照实验、以及高频成分谱测试；最后提出一个仅使用预测量的修正方案并验证其对单步和递归误差的改进。

Result: 所有四种模型的单步残差在预测极值附近呈现清晰弯曲分支；KRR误差与曲率相关性远强于与一阶导数相关性，形成两分支折叠，理论上推导出预测值与残差之间的近抛物线关系；KRR和Ridge的测试结果与粘性扩散平滑不足的假设一致，保留了过多小尺度结构，而树模型的证据较弱；所提出的修正方案在单步误差和递归推演中均有效降低误差。

Conclusion: 本文通过揭示代理模型误差与物理PDE项（扩散/粘性项）之间的系统性联系，证明部分机器学习替代模型（尤其核方法）在模拟Burgers方程时趋向于保留过小的尺度结构，根源在于粘性平滑处理不足；并证明基于纯预测量的简单修正即可显著改善模型在单步和递归预测中的表现。

Abstract: We study the local errors of classical machine-learning surrogate models, which approximate the time evolution of the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation. Four models are compared on the same prediction task, using the spatial grid values directly: radial basis function (RBF) kernel ridge regression (KRR), linear Ridge, ExtraTrees, and Random Forests. Across all four models, the one-step residual, defined here as the true value minus the predicted value at each grid point, forms clear curved branches near predicted maxima and minima. A more detailed analysis of KRR shows that these errors are much more strongly related to the second spatial derivative, which measures local curvature, than to the first spatial derivative. Near a smooth extremum, predicted value and curvature form a local two-branch fold. Under our local curvature-based model of the residual, this fold predicts a leading-order near-parabolic relation between predicted value and residual. This geometric result motivates a direct test of the Burgers advection (transport) and diffusion (smoothing) terms. For KRR and Ridge, regression tests on held-out trajectories, a control that breaks the spatial alignment of the diffusion term, and a spectral test of high-frequency content are consistent with insufficient viscous smoothing at moderate and high viscosity. In this case, the surrogate retains more small-scale structure than the true future state. The same physical explanation is much weaker for the tree models. Finally, a correction that uses only predicted quantities reduces both one-step error and error during recursive rollout, where each prediction is used as the next input.

</details>


### [186] [InfluenceField: A Differentiable Field with Interventionally Identifiable Causal Structure for Multimodal World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07874)
*Zihao Yang,Zijia Wang,Zhiqiu Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 InfluenceField，一种插入在视觉编码器与语言解码器之间的干预感知潜在场，通过连续空间表示和共享转移算子对局部视觉干预的传播与影响进行预测与建模。在 CausalVQA 上相较 backbone 提升 13.1 个百分点，消融与对照实验表明增益源于因果目标而非额外容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽能捕捉视觉-语言相关性，但无法预测局部视觉干预如何传播并影响下游答案。作者旨在通过显式建模干预的因果传播机制来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: InfluenceField 位于视觉编码器与语言解码器之间，将 patch 特征提升为连续空间表示，通过共享转移算子在多步上传播有向影响，并预测局部干预效应。训练联合优化四类目标：语言建模损失、跨环境不变性、反事实 rollout 监督和结构正则化。理论分析针对非线性有限基总体模型和线性特例给出了可识别性与稳定性保证。

Result: 在 CausalVQA 上，InfluenceField 相比 backbone 整体准确率提升 13.1 个百分点，其中规划与假设类别的提升最为显著。容量匹配的基线与结构对照实验证明，鲁棒性及事实-反事实一致性的提升归因于因果目标设计而非额外模型容量。

Conclusion: 理论结果证明，目标对齐的干预监督结合转移上的一步分离条件，可限制可接受表示为位置内重参数化，从而精确恢复完整转移的有向依赖图；线性特例给出了部分覆盖的精确刻画与有限损失稳定性界，场分析推导出系数干预的空间轮廓与共享通道校准结果。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models often capture visual-linguistic correlations but struggle to predict how local visual interventions propagate and affect downstream answers. We introduce InfluenceField, an intervention-aware latent field inserted between the visual encoder and language decoder. It lifts patch features into a continuous spatial representation, propagates directed influence over multiple steps, and predicts local intervention effects through a shared transition operator. Training jointly optimizes language modeling, cross-environment invariance, counterfactual rollout supervision, and structural regularization. For a nonlinear finite-basis population model, we show that target-aligned interventional supervision, together with a one-step separation condition on the transition, restricts admissible representations to within-location reparameterizations, so that the directed dependency graph of the full transition is recovered exactly. A linear specialization gives an exact partial-coverage characterization and a finite-loss stability bound, and the field analysis derives the spatial profile of coefficient interventions together with a shared-channel calibration result. On CausalVQA, InfluenceField improves overall accuracy over its backbone by 13.1 percentage points, with the largest gains on the planning and hypothetical categories. Capacity-matched baselines and structural controls attribute the gains in robustness and factual-counterfactual consistency to the causal objectives rather than to added capacity.

</details>


### [187] [CALM: Class-wise Agreement and Label-gated Disagreement Modulation for Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05884)
*Yifan Ying,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CALM方法，通过三级平滑信任门控（类别级、样本级、标签级）替代传统联邦蒸馏中的硬决策（阈值过滤/全信任），在高度非IID数据分布与异构客户端架构下提升蒸馏目标的可信度与性能，且不增加通信成本或辅助数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖参数平均，要求客户端架构同质且数据独立同分布；去中心化联邦蒸馏虽解决了这两个问题，但在严重非IID倾斜下，聚合教师目标的信任程度是渐进的，现有方法却做出"全有或全无"的生硬决策（如按阈值剔除离群教师、对幸存目标完全信任），导致蒸馏质量受损。

Method: 提出CALM，在三个层级以平滑信任门取代硬决策：(1) 类别级——按与同伴共识的契合度为各教师加权；(2) 样本级——按教师与该目标的偏差缩放蒸馏力度；(3) 标签级——按目标对样本真实标签的支持强度缩放。全程不增加通信开销或辅助数据。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、SVHN、OrganAMNIST和Google Speech Commands数据集上，结合Dirichlet标签倾斜与异构客户端架构，CALM持续优于均匀蒸馏和硬过滤蒸馏，并达到或超越其他异构联邦学习方法。

Conclusion: 以三级平滑信任门控替代硬阈值决策，能更精细地度量非IID环境下教师目标的可靠程度，从而显著提升去中心化联邦蒸馏在异构数据和异构架构条件下的性能，且无需额外通信代价。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning relies on parameter averaging, which forces clients to be doubly homogeneous: all must run an identical architecture, and accuracy degrades when local data are non-IID. Decentralized federated distillation sidesteps both: each client runs its peers' model snapshots as teachers on its own local data and distills from their soft predictions, with no server, no public data, and no shared architecture. Under severe non-IID skew, however, the trustworthiness of the aggregated teacher target is a matter of degree, yet existing pipelines make hard, all-or-nothing decisions: outlier teachers are discarded by threshold, and whatever target survives is trusted in full. We propose CALM, which replaces every hard decision with a smooth trust gate at three levels: per class, teachers are weighted by agreement with the peer consensus; per sample, distillation is scaled by the teachers' divergence from that target; and a label gate scales it by how strongly the target supports the sample's true label. None of this adds communication or auxiliary data. On CIFAR-10, SVHN, OrganAMNIST, and Google Speech Commands with heterogeneous client architectures under Dirichlet label skew, CALM consistently outperforms uniform and hard-filtered distillation and matches or exceeds competing heterogeneous-FL methods.

</details>


### [188] [GPU-Enabled Large-Scale Optimization Using Randomized Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08136)
*Pratik Rathore,Zachary Frangella,Parth Nobel,Xuning Hu,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: rlaopt是一个基于PyTorch的开源包，利用随机数值线性代数（RandNLA）算法进行大规模优化和科学计算，提供GPU加速的正定线性系统求解器和带约束/正则化的凸经验风险最小化求解器（NystromPCG、NysADMM、SAPPHIRE），并配备自然数学语法建模语言和可微分迭代特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于RandNLA的算法取得了显著进展，但很少有实现能将GPU加速与简单的问题指定接口相结合。rlaopt旨在填补这一空白，提供一个兼具GPU加速能力和易用建模接口的优化求解工具。

Method: rlaopt提供三类GPU支持的求解器：基于Nystrom PCG的正定线性系统求解器（NystromPCG）、基于算子分裂的NysADMM、以及随机梯度方法SAPPHIRE。同时包含一个建模语言，允许用户以自然数学语法指定问题，自动检查与所选求解器的兼容性并执行问题分解。求解器支持迭代过程中的微分，便于超参数调优等应用。

Result: 在岭回归、有界多项逻辑回归和有界弹性网络上进行实验，识别了随机预条件在何时能提升性能，并展示了GPU执行带来的显著加速效果。

Conclusion: rlaopt以Apache许可证开源发布，源码位于https://github.com/udellgroup/rlaopt，版本0.1.0可在PyPI上获取，为大规模优化和科学计算社区提供了一个GPU加速、易于使用且支持微分的RandNLA求解工具包。

Abstract: This paper introduces rlaopt, a PyTorch-based package for large-scale optimization and scientific computing using randomized numerical linear algebra (RandNLA). Despite substantial progress in RandNLA-based algorithms, few implementations combine GPU acceleration with a simple interface for specifying optimization problems. rlaopt addresses this gap by providing GPU-enabled solvers for positive-definite linear systems and convex empirical risk minimization with constraints and regularizers. These solvers use RandNLA to accelerate conjugate gradient (NystromPCG), operator splitting (NysADMM), and stochastic gradient methods (SAPPHIRE). Moreover, rlaopt includes a modeling language that lets users specify problems using natural mathematical syntax. rlaopt automatically checks compatibility with the selected solver and performs the required problem decomposition. The solvers also support differentiation through their iterations, enabling applications such as hyperparameter tuning. Experiments on ridge regression, bounded multinomial logistic regression, and bounded elastic net identify when randomized preconditioning improves performance and demonstrate substantial speedups from GPU execution. The package is open-source under an Apache license, with source code at https://github.com/udellgroup/rlaopt and version 0.1.0 available on PyPI.

</details>


### [189] [One Rate Is Not Enough: Adaptive Anisotropic Learning Rates for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05885)
*Huiyi Wang,Daijiao Liu,Lina Yao,Dong Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AnLR-LoRA，一种自适应各向异性学习率方法，为LoRA适配器的每个秩一分量分配独立的学习率，基于优化过程中的两个信号（函数空间速度和Adam SNR）在线计算，并按模块归一化保持全局学习率预算。实验表明该方法在多个基准上优于标准LoRA，且能更充分地利用秩容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数LoRA变体采用统一的全局学习率应用于每个适配器的每个秩一分量，忽略了模块内部的异质性。研究发现LoRA适配器的不同秩一分量更新速率极不均匀，低速度模块收敛到集中的奇异谱，未充分利用名义秩预算，从而限制了微调性能。

Method: 提出自适应各向异性学习率模型，为每个秩一分量赋予其独立的有效学习率。模型从AdamW优化过程中的两个信号在线计算学习率：函数空间速度（function-space velocity）和Adam信噪比（SNR），并按模块均值归一化以保持全局学习率预算。该方法为轻量级方案，不引入额外可训练参数。

Result: 在常识推理、自然语言生成和视觉指令微调等多个基准上，AnLR-LoRA一致性地优于标准LoRA，同时促进秩容量的更广泛利用。性能提升在广泛的全局学习率范围内保持稳健，并可迁移到其他LoRA变体中。

Conclusion: 统一学习率策略是LoRA性能受限的关键因素之一。通过按秩分量自适应调整学习率，AnLR-LoRA能有效缓解更新不均匀和奇异谱集中的问题，显著提升微调效果且不增加参数开销，是一种简洁有效的LoRA改进方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become the standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models. Most LoRA variants follow a uniform-LR convention, applying a single global learning rate across every rank-one component of every adapter. We show that this convention overlooks substantial within-module heterogeneity, where the rank-one components of a LoRA adapter update at highly uneven rates and low-velocity modules converge to concentrated singular spectra that underutilize the nominal rank budget. To address this, we propose an adaptive anisotropic learning-rate model that assigns each rank-one component its own effective learning rate, computed online from training-time signals and mean-normalized per module to preserve the global LR budget. AnLR-LoRA instantiates this model with two signals available during AdamW optimization, namely function-space velocity and Adam SNR, as a lightweight scheme with no extra trainable parameters. Across commonsense reasoning, natural language generation and visual instruction-tuning benchmarks, AnLR-LoRA consistently improves over LoRA while encouraging broader use of rank capacity, with gains that remain robust across a wide range of global learning rates and transfer cleanly to other LoRA variants.

</details>


### [190] [A First-Order Learning Algorithm for Online Resource Allocation with Constant Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05895)
*Menglong Li,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域在线资源分配问题，提出一种原始一阶学习策略，每期对当前请求类型对应的动作坐标执行一次梯度上升更新，无需求解线性规划，实现O(1)期望加性遗憾，且遗憾界不依赖于时域T，也无需流体线性规划的非退化假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线资源分配问题中，现有方法通常需要求解线性规划或依赖于非退化假设来获得良好的遗憾界。本文旨在设计一种更简单的原始一阶学习方法，在无需求解线性规划和非退化假设的情况下，仍能达到与T无关的常数遗憾界。

Method: 提出一种原始一阶学习策略：初始资源容量与时间范围成比例；每个周期观察到请求类型后，从有限菜单中选择一个动作；该策略每期仅对当前请求类型关联的动作坐标执行一次梯度上升更新，不需要求解任何线性规划。

Result: 该策略实现了O(1)的期望加性遗憾（相对于事后最优解），遗憾上界与时域T无关；同时不要求流体线性规划满足非退化假设。

Conclusion: 本文表明，通过简单的原始一阶梯度上升更新，即可在未知到达分布和资源容量受限的情况下获得与时间范围无关的常数遗憾，显著降低计算复杂度并放宽了理论假设条件。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon online resource allocation problem with initial resource capacities proportional to the horizon. In each period, a request type is observed and one action is chosen from a finite menu. Each action earns a reward and consumes a vector of resources. The arrival types are independent and identically distributed, but their probabilities are unknown. We present a primal first-order learning policy that, in each period, performs one gradient ascent update of the action coordinates associated with the current request type. The policy achieves $O(1)$ expected additive regret relative to the hindsight optimum, with a bound independent of the horizon $T$. It does not solve any linear program, and the regret bound does not require a nondegeneracy assumption on the fluid linear program.

</details>


### [191] [A dictionary learning framework for graphs via filters and optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05919)
*Jinchuan Liao,Dai Hai Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种图字典学习（GDL）框架，将每个图表示为由其滤波拉普拉斯矩阵导出的零均值高斯分布，通过滤波器图距离（fGOT）下的重心来近似观测图，并使用可处理的代理距离sfGOT进行端到端重构误差最小化，在聚类和分类任务上取得具有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字典学习方法在传统信号处理中取得了巨大成功，但将其推广到图结构数据时面临诸多挑战：图本身没有天然的向量表示、图之间的节点对应关系未知、以及全局结构信息难以被有效捕捉等。现有图字典学习方法往往依赖于固定图距离度量或需要已知节点对应关系，限制了其适用性与表达力。

Method: 每个观测图被建模为从滤波拉普拉斯导出的零均值高斯分布；在学习和推理中，该分布由一组可学习的原子图分布的滤波器Gromov-Wasserstein重心近似；重构误差通过代理滤波器图距离（sfGOT）度量，该度量能处理无节点对应关系的图且可微，通过反向传播进行端到端优化。此外，论文从希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则的角度对sfGOT进行了理论解释，揭示最小化两图间sfGOT距离等价于最大化其节点谱嵌入间的统计依赖性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的图聚类和图分类实验中，所提出的GDL框架相较于已有图字典学习方法取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 基于滤波图高斯分布与可微代理距离的图字典学习框架是一种有效的图表示学习方法，其理论与实证结果均支持该方法的优越性。

Abstract: We propose a graph dictionary learning (GDL) framework where each graph is represented as a zero-mean Gaussian distribution derived from its filtered Laplacian. Each observed graph is approximated by a barycenter over learned atom graphs, computed under the filter graph distance (fGOT), a graph comparison metric sensitive to global structural properties. The reconstruction error between the observed graph and its barycenter is measured by the surrogate fGOT (sfGOT) distance, a tractable approximation of fGOT that handles graphs without known node correspondence, and is minimized end-to-end via backpropagation. We further provide a novel interpretation of sfGOT through the lens of the Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion, showing that minimizing the sfGOT distance between two graphs is equivalent to maximizing statistical dependence between the spectral embedding of their nodes. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate competitive performance over existing GDL methods on graph clustering and classification tasks.

</details>


### [192] [AlphaRJM: Reward-Jump Memory for Stochastic Return-Guided Alpha Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08581)
*Sayan Dhan,Selvaraju Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaRJM，一种用于公式化Alpha发现的方法，通过Reward-Jump Memory（事件驱动的隐状态，仅在终端评估事件时更新）和动作条件SDE回报评论器（用随机粒子表示未来折现发现回报）来解决延迟反馈导致的池历史缺失与中间动作价值不确定的两大难点，在多个股票域和预测时域上取得稳健增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公式化Alpha发现是一个与池相关的符号搜索问题，反馈主要在完整表达式被评估时产生。这种延迟反馈导致两个耦合困难：一是保留的alpha池没有保留完整的评估反馈历史；二是中间构造动作的价值不确定，因其后果取决于最终完成的公式。这两个问题使得传统强化学习方法难以高效搜索。

Method: AlphaRJM包含两个核心组件：(1) Reward-Jump Memory——事件驱动的隐状态，在token构造期间保持固定，仅在终端评估事件时用实际的池奖励和评估结果更新；(2) 动作条件SDE回报评论器——用随机粒子表示未来折扣发现回报，通过分布的均值与不确定性指导动作选择，采用结合能量距离匹配、均值校准和跳跃正则化的分布贝尔曼目标进行学习。

Result: 实证结果表明，AlphaRJM在多个股票域、预测时域和随机种子下均取得强且稳定的性能提升。消融实验证实了持久评估历史、随机回报建模和分布监督三者之间互补的作用。

Conclusion: AlphaRJM通过引入事件驱动记忆与随机回报模型有效解决了延迟反馈下的公式搜索困难，验证了持久评估历史、随机回报建模与分布监督的互补价值，为延迟反馈符号搜索提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Formulaic alpha discovery is a pool-dependent symbolic search problem in which informative feedback is observed primarily when a complete expression is evaluated. This delayed feedback creates two coupled difficulties: the retained alpha pool does not preserve the full history of realized evaluation feedback, and the value of an intermediate construction action is uncertain because its consequence depends on the formula eventually completed. We introduce AlphaRJM, which addresses these difficulties through Reward-Jump Memory, an event-driven latent state that remains fixed during token construction and updates only at terminal evaluation events using the realized pool reward and evaluation outcome, and an action-conditioned SDE return critic that represents future discounted discovery returns with stochastic particles. The particles guide action selection through their mean and uncertainty and are learned using a distributional Bellman objective combining energy-distance matching, mean calibration, and jump regularization. Empirically, AlphaRJM delivers strong and stable gains across multiple equity universes, forecasting horizons, and random seeds, while ablations confirm the complementary roles of persistent evaluation history, stochastic return modeling, and distributional supervision.

</details>


### [193] [Rethinking the Evaluation of Efficiency Methods for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05933)
*Jiamu Zhang,Lingxi Zhang,Pengjun Lu,Qiyue Zhang,Yu-Neng Chuang,Zhengchen Li,Shuai Xu,Vipin Chaudhary,Hanjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有多智能体系统（MAS）效率优化方法的评估可能被高估，因为评测中常使用方法特定的提示词与起始拓扑，且许多实验场景本身并不需要MAS。作者构建了一个受控且MAS需求强的诊断基准，在共享骨干模型、代理注册表和运行时的条件下系统评估代表性方法，发现许多报告的增益具有设置依赖性，可能源于结构坍缩、禁用的工具路径或随机剪枝本身就已能保持性能，而非真正提升了MAS效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的多智能体系统随着模型规模和智能体数量增长带来高昂执行成本。现有剪枝、删边、紧凑结构搜索等方法宣称能降低开销，但其评测方式（方法特定提示词和起始拓扑）可能使增益无法归因于结构改动本身，且许多评测场景中单智能体或随机剪枝系统已能保持良好性能，导致对真实效率提升的误判。

Method: 构建一个受控且对MAS具有需求的诊断基准。在统一的骨干模型、智能体注册表和运行时环境下，对代表性MAS效率方法进行系统评测，并控制拓扑、规模、深度及工具使用等变量，以隔离和归因结构修改的真实效果。

Result: 发现大量已报告的效率增益依赖于特定设置，可能源于以下现象：(1) 结构坍缩导致系统退化为接近单智能体；(2) 工具通路被错误禁用而掩盖了能力损失；(3) 起始系统本身过大或冗余，随机剪枝即可保持精度，导致增益并非来自精细的结构优化。

Conclusion: 现有MAS效率评测存在系统性偏差，需要更严谨、更MAS需求强烈的标准化基准来公正评估效率方法的真实贡献，避免由设置性因素造成的虚假增益。

Abstract: Efficiency is increasingly important for Large Language Model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS), as larger models and more agents introduce substantial execution costs. Recent methods aim to make MAS cheaper by pruning agents, removing communication edges, or searching for compact structures. However, we argue that existing evaluations may overestimate their true ability to improve MAS efficiency. Reported gains are often measured under method-specific prompts and starting topologies, making them difficult to attribute to the proposed structural changes. Moreover, many reported successes appear in non-MAS-demanding settings, where a single agent or a randomly pruned system can already preserve strong performance. To study these issues, we introduce a controlled and MAS-demanding diagnostic benchmark for representative MAS efficiency methods. We evaluate methods under a shared backbone model, agent registry, and runtime, across controlled variations in topology, scale, depth, and tool use. Our analysis shows that many reported gains are setup-dependent and may arise from structural collapse, disabled tool pathways, or starting systems where random pruning already preserves accuracy, rather than robust improvements in MAS efficiency.

</details>


### [194] [Interpretable and Fair Generalized Additive Neural Networks via Multi-objective Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05946)
*Ziming Wang,Changwu Huang,Ke Tang,Yew-Soon Ong,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经网络广义加性模型（NN-based GAMs）提出可解释性的定量评估指标，并提出了一种基于多目标进化学习的多目标神经基模型（MONBM）框架，同时优化准确性、可解释性和公平性，通过部分重训练策略使进化多目标优化适用于深层模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NN-based GAMs研究主要关注提升准确性，对其可解释性缺乏系统性量化评估；同时，可解释性与公平性的联合显式优化及其权衡关系尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出显式定量指标评估NN-based GAMs的可解释性；构建基于多目标进化学习的MONBM框架，将准确性、可解释性和公平性作为三个优化目标并行考虑；开发部分重训练策略以将进化多目标优化扩展到深度模型架构。

Result: MONBM能够同时优化三个信任维度，获得一组在不同维度间具有不同权衡的模型集合；揭示了这些信任维度之间的复杂关系及其背后的原因；与最先进方法相比，验证了该方法的竞争力。

Conclusion: 多目标优化可与自解释模型相结合以揭示信任AI各维度间的关系，MONBM为同时兼顾准确性、可解释性与公平性提供了一套有效且可行的框架。

Abstract: Interpretability and fairness are two of the most emphasized dimensions in trustworthy artificial intelligence (AI). Various explainable AI methods have been introduced to improve interpretability. This paper focuses on neural network (NN)-based generalized additive models (GAMs), a class of self-interpretable models. While most existing research has prioritized improving the accuracy of NN-based GAMs, their interpretability remains largely underexplored. To address this gap, this paper introduces explicit quantitative metrics for evaluating the interpretability of NN-based GAMs, empirically examines their effectiveness, and explores strategies for improving interpretability within these models. In addition, the simultaneous and explicit optimization of both interpretability and fairness, along with their trade-offs and the underlying reasons, remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a multi-objective neural basis model (MONBM) framework based on multi-objective evolutionary learning to consider accuracy, interpretability, and fairness simultaneously. A partial retraining strategy is further developed to facilitate the practical application of evolutionary multi-objective optimization to deep model architectures. Based on MONBM, this paper reveals the complex relationships between these dimensions and the reasons behind these intricate relationships. This analysis demonstrates how multi-objective optimization can be combined with self-interpretable models to reveal relationships among trustworthiness objectives. In addition, MONBM obtains a set of models with different trade-offs between dimensions, and the competitiveness of the approach is validated by comparing it with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [195] [Transformers as In-Context Samplers: From Closed-Form Diffusion to Estimation-Free Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08981)
*Arman Adibi,Alireza Jafari,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Hadi Daneshmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明冻结的Transformer不仅能进行上下文学习（如线性回归），还能模拟迭代式生成采样器，从上下文样本中进行数据生成。作者构造性地证明了Transformer可以实现闭式及平滑闭式扩散采样器，其中softmax注意力负责计算责任权重和加权经验均值，前馈层实现Euler更新。为关联理论构建与预训练语言模型，作者研究了语义主题采样（如动物、食物、城市等词），并测量隐藏状态演化的U形几何模式及粒子能量曲线，证明Transformer可近似基于能量的采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论工作证明大型语言模型具备上下文学习能力，但该能力是否从监督学习（如线性回归）扩展到数据生成任务尚未明确。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，证明冻结的Transformer能够从上下文样本中模拟迭代式生成采样器，从而将上下文学习的适用范围从推断推广到生成。

Method: 作者首先通过构造性证明，展示Transformer可以实现在线/离线扩散采样器的闭式解形式及平滑形式，其中softmax注意力用于计算责任权重和加权经验均值（对应采样中的分布混合），前馈层负责实现Euler步更新。随后在实证层面，作者研究预训练语言模型在语义主题采样任务中的内部表征，使用归一化的隐藏状态向量来分析跨层的几何演化：中间层向均匀球面参考靠拢，输出层回归到主题相关的结构化表征。作者还在隐藏状态云上定义了相互作用粒子能量函数，观察到同样的U形模式。最后给出理论构造，证明Transformer能够近似基于能量的采样器，并逐层重现相同的U形能量曲线。

Result: 理论结果：(1) 证明了Transformer可以模拟扩散采样器（闭式及平滑闭式）；(2) 证明了Transformer可近似基于能量的采样器。实证结果：在预训练语言模型中进行语义主题采样时，隐藏状态在各层中呈现两阶段几何——中间层趋于均匀球面分布，输出层恢复主题相关结构；定义在隐藏状态云上的粒子能量呈现U形曲线，与理论构造逐层对应的行为一致。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和实证两个角度论证了Transformer的上下文学习能力可延伸到数据生成任务。核心洞见是softmax注意力在生成中承担了类似期望最大化或混合模型中的责任权重计算角色，前馈层执行梯度/扩散更新步骤，从而构成一种隐式的迭代采样机制。预训练语言模型的内部表征演化（U形能量曲线）与此机制吻合，说明这一现象不只是理论构造，而能在真实模型中观察到。

Abstract: A growing body of work establishes that large language models are not mere statistical memorizers, but are capable of in-context learning: performing inference at test time using only examples provided in the prompt, without any parameter updates. Prior theoretical work has shown that this capability extends to supervised learning tasks such as linear regression. We prove that in-context learning extends further to \emph{data generation}: frozen transformers can simulate iterative generative samplers from in-context samples. We first show that transformers can realize closed-form and smoothed closed-form diffusion samplers. The construction identifies a concrete generative role for softmax attention: it computes responsibility weights and weighted empirical averages, while feedforward layers implement Euler updates.
  To empirically relate these constructions to pretrained language models, we study \emph{semantic-topic sampling}: prompts consisting of words drawn from a common semantic category, such as animals, foods, or cities. Across transformer layers, the normalized hidden states exhibit a two-stage geometry: they move toward a uniform spherical reference in intermediate layers and then return to structured, topic-dependent representations near the output. We further measure an interacting-particle energy on these hidden-state clouds and observe the same U-shape pattern. We then prove that transformers can approximate an energy-based sampler, constructing the same U-shape energy across the layers.

</details>


### [196] [QGB-W$k$NN: Quantum Granular-Ball Learning for Robust Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05952)
*Suzhen Yuan,Dehang Chen,Lifeng Shen,Shuyin Xia,Jeremiah D. Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子粒球的高效可靠加权K近邻分类框架QGB-WkNN，通过量子粒球表示、纯度引导的HNSW优化和加权投票机制，在降低计算开销的同时提升噪声环境下的分类鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有K近邻分类方法在大规模数据场景下计算效率低下，且在噪声环境中鲁棒性有限，二者难以同时得到有效解决。

Method: 主要包括三部分：(1) 构建量子核粒球表示，在有限量子资源下降低检索冗余并增强非线性特征表达；(2) 提出粒球纯度引导的HNSW优化策略，利用结构可靠性指导层次图构建，缓解传统随机分层导致的局部最优问题；(3) 引入结合粒球相似度与纯度的加权投票机制，提升噪声环境下的分类决策可靠性。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，QGB-WkNN在分类准确率上具有竞争力，且在分类性能与计算成本之间展现出良好Pareto权衡；在多种噪声条件下的鲁棒性也得到一致提升。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的量子粒球学习为高效、鲁棒的近邻分类提供了一种有前景的新范式。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor classification is widely used in machine learning, yet existing methods often suffer from low computational efficiency and limited robustness in noisy environments. To jointly address these challenges, this paper proposes an efficient and reliable weighted $K$-nearest neighbor classification framework based on quantum granular balls, termed QGB-W$k$NN. The proposed framework improves computational efficiency by integrating quantum-enhanced granular-ball representation with hierarchical nearest-neighbor search, while enhancing classification reliability through a purity-aware weighted decision mechanism. Specifically, quantum-kernel granular balls are constructed to reduce retrieval redundancy and strengthen nonlinear feature representation under limited quantum resources. A granular-ball purity-guided HNSW optimization strategy is developed to exploit structural reliability for hierarchical graph construction during neighbor retrieval, alleviating the local optimality issue caused by conventional random layering. Finally, a weighted voting mechanism jointly incorporating granular-ball similarity and purity is introduced to produce more reliable classification decisions in noisy environments. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that QGB-W$k$NN achieves competitive classification accuracy while exhibiting favorable Pareto trade-offs between classification performance and computational cost. Moreover, the proposed framework consistently improves robustness under various noisy conditions, suggesting that reliability-aware quantum granular-ball learning provides a promising paradigm for efficient and robust nearest-neighbor classification.

</details>


### [197] [Nearly Tight Rademacher Bounds for Sparsely Activated Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09130)
*Xiaoyu Li,Zhizhou Sha,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了单隐层ReLU网络中input-dependent稀疏性（输入相关稀疏性）的统计复杂度。在Awasthi等人（COLT 2024）的模型框架下，宽度为s、每个输入最多激活k个单元、有效权重和偏置有界为W、B的假设类，其Rademacher复杂度满足上界CWR·min{k,√(sk/m)}·log^{3/2}(2m)+kB/√m。通过支持保持覆盖（support-preserving cover）和归一化链式论证去除了显式维度因子（至多对数因子），并给出了相匹配的下界。此外，完全球域上的零偏置稀疏网络至多只有2k个非零单元，复杂度为O(kWR/√m)；而当偏置界与WR相当时，在最坏情形下恢复到与维度对数相关的复杂度率。最后给出了agnostic minimax excess-risk界min{1,√(s/(km))}（至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的神经网络虽然整体可能使用所有隐藏单元，但每个单独输入往往只激活少量单元。作者希望刻画这种input-dependent sparsity如何影响统计学习复杂度（如Rademacher复杂度），特别是能否消除传统边界中依赖于网络宽度s的显式维度因子，并理解输入域结构（如球面与球体）对复杂度的影响。

Method: 采用与Awasthi等人（COLT 2024）一致的单隐层ReLU模型。技术上，利用支持保持覆盖（support-preserving cover）来对激活单元集合进行结构化枚举，再通过单一归一化链式论证（single normalized chaining argument）获得不显式依赖宽度的Rademacher复杂度上界。同时通过构造合适的i.i.d.边缘分布（marginals）得到下界，以验证上界的紧性（至多对数因子）。针对零偏置与有偏置的不同情形，分别使用球面上/球体上的构造（如球冠spherical-cap构造）来证明不同输入域条件下复杂度率的分化。

Result: 得到的关键结果包括：(1) 一般情形下Rademacher复杂度上界为CWR·min{k,√(sk/m)}·log^{3/2}(2m)+kB/√m，消除了显式维度因子；(2) 匹配的下界表明跨输入激活单元变化仍保留对宽度的依赖；(3) 输入域影响显著：零偏置网络在全球上至多2k个非零单元且复杂度为O(kWR/√m)，而偏置界~WR时恢复到最坏情形率（仅对数维度依赖）；(4) 对归一化有界损失且W、B~WR时，获得agnostic minimax excess-risk界~min{1,√(s/(km))}。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了单隐层ReLU网络在input-dependent sparsity假设下的统计复杂度。结果表明：稀疏激活可以消除由宽度引入的显式维度惩罚，且当偏置界适当时全输入域上的复杂度与采样域（仅对数因子差）相当。该工作深化了对神经网络中稀疏性与可学习性关系的理解。

Abstract: An input may activate few hidden units even when different inputs collectively use an entire network. We study the statistical complexity of this input-dependent sparsity in the one-hidden-layer ReLU model of Awasthi et al. (COLT 2024). For width $s$, at most $k$ active units per input, and effective weight and bias bounds $W,B$, every size-$m$ sample in the class's fixed radius-$R$ input domain satisfies $\mathcal{R}(S)\le CWR\min\{k,\sqrt{sk/m}\log^{3/2}(2m)\}+kB/\sqrt m$. A support-preserving cover and a single normalized chaining argument remove the previous explicit dimension factor, up to logarithms. Lower bounds on appropriate i.i.d. marginals match up to those logarithms, showing how changing active units across inputs retains a width dependence. The input domain matters: zero-bias networks sparse on the entire ball have at most $2k$ nonzero units and complexity $O(kWR/\sqrt m)$, whereas bias bounds comparable to $WR$ restore the worst-case rate on that same domain in only logarithmic dimension. A spherical-cap construction proves the latter claim without assuming sparsity merely on the sampling support. For a specified normalized bounded loss and biases comparable to $WR$, we also obtain agnostic minimax excess-risk bounds of order $\min\{1,\sqrt{s/(km)}\}$ up to logarithms.

</details>


### [198] [LoGIC: Budgeted Context Construction for Node-Level Graph In-Context Learning with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05955)
*Mingqi Yang,Zidong Guo,Jihui Yang,Wenming Zuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoGIC框架，研究表格基础模型在节点级图内上下文学习（ICL）中的上下文构建问题，通过结构化检索标记节点并结合辅助未标记halo节点，在降低attention计算和内存开销的同时，保持甚至超越全上下文性能，并支持冻结模型在百万节点图上的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于表格基础模型的图学习方法（如G2T-FM、GraphPFN）将节点编码为特征行并通过ICL进行预测，但当前协议使用完整训练表作为上下文，导致attention计算量随标记池二次增长，同时引入预处理和内存瓶颈。因此需要研究如何更高效地构建上下文，即在有限的标记上下文预算和未标记halo预算下，为查询节点选择最相关的标记节点和辅助节点。

Method: LoGIC通过三种检索通道（结构化、基于特征、覆盖度）选择标记节点，将每个上下文共享给图局部簇内的多个查询以降低重复计算；引入未标记halo节点用于适配器（adapter）的消息传递而不占用标签容量；通过无需测试标签的方式自动选择最优检索通道和上下文预算。在三个骨干配置（来自两个模型家族）上于GraphLand数据集上评估。

Result: 在三个骨干配置上，预算化上下文在保持本地可运行的性能的同时，达到与全上下文相当的完整上下文性能，并在大规模数据集上保持竞争力；相比全上下文和全图推断，显著降低了峰值内存需求。该方法还支持在百万节点图上进行无需重新训练的冻结图ICL。分析揭示了各检索通道的最佳适用场景及其与图结构属性的关联。

Conclusion: LoGIC表明，通过智能的上下文构建与预算分配，可以在不牺牲预测性能的前提下大幅降低表格基础模型在图ICL中的计算和内存开销，使冻结的基础模型能够扩展到百万节点级别的图，为大规模图上的上下文学习提供了可行的实用方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models have become powerful graph learners. Systems such as G2T-FM and GraphPFN encode each node as a feature row and make predictions through in-context learning (ICL), with labeled rows serving as the prompt. Current protocols employ the complete training table as context, causing attention to scale quadratically with the labeled pool and introducing preprocessing and memory bottlenecks. We investigate context construction for node-level graph ICL: which labeled nodes and auxiliary unlabeled nodes should constitute the prompt for specified queries. We formulate this allocation in terms of two resources: a labeled-context budget for predictive evidence and an unlabeled-halo budget for adapter message passing without using label capacity. We present LoGIC, which retrieves labeled nodes via structural, feature-based, and coverage channels, shares each context across the queries in a graph-local cluster, incorporates an unlabeled halo for adapter backbones, and chooses the channel and context budget without test labels. Across three backbone configurations drawn from two model families on GraphLand, budgeted contexts maintain locally runnable full-context performance, stay competitive with published large-dataset results, and markedly lower peak memory requirements compared with full-context and whole-graph inference. They further permit frozen graph ICL on million-node graphs without retraining. Our analysis identifies when retrieval channels work best and connects their behavior with graph properties.

</details>


### [199] [Machine Learning for Pre-Culture ESBL Risk Stratification to Guide Empiric Antibiotic Selection: A 12-Hospital Study of Enterobacteriaceae Cultures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05970)
*Aravind V. Kuruvikkattil,Lalitha Pranathi Pulavarthy,Rashmita Kudamala,Saptarshi Purkayastha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用12家医院、72,217名患者的132,955份培养样本，基于45项培养前EHR特征构建了成本敏感的XGBoost模型，用于预测ESBL表型（ESBL产生型肠杆菌感染）。模型在90%敏感性下达到95.8%的阴性预测值（NPV），可将ESBL后验概率降至4.2%，支持非ICU环境中安全的碳青霉烯保留策略，每1,000份培养中可避免307次不必要的广谱抗生素疗程。SHAP分析显示既往ESBL定殖是最强预测因子，剥夺特征缺失仅导致AUROC下降0.02，模型在严格IDSA定义下性能保持稳定（AUROC 0.766）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生在培养结果出来前48-72小时必须选择经验性抗生素治疗，面临两难困境：要么治疗不足（无法覆盖耐药的ESBL感染），要么过度使用碳青霉烯类抗生素（进一步推动耐药性扩散）。需要一个能够在培养送检时即预测ESBL表型的工具，以指导抗生素选择决策。

Method: 采用成本敏感的XGBoost模型，使用45个培养前的电子健康档案（EHR）特征，在12家医院的患者级分区下训练与验证。预测目标为ESBL表型（对头孢曲松、头孢他啶、头孢吡肟或哌拉西林-他唑巴坦耐药）。通过SHAP分析评估特征贡献，并在不同ESBL诊断定义、标本类型预测因子及类别不平衡校正条件下进行稳健性检验。

Result: 在90%敏感性下模型达到95.8%的NPV，ESBL后验概率降至4.2%。每1,000份培养可避免307次不必要的广谱抗生素疗程，代价是每1,000例漏诊14例ESBL病例。SHAP识别出既往ESBL定殖为最强预测因子；去除剥夺特征仅致AUROC下降0.020。在严格IDSA定义下AUROC为0.766，加入标本类型预测因子为0.764，无类别不平衡校正为0.762，不同菌株层级的AUROC范围为0.71-0.78。

Conclusion: 该模型可在非ICU环境中实现安全、公平的碳青霉烯保留决策支持，在维持高敏感性以不漏诊ESBL感染的同时显著减少不必要的广谱抗生素使用，且去除社会经济剥夺特征不影响模型公平性与性能。

Abstract: Empiric antibiotic therapy for suspected ESBL-producing Enterobacteriaceae must be selected 48-72 hours before culture results, forcing clinicians to choose between undertreating resistant infections and overusing carbapenems that drive further resistance. We developed a cost-sensitive XGBoost model predicting an ESBL phenotype (resistance to ceftriaxone, ceftazidime, cefepime or piperacillin-tazobactam) at culture ordering using 45 pre-culture EHR features across 132,955 cultures from 72,217 patients at 12 hospitals (14.41% with the ESBL phenotype). Cultures were partitioned at the patient level. At 90% sensitivity, the model achieved 95.8% NPV, reducing post-test ESBL probability to 4.2%, a threshold that may support safe carbapenem-sparing in non-ICU settings, while sparing 307 of every 1,000 cultures an unnecessary broad-spectrum course at the cost of 14 missed ESBL cases per 1,000. SHAP analysis identified prior ESBL colonization as the dominant predictor, ahead of prior organism burden and neighborhood deprivation; removing deprivation features caused minimal performance loss ($Δ\text{AUROC} = -0.020$), enabling equitable bedside deployment. Discrimination was unchanged under a strict IDSA ESBL-E definition (AUROC 0.766), with specimen type added as a predictor (0.764) and without any class-imbalance correction (0.762), and ranged from 0.71 to 0.78 across organism strata.

</details>


### [200] [DART: Distributional Adversarial Recurrent Training for Algorithm Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05988)
*Hieu Tran Bao,Phung Thanh Dang,Pham Quang Nhat Minh,Hoang Thanh Tung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DART（分布对抗递归训练）框架，通过用真实解周围的局部目标分布替代单点监督，并借助对抗目标对齐模型输出，以解决递归推理模型（RRM）在训练中因合法解空间稀疏而难以学习的问题。在Maze、Chess和masked Sudoku多个任务及多种RRM模型上，DART提升了求解质量、稳定性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归推理模型（RRM）通过隐藏空间的迭代计算实现从易到难的泛化，但传统的实例级（单点）监督在任务难度增加时变得低效：合法解在解空间中只占极小的区域，而非法解急剧增多，导致模型难以获得有效的学习信号。

Method: DART框架的核心做法是：将单点监督替换为围绕真实解（ground-truth）构建的局部目标分布，并通过对抗性目标将模型输出与该目标分布对齐。这种方法为训练提供更丰富的学习信号，促使模型在迭代过程中形成更稳定的轨迹以收敛到合法解。

Result: 在Maze、Chess和masked Sudoku三个任务上，采用多种RRM架构（包括Deep Thinking Systems和Tiny Recursive Models）进行评估，DART在求解质量、稳定性以及面对所评估的分布偏移时的鲁棒性方面均取得了改进。对比实验表明，DART的效果不能被标签平滑、高斯软化目标或渐进式训练所单独解释，且与稳定长时递归的训练方案互补。

Conclusion: DART被证明是一种在多种递归推理模型上提升鲁棒性的有前景的训练方法，其优势源于分布级监督信号而非单纯的目标软化，并且能够与现有的长时递归稳定化训练方案相结合，进一步改善模型性能。

Abstract: Recurrent reasoning models (RRMs) can solve structured problems, achieving easy-to-hard generalization through iterative computation in hidden space. These models are typically trained with instance-level supervision, which becomes increasingly problematic as task difficulty grows: valid solutions occupy a tiny region of the solution space, while invalid solutions proliferate rapidly. We propose Distributional Adversarial Recurrent Training (DART), a training framework that replaces single-point supervision with a local target distribution around the ground-truth solution and aligns model outputs with this distribution through an adversarial objective. DART provides a richer learning signal and encourages more stable iterative trajectories toward valid solutions. When evaluated on Maze, Chess, and masked Sudoku with multiple RRMs, including Deep Thinking Systems and Tiny Recursive Models, DART improves solution quality, stability, and robustness under the evaluated distribution shifts. Comparisons with label smoothing, Gaussian softened targets, and progressive training show that DART is not explained by target softening alone and is complementary to training schemes that stabilize long-horizon recurrence. These results identify DART as a promising approach for improving robustness across the evaluated recurrent reasoning models.

</details>


### [201] [Memory in Deep Time-Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06006)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Manh Nguyen,Van Dai Do,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"模型如何在即时输入之外保留和访问信息"这一共同视角审视时间序列深度学习的各种架构范式（循环网络、Transformer、SSM、检索增强、基础模型、智能体），将时间序列建模中的信息保留问题形式化为"记忆"问题，并提出一个统一分类框架，将现有方法沿着从内部记忆到外部记忆的光谱进行组织，识别研究缺口并展望开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列建模存在根本性局限：与预测相关的信息可能远超可行的输入窗口，而将历史压缩为固定大小的状态又可能丢弃未来才变得有用的信息。现有的各类时间序列架构（RNN、Transformer、SSM等）通常被孤立地按架构或建模时代研究，缺乏统一视角。作者认为这些问题本质上都可以归结为同一个核心问题——模型如何保留并访问即时输入之外的信息，即"记忆"问题。

Method: 提出将时间序列记忆机制组织在一条从内部记忆（编码于参数和固定大小状态中）到外部记忆（可寻址、可检索、甚至由智能体维护）的光谱上；构建统一的记忆机制分类体系，从"保留什么、如何写入和访问、如何持久化"三个维度审视三类外部记忆：显式模块、检索增强和智能体存储；并通过跨切面分析将这些机制映射到时间序列任务上。

Result: 建立了一个独立于底层骨干网络的、以记忆作为时间序列建模第一维度（first-class dimension）的研究框架；通过跨切面分析识别出当前方法学和评估体系中的空白；得到统一的三类外部记忆分类（显式模块、检索增强、智能体存储）及其共同框架下的描述。

Conclusion: 未来开放问题在于构建能够随时间环境演化而选择性保留、检索、修订和遗忘信息的记忆系统。时间序列建模应当从架构之争转向以记忆为核心的系统设计视角，使记忆成为与骨干网络无关的研究维度。

Abstract: Deep learning for time series has progressed through successive architectural paradigms, from recurrent networks and transformers to structured state-space models, retrieval-augmented predictors, foundation models, and tool-using agents. These developments are typically studied in isolation, organized by architecture or modeling era. We argue that they can instead be viewed through a common question of \emph{how does a time-series model retain and access information beyond its immediate input?} This question is motivated by a fundamental limitation of conventional time-series modeling: information relevant to a prediction may lie far beyond a feasible input window, while compressing history into a fixed-size state can discard information that may become useful later. We formulate this challenge as a \emph{memory} problem and organize existing time-series methods along a spectrum from internal memory, encoded in parameters and fixed-size states, to external memory that is addressable, retrievable, and increasingly maintained by agents. We then develop a unified taxonomy of memory mechanisms and review three classes of external memory, including explicit modules, retrieval augmentation, and agentic stores, under a common framework for what is retained, how it is written and accessed, and how it persists. A cross-cutting analysis maps these mechanisms to time-series tasks and identifies gaps in both methods and evaluation. We conclude by outlining open problems in building memory systems that can selectively retain, retrieve, revise, and forget information as temporal environments evolve. The result is a framework for studying memory as a first-class dimension of time series modeling, independent of the underlying backbone.

</details>


### [202] [Granular-Ball Quantum Clustering for Resource-Efficient and Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06016)
*Suzhen Yuan,Qilin Xie,Lifeng Shen,Shuyin Xia,Jermiah D. Deng,Guoying Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出粒球量子聚类（GBQC）框架，通过PCA引导的粒球结构抽象与量子特征学习紧密耦合，将核评估计算量减少80%，并利用量子内聚机制在希尔伯特空间中过滤噪声粒球以提升聚类鲁棒性，在合成、噪声、重叠和真实数据集上均优于代表性经典与量子聚类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子聚类利用量子特征表示挖掘超越欧几里得几何的复杂数据结构，但样本级核构建需要O(n²)次量子电路执行，在近期量子资源约束下成为主要瓶颈。现有方案无法解决效率-准确性两难困境：经典粒球聚类降低样本复杂度但依赖无法捕捉量子关联的欧氏度量；现有量子压缩方案则重效率轻结构保持，在非凸或噪声数据上性能退化。

Method: GBQC首先通过PCA引导的分裂策略将原始数据压缩为紧凑、有代表性的粒球，使核评估减少80%；随后通过量子内聚机制在希尔伯特空间过滤噪声粒球以增强聚类鲁棒性；最终在参数化量子学习框架中执行聚类任务。

Result: 在合成、噪声、重叠和真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，GBQC在聚类准确性和鲁棒性方面持续优于代表性经典与量子聚类方法；粒球压缩显著降低了量子核评估次数和计算开销，使量子聚类实验可在更大数据集上运行。

Conclusion: 粒球表示不仅可作为减少量子计算成本的压缩机制，还可作为有效的结构抽象机制，通过消除冗余和结构模糊的学习单元来提升聚类质量。

Abstract: Quantum clustering aims to exploit quantum feature representations to uncover complex data structures beyond conventional Euclidean geometry. Yet this sample-level kernel construction requires O(n^2) quantum circuit executions for n data points, creating a major bottleneck under near-term quantum resource constraints. Prior solutions fail to resolve this efficiency-accuracy dilemma: classical granular-ball clustering reduces sample complexity but relies on Euclidean metrics that cannot capture quantum correlations, while existing quantum compression schemes prioritize efficiency over structural preservation, degrading performance on non-convex or noisy data. Here we propose Granular-Ball Quantum Clustering (GBQC), a framework that tightly couples granular-ball structural abstraction with quantum feature learning. GBQC first compresses raw data into compact, representative granular balls via a PCA-guided splitting strategy, reducing kernel evaluations by 80% compared to full-sample methods. A quantum cohesion mechanism then filters noisy granules in Hilbert space to improve clustering robustness. Extensive experiments on synthetic, noisy, overlapping, and real-world datasets demonstrate that GBQC consistently achieves superior clustering accuracy and robustness compared with representative classical and quantum clustering methods. Meanwhile, the proposed granular-ball compression significantly reduces quantum kernel evaluations and computational overhead, enabling quantum clustering experiments on larger datasets within parameterized quantum learning frameworks. These results suggest that granular-ball representations serve not only as a compression mechanism to reduce quantum computational costs but also as an effective structural abstraction mechanism that improves clustering quality by eliminating redundant and structurally ambiguous learning units.

</details>


### [203] [IXPLORE: Bounded Ideal Point Estimation with Grid-Based Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06018)
*Fynn Bachmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IXPLORE是一种有界理想点估计算法，结合预测性拟合目标与稀疏感知似然函数，在五项基准数据集（涵盖调查、唱名投票和审议）上超越基于模型和基于机器学习的算法，尤其在稀疏响应用户场景下表现优异，并提供网格化贝叶斯后验推断以量化不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想点估计广泛应用于政治数据的分析和可视化，但空间模型的选择面临权衡：基于模型的方法（如IRT）依赖效用函数而非优化预测精度，而大多数机器学习替代方案在嵌入稀疏测试响应时难以泛化到训练数据之外。

Method: 提出IXPLORE算法，采用有界理想点估计，结合预测性拟合目标与稀疏感知似然函数；利用非线性特征变换进一步降低重构误差并保持视觉可解释性；在二维有界潜空间上应用基于网格的后验推断来量化不确定性。

Result: 在涵盖调查、唱名投票和审议的五项基准数据集上，IXPLORE在重构误差和插补误差方面均超越基于模型和基于机器学习的算法，尤其在稀疏响应用户场景中优势明显。非线性特征变换可进一步降低重构误差同时保持可解释性。

Conclusion: IXPLORE作为灵活的Python框架（PyPI发布），提供了有界且可解释的政治图谱构建方案，具备快速推断和强插补性能，是理想点估计领域的一种有效替代方法。

Abstract: Ideal point estimation is widely used to analyze and visualize political data. However, selecting the corresponding spatial model involves various trade-offs: while model-based approaches such as Item Response Theory (IRT) are based on utility functions rather than optimized for predictive accuracy, most Machine Learning (ML) alternatives struggle to generalize beyond training data when embedding sparse test responses. We introduce IXPLORE, a bounded ideal point estimation algorithm that combines a predictive fit objective with a sparsity-aware likelihood function. On five benchmark datasets spanning surveys, roll calls, and deliberation, this approach surpasses model-based and ML-based algorithms on reconstruction and imputation error - especially for users with sparse responses. Furthermore, we show that non-linear feature transforms can further reduce the reconstruction error while remaining visually interpretable. To quantify uncertainty, IXPLORE applies grid-based posterior inference on a bounded 2D latent space. Available as a Python package on PyPI, IXPLORE offers a flexible framework for constructing bounded, interpretable political maps with fast inference and strong imputation performance.

</details>


### [204] [Minimizing the Effect of Sleep Deprivation in the Forward-Forward Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06042)
*Joy Datta,Puja Saha,Rawhatur Rabbi,Nafiz Imtiaz Rafin,Swakkhar Shatabda,Md. Golam Rabiul Alam,Chad Mourning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Forward-Forward算法中睡眠剥夺问题提出多种缓解策略（替代激活、优化损失函数、阈值调整），通过模拟周期性休息和咖啡因刺激，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST数据集上实现严重睡眠剥夺条件下2%-62%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，在Forward-Forward算法中将两个pass分离并使其数据处理不平衡（模拟人类睡眠剥夺认知过程）会严重损害学习效率，本文旨在缓解这种灾难性影响。

Method: 采用三种技术途径：替代激活函数、优化损失函数和阈值调整；通过交替epoch中减少正向pass数量来模拟周期性休息阶段；此外探索咖啡因诱导刺激以提升睡眠剥夺条件下的性能表现。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST数据集上的实验表明，这些修改在睡眠剥夺情境下显著提升准确率，在严重睡眠剥夺设置（16个正向/清醒时期与1个负向/睡眠时期）中获得2%-62%的准确率增益。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅提升了算法在睡眠剥夺条件下的鲁棒性，还使其与人类认知的适应性机制更加对齐。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge posed by sleep deprivation in the Forward-Forward algorithm, where separating the two passes in this algorithm and imbalancing the data processing in the passes is considered an imitation of the cognitive processes observed in humans suffering from sleep deprivation. Previous research has demonstrated that sleep deprivation in the Forward-Forward algorithm has a catastrophic effect on learning efficacy. To mitigate this issue, we explore several approaches; these include alternative activation, optimized loss function, and threshold tuning. To simulate periodic rest, we reduce the number of positive passes in alternating epochs, creating short break phases. We additionally investigate the potential of caffeine-induced stimulation to enhance performance during sleep-deprived conditions. Experimental evaluations conducted on the MNIST and Fashion-MNIST datasets demonstrate that these modifications improve accuracy under the context of sleep deprivation. For example, a 2%-62% accuracy gain is observed in a severe sleep deprivation setting (16 positive or awake periods and 1 negative or sleep period). The approaches also enhance the resilience of the algorithm and its alignment with the adaptive mechanisms of human cognition.

</details>


### [205] [Data Quality Rule Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06053)
*Anna-Christina Glock,Thomas Hütter,Johannes Fürnkranz,Wolfram Wöß,Christine Dominka-Kiss,Lisa Ehrlinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LeDQeR，一种基于大语言模型的自动化数据质量规则生成方法。该方法通过"生成-过滤"框架，先使用LLM从脏数据元组生成候选规则，再通过四个过滤器确保规则的可执行性、正确性、泛化性和非冗余性，最终生成有效且紧凑的规则集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据验证（如客户和员工数据）在许多组织中至关重要，数据错误可能带来严重后果（如用药单位错误导致生命威胁、地址缺省导致投递失败）。企业使用基于规则的数据质量工具，但手工维护全面的规则集具有挑战性，领域专家往往会遗漏重要规则，尤其是在复杂领域和大数据量场景下，这一问题仍是实践中的开放问题。

Method: LeDQeR采用两阶段框架：(1) 生成阶段：利用大语言模型从观察到的脏数据元组中，针对给定的规则式DQ工具语法生成候选规则；(2) 过滤阶段：应用四种过滤技术分别确保候选规则的(i)可执行性、(ii)正确性、以及(iii)泛化性，并避免(iv)冗余性。

Result: 大量实验评估表明，LeDQeR能够在多种数据集和错误类型上生成有效且紧凑的规则集。

Conclusion: 自动化数据质量规则生成是可实现的，LLM结合系统化的过滤机制能够弥补人工规则维护的不足，为领域专家提供可解释且可执行的DQ规则，从而解决传统规则覆盖不全的问题。

Abstract: The validation of data, such as customer and employee data, is an important task in many organizations. Errors in data can have severe consequences. For example, a wrong drug unit in a patient record can lead to life-threatening medication errors, and a missing street number in an address to failed deliveries. Companies often employ rule-based enterprise data quality (DQ) tools, which allow domain experts to specify rules to validate the data over time. While rule-based DQ tools are computationally efficient and provide explainable reports, maintaining a comprehensive rule set manually is challenging, as domain experts often overlook essential rules, especially in complex domains and large data volumes. Hence, closing these gaps remains an open problem in practice. In this paper, we address the challenge of automated DQ rule generation. For this, we formalize a generalizable generate-filter framework and introduce LeDQeR, an LLM-based DQ rule generation approach. First, a large language model (LLM) generates candidates rules from an observed dirty data tuple for a given rule-based DQ tool syntax. Second, we apply four filter techniques that ensure the (i) executability, (ii) correctness, and (iii) generalizability, and avoid (iv) redundancy of the generated rules. An extensive experimental evaluation suggests that LeDQeR is able to produce effective and compact rule sets for various datasets and error types.

</details>


### [206] [Calendar-SPCA: Interpretable Representation Learning for Multi-Periodic Electricity Consumption Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06060)
*Carlos Quesada-Granja,Tony Castillo-Calzadilla,Carlos Rizo-Maestre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Calendar-SPCA，一种日历结构化的稀疏主成分分析方法，将电力消耗的多周期（日、周、年）日历几何结构直接纳入低维表示学习中，通过L1载荷惩罚与日历图上的全变差正则化，产出稀疏且局部连贯的载荷模式。在两个智能电表数据集上验证，其在保持高解释方差的同时实现显著的载荷稀疏性，并生成与日历坐标对齐的连贯且互补的日/周/季节模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期电力消费曲线呈现日、周、年等多种同步周期结构，但传统主成分分析及稀疏PCA方法忽视了这种已知的多周期领域知识，导致学习到的低维表示无法在原始时间坐标上保持结构可读性与局部连贯性。作者希望将日历几何结构直接嵌入表示学习方法，以提升载荷模式的可解释性与临床/实际可用性。

Method: 将特征域表示为循环日历轴的笛卡尔积，构造相应的日历图；在低秩因子分解中引入L1载荷稀疏惩罚与日历图上的全变差正则化，联合优化以得到稀疏且局部平滑的载荷模式。通过全因子实验系统表征稀疏性与日历一致性两个正则项的互补效应，并考察样本量、潜在维度及重复拟合的稳健性。在GoiEner和Low Carbon London两个独立智能电表数据集上进行评估与对比（经典稀疏PCA及SPCA-TV）。

Result: 在秩15时，Calendar-SPCA在GoiEner和Low Carbon London上分别保留了秩匹配PCA解释方差的96.92%和82.90%，同时平均载荷稀疏度达61.95%和81.50%。与经典稀疏PCA及SPCA-TV相比，Calendar-SPCA在保留大量低秩信息的同时，为潜在因子提供了原始日历坐标下的系统性组织。生成的成分呈现连贯互补的日、周、季节及联合局部化的日历模式，且两个数据集呈现各自特有的几何结构。

Conclusion: Calendar-SPCA成功地将多周期日历先验嵌入稀疏主成分学习，在解释方差与载荷稀疏性之间取得了有利平衡，同时显著提升了成分在原始时间坐标中的可读性与结构化组织能力，为智能电表长时序消费数据的低维表征与模式发现提供了一种领域感知的有效方法。

Abstract: Long-term electricity-consumption profiles exhibit several simultaneous periodic structures, including daily, weekly, and annual cycles. This work introduces Calendar-SPCA, a calendar-structured sparse principal component method that incorporates this known multi-periodic geometry directly into low-dimensional representation learning. The feature domain is represented as the Cartesian product of cyclic calendar axes, and a low-rank factorization is estimated using an L1 loading penalty together with graph total variation over the resulting calendar graph. The method therefore produces sparse and locally coherent loading patterns that remain directly readable in their original temporal coordinates. Calendar-SPCA is evaluated on two independent smart-meter datasets with different sample sizes and temporal resolutions: GoiEner and Low Carbon London. A factorial experiment characterizes the complementary effects of sparsity and calendar coherence and examines robustness across sample size, latent dimensionality, and repeated fits. At rank 15, Calendar-SPCA retains 96.92% and 82.90% of the explained variance of rank-matched PCA in GoiEner and Low Carbon London, respectively, while producing mean loading sparsities of 61.95% and 81.50%. Comparisons with classical sparse PCA and SPCA-TV further show that Calendar-SPCA adds a systematic organization of the latent factors in the original calendar coordinates while preserving substantial low-rank information. The resulting components form coherent and complementary daily, weekly, seasonal, and jointly localized calendar patterns, with dataset-specific geometries across the two datasets.

</details>


### [207] [ACE: Adapter Consolidation across Experts for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06072)
*Ahin Lee,Sehyun Yun,Joonha Park,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE（Adapter Consolidation across Experts），一种针对混合专家（MoE）模型的参数高效微调方法。ACE发现专家特定LoRA适配器在微调过程中存在功能冗余和退化，因此将冗余专家分组并用组共享的高阶LoRA模块替代专家单独的低秩适配器，同时引入分组执行将碎片化的专家级计算合并为更少更大的GEMM。在12个数据集和4个MoE骨干模型上，ACE在参数匹配的PEFT方法中取得最高平均精度，并较专家级LoRA获得1.31x-1.48x训练加速且不增加峰值内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE模型PEFT方法为每个专家附加独立的低秩适配器，但这种专家级设计导致三方面碎片化问题：一是适配容量被分割成狭窄的低秩更新；二是稀疏路由下梯度监督变得稀疏且不平衡；三是执行被分解为大量小型GEMM。作者发现这种专家级分离往往是不必要的，因为微调过程中部分LoRA适配器会变得功能相似，存在冗余。

Method: ACE方法包含两个核心步骤：1) 识别并分组功能冗余的专家，将组内专家各自的低秩适配器替换为组共享的高阶LoRA模块（总参数量与分组前保持一致，即在同一PEFT预算下）；2) 引入分组适配器执行机制，将碎片化的专家级适配器计算合并为更少、更大的组级GEMM，从而提升计算效率。

Result: 在12个数据集和4个MoE骨干模型上的评估中，ACE在3个具有完整基线覆盖的骨干模型上，于所有参数匹配的PEFT方法中取得最高平均精度。相比专家级LoRA，ACE提供1.31x至1.48x的wall-clock训练加速，且不增加峰值内存开销。

Conclusion: ACE通过揭示并利用专家适配器之间的功能冗余，证明了在MoE模型PEFT中专家级分离设计并非必要条件。通过将冗余专家适配器合并为组共享高阶LoRA模块并采用分组执行，ACE在同等参数预算下实现了更高的精度和显著更快的训练速度，为MoE模型的PEFT提供了更高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of mixture-of-experts (MoE) models commonly attaches a separate low-rank adapter to each expert. This expert-wise design fragments adaptation in three ways: capacity is split across narrow low-rank updates, gradient supervision becomes sparse and imbalanced under sparse routing, and execution is decomposed into many small GEMMs. We find that such expert-wise separation is often unnecessary, as subsets of LoRA adapters become functionally similar during fine-tuning, revealing redundancy among expert-specific adapters. Based on this redundancy, we propose ACE (Adapter Consolidation across Experts), which groups redundant experts and replaces their expert-specific adapters with group-shared higher-rank LoRA modules under the same PEFT budget. ACE further introduces grouped adapter execution, which consolidates fragmented expert-wise adapter computations into fewer, larger group-level GEMMs. Across evaluations covering 12 datasets and four MoE backbones, ACE achieves the highest observed mean accuracy among the parameter-matched PEFT methods on the three backbones with complete baseline coverage, while providing $1.31\times$ to $1.48\times$ wall-clock training speedup over expert-wise LoRA without increasing peak memory. Our code is available at https://github.com/UbiquitousAILab/ACE.

</details>


### [208] [FedSubMuon: Communication-Efficient Federated LLM Fine-Tuning via Structured Subspace Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06073)
*Shaolong Chen,Youming Tao,Shuzhen Chen,Falko Dressler,Qingqing Ye,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FedSubMuon和FedSubMuon-GT，一种通信高效的联邦Muon微调方法，通过在共享结构化子空间中优化紧凑系数矩阵，保持Muon的矩阵感知更新几何的同时减少通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调大语言模型受限于跨设备训练的高通信成本。已有的联邦Muon方法虽展示了矩阵感知优化的优势，但仍需传输完整层大小的更新和优化器状态。Muon直接应用于LoRA因子会改变优化目标并削弱其矩阵感知更新几何。

Method: FedSubMuon在共享结构化子空间中优化紧凑系数矩阵，保持Muon作用于单一矩阵值可训练对象；FedSubMuon-GT进一步使用投影梯度自适应跟踪任务相关梯度方向的子空间基。

Result: 在指令微调和数学推理实验上，FedSubMuon-GT在五组数据集-模型配对中的四组取得最佳整体准确率，FedSubMuon在所有匹配通信预算下表现最佳。在Dolly-15K上，最接近的通信基线在Llama-1B和Qwen-4B上分别需要5.5倍和1.4倍的额外总通信量。

Conclusion: FedSubMuon系列方法有效解决了联邦Muon微调中的通信瓶颈，通过子空间压缩同时保持了矩阵感知优化的优势，实现了通信效率与准确率的双重提升。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning adapts large language models (LLMs) to decentralized client data, but its scalability in cross-device training is often limited by the high communication cost. Muon is an optimizer that improves optimization performance by orthogonalizing momentum for matrix-valued parameters. Existing federated Muon methods demonstrate the benefit of matrix-aware optimization in federated learning, but still require transmitting full layer-size updates and optimizer state. A natural way to reduce communication is to directly apply Muon to LoRA factors, but this changes the optimized object and weakens Muon's matrix-aware update geometry. We propose FedSubMuon, a communication-efficient federated Muon fine-tuning method that optimizes compact coefficient matrices within shared structured subspaces. This design keeps Muon on a single matrix-valued trainable object, while reducing the client upload to compact coefficient matrices. We further introduce FedSubMuon-GT, an accuracy-oriented extension that uses projected gradients to adapt tracked subspace bases toward task-relevant gradient directions. Experiments on instruction tuning and mathematical reasoning show that FedSubMuon-GT achieves the best overall accuracy on four of five dataset-model pairs, while FedSubMuon performs best under all matched communication budgets. On Dolly-15K, the closest communication baseline requires 5.5 times and 1.4 times more total communication on Llama-1B and Qwen-4B, respectively.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Retraining-Free MoE Compression: A Cost-Normalized Study of Post-Compression Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06076)
*Sieun Hyeon,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 压缩后的MoE检查点应被视为"压缩初始化"而非最终产物，通过极小的后压缩微调阶段（仅3000个C4样本、单轮epoch）即可显著恢复丢失的性能。实验跨越两个MoE骨干模型、四种剪枝/合并方法、三个保留比率和28个基准，发现全参数LM微调最具成本效益，平均恢复了原始与压缩之间性能差距的37.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免重训练的MoE压缩方法将压缩后的检查点视为最终产物，忽略了这些检查点本质上可被当作"压缩初始化"，通过微小的后压缩调整阶段就能恢复大量性能，而这一潜力尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在两个MoE LLM骨干网络上，结合四种剪枝/合并方法与三种专家保留比率，在28个基准上比较LM微调与基于教师的KD，在匹配的小数据预算和GPU成本条件下，评估全参数和部分参数调整范围的后压缩微调效果。

Result: 仅用3000个C4样本和单轮epoch调整，全参数微调平均恢复原始与压缩性能差距的37.3%；LM微调比标准token级KD更具成本效益；在测试的调整范围中，全参数调整提供了最强的成本-恢复权衡。

Conclusion: 免重训练压缩应与小规模的后压缩调整相结合，以恢复压缩过程中丢失的相当一部分性能，表明压缩检查点应被视为初始状态而非最终部署模型。

Abstract: Retraining-free MoE compression reduces deployment memory by pruning or merging experts, but often treats the compressed checkpoint as the final artifact. We argue that this view is incomplete: compressed MoE checkpoints are better understood as compressed initializations that benefit from a tiny post-compression adjustment stage. Across two MoE LLM backbones, four pruning/merging methods, three expert-retention ratios, and 28 benchmarks, we compare LM fine-tuning and teacher-based KD under matched small-data budgets and measured GPU costs. Using only 3,000 C4 examples and a single epoch of adjustment, Full FT recovers 37.3% of the original-to-compressed performance gap on average. Moreover, LM fine-tuning is more cost-effective than standard token-level KD, and full-parameter adjustment gives the strongest cost--recovery trade-off among the tested scopes. These results suggest that retraining-free compression should be paired with small post-compression adjustment to recover a substantial portion of the performance lost during compression.

</details>


### [210] [PhenoBench: Mapping What a Deeply Phenotyped Human Cohort Can Tell Us](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06080)
*Gal Sapir,Alon Diament,Adva Wolf,Doron Yaya-Stupp,Dikla Gelbard Solodkin,Dana Azouri,Anat Etzion-Fuchs,Guy Lutsker,Eran Segal,Hagai Rossman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhenoBench是一个可执行的基准测试框架，基于包含13,000+参与者的Human Phenotype Project深度表型队列，定义了90个跨15个领域和26种输入模态的临床任务，用于系统评估多模态纵向数据在不同健康问题上的预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度表型队列整合了临床、影像、分子和可穿戴设备等多尺度数据，但异构分析之间缺乏直接可比性，难以确定哪些测量对哪些健康问题具有预测价值，需要一个标准化的可执行基准框架。

Method: PhenoBench通过为每个问题固定目标、合格人群、时间点和允许信息，并定义包含数据划分、指标、基线和声明边界的评估契约，构建了90个临床任务。使用该框架比较了三种新兴表格基础模型（160个匹配回归比较，52个任务）和14种语言模型（40个任务）。

Result: 测量显示出问题依赖和表征依赖的预测价值（正向、接近零和负向变化）。表格基础模型在总体上优于标准任务特定模型，但相对岭回归的中位改进仅为0.004 R²（95% CI, 0.002–0.006）。语言模型在部分任务上无需队列特定拟合即可做出有信息量的预测，但存在任务特定能力缺口和规模性失败，很少超越基于相同字段拟合的模型。

Conclusion: PhenoBench将多模态纵向队列转化为一个版本化、可审计的评估系统，允许在不重新定义现有比较的情况下添加新问题、测量和模型，为深度表型数据的健康相关问题提供标准化的模型评估基础设施。

Abstract: Deeply phenotyped cohorts combine clinical, imaging, molecular, and wearable observations across timescales from seconds to years. This breadth can reveal which measurements inform which health-related questions, but heterogeneous analyses are not directly comparable. We present PhenoBench, an executable benchmark built around the Human Phenotype Project, in which more than 13,000 participants have completed the initial visit. Each question fixes the target, eligible population, timing, and allowed information; its evaluation contract specifies the split, metric, baseline, and claim boundary. The benchmark defines 90 clinically grounded tasks across 15 domains and 26 input modalities. Measurements showed question- and representation-dependent predictive value, including positive, near-zero, and negative changes in held-out performance relative to matched baselines. We used PhenoBench to evaluate emerging tabular foundation models across 160 matched regression comparisons spanning 52 tasks. These models ranked above standard task-specific models in aggregate but, averaged across the three pretrained models within each cell, improved on ridge by a median of only 0.004 $R^2$ (95% CI, 0.002--0.006). We then used the same cohort data and evaluation contracts to evaluate 14 language models, collectively covering 40 tasks spanning phenotype recovery, classification, follow-up forecasting, and participant ordering. Without cohort-specific fitting, language models made informative predictions on some tasks, but showed task-specific capability gaps, shared failures of scale, and rarely surpassed models fitted on the same fields. PhenoBench turns a multimodal longitudinal cohort into a versioned, auditable evaluation system where new questions, measurements, and models can be added without redefining existing comparisons.

</details>


### [211] [Learning to Price and Stock Under Contextual and Censored Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06083)
*Zean Han,Zezhen Ding,Jiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向现代零售商的联合定价与库存控制框架，将需求建模为基函数的线性组合（含未知系数），同时处理情境因素和丢失销售（需求截断观测）问题。提出的算法在凹收益条件下达到O(K√T log T)的遗憾界，一般情形下达到O(K^{2/3}T^{2/3}(log T)^{1/2})，并给出匹配下界证明最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售实践中需求受多种情境因素影响且存在丢失销售导致需求观测被截断的问题，而现有方法未能同时考虑情境信息与截断的需求观测，存在研究空白。

Method: 将需求建模为基函数线性组合（未知系数），在情境变化下自适应地作出定价与库存决策；设计了高效算法求解该联合决策问题。

Result: 凹收益条件下遗憾界为O(K√T log T)；一般情形下为O(K^{2/3}T^{2/3}(log T)^{1/2})；提供匹配的遗憾下界，证明算法达到最优；多场景数值实验验证算法有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架首次同时纳入情境信息和截断需求观测，算法在理论上达到最优遗憾界，并通过数值实验验证其在实际多样场景中的有效性。

Abstract: To make optimal joint pricing and inventory control decisions is a critical challenge for modern retailers. In practice, retailers face changing market conditions where demands are influenced by various contextual factors, while simultaneously dealing with the difficulty of lost sales that obscure true demand information. However, existing approaches often fail to account for both contextual information and censored demand observations. We address this gap by presenting a framework where we model demand as a linear combination of basis functions with unknown coefficients, allowing for adaptive pricing and inventory decisions that respond to changing contexts. We propose an efficient algorithm to achieve regret bound $\mathcal{O}(K\sqrt{T}\log T)$ under concave revenue conditions and $\mathcal{O}(K^{2/3}T^{2/3}(\log T)^{1/2})$ for the general case, with matching lower bounds confirming optimality. Extensive numerical experiments across diverse scenarios demonstrate our algorithm's effectiveness.

</details>


### [212] [Connectome-to-Function: Conditional Generative Latent Representations for Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06093)
*Zhuolin Yu,Xingyu Liu,Yuanhao Jia,Yunhang Xiao,Hairuo Xue,Feihan Sun,Guozhang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种条件生成式潜变量框架，将神经元连接组图谱编码为紧凑的结构空间，在给定节点级条件下实现连接的重建与生成，并通过水库计算实验发现潜在空间能够捕获功能差异，揭示了记忆、预测和分类任务中不同的结构机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组图谱提供了理解大脑回路结构与计算功能的基础，但这类图是高维、稀疏且对局部结构变化敏感的。现有方法依赖手工设计的结构描述符或任务特定预测器，难以同时做到生成性（generative）与功能意义（functionally meaningful）的表征，因此缺乏一个统一的框架来连接结构与计算。

Method: 提出一个条件生成式潜变量框架：首先将连接组图编码为紧凑的结构潜空间，并利用可用的节点级条件信息（如神经元属性）指导图的（重）构建与生成。从潜空间可以重建观测连接（平均边重建AUC达0.910）并生成新的候选连接组。随后将连接组派生的图用作水库计算（reservoir computing）的循环计算基底，以评估潜在空间是否能捕获功能变异。

Result: 在重建任务上达到平均边重建AUC最高0.910；在功能分析中，学习到的潜空间能捕获水库计算实验中的功能变异，交叉验证R²值最高约0.87。可解释性分析揭示任务特异的结构机制：记忆性能与相互循环连接度相关，预测和分类任务则更依赖于循环网络的谱性质。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个将神经连接结构与计算能力联系起来的AI-for-science方法，提供了一种具有生成性和可解释性的基础，用于研究不同结构机制如何塑造计算容量，为连接组—计算映射提供了统一的分析框架。

Abstract: Connectomes, graph-level maps of neurons and their synaptic connections, provide a structural basis for understanding how brain circuits support function and computation. However, mapping connectome structure to computation remains difficult because these graphs are high-dimensional, sparse, and sensitive to local structural variation. Existing approaches often depend on hand-crafted structural descriptors or task-specific predictors, which limits their ability to represent connectomes in a form that is both generative and functionally meaningful. We propose a conditional generative latent framework that encodes connectome graphs into a compact structural space while using available node-level conditions to guide reconstruction and generation. From this space, the model can reconstruct observed connectivity with a mean edge-reconstruction AUC up to 0.910 and generate new candidate connectomes, enabling a unified analysis of graph structure and computational behavior. Using connectome-derived graphs as recurrent computational substrates, we found that the learned latent space captures functional variation across reservoir-computing experiments, with cross-validated $R^2$ values up to approximately 0.87. Interpretability analysis further revealed task-specific structural mechanisms: in our examples, memory performance is associated with reciprocal recurrent connectivity, whereas prediction and classification are more strongly associated with spectral properties of the recurrent network. These findings suggest an AI-for-science approach to linking neural connectivity to computation and provide a generative and interpretable basis for studying how distinct structural mechanisms shape computational capacity.

</details>


### [213] [VERPO: Verified Evidence Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06100)
*Haijiang Li,Chengyu Lv,Yi Zhang,Zhibing Zhang,Rui Qian,Yuchen Zhang,Xiaofan Zhang,Mingshan Wang,Xiaofei Jing,Yu Tong,Cangqi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERPO是一种验证证据正则化策略优化框架，将证据视为策略修正的建议而非不加区分的模仿目标，在保留结果目标的同时，通过Fisher证据对比和分步ZPD控制器实现细粒度的token级监督，在五个科学推理和工具使用任务上超越了最强的对比基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列级别的可验证结果奖励无法指出哪些token级决策应保留或修改。已有方法如证据条件教师提供稠密监督，但盲目模仿证据可能会引入与任务成功无关的格式或推理风格偏移，导致从结果中恢复正确的结构化推理存在困难。

Method: VERPO将证据作为策略修正的提议而非复制的目标。核心组件包括：(1) 无证据的参考恢复通道与带符号的token级证据修正通道分离；(2) Fisher证据对比机制沿证据存在方向衰减修正幅度；(3) 停止梯度的token级ZPD控制器，根据局部奖励对齐度和Fisher移动代价调整接受度，参考通道不依赖接受度控制。

Result: 在五个科学推理和工具使用任务上，每个骨干网络上最佳变体的平均得分超过最强的对比基线：Qwen3-4B从0.6826提升至0.6857，Qwen3-8B从0.6895提升至0.7058，Llama-3.2-1B从0.4751提升至0.5657。

Conclusion: VERPO表明，将证据视为有筛选的、经过正则化的修正建议，而非无条件模仿的对象，能够更有效地利用稠密证据监督，在保证结果目标的同时提高策略性能，尤其在小模型（如1B规模）上带来显著提升。

Abstract: Verifiable outcome rewards guide language-model post-training, but sequence-level advantages do not identify which token-level decisions should be preserved or revised. Evidence-conditioned Teachers provide denser supervision by replaying sampled trajectories with privileged feedback. Yet indiscriminate imitation risks transferring formatting or reasoning-style shifts that do not support task success. We introduce VERPO, a Verified Evidence Regularized Policy Optimization framework that treats evidence as a proposal for policy correction while retaining the outcome objective. It separates evidence-free reference restoration from signed token-level evidence corrections. Fisher Evidence Contrast attenuates corrections along an estimated evidence-presence direction. A stopped token-wise ZPD controller scales acceptance according to local reward alignment and Fisher movement cost, while the reference channel remains independent of acceptance. Across five scientific-reasoning and tool-use tasks, the best variant on each backbone exceeds the strongest compared baseline in average score. The averages rise from 0.6826 to 0.6857 on Qwen3-4B, from 0.6895 to 0.7058 on Qwen3-8B, and from 0.4751 to 0.5657 on Llama-3.2-1B.

</details>


### [214] [FANS: Federated Adaptive Network Search Learning for Heterogeneous Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06106)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Xingwei Wang,Bo Yi,Liang Zhao,Alireza Furutanpey,Ziyi Chen,Qiang He,Keqin Li,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超网络（Hypernetwork）的异构联邦学习（HFL）框架FANS，通过学习共享的架构搜索空间而非固定客户端模型集合，配合联邦并行缩放（FPS）算法，利用自蒸馏实现多子网络并行联合训练，从而大幅扩展可搜索子网络池并改善精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HFL方法通常将训练绑定在少量预定义模型配置上，导致模型架构覆盖范围有限，难以适应设备间多样化的资源预算差异。

Method: 提出FANS超网络框架，学习一个共享的架构搜索空间以替代固定的客户端模型集合；提出FPS算法，联邦环境中并行采样多个子网络并联合训练，通过自蒸馏让较大子网络在本地更新中监督较小子网络。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和MNLI数据集上分别采用ResNet-18、DenseNet-121和BERT-base进行评估，FANS将可行子网络池扩大数个数量级（如ResNet-18场景下4,680个候选 vs. 现有方法的4个），并在平均精度-效率权衡上优于代表性HFL基线。通过资源层级模拟设备异构性，从精度、参数量和MACs等维度进行全面评估。

Conclusion: 通过学习共享架构空间而非固定模型集合，FANS有效突破传统HFL架构覆盖受限的瓶颈，在大幅扩展候选子网络规模的同时改善精度与效率的折中表现。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Federated Learning (HFL) aims to train models across devices with diverse resource budgets while preserving data privacy. Existing HFL methods typically bind training to a small predefined menu of model configurations, which limits architectural coverage. To address this bottleneck, we introduce Federated Adaptive Network Search (FANS), a hypernetwork-based framework that learns a shared architecture space rather than a fixed set of client models. To optimize this shared space efficiently, we propose the Federated Parallel Scaling (FPS) algorithm, which jointly trains multiple sampled subnetworks in parallel with self-distillation so that larger sampled subnetworks can supervise smaller ones during local updates. We evaluate FANS on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and MNLI using ResNet-18, DenseNet-121, and BERT-base, respectively. Across all benchmarks, FANS expands the feasible subnetwork pool by orders of magnitude (e.g., 4,680 candidates for ResNet-18 vs. 4 in existing methods) and improves the average accuracy-efficiency trade-off relative to representative HFL baselines. Device heterogeneity is emulated through resource tiers, and evaluation covers accuracy, parameter count, and MACs.

</details>


### [215] [DataFlex-RL: An Evaluation Platform for RLVR Data Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06107)
*Hao Liang,Mingrui Chen,Hengyi Feng,Meiyi Qiang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DataFlex-RL，一个用于比较RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）中数据策略的评估平台。在统一GRPO配方下，对13种配置、12个匹配种子、12个基准进行了评估，发现没有任何rollout选择/重加权方法或自适应混合策略能显著且可重复地超越均匀GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中的数据策略（如何选择和加权rollout、哪些领域贡献到后续批次）对训练过程和性能有潜在影响，但缺乏一个统一的评估平台来系统比较这些选择，导致领域内难以得出可靠的结论。

Method: 构建DataFlex-RL评估平台，在统一的GRPO训练配方下对多种数据策略进行受控比较。主实验使用Qwen2.5-7B-Base、12个数学/逻辑/科学基准、12个匹配种子，评估13种配置（包括rollout选择方法、重加权方法和自适应混合方法）；并扩展至Llama-3.1-8B-Base进行修正的12种子验证；同时通过不同的基准汇总方式量化评估敏感性。

Result: 均匀GRPO相对未训练基线的域均衡平均准确率提升7.76个百分点。8种rollout选择/重加权方法均未在配对95%置信区间内显著优于均匀采样；3种自适应混合方法也未能超过固定等比例混合。对Llama-3.1-8B-Base的扩展也未显示一致的优胜者。评估敏感性分析发现九次运行在数学为主的六基准与12基准汇总下的排名负相关（相关系数-0.33），而保留全部12基准的汇总具有较高一致性。

Conclusion: 在所研究的受控设置中，更改数据策略确实会可测量地改变训练过程，但未能产生可重复的、超越均匀训练的改进。这提示RLVR中更复杂的数据策略可能并非必要，均匀采样+GRPO仍是稳健的基线选择。

Abstract: Data policies for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) determine which rollouts are used, how strongly they are weighted, and which domains contribute to subsequent training batches. We introduce DataFlex-RL, an evaluation platform for comparing these choices under a common GRPO recipe. Our primary experiment evaluates 13 configurations across 12 matched seeds using Qwen2.5-7B-Base and 12 mathematics, logic, and science benchmarks.
