Uniform GRPO improves the domain-balanced average accuracy by 7.76 percentage points over the untrained checkpoint. None of the eight rollout-selection or reweighting methods achieves a paired 95% confidence interval that excludes zero relative to uniform sampling, and none of the three adaptive mixtures outperforms a fixed equal mixture at the same level of precision. A corrected 12-seed extension on Llama-3.1-8B-Base places the additional methods on the same score scale as the original controls, but does not reveal a consistent winner in terms of observed mean performance. We also quantify evaluation sensitivity by rescoring nine Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct runs using a math-heavy six-benchmark summary, consisting of five mathematics benchmarks and GPQA-Diamond but no logic benchmark, and comparing it with the domain-balanced 12-benchmark summary. The resulting rankings are negatively correlated, with a correlation coefficient of -0.33, whereas summaries that retain all 12 benchmarks largely agree. Across the controlled settings studied here, changing the data policy measurably changes the training process but does not produce a reproducible improvement over uniform training.

</details>


### [216] [Sparse Incident-Cluster Learning for 12-hour Port Flood Pre-warning in Digital-Twin Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06109)
*Jie Zhang,Qiang Ni,David Windridge,Huan X. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对港口洪水数字化孪生中的12小时预警问题，提出了一种基于事件簇学习的评估框架，解决了传统行级分类因同一事件时间窗同时进入训练与评估数据而导致性能高估的问题。以利物浦为四簇主要案例，结合弹性能网（ElasticNet）与XGBoost对比，top-10 ElasticNet达到平均F2=0.696，优于无top-k截断（0.633）及全特征加权XGBoost（0.681），并设计了包含特征选择、簇分组、负标签对照、随机top-k对照及告警时段评估的完整验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 港口洪水数字化孪生需要在破坏发生前向操作人员发出预警，然而官方预警事件数量稀少且相邻观测间存在时间依赖性，导致传统的行级分类因同一事件的数据窗同时出现在开发集和评估集中而高估模型性能。因此需要一种能正确处理事件内时间相关性的学习与评估框架。

Method: 将12小时港口洪水预警形式化为事件簇学习问题，设计数字孪生分析模块，使用八点水位历史、预测时点上下文协变量及可解释的短窗动态特征。评估协议包含折内稀疏特征选择、告警簇分组、负标签对照、100次重复随机top-k对照及告警时段评估；主要方法为十变量ElasticNet（经top-k截断），并与全特征加权XGBoost进行非线性基准对比；利物浦为主案例，辅以Humber/Hull代理及Wessex South数据做协议迁移检验。

Result: 利物浦各折中top-10 ElasticNet平均F2=0.696，高于无top-k截断时的0.633及全特征加权XGBoost的0.681。该模型为最强ElasticNet变体，仅用十个预测变量即可与非线性基准竞争，且超出100次重复随机broad与同族子集的水平第五百分位。上下文协变量提供强预测锚点，局部动力学提供了可物理解释的补充信号。预警时段回顾将风险分数转化为告警片段并度量告警时长与虚假片段负担。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个经过离线评估的分析与验证模块，可集成到港口数字孪生系统中，为洪水发生前约12小时提供可靠、可解释且经过严格统计验证的预警能力。

Abstract: Port flood digital twins require analytics that warn operators before disruption, but official warning incidents are often few and adjacent observations are temporally dependent. Row-level classification can therefore overstate performance by placing windows from the same event in both model-development and evaluation data. We formulate 12-hour port flood pre-warning as an incident-cluster learning problem and evaluate a digital-twin analytics module using eight-point water-level histories, prediction-time contextual covariates, and interpretable short-window dynamics. The protocol combines fold-specific sparse feature selection, warning-cluster grouping, negative-label controls, 100-repeat random top-k controls, and alert-episode evaluation. Liverpool is the primary four-cluster case study, with harmonised Humber/Hull-proxy and Wessex South data used for protocol-transfer checks. Across the Liverpool folds, the top-10 ElasticNet model achieves mean F2 = 0.696, compared with 0.633 without top-k truncation and 0.681 for full-feature weighted XGBoost. It is the strongest ElasticNet variant, remains competitive with the nonlinear reference using only ten predictors, and exceeds the repeat-level 95th percentile of broad and same-family random subsets. Contextual covariates provide a strong prediction-time anchor, complemented by physically interpretable local dynamics. Historical replay converts risk scores into alert episodes and measures alert duration and false-episode burden. The result is an offline-evaluated analytics and validation module designed for integration into a port digital twin.

</details>


### [217] [Compressed Recurrent Feedback in Tsetlin Machines: A Reproducible Boolean-FSM Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06133)
*Ankit Kumar,Utkarsh Raj,Rishad Shafik,Sudip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定宽度的递归Tsetlin机（RTM）变体，通过XOR折叠将480个子句激活压缩为96个递归位，并在两时间尺度上保留与阈值化。在布尔有限状态机基准上，该方法达到61.47%和62.94%的准确率，与原始子句反馈差距小于1个百分点，但递归宽度缩小10倍、执行时间降低约4倍。门控神经模型仍更准确，且无反馈对照在多数情况下性能相当甚至略优，凸显了序列模型基准测试中无反馈对照的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小设备上的顺序推理需要模型在不重复处理长输入记录的情况下保留有用历史。RTM通过将布尔子句输出作为下一时间步输入来提供记忆，但直接反馈随子句库规模增长，导致递归输入不必要地过宽。作者旨在缩小递归接口宽度，降低计算开销。

Method: 将子句激活通过异或（XOR）折叠进行组合，在两个时间尺度上保留折叠后的位，再经阈值化还原为二值状态。该设计将480个子句激活压缩为96个递归位。实验使用可复现的布尔有限状态机基准，包含明确的状态转移规则、数据划分和随机种子。

Result: 在144次运行中，压缩模型在两个任务族上分别获得61.47±6.74%和62.94±9.92%的准确率。原始子句反馈的均值变化小于1个百分点，但递归宽度扩大10倍，宿主执行时间增加4.38倍和3.71倍。门控神经模型仍更准确；无反馈控制（仅保留短期输入历史）达到相当或略高的准确率。

Conclusion: 在该基准上，折叠方法以更窄的递归接口与原始反馈在小经验误差范围内相当。此外，研究强调了在序列模型基准测试中加入无反馈递归对照的迫切必要性，否则可能高估递归机制本身的实际贡献。

Abstract: Sequential inference on small devices requires a model to retain useful history without repeatedly processing a long input record. A Recurrent Tsetlin Machine (RTM) provides this memory by returning Boolean clause outputs from one time step as inputs to the next. Direct feedback, however, grows with the clause bank and can make the recurrent input unnecessarily wide. This paper investigates a fixed-width alternative. We combine clause activations by exclusive-OR (XOR) folding, retain the folded bits at two time scales, and threshold them back to a binary state. The resulting design reduces 480 clause activations to 96 recurrent bits. We evaluate the method on a reproducible Boolean finite-state-machine benchmark with explicit transition rules, data splits, and random seeds. Across 144 runs, the compressed model obtains $61.47 \pm 6.74\%$ and $62.94 \pm 9.92\%$ accuracy on the two task families. Raw clause feedback changes these means by less than one percentage point, while increasing the recurrent width tenfold and measured host execution time by $4.38\times$ and $3.71\times$. Gated neural models remain more accurate, and a no-feedback control retaining only short input history achieves comparable or slightly higher accuracy. On this benchmark, folding matches raw feedback within small empirical margins at a much narrower interface; these findings also underscore the critical necessity of no-feedback recurrence controls when benchmarking sequence models.

</details>


### [218] [Rethinking One-Shot Federated Graph Learning: Training-Free Statistical Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06154)
*Shutong Zheng,Sijia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPEAR，一种完全免训练的框架，将一次性联邦图学习重构为统计估计问题，直接在图数据的原始特征空间中计算拓扑平滑的类原型，并通过样本量自适应的收缩估计器聚合，解决了极端非IID条件下局部GNN训练导致的跨客户端表示不对齐问题，在7个基准上取得最优精度且至少快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一次性联邦图学习方法都假设局部GNN训练必不可少，但经验观察发现极端非IID条件下局部GNN训练会产生严重的跨客户端表示不对齐，反而成为误差的主要来源而非解决方案。因此需要重新审视一次性联邦图学习的基本范式。

Method: 将一次性FGL重构为统计估计问题，提出SPEAR框架：在原始特征空间中直接从局部图计算拓扑平滑的类原型（完全免训练），服务器端使用样本量自适应的收缩估计器聚合这些原型，对不可靠的局部估计进行降权，生成鲁棒的全局类原型。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上，SPEAR在极端异构条件下持续取得最先进精度；相比所有基线至少快一个数量级，相比生成式和蒸馏类方法可达数个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，免训练的统计估计方法而非局部GNN优化，才是实现鲁棒且高效的一次性联邦图学习的关键。这颠覆了该领域'局部训练必不可少'的普遍假设。

Abstract: One-shot federated graph learning generally aims to train Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) across clients with disconnected subgraphs in a single communication round. Existing methods predominantly design advanced optimization strategies under the premise that local GNN training is indispensable. However, empirical observations reveal that under extreme non-IID conditions, local GNN training suffers from severe cross-client representation misalignment, becoming a major source of error rather than a remedy. Motivated by this, we reformulate one-shot FGL as a statistical estimation problem. We propose SPEAR (Statistical Prototype Estimation with Adaptive Reliability), a completely training-free framework that directly computes topology-smoothed class prototypes from local graphs in the original feature space. The server then aggregates these prototypes using a sample-size-adaptive shrinkage estimator that down-weights unreliable local estimates, producing robust global class prototypes. Extensive experiments across seven benchmarks demonstrate that SPEAR consistently achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under extreme heterogeneity. Moreover, SPEAR delivers at least an order-of-magnitude speedup over all baselines, reaching several orders of magnitude against generative and distillation-based methods. Our findings suggest that training-free statistical estimation, rather than local GNN optimization, provides the key to robust and efficient one-shot federated graph learning. The code is available at https://github.com/Yodeesy/SPEAR .

</details>


### [219] [All for 1-Bit: Towards Genuine 1-Bit Post-Training Quantization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06161)
*Zhixiong Zhao,Zukang Xu,Guangyu Sun,Lifeng Liu,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AF1（All for 1-Bit），一个真正的1比特后训练量化（PTQ）框架，用于大语言模型。通过Null-space-Aware Binary Factorization（NABF）和Hierarchical Shapley Allocation（HiSA）两个互补组件，在严格的1.0 BPW预算下保持模型精度。实验在LLaMA、Qwen和Gemma模型系列上验证，相比BF16平均获得2.5倍推理加速和90%以上的内存缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于二值化的PTQ方法通常因隐藏开销而远超标称的1比特存储目标，无法实现真正的1比特压缩。LLM的存储和内存带宽需求巨大，阻碍高效部署，亟需在严格的1比特预算下保持精度的方案。

Method: AF1包含两个互补组件：(1) Null-space-Aware Binary Factorization (NABF)：通过Hessian感知的代理重参数化、零空间感知的二值分解和仅缩放全局重建来提升二值重建质量；(2) Hierarchical Shapley Allocation (HiSA)：利用层级Shapley敏感性分配结构容量。两者协同在PTQ场景下、1.0-BPW严格预算内保持模型精度。

Result: 在LLaMA、Qwen和Gemma模型家族上的实验显示，AF1在困惑度和零样本准确率上持续优于现有二值化PTQ方法。相比BF16，AF1平均实现2.5倍推理加速和超过90%的内存缩减。

Conclusion: AF1提供了一条实用路径，实现了可部署的真正1比特LLM压缩，兼顾了严格的存储预算与模型精度保持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress, yet their massive storage and memory-bandwidth demands still hinder efficient deployment. Weight binarization is a promising solution, but existing binarization-based post-training quantization (PTQ) methods usually far exceed the nominal 1-bit storage target due to hidden overhead. To address this gap, we propose All for 1-Bit (AF1), a genuine 1-bit PTQ framework for LLMs. AF1 comprises two complementary components: (1) Null-space-Aware Binary Factorization (NABF) for improving binary reconstruction through Hessian-aware surrogate reparameterization, null-space-aware binary factorization, and scale-only global reconstruction; and (2) Hierarchical Shapley Allocation (HiSA) for assigning structural capacity using hierarchical Shapley sensitivity. Together, they preserve model accuracy under a strict 1.0-BPW budget in the PTQ setting. Experiments on LLaMA, Qwen, and Gemma families show that AF1 consistently outperforms existing binarization-based PTQ methods in perplexity and zero-shot accuracy. Compared with BF16, AF1 achieves an average 2.5 times inference speedup and over 90% memory reduction across evaluated models, providing a practical path toward deployable genuine 1-bit compression for LLMs. The code for reproducibility is available at https://github.com/Kishon-zzx/AF1.

</details>


### [220] [Decision-Aware Suffix Prediction and Reasoning of Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06169)
*Henryk Mustroph,Stefanie Rinderle-Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将决策挖掘与后缀预测相结合的神经符号框架（decision-aware suffix prediction），通过挖掘的决策规则对预测事件进行推理，以提升短前缀和稀有流程变体场景下的后缀预测准确性，同时增加模型的内在可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于神经网络的流程后缀预测方法在平均表现上良好，但在短前缀或稀有流程变体场景下表现不佳。原因在于此类场景中正确的路径依赖于案例级和事件级属性的分支决策信号，而NN模型倾向于过度加权密集的事件标签，对这些属性特征关注不足。决策挖掘能从事件日志中提取此类决策规则，但此前仅用于事后分析和假设分析，尚未应用于后缀预测任务。

Method: 提出一个决策感知后缀预测框架，属于神经符号方法。该框架将决策挖掘提取出的决策规则与神经后缀预测模型相结合，在模型预测事件序列后，利用挖掘的决策规则对预测结果进行逻辑推理和约束/矫正，从而实现对预测事件的符号推理。实验在三个后缀预测器上对四个事件日志进行评估。

Result: 在四个事件日志中的三个上，该框架能够在多个后缀预测器上提升后缀预测性能，尤其在短前缀场景中提升显著，同时也能改进稀有流程变体的预测效果。

Conclusion: 决策感知后缀预测框架是一种有效的神经符号融合方法，通过引入决策规则为后缀预测提供了内在可解释性，并在短前缀和稀有变体等困难场景中带来了实质性的性能改进。

Abstract: Suffix prediction forecasts the remaining sequence of events of a running case until completion. Most approaches rely on neural networks trained on event logs, which, on average, perform well but struggle with short prefixes or targets belonging to a rare process variant. In such scenarios, the correct path may cross multiple branching decisions, determined primarily by case- and event-level attributes, a signal that NN-based suffix prediction models tend to underweight because they may heavily weight (dense) event labels. Decision mining extracts rules for such decisions from the event log, but has so far been applied only to post-hoc and what-if analysis, not suffix prediction. We therefore extend suffix prediction with decision mining, introducing a decision-aware suffix prediction framework, a neuro-symbolic approach that enables reasoning about predicted events via mined decision rules. Experiments on three of four event logs and three suffix predictors show that the framework can improve suffix prediction, especially for short prefixes but also for rare process variants, and adds intrinsic interpretability.

</details>


### [221] [FMMO: Detecting the Divergence Between Local Attribution and Global Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06173)
*Muhammad Rehman Zafar,Ali El-Sharif,Naimul Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FMMO框架，揭示局部XAI方法（如TreeSHAP）在模型漂移时误导性稳定，无法检测到受保护群体错误率上升的公平性失效，通过结合全局替代模型与利用率测量实现漂移监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后模型漂移是算法问责的关键风险，尤其当真实标签延迟时性能退化会成为"静默失败"。流行局部归因方法（如TreeSHAP）在模型可靠性崩溃时可能表现出误导性的稳定性，即局部解释稳定但全局分布漂移已经发生，导致受保护群体的公平性损害（如假阳性率飙升）无法被现有XAI工具发现。

Method: 提出FMMO（模型监控与可观测性框架），集成全局替代模型与模型利用率测量，将局部解释稳定性与全局分布漂移之间的不一致性暴露出来，弥补局部XAI方法在漂移检测上的公平性盲区。

Result: 在基准数据集、合成数据集和真实世界数据集上验证，表明局部XAI方法无法捕捉漂移诱导的差异化影响，导致受保护群体假阳性率大幅上升而特征归因保持不变；FMMO成功缓解了这一盲区并检测到被标准局部XAI工具遗漏的歧视性退化。

Conclusion: 局部XAI不能单靠作模型监控手段，需要结合全局层面的分布漂移感知工具。FMMO为利益相关者提供了检测歧视性退化的途径，弥补了标准局部XAI观测的失效场景，对算法问责与公平性保障具有重要意义。

Abstract: Post-deployment drift poses a critical risk to algorithmic accountability, particularly when ground truth labels are delayed and performance degradation becomes a "silent failure". While Explainable AI (XAI) is often relied upon to audit these shifts, we demonstrate that popular local attribution methods (e.g., TreeSHAP) can exhibit misleading stability even as model reliability collapses. In this paper, we propose a Framework for Model Monitoring and Observability (FMMO) designed to expose the divergence between local explanation stability and global distribution shifts. Using benchmark, synthetic, and real-world datasets, we show that local XAI methods fail to flag drift-induced disparate impact, specifically where False Positive Rates spike for protected groups while feature attributions remain unchanged. By integrating global surrogate models with model utilization measurements, FMMO mitigates this fairness blind spot, ensuring that stakeholders can detect discriminatory deterioration that standard local XAI tools overlook.

</details>


### [222] [Spectral Prioritized Sweeping in Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06186)
*Hung Pham,Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图拓扑增强框架（GTA-PS，别名谱优先扫描 SPS），通过在标准优先扫描（PS）的 Bellman 残差信号上叠加基于图拉普拉斯逆的扩散分量，解决非平稳奖励设置下 PS 因残差仅沿已实现备份传播而导致的短视问题。该方法为固定动力学、变化奖励的场景提供一个即插即用的排序分数，并经 SLEM 调度器控制拓扑贡献的退火幅度。实验表明其在 FourRooms 和 GARNET 域上优于标准 PS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Prioritized Sweeping 在非平稳奖励环境下存在短视问题：当局部奖励发生偏移后，Bellman 残差只能通过已执行的备份沿状态图逐跳传播，在有限的重规划预算下，处于瓶颈处或拓扑上距离较远的状态估计可能长期保持旧值而得不到及时更新。作者希望设计一种新的优先级分数，使重视规划能瞬间将活跃优先级传播到所有相关状态，而不受备份路径拓扑限制。

Method: 提出 Graph Topology Augmentation (GTA) 框架：1）利用策略平滑构造一条转移链，并取其入拉普拉斯与出拉普拉斯；2）将标准 PS 的 Bellman 残差分数与"正则化拉普拉斯逆的混合"所产生的扩散信号进行增强组合，即用图的分辨率算子（resolvent）及其扩散语义来扩散残差量级；3）引入基于次大特征值模（SLEM）的调度器对拓扑贡献进行退火，使扩散尺度自适应于链的混合机制；4）提供可证明的结论：前向势与几何折扣残差传播一致，并保证 GTA-PS 能瞬时对所有状态给出活跃优先级。作为 drop-in 排序分数，可嵌入精确 DP 或 Dyna 式宿主规划器中。

Result: 作者证明 GTA-PS 的前向势与几何折扣残差传播一致，且能从算法层面即时激活所有状态的优先级。在 FourRooms 与 GARNET 两个表格型基准域上，无论嵌入精确 DP 还是 Dyna 风格的宿主规划器，GTA-PS 相比标准 PS 均展现出更高的重规划效率（在有限备份预算下更快收敛到准确价值函数）。

Conclusion: 图拓扑增强思路有效地弥补了标准优先扫描在非平稳奖励场景下的拓扑短视缺陷：通过拉普拉斯扩散使残差信号绕过备份路径瓶颈全局传播，配合 SLEM 退火调度的自适应，提供了一种理论可证实、实践表现更优的通用优先级排序方案。该工作为后续将图谱理论引入模型驱动型强化学习重规划机制提供了范本。

Abstract: Prioritized Sweeping (PS) accelerates model-based reinforcement learning by selecting backups according to Bellman residual magnitude. In nonstationary reward settings, however, the canonical priority score is shortsighted: after a localized reward shift, residuals propagate only through realized backups, so bottlenecked or topologically distant state estimates may remain static under a limited replanning budget. We introduce the Graph Topology Augmentation framework, which employ the graph's resolvent and its diffusion semantic, to augment the inquired signal. Our application, Graph Topology Augmentation for Prioritized Sweeping (GTA-PS), or which the alias Spectral Prioritized Sweeping (SPS) might be more universal, provides a drop-in ordering score for the setting of fixed dynamics and changing state rewards. GTA-PS uses a smootherized policy, inducing a transition chain, with its in- and out-Laplacian. The standard priority key is augmented with a mixing of regularized Laplacian inverses diffusing the residual magnitude. Furthermore, the topology contribution is annealed by a scheduler based on the Second Largest Eigenvalue Modulus (SLEM), allowing its scale to adapt to the chain's mixing regime. We prove that the forward potential coincides with geometric discounted residual propagation and show that GTA-PS gives active priority instantly to all states. Tabular experiments on FourRooms and GARNET domains demonstrate improved replanning efficiency over standard PS under both exact DP and Dyna-style host planners.

</details>


### [223] [EgoNeMo: Transferable Map of Pedestrian Dynamics via Egocentric LiDAR Scan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06195)
*Azusa Sawada,Allan Wang,Hideo Saito,Aaron Steinfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可迁移的动力学地图（MoD）框架，仅利用以自我为中心的3D LiDAR点云即可在未知环境中泛化，克服了传统MoD方法受限于特定场地、需要在新位置累积极大量轨迹数据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoD方法虽能编码人类运动特征以支持轨迹预测和安全导航，但具有场地特异性，需要在每个新位置累积极大量轨迹数据，这极大地限制了其实际部署和应用。

Method: 该框架基于神经隐式建模思想，训练一个跨多样环境的连续、基于LiDAR的MoD估计器。提出位置平衡采样策略缓解真实轨迹数据的稀疏性和时间偏差；采用多任务学习架构联合预测运动分布与空间频率分数图，并通过可见性感知损失增强空间频率分数图以补偿不完整观测。

Result: 综合实验表明，即使在训练数据高度稀疏的情况下，该方法也能仅凭单次瞬时的LiDAR扫描有效重建未知位置底层的运动地图；且该改进能增强下游轨迹预测的可靠性。

Conclusion: 提出的可迁移MoD框架成功解决了传统方法在新环境中的泛化瓶颈，证明基于LiDAR的隐式建模可在未知环境中从单帧点云中重建运动地图，并为下游预测任务带来增益。

Abstract: This paper proposes a transferable Map of Dynamics (MoD) framework that generalizes to unknown environments using only egocentric 3D LiDAR point clouds to overcome the long-standing limitation of traditional MoD methods. While MoDs are essential for encoding human motion characteristics to enable accurate pedestrian trajectory prediction or safe robot navigation, traditional approaches suffer from site-specificity, requiring exhaustive trajectory accumulation at every new location. Extending recent advances in neural implicit modeling, our framework trains a continuous, LiDAR-based MoD estimator across diverse environments. To mitigate the inherent sparsity and temporal bias of real-world trajectory data, we introduce a position-balanced sampling strategy and a multi-task learning architecture that jointly predicts motion distributions and a spatial frequency score map. The latter is further augmented by visibility-aware losses to compensate for incomplete observation data. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively reconstructs underlying motion maps even in unknown locations from a single instantaneous LiDAR scan, despite highly sparse training data. Finally, we show that our improvements enhance the reliability of downstream trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [224] [SeaCausal-FL: Federated Fuzzy Causal Learning for Maritime IoT Fault Diagnosis and Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06257)
*Yuhang Qiu,Haihan Zhu,Koteeswaran Seerangan,Longsheng Zhu,Xiong Wang,Yijun Lu,Zheng Lin,Fangmin Ren,Jialiang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SeaCausal-FL，一种用于海事物联网中海洋发动机故障诊断的联邦模糊因果学习框架，结合共享时序诊断路径与机制条件因果推理，在异构数据分布下实现高精度诊断并支持反事实推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事物联网中的海洋发动机故障诊断面临三大挑战：数据分布式拥有导致的隐私问题、异构故障分布以及持续变化的运行工况。现有方法难以在不共享原始数据的前提下，处理分布式数据中不确定且重叠的运行机制，并实现可靠的因果推理。

Method: SeaCausal-FL包含：1) 共享时序诊断路径提取共性时序特征；2) 区间二型模糊层表示不确定和重叠的运行机制；3) 每个机制关联一个物理约束的结构因果模型；4) 聚合前基于运行上下文、因果结构和条件干预-响应签名进行机制对齐；5) 模型参数按样本、类别、机制和机制-类别证据进行加权聚合（而非仅按客户端样本量）；6) 学习到的结构方程支持基于外展-行动-预测（abduction-action-prediction）的区间反事实推理。

Result: 在海洋发动机故障数据集上，四个客户端分区平均F1达87.07%，AUROC和AUPRC分别达98.98%和94.81%；在未见负载和故障类型遗漏的情况下保持强性能。在因果基准上，Edge-F1约0.58，Edge-AUPRC约0.68，系数RMSE降至约0.14，并提供良好的反事实估计和干预决策。

Conclusion: SeaCausal-FL通过将模糊机制建模、物理约束因果学习与联邦聚合相结合，有效解决了分布式海事故障诊断中数据异质性和运行条件变化的问题，同时实现了预测准确性与因果可解释性的兼顾，为可靠的海事物联网故障诊断提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reliable marine-engine fault diagnosis in maritime IoT is challenged by distributed data ownership, heterogeneous fault distributions, and continuously changing operating conditions. This paper proposes SeaCausal-FL, a federated fuzzy causal learning framework that combines a shared temporal diagnostic path with mechanism-conditioned causal reasoning. An interval type-2 fuzzy layer represents uncertain and overlapping operating mechanisms, while each mechanism is associated with a physics-constrained structural causal model. Before aggregation, locally learned mechanisms are aligned using operating context, causal structure, and conditional intervention-response signatures. Model parameters are then aggregated according to sample, class, mechanism, and mechanism-class evidence instead of client sample size alone. The learned structural equations further support interval counterfactual reasoning through abduction, action, and prediction. Experiments on a marine-engine fault dataset and a real-data-calibrated semi-synthetic causal benchmark show that SeaCausal-FL achieves an average F1 score of 87.07% across four client partitions, with AUROC and AUPRC of 98.98% and 94.81%, respectively. It also maintains strong performance under unseen loads and fault-type omission during training. On the causal benchmark, SeaCausal-FL reaches an Edge-F1 of approximately 0.58 and an Edge-AUPRC of 0.68, reduces coefficient RMSE to about 0.14, and provides favorable counterfactual estimation and intervention decisions.

</details>


### [225] [Representation Learning for Sample-Efficient CATE Estimation by Leveraging Multiple Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06294)
*Maitreyi Swaroop,Shikha Bhat,Samantha Rodriguez,Tamar Krishnamurti,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用历史数据中广泛测量的大量辅助结果（outcomes）来学习高维协变量的低维表示，从而在有限实验样本下更高效地估计条件平均处理效应（CATE）。理论上证明了在代理性（surrogacy）条件下CATE可以被识别，并刻画了假设不完全满足时的偏差-方差权衡；实验在合成数据和半合成医疗数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CATE估计对于精准干预至关重要，但许多应用中实验样本有限，导致从高维协变量估计异质性效应困难。传统方法要么受困于维数灾难，要么直接使用现成的降维方法，但这些方法未必保留处理异质性。然而这些领域通常拥有覆盖广泛结果的大规模历史数据集，这一监督来源在实际中很少被利用。

Method: 遵循因果表征学习（causal representation learning）的框架，假设高维协变量具有更低维的潜在底层动态。利用历史数据中测量的多种辅助结果作为监督信号，学习协变量的低维表示。理论上证明了当辅助结果满足一组代理性条件（surrogacy conditions）且表示保留了相关协变量信息时，用学习到的低维表示替换高维协变量可以识别原始CATE。同时刻画了假设不完全成立时的偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 理论上，当代理条件和表示充分性满足时，基于表示的CATE估计具有更高的样本效率（结合已有的维度依赖率可推出）；当假设不完全成立时，只要维度降低带来的方差缩减超过压缩引入的偏差，基于表示的估计器仍可实现更低的误差。实验在合成数据和半合成医疗数据上验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 利用领域内丰富的历史结果数据来学习协变量的低维表示，可以有效地缓解有限实验样本下高维CATE估计的维数灾难问题，在理论和实践上均取得了有说服力的结果。

Abstract: Estimating conditional average treatment effects (CATE) enables efficient targeting of interventions, but many applications have limited experimental samples, making it difficult to estimate heterogeneous effects from high-dimensional covariates. In such settings, policymakers and medical practitioners often succumb to the curse of dimensionality or apply off-the-shelf dimension reduction methods that may not preserve treatment heterogeneity. Yet these domains often come with large historical datasets measuring a wide range of outcomes -- a source of supervision that is rarely exploited in practice. Following causal representation learning, we hypothesize that such domains with high-dimensional covariates have lower-dimensional underlying dynamics. We can thus leverage the diverse outcomes measured in historical data to learn a lower-dimensional representation of the covariates. Theoretically, we prove that when the auxiliary outcomes satisfy a set of surrogacy conditions and the representation retains relevant covariate information, the original CATE is identified when the high-dimensional covariates are replaced by the learned representation. Combined with existing dimension-dependent rates for CATE estimation, the result implies greater sample-efficiency on the same experimental sample. Additionally, we characterize the bias-variance tradeoff when the assumptions do not hold perfectly, and show that the representation-based estimator can still achieve lower error when the reduction in estimator variance outweighs the bias due to compression. Empirically, we evaluate the method on synthetic data and semi-synthetic medical data.

</details>


### [226] [Linear Algebra Foundations of Efficient Attention: A Phase Reversal in Rank Collapse Under SVD Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06341)
*Anjaneya Teja Sarma Kalvakolanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了14篇关于线性代数技术在transformer基础模型研究中应用的同行评审论文，重点关注自注意力输出秩的推导、利用秩塌缩的压缩方法、以及低秩KV缓存投影与线性注意力和状态空间模型之间的半可分矩阵对偶性。作者发现了一个原创发现：使用SVD压缩注意力投影对网络秩塌缩有双重效应——在初始化时强烈抑制，但在预训练模型上却加速秩塌缩（在GPT-2 124M、GPT-2 Medium 355M和Pythia-160M上验证），且该效应通过受控因果分解归因于SVD压缩时选择的子空间而非算子范数的降低，分别解释了初始化阶段约76%和预训练权重下约83%的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到该文献领域存在一个开放问题：压缩方法与网络自然秩塌缩之间的交互作用尚不明确。这一空白促使他们系统性地综述14篇相关工作，并针对这个未解问题开展原创性研究。

Method: 论文采用两种研究方法：(1) 对14篇peer-reviewed工作按三个主题（自注意力输出秩的推导与性质、利用秩塌缩的压缩方法、低秩KV缓存投影）进行统一综述；(2) 通过受控因果分解实验，在GPT-2 124M、GPT-2 Medium 355M和Pythia-160M模型上，使用四种秩估计方法测度SVD压缩在不同初始化/预训练条件下的秩塌缩影响，并将效应分解为子空间选择与算子范数缩减两个因素。

Result: SVD压缩在初始化阶段强烈抑制秩塌缩，但在预训练模型上反而加速秩塌缩。效应在所有压缩比上一致，且与四种秩估计方法的结果一致。因果分解表明，SVD压缩时选择的子空间（而非算子范数的缩减）是主导因素，分别解释了初始化约76%和预训练权重约83%的效应，从而精化了标定感知压缩的观点并解释了其为何优于朴素SVD截断。对象混叠伪影风险极低。

Conclusion: 该研究为秩塌缩与压缩方法的交互提供了新的定量理解，指出SVD的子空间选择机制（而非范数缩减）是核心驱动力，为标定感知压缩方法为何优于朴素SVD截断提供了理论解释，并统一了14篇相关文献的框架。

Abstract: Linear algebra provides the framework of concepts (matrix rank, singular value decomposition (SVD), and eigendecomposition) that modern artificial intelligence employs to encode, compress, and propagate information through neural networks. This paper unifies fourteen separate peer-reviewed works analyzing the usage of these techniques in the context of transformer-based foundation model research, focusing on three areas of the topic: derivations and properties of self-attention matrices' output rank, compression methods that purposefully utilize this phenomenon, and the low-rank key-value (KV) cache projection and its semiseparable-matrix duality to linear attention and state-space structured models. We were motivated to conduct this work after observing an open problem in this literature: the interplay of the mentioned compression methods with natural rank collapse of the network. With this paper, we report an original finding that using SVD compression of attention projections actually has the opposite effect on the rank collapse of the network: while it strongly suppresses it at initialization, it accelerates on pretrained models (for GPT-2 124M, GPT-2 Medium 355M, and Pythia-160M) with minimal risk of object aliasing artifacts appearing (verified on all compression ratios) and is consistent across four rank estimation methods. A controlled causal decomposition of the effect in both settings showed that the reason for this behavior can be explained by the choice of the subspace SVD makes when compressing the matrix better than the reduction of the operator norm it achieves, explaining roughly 76% of the effect at initialization and 83% on the pretrained weights, providing a refinement to the calibration-aware compression viewpoint and an explanation of why it outperformed naive SVD truncation.

</details>


### [227] [Robust Dynamic Expansion for Continual Learning under Backdoor Attacks via Purification and Selective Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06346)
*Keyu Lin,Fei Ye,Qihe Liu,Shijie Zhou,Jiguo Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出并研究了"后门攻击下的持续学习"(CLUBA)这一新问题设定，并设计了一个鲁棒的动态扩展框架，通过样本纯化、选择性恢复和鲁棒专家路由三个模块，在持续学习中同时缓解灾难性遗忘、保持适应能力并抵御后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习使模型能从顺序到达的任务中获取新知识并保留旧知识，但实际应用中任务流可能来自不可信来源，包含后门投毒样本。这给持续学习器的稳定性、可塑性及安全性带来了严峻挑战。现有的持续学习方法和后门防御方法均无法同时解决该问题。

Method: 提出一个鲁棒的动态扩展框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Bi-Prototype Purification (BPP) 利用特征空间中的语义差异识别可疑样本；(2) Gradient Discrepancy-based Robustness Optimization (GDBRO) 基于净化后的数据，通过伪标签校正和梯度一致性评估选择性恢复有信息量的投毒样本；(3) Robust Feature Consistency-based Expert Selection (RFCBES) 构建扰动感知的类原型，在输入被污染或偏移时实现可靠的专家路由。

Result: 论文尚未提供具体的实验结果数据，但从方法设计上表明该框架能够同时实现样本净化、选择性恢复和鲁棒专家路由，在持续学习场景下提升对后门攻击的鲁棒性，同时维持模型的稳定性和可塑性。

Conclusion: 该工作将后门攻击防御与持续学习问题有机结合，提出了一套统一的鲁棒持续学习框架，解决了在顺序学习过程中同时缓解灾难性遗忘和防止恶意监督吸收的挑战。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) enables models to acquire new knowledge from sequentially arriving tasks while retaining previously learned knowledge. However, in practical scenarios, task streams collected from untrusted sources may contain backdoor-poisoned samples, posing a critical challenge to the stability, plasticity, and security of continual learners. In this work, we investigate a challenging setting termed Continual Learning Under Backdoor Attack (CLUBA), where each incremental task may involve a small proportion of maliciously manipulated training samples. Unlike conventional continual learning or backdoor defense scenarios, CLUBA requires models to simultaneously mitigate catastrophic forgetting, preserve adaptation capability, and prevent the absorption of malicious supervision during sequential updates. To address this challenge, we propose a robust dynamic-expansion framework that integrates sample purification, selective recovery, and robust expert routing into a unified continual learning paradigm. Specifically, we introduce Bi-Prototype Purification (BPP) to identify suspicious samples by exploiting semantic discrepancies in feature space. Based on purified data, Gradient Discrepancy-based Robustness Optimization (GDBRO) selectively recovers informative poisoned samples through pseudo-label correction and gradient consistency evaluation, improving robustness while maintaining model plasticity. Furthermore, Robust Feature Consistency-based Expert Selection (RFCBES) constructs perturbation-aware class prototypes to enable reliable expert routing under corrupted or shifted inputs.

</details>


### [228] [Robust Conformal Consensus: Multi-Agent LLM-as-a-Judge Interval Evaluation with Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06367)
*Lihui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向多智能体LLM-as-a-Judge评估的鲁棒不确定性估计框架，通过为多个LLM评估者的评分构建共形预测区间，实现更稳定和可靠的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge范式在自然语言生成评估中应用广泛，但评估过程中的不确定性尚未被充分探索，限制了其在真实场景中的可靠性。现有的共形预测方法通常仅应用于单一LLM评估者，忽视了不同LLM评估者之间引入的变异性。

Method: 提出用于多智能体LLM-as-a-Judge评估的鲁棒不确定性估计框架。基于共形预测，为多个LLM评估者产生的评分分别构建预测区间，并通过综合考虑来自不同LLM评估者的区间来获得更稳定的不确定性估计。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法能生成具有覆盖保证的有效预测区间，并且基于区间的跨多评估者聚合能带来更稳定的评估结果。

Conclusion: 多智能体共形预测框架能有效提升LLM-as-a-Judge评估的可靠性与稳定性，为不确定性量化在多评估者场景下的应用提供了新路径。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has emerged as a promising paradigm for evaluating natural language generation. However, the uncertainty associated with such evaluations remains largely unexplored, which limits their reliability in real-world applications. Although conformal prediction offers a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, existing approaches typically apply it to a single LLM judge, overlooking the variability introduced by using different LLM evaluators. In this work, we propose a robust uncertainty estimation framework for multi-agent LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. Our approach constructs conformal prediction intervals for LLM-based scores from multiple LLMs. By considering intervals from different LLM judges, we obtain more stable and reliable uncertainty estimates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces valid prediction intervals with coverage guarantees, and that interval-based aggregation across multiple judges leads to more stable evaluation outcomes.

</details>


### [229] [Parameterized and Streaming Algorithms for Euclidean Fair $k$-Center Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06384)
*Zeyu Lin,Chaoqi Jia,Longkun Guo,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究欧几里得空间中的公平k-center聚类问题。提出了近似比为2.732的参数化算法，并通过单遍流式计算框架扩展到大规模数据得到4.464的近似比。进一步改进后的比率分别为2.414和3.828（但运行时在k上呈指数级）。为保证多项式时间复杂度，设计了单遍流算法，近似比为4.732，可进一步改进到4.42，优于现有最佳方法。实验表明所提方法在聚类精度上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性在机器学习中日益重要，公平k-center聚类作为基础问题受到广泛关注。该问题要求将数据集划分为m个不相交组，选择k个中心点，并约束从每组中选择的中心数量上限，以最小化任意数据点到其分配中心的最大距离。现有方法在欧几里得空间中的近似比和计算效率方面仍有改进空间，特别是在大规模数据场景下。

Method: 首先为欧几里得公平k-center开发了一个参数化近似算法（近似比2.732），并将其作为后处理阶段融入单遍流式框架用于大规模数据（近似比4.464）。通过允许在k上指数级的运行时，比率可进一步分别改进到2.414和3.828。为保持多项式时间复杂度，设计了单遍流算法，获得4.732的近似比，并进一步改进至4.42。

Result: 在欧几里得空间中，参数化算法的近似比为2.732，结合流式框架后为4.464；放开复杂度约束后分别可达2.414和3.828；多项式时间流算法的近似比为4.732，改进后为4.42，优于现有最优的近似比。广泛实验表明，所提方法在聚类精度上显著超过最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 本文在欧几里得公平k-center问题上取得了多个改进的近似比结果，覆盖参数化算法和流式算法两种场景，兼顾了近似质量与计算效率，并通过实验验证了实际聚类精度的优越性。

Abstract: Motivated by the growing importance of fairness in machine learning, fair $k$-center clustering has attracted considerable research attention as a fundamental problem. In this problem, a dataset is partitioned into $m$ disjoint groups, and the objective is to select $k$ data points as centers, subject to upper bounds on the number of centers chosen from each group, aiming to minimize the maximum distance between any data point and its assigned center. Focusing on Euclidean spaces, which are ubiquitous in machine learning applications, we first develop a parameterized approximation algorithm for Euclidean fair $k$-center with an approximation ratio of $2.732$. By incorporating this algorithm as a post-processing stage into a one-pass streaming framework for large-scale data, we obtain an approximation ratio of $4.464$. These ratios can be further respectively improved to $2.414$ and $3.828$ with a runtime exponential on $k$. To ensure polynomial-time complexity, we further design a one-pass streaming algorithm with an approximation ratio of $4.732$, which can be further improved to $4.42$, outperforming the state-of-the-art ratio. Finally, extensive experiments show that our methods significantly outperform state-of-the-art approaches in terms of clustering accuracy.

</details>


### [230] [Are Verifier Errors Independent Within a GRPO Group? Evidence from Qwen2.5 Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06386)
*Esther Xin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究调查了基于群体的强化学习（RLVR）中，由同一验证器对多次采样完成结果进行评分时存在的高度簇内相关性。在24,998组由Qwen2.5-1.5B生成的8个完成结果的组中，估计组内验证器错误相关系数为0.530（95%CI: 0.500-0.560），等效样本量降至1.70。相关性因答案形式而异（分数、根式、符号表达式和区间比单位标注和百分号聚类更强），且在最多0.83%的组中出现在不同规则验证器配置下的优势符号分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的RLVR分析通常假设验证器错误在完成结果之间是独立的，但这种假设可能忽略因共享答案格式（如计算过程或数学表达式结构相似）而产生的依赖关系，从而高估有效样本量并低估噪声的影响。

Method: 在MATH、GSM8K和DeepMath-103K数据集上，使用Qwen2.5-1.5B模型生成24,998组、每组8个完成结果；估计合并的组内验证器错误相关系数，采用可交换误差模型计算设计效应调整后的有效样本量，并跨四种基于规则的验证器配置进行组相对优势的重放分析，还将依赖关系按不同答案形式进行分层比较。

Result: 组内验证器错误相关系数为0.530；八完成结果组经设计效应调整后的有效样本量仅为1.70。依赖程度在不同答案形式间差异显著：分数、根式、符号表达式和区间表现出更强的聚类，而单位标注和百分号较弱。在最多0.83%的组中，不同验证器配置给出了相互矛盾的优势判定。

Conclusion: 在RLVR中，同一提示下的多个完成结果存在显著的组内验证器错误相关，反映了共享提示难度与共享答案形式等影响。这提示需要对验证器噪声进行提示级别和答案形式感知的分析，而不能仅依据聚合错误率来刻画验证器行为。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) scoresmultiple completions per prompt using automatic verifiers. Analysesbased on independent verifier errors may overlook dependence associatedwith shared answer formats. We investigate this dependence in24,998 groups of eight completions generated by Qwen2.5-1.5B onMATH, GSM8K, and DeepMath-103K. We estimate a pooled within-groupverifier-error correlation of 0.530 (95% confidence interval:0.500--0.560). Under an exchangeable-error model, this correspondsto a design-effect-adjusted effective sample size of 1.70 for aneight-completion group. Dependence varies substantially across answerforms: fractions, radicals, symbolic expressions, and intervals exhibitstronger clustering than unit annotations and percent signs. Replayinggroup-relative advantages across four rule-based verifier configurationsidentifies at least one advantage-sign disagreement in up to 0.83% ofgroups. Because a group is repeated sampling for one prompt, thiswithin-group clustering may reflect shared prompt difficulty as well asshared answer form, and we do not attempt to separate the two here.Unlike studies of correlated judgments across multiple evaluators, ouranalysis examines dependence across completions scored by the sameverifier. These findings motivate prompt- and answer-form-aware analysesof verifier noise rather than characterizations based solely onaggregate error rates.

</details>


### [231] [MetaRSI / RSI2: A Meta-Recursive Self-Improving System for Recursive Self-Improving Systems Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06396)
*Zihan Tan,Leixin Sun,Zitong Shi,Yitao Liu,Jiajun Wu,Nathaniel Brooks,Jiaru Qian,Xiaoran Shang,Suyuan Huang,Yi Ding,Yangxu Liao,Mukai Li,Qiushi Sun,Shudong Liu,Xuankun Rong,Xiaohang Yu,Zhuo Chen,Hejia Geng,Chenxin Li,Aozhou Wang,Zengji Tu,Robert Tang,Yuxin Zhan,Eric Jiang,Yuxin Wu,Jianqing Zhang,Xiao Liang,Fang Wu,Haochi Zhang,Alexander Marlow,Guancheng Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaRSI-v1框架，将递归自我改进（RSI）从仅限于编码和正式基准（如数学、科学问答等机器可检查领域）扩展到真实多样的科学、工程和元科学领域。该框架通过三种类型化算子的调度组合（Data-RSI扩充能力并标记边界、Harness-RSI通过五槽脚手架编辑不改动权重、Model-RSI通过有界训练将能力内化到参数中）实现统一范式下的自我改进，并由双轴优化器和元级策略共同调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归自我改进仅在编码和正式基准上得到验证，受限于机器可检查的格式边界，无法处理开放性问题、仅靠论证/复现/测量来判定正确性的通用能力提升。作者认为RSI必须跨越真实、多样化的科学、工程与元科学领域运作，而非仅仅在形式化评估易于处理的狭窄切片内改进。

Method: 提出MetaRSI-v1，改进被定义为三个类型化算子在一个统一范式下的调度组合：Data-RSI扩充现有能力并标记其边界；Harness-RSI编辑五槽脚手架且不触碰权重；Model-RSI通过有界训练将能力内化到参数。三者共享同一循环内核与工件词汇表，使数据、脚手架和模型更改可组合而非互斥。双轴优化器联合决定算子顺序及各算子的提议策略，元级策略跨期限修正调度。目标模型在自身循环中扮演所有角色，无外部教师。

Result: 作者在该领域标准评估下（代码与闭式科学）对MetaRSI-v1进行了验证，在无外部教师的条件下取得成效。框架将自我改进从单一表面编辑重构为跨完整模型生产管道的组合，开辟了模型路径（通过训练内化能力）与脚手架路径（不改变权重、可通过接口扩展到任何模型）两种途径，Data-RSI被重新定义为两条路径共享的基础底物。

Conclusion: MetaRSI-v1将RSI从单表面的编辑重构为全模型生产管道上的操作组合，并通过可证伪法则产出关于循环存在于何处、算子如何组合以及监督能带来何种收益的规律性结论，为将自我改进推广到超越形式化可检查边界的通用能力奠定框架基础。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) lets a system improve the model-building machinery from its own failures, so every later model inherits the gain. Yet RSI has been validated almost exclusively on coding and formal benchmarks such as science QA and mathematics. This format bound limits RSI to improvement within a machine-checkable slice, not general capability where questions are open and correctness is settled by argument, replication, or measurement. We argue RSI must next operate across real, diverse scientific, engineering, and meta-scientific domains, not where formal evaluation is merely tractable. To that end we present MetaRSI-v1, where improvement is the scheduled composition of three typed operators over one unified paradigm. Data-RSI amplifies existing competence and marks its boundary; Harness-RSI edits a five-slot scaffold without touching weights; Model-RSI internalizes capability into parameters through bounded training. Sharing one loop kernel and artifact vocabulary, they make data, scaffold, and model changes composable rather than exclusive. A two-axis optimizer jointly decides operator order and each operator's proposal policy, while a meta-level policy revises the schedule across terms. We validate MetaRSI-v1 under the field's standard evaluations, on code and closed-form science, with no external teacher: the target model plays every role in its own loop. MetaRSI-v1 reframes self-improvement from a single-surface edit to a composition across the full model-production pipeline, opening two paths: a model route internalizing capability through training, and a harness route leaving weights untouched and thus extending self-improvement to any model reachable through an interface, with Data-RSI redefined as the shared substrate feeding both. The framework further yields refutable laws on where loops exist, how operators compose, and what supervision buys.

</details>


### [232] [Bi-HYCO: Bi-Objective Cooperative Learning for PDE Parameter Identification under Fragmented Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06511)
*Umberto Biccari,Jun Chen,Roberto Morales,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Bi-Objective HYCO（Bi-HYCO）框架，这是一个合作式框架，同时保留物理模型与合成模型各自的表示及局部观测目标，并在无标签的交互点耦合其预测状态，使两者在共同状态空间中进行通信。通过向量值目标及加权标量化实现计算，理论证明了确定性共享观测算法的收敛性，并在椭圆传输与二维Navier-Stokes实验中验证了参数与状态重构性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理模型和合成模型可能描述同一个受PDE控制的系统的互补方面，但它们接收到的观测数据可能不同且碎片化。现有方法通常要求两种模型整合或舍弃其中一种表示，如何在保留两者各自优势的同时实现有效协同是一个未解决的问题。

Method: Bi-HYCO框架：保留物理模型与合成模型两个表示及其各自的局部观测目标，在无标签的交互点（不含测量、不增加数据）上对两者的预测状态进行耦合，以此作为共同状态空间中的通信机制。两个目标构成向量值目标函数，采用加权标量化获得计算实现。对于固定交互点的确定性共享观测算法，在Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz类假设下证明了交替序列的充分下降性和有限长度性质，并收敛到混合临界点。

Result: 在椭圆传输和二维Navier-Stokes实验中，实现了参数与状态重构，考察了噪声与标量化效应，并与PINN/XPINN基线进行了对比。消融实验表明，在保留聚合但去除状态交互的情况下，参数恢复性能下降，尤其在Navier-Stokes问题中更为显著。

Conclusion: Bi-HYCO表明通过交互点进行状态协同通信是有益的，状态交互是框架有效的关键组件而非仅靠目标聚合；该框架在理论收敛性（Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz框架下的混合临界点收敛）与实证性能两方面均得到验证。

Abstract: Physical and synthetic models may describe complementary aspects of the same PDE-governed system while receiving different, possibly fragmented, observations. We propose Bi-Objective HYCO (Bi-HYCO), a cooperative framework that retains both representations and their local observational objectives while coupling their predicted states at unlabeled interaction points. These points contain no measurements and do not augment the data; they provide a communication mechanism in the common state space. The two criteria form a vector-valued objective, and weighted scalarizations provide computational realizations. For the deterministic shared-observation algorithm with fixed interaction points, we prove sufficient decrease and finite length of the whole alternating sequence, which converges to a mixed critical point under the stated Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz-type assumptions. Elliptic transmission and two-dimensional Navier-Stokes experiments assess parameter and state reconstruction, noise and scalarization effects, and PINN/XPINN references. Ablations show that removing state interaction while retaining aggregation deteriorates parameter recovery in the tested configurations, particularly for Navier-Stokes.

</details>


### [233] [On BatchNorm Forward Modes in Value-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06421)
*Daniel Palenicek,Mikael Henaff,Scott Fujimoto,Koustuv Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了批归一化（BN）在离散动作价值学习（Atari）中性能退化的问题，发现核心原因在于前向传播时使用运行统计量（running statistics）还是批次统计量（batch statistics）的选择。通过在C51和PQN中正确配置BN的统计量模式，可逆转性能退化，在26个Atari游戏上达到甚至超越LayerNorm基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批归一化在连续控制Actor-Critic方法（如CrossQ）中显著提升样本效率，但近期研究报道其在Atari离散动作价值学习中性能退化。由于离散Q网络不存在CrossQ中所识别的动作输入分布不匹配问题，这一退化令人意外，亟需理解其根本原因并找到修复方案。

Method: 论文系统比较了BN在特定前向传播点使用running statistics与batch statistics对离散价值学习的影响。具体针对目标网络（target-based）的C51和无目标（target-free）的PQN算法：在C51中，将BN的bootstrap前向改成batch-statistic模式；在PQN中，对动作选择和bootstrap均使用batch statistics。并在多达12个更新-数据比（UTD）下测试可扩展性，在400M帧的26个Atari游戏上评估。

Result: 在C51中，切换BN bootstrap前向为batch-statistics模式显著优于未归一化和LayerNorm基线，且UTD比率最高达12时仍能稳定扩展。在PQN中，对动作选择和bootstrap均采用batch statistics可从不佳的running-statistics配置中恢复性能。跨26个Atari游戏在400M帧下的最终聚合得分，该配置高于使用LayerNorm的PQN。

Conclusion: 精心配置的BN可以大幅提升离散动作价值学习性能，BN的前向统计量协议是算法规范中不可或缺的重要组成部分。此前BN在离散强化学习中的失败并非BN本身的问题，而是其使用方式的配置错误。

Abstract: Batch normalization (BN) substantially improves sample efficiency in continuous-control actor-critic methods such as CrossQ, yet recent studies report performance degradation in discrete-action value learning on Atari. These failures are surprising because discrete Q-networks lack the action-input distribution mismatch identified by CrossQ. We show for target-based C51 and target-free PQN that the simple choice between running and batch statistics at specific forward passes can reverse this degradation. In C51, switching the BN bootstrap forward to batch-statistic mode significantly improves performance over unnormalized and LayerNorm baselines and scales stably with update-to-data ratios up to 12. In PQN, using batch-statistics for both action selection and bootstrapping recovers performance from the failing running-statistic configuration. Across 26 Atari games at 400M frames, this configuration achieves a higher final aggregate score than PQN with LayerNorm. Our results show that carefully configured BN can substantially improve discrete-action value learning, and that its forward protocols are an essential part of the algorithm specification.

</details>


### [234] [Constrained Online Learning with Noisy Constraint Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06921)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带对抗性约束的在线凸优化问题，约束值和梯度通过无偏噪声观测。在高斯值噪声下，期望遗憾和硬违规的最大值存在 Ω(min{σ,1}T/log^7T) 的最坏情况下限，排除了任何联合 O(T^{1-δ}) 保证。为此引入 LEDGER 算法，跟踪观测到的净消耗并采用预算违规（固定时域内任意窗口的最大累计超支）作为衡量标准。在共同可行性和条件有限方差反馈假设下，LEDGER 达到 O(√T/V) 期望遗憾与 O(√V·T^{3/4}+σ√T) 期望预算违规，无需 Slater 条件。还扩展到可预测可行比较路径的动态遗憾场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带噪声约束的在线凸优化研究未能解决约束值和梯度仅通过无偏噪声观测时的联合遗憾与非违规保证问题。作者首先证明即使梯度已知，高斯值噪声下也无法实现任何固定 δ 的联合 O(T^{1-δ}) 保证（存在 Ω(min{σ,1}T/log^7T) 下限），因此需要重新定义更加宽松的约束满足衡量标准——预算违规——并设计能够同时保证遗憾和预算违规次线性增长的算法。

Method: 引入 LEDGER（Leveraged Drifting with Balanced Budget Enforcement and noiseless Redress tracking）算法。核心机制是：(1) 维护一个非负余额来追踪观测到的净消耗，允许超过硬约束但在有限预算内；(2) 在当前反馈噪声之前设置约束权重（权重基于历史信息而非当前噪声反馈），从而避免噪声的即时干扰；(3) 在共同可行性（common feasibility）和条件有限方差（conditional finite-variance）反馈假设下推导期望遗憾和预算违规的上界。算法参数 V 可在 [T^{-1/2}, 1] 范围内调节遗憾与预算违规之间的权衡。对于动态遗憾场景，算法可预测可行比较器路径，无需共同可行性或路径长度输入；其预算界则需取决于最短可行路径（乘以维度因子）。

Result: LEDGER 在固定问题参数下达到：O(√T/V) 期望遗憾和 O(√V·T^{3/4}+σ√T) 期望预算违规，其中 V∈[T^{-1/2},1]。在 V=1 处给出 (O(√T), O(T^{3/4})) 的对；在 V=T^{-1/6} 处给出 (O(T^{2/3}), O(T^{2/3})) 的均衡对；在预算重点端点 V=T^{-1/2} 处给出 (O(T), O(√T))。全部无需 Slater 条件。动态遗憾更新获得 O((1+E[P_T])√T/V) 的期望动态遗憾，其预算界取决于最短可行路径（含维度因子）。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立硬约束联合保证的下界（即便在梯度已知时噪声值约束也无法实现联合 O(T^{1-δ}) 保障），打破了在标准遗憾-硬违规框架下改进的可能性，转而提出预算违规框架和 LEDGER 算法，在全新框架下实现了遗憾与预算违规的次线性帕累托权衡曲线。该框架天然规避了下界，且不依赖 Slater 条件，还推广到了动态遗憾的设定中，提供了系统的理论分析。

Abstract: We study constrained online convex optimization with adversarial constraints when constraint values and gradients are observed through unbiased noise. Gaussian value noise of standard deviation $σ$ yields a worst-case lower bound of $Ω(\min\{σ,1\}T/\log^7T)$ on the maximum of expected regret and expected hard violation, even with known gradients. This rules out any jointly $O(T^{1-δ})$ guarantee for fixed $δ>0$ and fixed positive noise level. We therefore study budget violation: the largest cumulative overspend over any window within a fixed horizon. We introduce \LEDGER, which tracks observed net consumption in a nonnegative balance and sets constraint weights before the current feedback noise. Under common feasibility and conditional finite-variance feedback, for fixed problem parameters, \LEDGER\ achieves $O(\sqrt T/V)$ expected regret and $O(\sqrt V\,T^{3/4}+σ\sqrt T)$ expected budget violation for $V\in[T^{-1/2},1]$. This gives the pair $(O(\sqrt T),O(T^{3/4}))$ at $V=1$ and $(O(T^{2/3}),O(T^{2/3}))$ at $V=T^{-1/6}$, without a Slater condition. The budget-focused endpoint $V=T^{-1/2}$ gives $(O(T),O(\sqrt T))$. The same update yields $O((1+E[P_T])\sqrt T/V)$ expected dynamic regret for predictable feasible comparator paths, without common feasibility or path-length input. Its budget bound instead depends on the shortest feasible path, up to a dimension factor.

</details>


### [235] [Sparse Oblique Rule Boosting for Simpler Additive Rule Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06426)
*Shahrzad Behzadimanesh,Pierre Le Bodic,Geoffrey I. Webb,Mario Boley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的符号规则集成学习方法，通过引入带有可学习稀疏线性变换的逻辑命题（即 $\mathbf{x}^T\mathbf{w} \geq t$ 形式的斜向规则条件），将传统轴平行决策区域推广为具有斜面的通用多面体区域，从而在保持规则可解释性的同时降低模型复杂度并提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的小型可加符号规则集成依赖轴平行分区的决策区域（基于单个变量与阈值的合取命题），虽然单个规则高度可解释且能通过梯度提升高效学习，但在缺乏精心构造的输入特征时，需要增加规则的数量和复杂度来达到足够精度，从而损害模型的可解释性。

Method: 将传统轴平行规则条件推广为带有可学习稀疏线性变换的逻辑命题 $\mathbf{x}^T\mathbf{w} \geq t$，其中 $\mathbf{w}$ 为可学习的稀疏权重向量，使决策区域成为具有斜面的通用多面体；提出基于加权逻辑回归的梯度提升学习方法来实现该模型。

Result: 在14个回归与分类任务上的实证结果表明，所提方法在维持相同或更好预测精度的同时，实现了比竞争基线更低的模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了可解释性与准确性之间的有利权衡，并减少了对手动特征工程的依赖。

Abstract: Small additive ensembles of symbolic rules offer interpretable prediction models. Traditionally, these ensembles use rule conditions based on conjunctions of simple threshold propositions $x \geq t$ on a single input variable $x$ and threshold $t$, resulting geometrically in axis-parallel polytopes as decision regions. While this form ensures a high degree of interpretability for individual rules and can be learned efficiently using the gradient boosting approach, it relies on having access to a curated set of expressive input features so that a small ensemble of axis-parallel regions can describe the target variable well. Absent such features, reaching sufficient accuracy requires increasing the number and complexity of individual rules, which diminishes the interpretability of the model. Here, we extend classical rule ensembles by introducing logical propositions with learnable sparse linear transformations of input variables, i.e., propositions of the form $\mathbf{x}^T\mathbf{w} \geq t$, where $\mathbf{w}$ is a learnable sparse weight vector, enabling decision regions as general polyhedrons with oblique faces. We propose a learning method using gradient boosting based on a weighted logistic regression. Empirical results across 14 regression and classification tasks demonstrate that the proposed method achieves lower model complexity than competitive baselines while maintaining similar or better predictive accuracy. Hence, the approach provides a favorable trade-off between interpretability and accuracy and reduces the reliance on manual feature engineering.

</details>


### [236] [MpSub: A Momentum $p$-Dimensional Subspace Trust-Region Method for Derivative-Free Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07666)
*Yuyang Wang,Haoyu Yao,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动量p维子空间信赖域方法（MpSub），用于大语言模型的全参数微调，消除学习率调节需求。在每次迭代中，MpSub在p维子空间内搜索：一个方向保留最近接受步的历史动量，其余方向通过随机采样探索，利用中心差分估计子空间梯度，基于线性信赖域模型计算试验步，并根据预测与实际损失减少的一致性自适应调整信赖域半径，从而完全消除学习率。在8,400次前向传播预算下对OPT-125M和OPT-350M在CommitmentBank上进行微调，两种模型均使用相同的预设参数，MpSub在三个随机种子下达到0.673和0.690的平均测试准确率，与调优后的MeZO（0.685）相当但无需任何学习率搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全参数微调大语言模型由于反向传播存储激活和梯度，内存开销巨大。零阶优化通过损失评估估计更新方向避免这一问题，但现有方法需要为每个模型和任务调优敏感的学习率，缺乏无需调参的鲁棒方案。

Method: MpSub在每次迭代搜索p维子空间：一个方向保留最近接受步骤的历史动量，其余p-1个方向通过随机采样探索。子空间梯度通过中心差分估计，由线性信赖域模型计算试验步，信赖域半径根据预测与实际损失减少的一致性自适应更新，从而消除学习率。评估共享一个小批量，方向从种子原位重新生成，仅使用前向传播。理论分析在光滑确定性目标和非正交高斯方向下约束有限差分误差、量化子空间捕获的梯度能量，并在有保护半径更新下证明梯度范数几乎必然收敛到零。

Result: 在8,400次训练目标前向传播的匹配预算下，微调OPT-125M和OPT-350M于CommitmentBank。两种模型大小使用相同的预设参数，MpSub在三个随机种子下达到0.673和0.690的平均测试准确率，匹配调优后的MeZO（0.685）且无需学习率搜索。此外，MpSub在光滑确定性目标下证明了几乎必然的收敛性。

Conclusion: MpSub通过动量保留方向与随机探索方向结合的p维子空间搜索，配合自适应信赖域机制，成功消除了零阶微调中对学习率的手动调节需求，在性能和收敛性上有理论保证与实证验证，为大语言模型低内存微调提供了无需调参的有效方案。

Abstract: Full-parameter fine-tuning of large language models has substantial memory costs because backpropagation stores activations and gradients. Zeroth-order optimization avoids this by estimating update directions from loss evaluations, but existing methods require tuning a sensitive learning rate for each model and task. We propose the momentum $p$-dimensional subspace trust-region method (MpSub). At each iteration, MpSub searches within a $p$-dimensional subspace: one direction preserves historical momentum from the most recent accepted step, while the remaining directions explore via fresh random sampling. The subspace gradient is estimated by central differences, a trial step is computed from a linear trust-region model, and the trust-region radius adapts according to the agreement between predicted and observed loss reduction, eliminating the learning rate. For LLM fine-tuning, evaluations within an iteration share a minibatch, and directions are regenerated in place from seeds, using forward passes alone. For smooth deterministic objectives under unorthogonalized Gaussian directions, we bound the finite-difference error, quantify gradient energy captured by the subspace, and prove that $\lim_{k\to\infty} \|\nabla f(x_k)\|_2 = 0$ almost surely under a safeguarded radius update. Under a matched budget of 8,400 training-objective forward passes, we fine-tune OPT-125M and OPT-350M on CommitmentBank. With the same preset parameters at both model sizes, MpSub attains mean test accuracies of 0.673 and 0.690 over three seeds, matching tuned MeZO (0.685) without any learning-rate search.

</details>


### [237] [A Theoretical Framework for Masked Pretraining (MPT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06460)
*Qi Zhang,Runyu Zhou,Yifei Wang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理论框架来分析掩码预训练（MPT），建立了MPT与对比学习之间的理论联系，发现掩码隐式创建语义相似的正样本对并通过重构损失拉近其特征。同时指出MPT存在维度坍缩问题，提出Uniformity增强的U-MPT损失及新的掩码策略，从理论上保证了下游任务性能并验证了在多个真实数据集上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码预训练（MPT）在多个领域已成为一种极具前景的自监督学习范式，并在多种下游任务中取得了显著性能，但其背后的工作机制缺乏充分的理论理解，尤其是掩码在提取有意义的表征中的关键作用尚不明确。

Method: 提出一个新的理论框架来分析MPT，建立其与对比学习之间的理论关联，证明掩码技术隐式地创建语义相似的正样本对且重构损失将其在特征空间中拉近；基于此指出维度坍缩问题并提出U-MPT损失来增强表征均匀性；同时建立U-MPT的下游任务保证，并基于理论分析提出一种新的掩码策略。

Result: U-MPT损失有效解决了维度坍缩问题，在多种下游任务（包括线性评估、跨数据集微调和分布外泛化）中的真实数据集上带来了显著改进；提出的新掩码策略增强了下游性能，并通过理论视角解释了现有掩码策略的改进来源。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了MPT理论理解的空白，将MPT与对比学习统一到同一理论框架下，揭示了掩码在表征学习中的核心机制，并为设计更优的掩码策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Recently, Masked Pretraining (MPT) based on reconstruction pretraining tasks has risen to a promising self-supervised learning paradigm across various domains and achieves remarkable performance in multiple downstream tasks. However, the theoretical understanding of the working mechanism behind MPT is still limited. In this paper, we introduce a new theoretical framework to analyze MPT and understand the crucial role of masking in extracting meaningful representations. We establish theoretical connections between MPT and another popular self-supervised paradigm: contrastive learning. We prove that the masking technique implicitly creates positive pairs that are semantically similar and the reconstruction loss pulls them together in the feature space. Besides, as a result of the implicit alignment, we point out the dimensional collapse issue of MPT and propose a Uniformity-enhanced MPT (U-MPT) loss that can effectively address this issue and bring significant improvements in downstream tasks including linear evaluation, cross-dataset fine-tuning and out-of-distribution generalization on real-world data sets. Furthermore, we establish downstream guarantees of U-MPT and theoretically analyze the influence of masking strategies. Based on the theoretical analysis, we propose a new masking strategy which enhances the downstream performance of MPT and explains current improvements of masking strategies with our theoretical perspective.

</details>


### [238] [A Theoretical Analysis of Generalization Dynamics in Neural Networks under Gradient Descent with Weight Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07755)
*Yuqing Wang,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis,Mikhail Belkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个理论框架，刻画数据、网络架构和训练动态如何共同影响训练过程中的泛化表现。研究ℓ²损失下带权重衰减的梯度下降训练的广类神经网络，证明其收敛到经验损失全局最小值的邻域；通过输入空间划分将总体误差分解为数据误差、优化误差和预测变异误差并分别进行界分析。针对预测变异误差提出局部近似齐次性，推导沿训练轨迹的逐单元和逐层上界，进而给出改进泛化的必要条件（解释分层泛化差异）和延迟泛化的充分条件（理论上刻画grokking现象）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解泛化仍是机器学习领域的核心挑战，因为它需要同时考虑数据、架构和训练动态三方面因素。现有理论通常孤立地看待这些因素，缺少一个统一框架来阐明三者在整个训练过程中如何协同决定泛化性能，尤其是对groking等延迟泛化现象缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 针对在ℓ²损失下由带权重衰减的梯度下降训练的广类神经网络：(1) 证明梯度下降收敛到经验损失全局极小值点的邻域；(2) 基于输入数据划分空间，将总体误差分解为数据误差、优化误差和预测变异误差三个部分并分别界；(3) 针对衡量学习函数振荡的预测变异误差，提出（局部）近似齐次性假设，并推导出其在训练轨迹上的逐单元（cellwise）和逐层（layerwise）演化上界。

Result: 推导预测变异误差的逐单元与逐层界，产生两个重要结论：一是改进泛化的必要条件可用于解释不同层的泛化差异行为；二是延迟泛化的充分条件为groking现象提供了理论刻画。

Conclusion: 该框架首次在统一理论视角下将数据、架构与训练动态相结合来解释泛化，不仅给出了训练收敛性和误差分解的严格保证，还通过预测变异误差的界为分层泛化差异与grokking提供了可验证的理论解释，推进了对神经网络泛化机制的理解。

Abstract: Understanding generalization remains a central challenge in machine learning because it requires jointly considering data, architecture, and training dynamics. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that characterizes how these factors jointly shape generalization performance throughout training. More precisely, we study a broad class of neural networks trained under the $\ell^2$ loss by gradient descent (GD) with weight decay, and prove the convergence of GD to a neighbourhood of the global minimizers of the empirical loss. By partitioning the space based on the input data, we then decompose the population error into data error, optimization error, and prediction variation error, and bound them separately. In particular, for the prediction variation error, which measures the oscillations of the learned function, we propose (local) approximate homogeneity and derive explicit cellwise and layerwise bounds for its evolution along the training trajectory. These bounds yield two important implications: a necessary condition of improved generalization explains differences in layerwise generalization behavior; a sufficient condition describes delayed generalization and provides a theoretical characterization of grokking.

</details>


### [239] [Local and Global Stability in Performative Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06467)
*Debmalya Mandal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究执行式强化学习（performative RL）中策略混合体的稳定性问题，区分了局部混合稳定性（等价于平稳性）与全局混合稳定性，证明加权逐状态Hedge动态在任意（甚至不连续）环境映射下以O(1/√T)速率驱动局部稳定性间隙趋于零；引入比Lipschitz敏感性更弱的有限转移范围假设，给出全局稳定性的收敛上界与匹配下界，并扩展到n人对局执行式马尔可夫博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有执行式强化学习的收敛性保证依赖于环境映射π↦(P_π, r_π)的Lipschitz敏感性假设，但这类假设难以验证，且在多人最佳响应动力学等场景中不成立。作者希望研究策略混合体的稳定性，探索是否可以像执行式预测中那样通过随机化消除敏感性假设的需求。

Method: 论文提出两种稳定性概念：(1) 局部混合稳定性，基于占用加权的领域松弛，被证明等价于平稳性条件；(2) 全局混合稳定性，用于认证对任意偏离策略的鲁棒性。使用加权逐状态Hedge算法分析局部稳定性的收敛率，引入"有限转移范围"假设（严格弱于Lipschitz敏感性）分析全局稳定性，并将两种概念推广至n人对局执行式马尔可夫博弈。

Result: (1) 加权逐状态Hedge在任意不连续环境映射下，以O(1/√T)速率将局部稳定性间隙驱动到零（精确和轨迹反馈均可）；(2) 证明局部稳定与全局稳定两个概念本质不同——存在实例使局部稳定性精确达到但所有混合体的全局稳定性间隙远离零；(3) 在有限转移范围假设下，未加权逐状态Hedge收敛到O(γε_P/(1-γ)³)的地板，并证明匹配下界Ω(γε_P/(1-γ))（轨迹反馈），该地板不可避免；(4) 在多玩家对局中，无假设实现局部稳定性，对执行式马尔可夫势博弈实现全局稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，与执行式预测不同，在执行式强化学习中随机化（混合策略）并不能完全消除敏感性假设的需求。局部混合稳定性在极弱的条件下（甚至不连续映射）即可通过简单算法实现，但全局混合稳定性需要比Lipschitz更弱但依然存在"有限转移范围"假设，并存在不可消除的地板。两者本质不同，且推广到多智能体博弈的情形后，为执行式强化学习与执行式马尔可夫博弈的收敛性分析提供了更清晰的图景。

Abstract: In performative reinforcement learning the deployed policy shapes the environment that generates the learner's future data, and the natural solution concept is a performatively stable policy that is optimal in the environment it induces. Existing convergence guarantees rely on Lipschitz sensitivity assumptions on the environment map $π\mapsto (P_π, r_π)$, which are hard to verify and fail in settings such as multi-agent best-response dynamics. We instead study stability for mixtures of policies, and show that the resulting picture is fundamentally different from performative prediction, where randomization removes the need for any sensitivity assumption. We distinguish local mixed stability, an occupancy-weighted first-order relaxation that we show is equivalent to stationarity, from global mixed stability, which certifies against arbitrary deviating policies. Our first result is that a weighted per-state Hedge dynamic drives the local stability gap to zero at an $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate for an arbitrary, possibly discontinuous, environment map, both with exact and with trajectory feedback. The two notions genuinely differ: we exhibit an instance where local stability is achieved exactly but every mixture has global stability gap bounded away from zero. For global stability we introduce a bounded transition range assumption, strictly weaker than Lipschitz sensitivity, under which unweighted per-state Hedge converges up to a floor of $O(γε_P/(1-γ)^3)$, and we prove a matching-in-$ε_P$ lower bound of $Ω(γε_P/(1-γ))$ under trajectory feedback, so this floor is unavoidable. Finally, we extend both notions to $n$-player performative Markov games, obtaining local stability with no assumption on the joint environment map or game structure, and global stability for performative Markov potential games.

</details>


### [240] [Sparse Data Augmentation for Optimization with Provable Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08133)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了几何机器学习中非凸优化问题的高效数据增强方法。在群变换规模大或仅可通过采样访问的情况下，完全增强目标函数代价过高。作者证明了在适当正则条件下，使用预先固定的少量变换样本的稀疏增强目标上进行梯度下降，能以O((log|G|+log(1/δ))/ε²)次群变换查询返回完全增强目标的ε-驻点，优于组随机梯度下降的O(1/ε⁴)查询复杂度。证明基于群诱导算子的谱性质和表示论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何机器学习中常用数据增强通过对变换群G上的经验损失求平均来提升不变性，但完全增强目标需要访问群中每个元素，当群G规模大或仅能通过采样访问时代价过高。因此需要探索能否用预先固定的小样本变换来近似完全增强目标，在优化过程中重复使用这些样本以降低计算成本。

Method: 作者理论上分析了在稀疏增强目标（使用优化前固定采样的少量变换）上进行梯度下降的收敛性质。核心证明技术包括：(1)利用群诱导算子的谱性质，建立随机群平均对全群平均梯度场的均匀逼近；(2)借助表示论工具刻画逼近误差；(3)对比三种策略：完全增强目标的GD、每次迭代采样新变换的组SGD、以及固定稀疏增强的GD。

Result: 在概率至少1-δ下，固定稀疏增强的GD使用O((log|G|+log(1/δ))/ε²)次群变换查询即可返回完全增强目标的ε-驻点，显著优于组SGD的O(1/ε⁴)次查询复杂度。因此在变换查询次数上，固定稀疏增强的GD优于完全增强GD和组SGD。

Conclusion: 稀疏固定增强策略是一种高效且可行的替代方案，能够以远少于组SGD的变换查询次数达到相同精度。证明中使用的谱分析和表示论技术具有独立价值，可用于其他涉及群平均梯度逼近的优化问题。

Abstract: In nonconvex optimization problems arising in geometric machine learning, data augmentation is commonly used to promote invariance by averaging empirical losses over transformations of the data. Computing the fully augmented objective, however, requires access to every element of the transformation group $G$, which may be prohibitively expensive when $G$ is large or accessible only through sampling. We study whether full augmentation can instead be approximated using a small, fixed sample of transformations acquired before optimization and reused thereafter. Under suitable regularity conditions, we show that, with probability at least $1-δ$, gradient descent (GD) on the resulting sparsely augmented objective returns an $\varepsilon$-stationary point of the fully augmented objective using $\mathcal{O}\bigl((\log |G|+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon^2\bigr)$ group-transformation-oracle queries. By comparison, standard group stochastic gradient descent (group-SGD), which samples a fresh transformation at every iteration, uses $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon^4)$ transformation queries. Therefore, gradient descent with fixed sparse augmentation requires fewer transformation queries than both GD applied to the fully augmented objective and group-SGD. Our proof techniques, which may be of independent interest, establish a uniform approximation of the full group-averaged gradient field by a random group average using spectral properties of group-induced operators and tools from representation theory.

</details>


### [241] [Sector-Mean: Deterministic Initialization of K-Means Centroids via Angular Sector Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06468)
*Abhiyan Dhakal,Pranish Kafle,Rajani Chulyadyo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sector-Mean初始化策略，一种确定性的K-Means聚类初始化方法，通过围绕全局质心将二维数据空间划分为角度扇区并用扇区均值初始化质心，时间复杂度为O(N)。实验表明其在保持与K-Means++和Max-Min相当聚类质量的同时，显著降低初始化时间（较二者分别减少74.9%和59.8%）并使迭代次数最少（较二者分别少约5%和16%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-Means是最广泛使用的聚类算法之一，但其聚类效果和收敛速度高度依赖于初始质心的选取，这是该算法的主要性能瓶颈。现有初始化方法（如随机初始化、K-Means++、Max-Min）虽各有优势，但存在初始化计算开销大或效果不稳定等问题，因此需要一种既高效又能保证聚类质量的确定性初始化策略。

Method: 提出Sector-Mean确定性初始化算法：以全局质心为中心，将二维数据空间划分为若干角度扇区，然后使用各扇区内数据点的均值作为初始质心。该方法时间复杂度为O(N)。在评估中，使用SIPU和Birch两个二维基准数据集及多个真实数据集，在相同Lloyd迭代条件下与随机初始化、K-Means++和Max-Min初始化进行对比。

Result: 通过Friedman检验(p<0.05)和Nemenyi事后比较的统计分析表明，Sector-Mean在聚类质量上与K-Means++和Max-Min相当。在计算效率上，Sector-Mean较K-Means++减少74.9%的初始化时间，较Max-Min减少59.8%；同时取得最低平均迭代次数，比K-Means++少约5%，比Max-Min少约16%。

Conclusion: Sector-Mean初始化是一种确定性且计算高效的K-Means初始化策略，在不牺牲聚类质量的前提下显著降低初始化开销并加快收敛速度，是K-Means++和Max-Min等传统方法的有效替代方案。

Abstract: K-Means is one of the most widely used clustering algorithms, but its susceptibility to initial centroid selection remains a primary bottleneck for its convergence speed and clustering accuracy. This paper proposes Sector-Mean Initialization, a deterministic initialization strategy with O(N) time complexity that partitions the two-dimensional data space into angular sectors around the global centroid and initializes centroids using sector-wise means. We evaluate the method on established two-dimensional benchmarks (SIPU, Birch) and multiple real-world datasets, comparing against random, K-Means++, and Max-Min initialization under identical Lloyd iterations. The statistical analysis of Friedman's test (p<0.05) and Nemenyi post-hoc comparison indicates that, while delivering equivalent clustering quality as K-Means++ and Max-Min, Sector-Mean offers significant computational efficiency. Experimental results show that Sector-Mean reduces the initialization time by 74.9% and 59.8% in comparison to K-Means++ and max-min, respectively. And, it yields the lowest average number of iterations, achieving approximately 5% fewer iterations than K-Means++ and 16% fewer than max-min. These results highlight that Sector-Mean initialization offers a deterministic and computationally efficient initialization strategy while preserving cluster quality.

</details>


### [242] [One Step, One Lead: Mitigating Higher-Order Interference in Multi-Domain Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Step Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06469)
*Zihan Lin,Xiaohan Wang,Jie Cao,Jiajun Chai,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSOL方法，解决RL在多领域联合训练中因顺序干扰（sequential interference）导致单领域性能退化的问题。OSOL利用相邻检查点的token对数概率足迹检测输出空间中连续的更新部分相互逆转现象，并通过漂移排序、自适应缩放的校正项嵌入GRPO更新中，在Qwen3-30B-A3B上达到0.4822的领域宏平均，较最强基线提升5.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM跨多域RL联合训练时通常出现单领域性能退化与优化不稳定，现有诊断方法仅从单步视角使用一阶梯梯度对齐或曲率近似来识别干扰，但该视角会遗漏一种关键的顺序干扰形式：相同点的领域梯度即使近似正交，连续的实际更新仍可能在输出空间中部分相互逆转（跨步输出回溯）。因此需要一种能够直接从相邻checkpoint捕获此交互且无需重建同阶梯曲率的方法。

Method: OSOL由三部分组成：(1) 每次迭代指定一个焦点领域；(2) 利用前一个checkpoint的token对数概率足迹（consecutive token log-probability footprints）作为输出空间中的局部二阶交互度量，对token级回弹风险进行排序；(3) 在标准GRPO更新中应用漂移排序的、自适应缩放的校正项。该方法无需显式的高阶微分，即可抑制目标跨步输出回溯成分。

Result: 受控研究表明：跨步回弹比同点梯度诊断与后续任务损伤的关联更强；前述足迹对未来回弹风险的排序比Hessian代理方法更精确。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，OSOL的域宏平均达到0.4822，比最强对比基线提升5.7%。

Conclusion: 跨步（相邻checkpoint间）的顺序干扰是多领域RL训练中性能退化的关键因素，而单步梯度诊断不足以揭示此类问题。OSOL通过利用相邻时间点间的输出空间足迹进行校正，在不引入显式高阶微分的条件下有效缓解了多域RL的回溯干扰，证明检测顺序交互是提升多域RL稳定性的有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) across multiple domains can broaden the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet joint training often degrades individual-domain performance and can destabilize optimization. Existing work typically diagnoses such interference from a single-step view using first-order gradient alignment or curvature-based proxies. We show that this view can miss a critical form of sequential interference: same-point domain gradients may remain nearly orthogonal even when consecutive realized updates partially reverse one another in output space. We further show that consecutive token log-probability footprints recover this interaction directly from adjacent checkpoints as a local second-order interaction in output space, without explicitly reconstructing same-step curvature. Building on this insight, we propose OSOL, which designates a focus domain at each iteration, uses the preceding checkpoint footprint to rank token-level rebound risk, and applies a drift-ranked, adaptively scaled correction within the standard GRPO update. Our analysis shows that this correction suppresses the targeted cross-step output backtracking component. Controlled studies further show that cross-step backtracking is more strongly associated with subsequent task damage than same-point gradient diagnostics, while the preceding footprint ranks future rebound risk more accurately than Hessian-based proxies. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, OSOL reaches a domain-macro average of 0.4822, improving by 5.7% over the strongest compared baseline, without explicit higher-order differentiation.

</details>


### [243] [Adaptively Incorporating Directional Hints into Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08277)
*Alexander Ryabchenko,Jian Qian,Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出控制变量零阶下降（CV-ZOD）框架，利用方向提示（directional hints）加速非凸函数的零阶优化。通过控制变量法改进经典零阶梯度估计器，实现收敛速率在一阶O(1/T)与零阶O(d/T)之间自适应插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化（仅依赖函数值）在高维非凸问题中收敛速度慢（O(d/T)），而一阶优化需要精确梯度（O(1/T)）。实际中可获取"方向提示"——即廉价但不完全准确的梯度方向近似（由线性子空间给出），但现有引导方法在提示质量下降时会停滞。需要一个能自适应利用提示并对其质量保持鲁棒的新框架。

Method: 提出Control-Variate Zeroth-Order Descent (CV-ZOD)框架：在经典零阶梯度估计器中引入控制变量（control variate），其参照向量可根据方向提示灵活设定。包含两部分：(1) 理论上界——每步最优设定参照向量和步长的oracle算法，其收敛率插值于一阶O(1/T)与零阶O(d/T)之间，取决于轨迹上提示质量；(2) 实用性变体——无需预知提示质量，即可达到与oracle相同的保证（仅差对数因子）。

Result: 在仿真驱动的科学优化任务上验证，CV-ZOD在非凸地形上持续取得进展，而传统零阶下降收敛较慢、现有引导方法在引导质量退化时停滞。理论结果证明收敛速率可按提示质量自适应插值于O(1/T)与O(d/T)之间。

Conclusion: CV-ZOD有效弥合了一阶与零阶优化之间的差距，通过对低质量方向提示的鲁棒性设计，在非凸科学计算优化中取得了优于基线方法的持续进展。

Abstract: We study zeroth-order optimization of non-convex functions with the aid of directional hints, which are cheap but potentially inaccurate approximations of the true gradient direction, given by linear subspaces at each iteration. To leverage these hints adaptively while maintaining robustness to their quality, we introduce Control-Variate Zeroth-Order Descent (CV-ZOD), a new framework that refines the classical zeroth-order gradient estimator with a control variate that can be set based on the directional hints. We first show that the oracle algorithm that optimally sets the reference vector and step size at each iteration achieves a convergence rate that interpolates between the first-order $O(1/T)$ rate and the zeroth-order $O(d/T)$ rate, depending on the quality of the hints along the trajectory. We then develop a practical variant of CV-ZOD that achieves the same oracle guarantee up to logarithmic factors, without any prior knowledge of the hint quality. We validate the method empirically on simulation-based scientific optimization tasks, demonstrating sustained progress on non-convex landscapes where zeroth-order descent is slower and existing guided methods stall as guidance deteriorates.

</details>


### [244] [Steering Under Compression: Dose-Response, Capability Cost, and Failure Asymmetry in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06473)
*Saurav Bhandari,Benjamin Wade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了激活导向（activation steering）在仅权重量化（INT8和NF4）条件下的行为模式。发现情感导向在量化后完好保留，而推理长度导向表现出不对称的剂量-反应关系：延长是渐进的但最终崩溃，缩短是阶跃函数且容易失败。研究还揭示了一个方法论陷阱（朴素iso-effect阶梯在具有下限的目标上锚定在崩溃底线上），并引入审查构造方法来恢复可解释的交叉点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活导向和量化推理在实际部署中越来越多地同时使用，但两者之间的相互作用尚未被系统研究。理解这种交互对于确保量化模型仍能可靠地进行行为控制至关重要。

Method: 使用iso-effect框架在匹配行为效果的条件下比较能力成本。在四种7-9B开源模型上、于INT8和NF4仅权重量化条件下，分别针对情感导向和推理长度导向两个行为目标进行系统实验。同时纠正了一个GSM8K解析器伪影，引入审查（censored）构造方法，并预注册了三标签等效判定规则。

Result: 情感导向在量化下保持不变（INT8对比-0.010，等效）；推理长度表现出不对称剂量-反应：延长渐进而终崩溃，缩短为阶跃函数且只能缩短12-30%即失效。Mistral-NF4存在显著基线能力下降（0.545到0.365）。尽管行为方向在量化中保留（余弦相似度0.989-0.998 INT8，0.945-0.990 NF4），压缩可能主导导向干预的效果。

Conclusion: 激活导向的"行为方向"在量化后能可靠保留，但"成本结构"会改变。不同行为目标（情感vs推理长度）对量化的鲁棒性存在本质差异，推理长度导向更容易受量化影响。研究还暴露并修复了iso-effect框架在floor-bounded目标上的方法论缺陷。

Abstract: Inference-time activation steering enables behavioral control of large language models without parameter modification, while post-training quantization reduces memory and compute costs for deployment. Despite their growing convergence in practice, the interaction between these two techniques remains uncharacterized. We systematically study activation steering under weight-only quantization (INT8 and NF4) across four open-weight 7-9B models and two behavioral targets: judged sentiment and judge-free reasoning length. Using an iso-effect framework that compares capability costs at matched behavioral effect, we find that sentiment steering survives quantization intact. After correcting a GSM8K parser artifact with a uniform v2.3.1 rescore, the pooled INT8 contrast is -0.010 (90% CI [-0.026, +0.007]), descriptively Equivalent under the preregistered three-label rule, while NF4 remains Inconclusive at -0.017 ([-0.067, +0.033]). In contrast, reasoning length exhibits a surprising asymmetric dose-response: lengthening is graded but terminates in cap-runaway and collapse, while shortening is a step function with only 12-30% shortening (model-dependent) before discontinuous failure. We expose a methodological pitfall: the naive iso-effect ladder anchors on the collapse floor for floor-bounded targets, and we introduce a censored construction that restores interpretable crossings. We also quantify a substantial baseline capability shift for Mistral-NF4 (0.545 to 0.365 GSM8K at alpha=0), demonstrating that compression can dominate the steering intervention. Despite this, steering vectors remain highly collinear with their FP16 siblings (cosine similarity 0.989-0.998 for INT8, 0.945-0.990 for NF4), confirming that the behavioral direction survives quantization even when the cost structure does not. All code and data are released.

</details>


### [245] [PAC-Bayesian Bounds for Learning Partially Observed Stochastic Linear Time-Invariant State-Space Systems with Inputs and Sub-Gaussian Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08740)
*Mihaly Petreczky,Mohamad Al Ahdab,John Leth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了部分可观测的线性时不变（LTI）随机动态系统（状态空间形式、含输入及次高斯噪声）的PAC-Bayesian误差界，将预测误差期望与学习数据上的预测误差联系起来，并可进一步推导参数估计误差的有限样本界，适用于广泛的系统辨识算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中PAC-Bayesian界广泛用于刻画从有限数据学习到的模型的预测能力，但这类界在动态系统/系统辨识领域尚缺乏系统的推导。作者旨在为部分可观测的LTI随机动态系统提供rigor的有限样本理论保证。

Method: 基于PAC-Bayesian理论框架，对部分可观测的LTI状态空间模型（含输入与控制项、次高斯噪声假设）推导预测误差的PAC-Bayesian上界；利用该结果进一步建立参数估计误差的界，从而为系统辨识算法提供有限样本误差保证。

Result: 得到了部分可观测LTI系统的PAC-Bayesian预测误差界，并证明该界可用于导出参数估计误差的界，进而为一大类系统辨识算法提供预测误差和参数估计误差的有限样本界。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了PAC-Bayesian理论在动态系统辨识领域的空白；由于LTI系统是RNN的子类，该结果可作为迈向RNN的PAC-Bayesian界的初步步骤。

Abstract: In this paper we derive a Probably Approximately Correct (PAC)-Bayesian error bound for partially observed linear time-invariant (LTI) stochastic dynamical systems in state-space form with inputs and sub-Gaussian noise. Such bounds are widespread in machine learning, and they are useful for characterizing the predictive power of models learned from finitely many data points. The bound derived in this paper relates the expectation of prediction errors with the prediction error generated by the model on the data used for learning. In addition, we show that it can also be used to derive bounds for the parameter estimation error. In turn, this allows us to provide finite-sample error bounds for the prediction error and parameter estimation error for a wide class of system identification algorithms. Furthermore, as LTI systems are a sub-class of recurrent neural networks (RNNs), these error bounds could be a first step towards PAC-Bayesian bounds for RNNs.

</details>


### [246] [Learning Kernels by Alignment for Multiclass Bayes Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06474)
*Hollan Haule,Alfredo Gonzalez-Sulser,Javier Escudero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过CLaI框架证明协作学习可视为核对齐过程、协作推理等价于基于Parzen窗密度估计的核贝叶斯分类，并将余弦相似度推广为学习得到的马氏距离，扩展到多分类场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核方法需要预先选择核函数，而作者希望证明核可以通过对齐过程学习得到，从而不需要人工预设核。

Method: 将CLaI重新诠释为核对齐与核贝叶斯分类的统一框架；将余弦相似度替换为学习的马氏距离；扩展到多分类设置。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、PathMNIST和SleepEDF上，马氏距离版本相比余弦版提高了准确率、更快收敛、校准误差更低；辅助实验显示CLaI产生与高斯过程同型的潜在信号。

Conclusion: 建立了统一表示学习、核对齐与贝叶斯分类的可学习核框架，自然支持多分类。

Abstract: Kernel methods separate data representation from decision-making, but typically require the kernel to be chosen in advance. We show that this kernel can instead be learned by alignment, and develop the resulting framework through the recently introduced Collaborative Learning and Inference (CLaI). We show that Collaborative Learning can be viewed as a kernel alignment process, in which an embedding is trained so that its induced similarity matches a label-derived target kernel. We also prove that Collaborative Inference is equivalent to kernel Bayes classification with Parzen-window density estimation. Motivated by these perspectives, we generalise CLaI by replacing cosine similarity with a learned Mahalanobis distance and extend it to multiclass classification. On CIFAR-10, PathMNIST, and SleepEDF, the Mahalanobis formulation improves accuracy, converges faster, and yields lower calibration error than the cosine-based variant. Auxiliary experiments further support these connections, showing that CLaI produces latent signals of the same form as a Gaussian process, while achieving competitive calibration on sepsis prediction. Together, these results establish a principled learned-kernel framework that unifies representation learning, kernel alignment, and Bayesian classification, and extends naturally to the multiclass setting.

</details>


### [247] [How Does Parameter Pruning Reshape DNN Representations? An Interaction-Driven Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06483)
*Fangbo Li,Junpeng Zhang,Qihan Ren,Quanshi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同参数被剪枝时导致深度神经网络（DNN）性能退化程度差异的内部因素。通过考察剪枝操作如何影响DNN编码的交互模式，发现随着剪枝比例逐渐增大，DNN编码的交互模式表现出明显的三阶段动态：模型性能在大幅下降之前不受显著影响，直到剪枝开始移除低阶交互；低阶交互具有强泛化性。DNN性能对某些模块剪枝的高敏感性归因于剪枝是否移除了可泛化的低阶交互模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解DNN中不同参数被剪枝时性能退化程度差异巨大的内在原因，即为什么剪枝某些参数会导致显著性能退化，而剪枝其他参数却影响不大。

Method: 通过分析剪枝操作如何影响DNN所编码的交互模式来阐释性能退化现象，观察逐步增大剪枝比例时交互模式的动态变化规律。

Result: 发现DNN编码的交互模式在剪枝过程中呈现三阶段动态：性能在低阶交互被移除前不受显著影响；低阶交互表现出强泛化性；DNN对某些模块剪枝的高敏感性取决于剪枝是否移除了具有泛化性的低阶交互模式。

Conclusion: DNN剪枝导致性能退化的关键原因在于是否破坏了可泛化的低阶交互模式，而非笼统地取决于被剪枝的参数数量或位置。这一发现为理解剪枝敏感性提供了基于交互模式的理论解释。

Abstract: This study focuses on the scientific problem of understanding internal factors that govern the diverse performance degradation of deep neural networks (DNNs) when different parameters are pruned. In order to explain why pruning certain parameters leads to significant performance degradation but pruning other parameters does not, we examine how the pruning operation affects the interaction patterns encoded by the DNN. We find that when we progressively increase the pruning ratio, the interaction patterns encoded by DNNs exhibit a distinct three-phase dynamics, \emph{i.e.}, model performance is not largely affected until the pruning operation begins to remove low-order interactions, and low-order interactions exhibit strong generalizability. Moreover, we find that the high sensitivity of DNN performance to the pruning of certain modules is attributed to whether the pruning operation removes generalizable low-order interaction patterns.

</details>


### [248] [Second-Order Smooth Planning with Optimal-Transport Bellman Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06484)
*Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SecondOrderSmoothCruiser（二阶平滑巡洋舰）算法，通过将最优传输平滑化引入熵正则化Bellman备份的动作分布上，利用二阶（三次余项）Taylor正则性，将SmoothCruiser类规划器的oracle复杂度从一阶（β=2）的Õ(ε⁻⁴)提升到二阶（β=3）的Õ(ε⁻³)，并建立了熵正则化、最优传输正则化与无正则化目标之间的显式正则化偏差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于生成模型进行规划时，希望以尽可能少的模拟器调用次数来估计状态价值。SmoothCruiser通过利用熵正则化Bellman备份的光滑性实现了与问题无关的Õ(ε⁻⁴)复杂度，但其估计器仅为一阶精度，这限制了其采样效率，因此需要更高阶的方法来进一步降低oracle复杂度。

Method: 首先从理论上证明SmoothCruiser型规划器的样本复杂度指数由局部Taylor余项阶数β决定，一般化复杂度为Õ(ε^{-(2+2/(β-1))})。随后提出在动作分布上使用最优传输光滑化的Bellman备份，该备份具有闭式表达式、策略梯度和Lipschitz Hessian，其二次修正项可被无偏叉积估计器估计，从而实现β=3的立方余项，构成SecondOrderSmoothCruiser算法。
