Abstract: We propose a class of high-order fully-discrete entropy stable (ES) explicit discontinuous Galerkin (DG) solvers for the compressible ideal magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) equations. Our main theoretical contribution is the introduction of a novel generalized-path-decomposition framework for MHD equations in Godunov's symmetric form. By innovatively interpreting the interior volume integral of the non-conservative source term as a path integral along a generalized path constructed by the solution polynomial, we establish the weak cell entropy inequality for the fully-discrete DG schemes. This overarching framework also accommodates other existing DG solvers based on the symmetric form. Combined with a carefully designed ES limiter, the proposed scheme satisfies the genuine fully-discrete cell entropy inequality. With this property, a Lax--Wendroff-type theorem can be obtained to show that the solution limit satisfies the entropy condition. Finally, the scheme is naturally compatible with the locally divergence-free space. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the scheme's low numerical dissipation and strong robustness.

</details>


### [285] [A covariance-based reduced-order framework for solving acoustic scattering problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07083)
*Shiwei Sun,Hai Zhang,Jinrui Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理感知的降阶模型（ROM）方法，用于高效计算波散射问题。该方法利用感应对比源密度的内在低秩结构，将入射波建模为服从指定先验概率度量的随机变量，将对比源重构为空间随机场，并通过Karhunen-Loève（KL）展开构建降阶基。为克服高保真协方差矩阵的组装成本，作者引入了一种非侵入式、物理信息驱动的图神经网络（GNN）代理模型，学习从散射体几何到源相关核的映射，实现高效的离线-在线计算范式。大量数值实验表明该框架在不牺牲精度的前提下实现了稳健的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型降阶技术通常将散射问题视为通用参数化系统，忽视了其内在的物理结构，导致实际应用中效率受限。作者旨在开发一种利用散射问题固有物理低秩结构的降阶算法框架，以克服传统ROM方法的局限性。

Method: 提出一个算法框架：(1) 将入射波建模为带先验概率度量的随机变量，把对比源密度视为捕捉空间相关性和物理交互的空间随机场；(2) 通过KL展开构造降阶基以提取散射过程的主导特征；(3) 引入非侵入式、物理信息驱动的GNN代理来学习从散射体几何到源相关核的复杂映射，避免高昂的高保真协方差矩阵组装成本，实现高效的离线-在线计算范式。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，所提出的框架在与全阶模型相比不牺牲精度的前提下，实现了稳健的计算加速。

Conclusion: 该物理感知降阶方法有效利用了波散射问题中的物理低秩结构，并通过GNN代理架构解决了协方差矩阵组装的计算瓶颈，为大规模散射配置提供了高效且准确的计算方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a physics-aware reduced-order method (ROM) for the efficient computation of wave-scattering problems. Standard model order reduction techniques, typically treating scattering as generic parameterized systems, frequently overlook the underlying physical structure, limiting their effectiveness in practice. To address this limitation, we propose an algorithmic framework that utilizes the intrinsic low-rank structure of the induced contrast source density. By modeling the incident wave as a random variable governed by a specified prior probability measure, we formulate the contrast source as a spatial random field whose covariance function captures essential spatial correlation and physical interactions. The reduced-order basis is then constructed via the Karhunen-Loève (KL) expansion, effectively extracting the dominant features from the scattering process to resolve multiple scattering scenarios. A central algorithmic contribution is the efficient reconstruction of the covariance matrix for arbitrary scatterer geometries and specified incident wave priors. To circumvent the prohibitive computational cost of assembling high-fidelity covariance matrices, we introduce a non-intrusive, physics-informed graph neural network (GNN) surrogate to learn the complex mapping from scatterer geometry to the source correlation kernel, enabling a highly efficient offline-online computational paradigm suitable for large-scale scattering configurations. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves robust computational acceleration over full-order models without sacrificing accuracy.

</details>


### [286] [On Prior-to-Posterior Stability in the Wasserstein Metric for Bayesian Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07197)
*Lianghao Cao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯逆问题中先验到后验映射的稳定性，在先验和后验扰动均用同一Wasserstein度量W_p（p≥1）衡量时，给出了保证一致、Hölder和Lipschitz稳定性的条件，并通过两层Darcy逆问题的数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯逆问题中的先验通常通过离散化、超参数估计或生成建模来近似，理解先验近似误差如何传播到后验及后续预测非常重要，但目前缺乏在统一Wasserstein度量下对先验到后验映射稳定性的系统研究。

Method: 在W_p度量下分析先验到后验映射的稳定性：1）对有界且在有界集上一致连续的似然函数及具有一致可积p阶矩和共同正证据下界的先验类，证明一致稳定性；2）利用耦合论证，在似然全局Hölder正则性和先验高阶矩有界条件下获得具最优指数的Hölder估计；3）通过插值论证，在一致Poincaré界和全局Lipschitz负对数似然（其本质振荡在先验下一致有界）条件下建立Lipschitz稳定性。

Result: 发现对于p>1，Lipschitz似然不一定给出Lipschitz稳定性，即使先验有有界支撑也是如此。对可分离Hilbert空间上的高斯先验、有界Lipschitz正演模型和加性高斯噪声，所得估计给出了后验W_2界，即使先验扰动彼此奇异也成立。两层Darcy逆问题的数值实验验证了预测的Hölder和Lipschitz速率，以及Lipschitz感兴趣量的后验均值和标准差误差的可控性。

Conclusion: 本文系统地刻画了贝叶斯逆问题中先验到后验映射在W_p度量下的稳定性条件，揭示了不同似然正则性和先验类假设下所能达到的稳定性阶次，为理解先验近似误差在贝叶斯逆问题中的传播提供了理论基础和实用指导。

Abstract: Priors in Bayesian inverse problems are often approximated through discretization, hyperparameter estimation, or generative modeling. Understanding how prior approximation errors propagate to the posterior and subsequent predictions is therefore important. In this work, we study the stability of the prior-to-posterior map where both prior and posterior perturbations are measured in the same Wasserstein metric $W_p$, $p\geq1$. We identify conditions on likelihood regularity and admissible prior classes that ensure uniform, Hölder, and Lipschitz stability. For bounded likelihoods that are uniformly continuous on bounded sets, uniform stability holds over prior classes with uniformly integrable $p$-th moments and a common positive evidence lower bound. With global Hölder regularity of the likelihood and uniform bounds on higher prior moments, a coupling argument leads to a Hölder estimate with a sharp exponent. For $p>1$, a Lipschitz likelihood need not give Lipschitz stability, even for priors with bounded support. We establish Lipschitz stability through an interpolation argument under uniform Poincaré bounds and a globally Lipschitz negative log-likelihood with uniformly bounded essential oscillation under the priors. For Gaussian priors on separable Hilbert spaces and bounded Lipschitz forward models with additive Gaussian noise, these estimates give posterior $W_2$ bounds even for mutually singular prior perturbations. Numerical experiments for a two-layer Darcy inverse problem illustrate the predicted Hölder and Lipschitz rates and the resulting control of errors in the posterior mean and standard deviation of a Lipschitz quantity of interest.

</details>


### [287] [Analysis of the Ill-Conditioning of the Discrete Inverse Laplace transform in Monte Carlo Simulations of Quantum Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07208)
*Phil-Alexander Hofmann,Thomas Chuna,Tobias Dornheim,Michael Hecht*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了从虚时量子蒙特卡罗数据解析延拓到实频谱这一问题中，连续逆双侧拉普拉斯变换的内在病态性与有限维离散化条件数之间的关系。通过将离散问题转化为对角缩放的单变量Vandermonde系统（以及施加细致平衡对称后的Chebyshev-Vandermonde系统），作者推导了条件数的显式上下界，揭示了超指数增长行为，并证明低维表示与细致平衡对称能显著缓解病态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析延拓是将虚时量子蒙特卡罗数据转换为实频谱的关键步骤，但本质上涉及严重不适应（ill-posed）的双侧拉普拉斯逆变换。实践中虽然常用低维谱表示和细致平衡对称来获得稳定结果，但其数学机理尚不清楚。作者旨在严格区分连续问题的内在病态性与有限维离散化的条件数，并从数学上解释为何这些经验方法有效。

Method: 作者将等距采样与重构网格下的离散逆问题表达为对角缩放的单变量（monomial）Vandermonde矩阵问题，其中节点呈指数分布；施加物理细致平衡对称后转化为对角缩放的Chebyshev-Vandermonde系统。利用这两类矩阵的代数结构，推导出条件数关于物理参数与离散参数的显式上下界，并分析其在不同参数区间的渐近行为。

Result: （1）无约束离散化的条件数随重构维数呈超指数增长，且仅增加虚时采样点无法消除；（2）细致平衡对称能显著改善条件数，尤其在预渐近（pre-asymptotic）区域效果明显，但渐近超指数依赖仍然存在；（3）两种情形下均存在低维区间，其中超指数贡献被抑制，稳定重构可行。

Conclusion: 该工作从数学上解释了低维谱表示和细致平衡对称在解析延拓中有效的原因：虽然无法消除问题固有的不适应本质，但能显著缓解有限维离散化的不适定程度，推迟灾难性超指数增长的起始点，从而扩大数值上可访问的重构范围。

Abstract: Analytic continuation of imaginary-time quantum Monte Carlo data to real-frequency spectra requires the inversion of a severely ill-posed two-sided Laplace transform and arises naturally in quantum many-body calculations of dynamic properties. In this work, we distinguish the intrinsic ill-posedness of the continuous inverse two-sided Laplace problem from the conditioning of its finite-dimensional discretization. For equidistant sampling and reconstruction grids, we express the discrete problem via a diagonally scaled monomial Vandermonde matrix with exponentially distributed nodes. Imposing the physical detailed-balance symmetry transforms the discretization into a diagonally scaled Chebyshev-Vandermonde system. Exploiting these structures, we derive explicit lower and upper bounds on the condition numbers in terms of the physical and discretization parameters. For the unconstrained discretization, our bounds reveal super-exponential growth of the condition number with the reconstruction dimension, which cannot be removed by increasing the number of imaginary-time samples alone. Detailed-balance substantially improves the conditioning, especially in the practically relevant pre-asymptotic regime, although the asymptotic super-exponential dependence remains. In both cases, our bounds identify a low-dimensional regime in which the super-exponential contribution is suppressed and stable reconstruction remains feasible. These results provide a mathematical explanation for the effectiveness of low-dimensional spectral representations and detailed-balance in analytic continuation. While they do not remove the fundamental ill-posedness, they substantially mitigate the ill-conditioning of its finite-dimensional discretization, delay the onset of its catastrophic super-exponential growth and thereby enlarge the range of numerically accessible reconstructions.

</details>


### [288] [Frequency Domain Biot--Allard Equations for Isotropic and Anisotropic Poroelastic Media: Two-field formulations and iterative splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07388)
*Morten Jakobsen,Jakob Seierstad Stokke,Kundan Kumar,Florin Adrian Radu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Biot–Allard动态孔隙弹性方程的一种频域表述，适用于具有记忆效应的各向异性非均匀介质。通过两种等价的二场表示（位移-压力与速度-压力率），建立了稳定的迭代分裂方案，并在拟静态极限下恢复了斜对称耦合结构。频域设置消除了卷积记忆项，将时变问题转化为一族便于并行计算的椭圆边值问题，并通过共形Galerkin有限元离散和伪谱大尺度波动模拟验证了方法的鲁棒性与频率相关衰减的捕捉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Biot动态孔隙弹性方程在描述含衰减与色散的多孔介质波传播中至关重要，但其时域形式包含卷积记忆项和复杂的耦合算子，为数值求解和反演带来了挑战。作者旨在发展一种能够在频域中严格建模并高效求解的框架，以消除记忆卷积、简化算子结构，并支持大规模并行计算与多频反演。

Method: 采用频域中的Biot–Allard方程，引入频率相关耗散。提出两种等价的二场变分表示：位移-压力形式与速度-压力率形式。借助后者在拟静态极限下恢复的斜对称结构，设计了L-稳定迭代分裂格式。理论部分证明了耦合算子的连续性与对角块的强制性；离散上采用共形Galerkin有限元保持块结构，另用伪谱方法演示离散无关性的大规模波动模拟。

Result: 数值实验确认了该方法对频率相关衰减的鲁棒捕捉能力；大尺度伪谱波动模拟展示了离散方案独立性。理论上证明了分裂格式收敛所需的关键算子性质（耦合算子连续、对角块强制、拟静态极限下斜对称结构恢复）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了在复杂多孔介质中模拟波动现象的严格且高效的框架，兼顾了理论完备性与计算可行性，特别适合并行计算和多频反演场景。

Abstract: We present a frequency-domain formulation of Biot's dynamic poroelastic equations with frequency-dependent dissipation (Biot--Allard) for anisotropic, heterogeneous media with memory effects. Two equivalent two-field representations--a displacement-pressure and a velocity-pressure-rate formulation--enable stabilized iterative splitting. While coupling operators generally lack an adjoint or skew-adjoint relationship at finite frequencies, the velocity--pressure-rate representation restores a skew-adjoint structure in the quasi-static limit. We prove continuity of the coupling operators and coercivity of the diagonal blocks, essential for convergence of the L-stabilized splitting scheme. The frequency-domain setting eliminates convolutional memory terms, incorporates attenuation and dispersion via complex-valued parameters, and reduces the time-dependent problem to a family of elliptic boundary-value problems suited for parallel computation and multi-frequency inversion. A conforming Galerkin finite element discretization preserves block structure, and numerical experiments confirm robustness and capture frequency-dependent attenuation. To illustrate discretization independence, we include a large-scale wave simulation using a pseudo-spectral method. This work provides a rigorous and efficient framework for modeling wave phenomena in complex porous media.

</details>


### [289] [Analysis of a Surface Crouzeix-Raviart Element for the Stokes Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07527)
*Manisha Chowdhury,Carolin Mehlmann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究三维空间中二维曲面上的表面Stokes系统，采用非协调Crouzeix-Raviart有限元与分片常数压力元进行离散。针对非协调空间上离散Korn不等式不成立导致的伪振荡问题，引入边跳惩罚项恢复双线性项的强制性与离散Korn不等式，并证明该速度-压力元对满足离散inf-sup条件，同时推导出能量范数与L^2范数下的最优先验误差估计，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲面上向量值流动问题的有限元离散日益受到关注，但非协调Crouzeix-Raviart元逼近曲面Stokes流时，因对称应变率张量的离散Korn不等式不成立，会产生速度场的伪振荡，亟需有效的稳定化策略。

Method: 在曲面的多面体近似上使用非协调Crouzeix-Raviart有限元逼近速度场，分片常数函数离散压力。通过添加边跳惩罚项稳定动量方程，恢复双线性项的强制性，使稳定化版本满足离散Korn不等式；同时验证速度-压力离散空间对满足inf-sup兼容性条件。

Result: 建立了稳定化离散格式的强制性与离散Korn不等式，证明了离散inf-sup条件成立，并推导出能量范数与L^2范数下的最优先验误差估计，数值实验证实了理论分析结果。

Conclusion: 边跳惩罚稳定化的Crouzeix-Raviart元结合分片常数压力元是曲面Stokes问题的一种稳定且收敛的离散方案，具有最优阶误差收敛表现。

Abstract: Recently, increasing attention has been paid to finite element discretizations of vector-valued flow problems posed on curved surfaces. In this work, we study a surface Stokes system defined on a two-dimensional manifold embedded in three-dimensional space. The surface velocity field is approximated using a nonconforming Crouzeix-Raviart finite element on a polyhedral approximation of the surface, while the pressure is discretized by piecewise constant functions. The governing equations involve the symmetric strain-rate tensor, whose approximation with Crouzeix-Raviart finite elements leads to spurious oscillations in the velocity field because the discrete Korn inequality fails on the nonconforming space. We therefore stabilize the momentum equation by adding an edge-jump penalty term, which restores the coervity of the bilinear term as the discrete Korn inequality holds for this stabilized version. Additionally, we establish that this finite element pair of velocity and pressure spaces satisfies the discrete inf-sup compatibility condition. Furthermore, we derive optimal a-priori error estimates in both the energy norm and the $L^2$-norm. These theoretical results are corroborated by numerical experiments.

</details>


### [290] [A numerical method for two-dimensional Boussinesq-Full Dispersion systems modelling internal wave propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07659)
*Angel Durán*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对两层流体系统中内波传播的二维渐近模型，研究了周期初值问题的适定性，分析了基于谱Fourier-Galerkin方法的半离散近似的收敛性，并用隐式中点法则进行时间离散，最后通过数值实验验证了全离散格式的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内波在两层流体系统中的传播是一个重要的物理现象，其数值模拟需要可靠的数学基础。本文旨在为这类二维渐近模型建立严格的理论保证（适定性与收敛性），并设计有效的全离散数值格式。

Method: 首先研究二维两层系统中内波渐近模型（关于界面位移和速度的PDE）在周期初值条件下的适定性；随后采用谱Fourier-Galerkin方法进行空间半离散并分析收敛性；再对半离散系统使用隐式中点法则进行时间积分，形成全离散格式。

Result: 建立了周期初值问题的适定性结果，证明了谱Fourier-Galerkin半离散近似的收敛性，并刻画了隐式中点法则全离散格式的若干性质，数值实验展示了该格式的良好性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为两层系统中内波传播渐近模型提供了完整的理论分析与可靠的数值方案，谱Galerkin空间离散结合隐式中点时间离散在全离散层面具有良好性能。

Abstract: The paper is concerned with the numerical approximation of some two-dimensional asymptotic models for the propagation of internal waves in a two-layer system in the form of pde's for the interfacial deviation and the velocity. After the study of well-posedness of the periodic initial-value problem, the convergence of a semidiscrete approximation based on a spectral Fourier-Galerkin method is analyzed. The resulting semidiscrete system is then integrated numerically in time with the implicit midpoint rule. Some properties of the fully discrete scheme are described and its performance is illustrated with some numerical experiments.

</details>


### [291] [Mapping for Approximation: A Unified View of Rescaled, Variably Scaled and Rational Kernel Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07743)
*Stefano De Marchi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的视角：在保持采样值不变的前提下，通过对表示空间施加合适的映射来改进经典近似方法，并统一了残差径向基函数插值、变尺度核、映射基与伪节点、有理核展开等多种此前独立提出的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典近似方法通常通过改变采样集、增加数据点或更换基函数来改进，但作者提出一种互补观点：保持采样值固定，仅通过适当映射修改表示方式。这一观点能统一多个原本为不同目的引入的构造，并揭示其共性原理。

Method: 作者为这些映射机制建立了统一的数学记号框架，并分别分析其逼近性质与稳定性。具体涵盖：通过归一化强制精确复现常数的残差RBF插值；通过尺度函数将数据提升到高维流形以改变几何的变尺度核；不重新采样而映射逼近空间的映射基与伪节点；以及可视为试验空间非线性映射的有理核展开。同时解释了不连续与有理变体如何融入同一框架。

Result: 可复现的数值实验展示了三方面效果：常数精确复现、通过数据模仿尺度函数降低误差、以及映射多项式基对Runge现象的抑制。实验结果支持一个一般性原理：映射提供了一种灵活的方式来使逼近空间适应数据几何与正则性，而无需修改原始观测数据。

Conclusion: 映射方法是一种不改变原始数据即可灵活调整逼近空间以适应数据几何与正则性的通用手段，统一了多种现有构造并为后续研究提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: Classical approximation methods are usually improved by changing the sampling set, increasing the number of data points, or selecting a different basis. We advocate a complementary viewpoint: keep the sampled values fixed and modify the representation through a suitable mapping. This viewpoint unifies several constructions that were originally introduced for different purposes. Rescaled radial basis function interpolation maps the interpolation operator through a normalization that enforces exact reproduction of constants. Variably scaled kernels map the geometry by lifting the data to a higherdimensional manifold determined by a scale function. Mapped bases and fake nodes map the approximation space without resampling the data, while rational kernel expansions can be interpreted as nonlinear mappings of the trial space. We develop a common notation for these mechanisms, summarize their approximation and stability properties, and explain how discontinuous and rational variants fit the same framework. Reproducible numerical experiments illustrate constant reproduction, error reduction through data-mimicking scale functions, and the suppression of the Runge phenomenon by mapped polynomial bases. The results support a general principle: mappings provide a flexible way to adapt approximation spaces to data geometry and regularity without modifying the original observations

</details>


### [292] [LoCCA: Localized Chebyshev Cross Approximation for Kernel Matrix Factorization via Nodal Perturbation Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07882)
*Sumit Singh,Shrirup Dutta,Sivaram Ambikasaran*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出Local Chebyshev Cross-Approximation (LoCCA)框架，将光滑多项式插值与灵活矩阵分解相结合。LoCCA动态地将理想网格节点映射到非结构化点集中的最近物理邻居，并通过新的扰动理论证明将Chebyshev节点置换到物理数据点上仍能保持数学稳定性与精度。数值实验表明LoCCA在不规则几何上提供严格可靠的误差控制，实现显著加速和近最优矩阵压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则网格的方法在处理不规则几何和非结构化点集时失效或误差不可控，而灵活的矩阵分解方法又缺乏严格的理论保证。需要一种数据驱动的框架，能够在不规则几何上同时兼顾光滑多项式插值的理论稳健性（如Chebyshev节点）与矩阵分解的灵活性和压缩效率。

Method: LoCCA框架的核心思路是将理想Chebyshev网格节点动态映射到非结构化点集中距离最近的物理邻居上。作者提出新的扰动理论分析，证明对Chebyshev节点进行位置扰动（置换到物理数据点）不会导致误差爆炸，即保证数值稳定性和精度保持。该框架结合光滑多项式插值的理论优势与灵活的矩阵因子分解的自适应性。

Result: 数值实验表明LoCCA在不规则几何上能够提供严格且可靠的误差控制，而传统基于网格的方法在这些场景下失效。LoCCA实现了显著的加速效果，并达到接近最优的矩阵压缩比。

Conclusion: LoCCA是一种兼具理论保障与实用价值的通用框架，扩展了Chebyshev插值的适用范围至任意非结构化点集，在不规则问题上兼顾精度、稳定性与计算效率，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We propose Local Chebyshev Cross-Approximation (LoCCA), a data-driven framework that bridges smooth polynomial interpolation with flexible matrix factorizations. LoCCA dynamically maps ideal grid nodes to their nearest physical neighbors within unstructured point sets. We support this framework with a new perturbation theory, proving that displacing Chebyshev nodes onto physical data points retains mathematical stability and accuracy without explosive error growth. Numerically, LoCCA provides strict, reliable error control even on irregular geometries where standard grid-based methods fail, achieving substantial speedups and near-optimal matrix compression.

</details>


### [293] [Threshold dynamics for subdiffusive grain growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07938)
*Marvin Fritz*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种新的分数阶阈值动力学算法，用于次扩散界面运动和多相晶粒生长模拟。该方法将Caputo时间导数的L1离散与Helmholtz-resolvent阈值化步骤相结合，通过L1卷积产生凸历史平均，再经Helmholtz扩散求解和阈值投影。当α=1时退化为Merriman-Bence-Osher格式的Helmholtz-resolvent类比；0<α<1时引入次扩散时间记忆。建立了变分刻画、极大值原理与比较原理及稳定性，并导出记忆尾估计和有限维钉扎判据。数值实验显示较小的分数阶在测试区间内导致更快的粗化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究次扩散条件下的界面运动与晶粒生长动力学，扩展经典阈值动力学（如MBO方案）至分数阶次扩散框架，并系统分析其数值性质与粗化行为。

Method: 将Caputo时间导数的L1离散化（产生凸历史平均）与Helmholtz-resolvent阈值化步骤结合；数学上建立变分刻画、极大/比较原理与稳定性；推导记忆尾估计与有限维钉扎判据（基于显式resolvent-压缩假设）；多相等张力方案在周期网格上实现。

Result: 理论上建立了该算法的变分框架、极大值与比较原理、稳定性，以及记忆尾估计和钉扎判据。数值上，固定步长的实验中较小分数阶α导致更快的粗化速率；固定光滑长度的比较揭示了L1历史对后续更新的影响。

Conclusion: 该分数阶阈值动力学算法在次扩散界面运动中是有效且稳定的；分数阶参数可通过时间记忆显著调控晶粒粗化动力学，这在测试区间内表现为更小的α加速粗化。

Abstract: We propose a new fractional threshold-dynamics algorithm for subdiffusive interface motion and apply it to multiphase grain-growth simulations. The method combines the L1 discretization of the Caputo time derivative with a Helmholtz-resolvent thresholding step. At each time level, the L1 convolution produces a convex history average of the previously thresholded states; this history-dependent field is then propagated by a Helmholtz diffusion solve and projected back to pure phases by thresholding. For $α=1$, the method reduces to a Helmholtz-resolvent analogue of the Merriman-Bence-Osher scheme, whereas for $0<α<1$ it introduces a subdiffusive temporal memory. We establish a variational characterization, maximum and comparison principles, and stability for the continuous Helmholtz diffusion step. We also derive a memory-tail estimate and a finite-dimensional pinning criterion under an explicit resolvent-contraction hypothesis. The equal-tension multiphase scheme is implemented on periodic grids. The numerical experiments investigate the joint influence of the L1 history and the smoothing length. The fixed-step runs show faster coarsening for smaller fractional orders in the tested regime, while a comparison at fixed smoothing length examines the influence of the L1 history on successive updates.

</details>


### [294] [A third-medium approach for electro-thermo-mechanical contact considering Joule heating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08031)
*Maximilian Vorwerk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于第三介质概念的电-热-力接触公式，在统一有限元框架内无需显式接触面追踪即可模拟机械接触、电流传递和热传导的耦合过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接器在技术系统中普遍存在，其机械接触常与电流传递和热产生相互耦合。现有方法难以在同一框架内统一描述这些交互过程，因此需要一种无需显式接触面追踪的耦合接触公式。

Method: 采用第三介质概念建立电-热-力接触公式；通过依赖于变形的切换函数控制界面闭合时电学和热学输运的启动；电导率通过焦耳热耦合到瞬态热问题，同时电导率的温度依赖性反馈影响电流流动；位移、电势和温度场采用整体（单块）求解，并结合独立插值的变形梯度类场以实现第三介质在严重压缩下的鲁棒正则化。

Result: 数值算例展示了接触诱导的电流传递、随后的焦耳热和瞬态温度演化，以及粗糙界面渐进闭合过程中的局部电流路径。

Conclusion: 该统一有限元框架能够有效模拟电-热-力耦合接触行为，无需显式接触面追踪，适用于连接器等涉及多物理场耦合的复杂技术系统。

Abstract: Connectors are ubiquitous in technical systems and frequently combine mechanical contact with electric current transfer and heat generation. To capture these interacting processes, an electro-thermo-mechanical contact formulation based on the third-medium concept is proposed. Mechanical contact, electric current flow, and heat conduction are represented within a unified finite element framework without explicit contact-surface tracking. Deformation-dependent switching functions govern the onset of electrical and thermal transport during interface closure. Electrical conduction is coupled to the transient thermal problem through Joule heating, while the temperature dependence of the electrical conductivity provides the corresponding feedback on the current flow. The coupled displacement, electric potential, and temperature fields are solved monolithically and combined with an independently interpolated deformation-gradient-like field for robust third-medium regularization under severe compression. Numerical examples demonstrate contact-induced current transfer, subsequent Joule heating and transient temperature evolution, as well as localized current paths during progressive closure of rough interfaces.

</details>


### [295] [Finite-Precision Symmetric Krylov Methods: Exact Rounding Examples, Block Paige Identities,and a Variable-Block Lanczos Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08066)
*Mohit Sinha*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了短递归Krylov方法在浮点运算中与精确算术等价的实现可能发散的问题。具体研究了最速下降法中存储残差的递归更新与几何衰减条件、Hestenes-Stiefel共轭梯度法与直接Lanczos-Galerkin方法在浮点下的不同表现、迭代精度与收敛步数间的权衡（含可计算的停止准则），以及块Lanczos方法中Householder正交化与奇异值截断的向后误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短递归Krylov方法在精确算术中等价但在浮点运算中可能产生不同甚至发散的结果，这一数值稳定性问题对算法可靠性构成挑战。论文旨在揭示这种分歧的具体机制，并为达到指定向后误差提供精度下界与实用的停止判据。

Method: 作者构建了最速下降法递归更新残差的精确可表示二循环反例；利用互补收敛定理给出存储残差几何衰减的充分条件；对CG实现与Lanczos-Galerkin方法在浮点下的差异进行对比分析；结合多项式估计与数值实验研究精度与迭代数之间的权衡；对块Lanczos采用Householder正交化与奇异值截断，在分量（componentwise）与范数（normwise）误差界下推导受控局部递推及精确块Lanczos关系；引入块形式的Paige恒等式与克雷姆矩阵论证分析Ritz向量重叠与附近Ritz值数量。

Result: CG在四步更新后精确求解，而二步投影系统出现不一致；基于谱包络给出可比较的收敛界；对稠密矩阵从左到右求值顺序证明了n步内达到指定向后误差所需的充分精度下界及可计算停止测试；块Lanczos分析表明计算系数满足局部受控递推，在更大空间中存在附近对称问题的精确块Lanczos关系，并通过块Paige恒等式和Gram矩阵论证刻画了Ritz向量重叠演化及额外Ritz值数目。

Conclusion: 短递归Krylov方法的浮点行为本质上不同于精确算术行为，需通过明确的精度下界、可计算的停止判据和较严格的误差分析框架（如Paige与Greenbaum方法）来保障可靠性。工作精度与迭代次数之间存在可量化的权衡，块Lanczos的数值行为可由局部递推与扩展空间中的邻近对称问题精确刻画。

Abstract: Short-recurrence Krylov methods that are equivalent in exact arithmetic often diverge in floating-point arithmetic. To illustrate this, we provide an exactly representable two-cycle for steepest descent with a recursively updated residual. A complementary convergence theorem gives a sufficient condition under which the stored residual decreases geometrically. We then show that a second positive definite family yields different outcomes for a Hestenes--Stiefel Conjugate Gradient (CG) implementation and a direct Lanczos--Galerkin approach. CG finds the exact solution after four updates, whereas the two-step projected system becomes inconsistent. Under stated perturbation and transfer hypotheses, a common spectral enclosure gives comparable convergence bounds. For a dense, left-to-right evaluation order, we prove a sufficient precision bound for a prescribed backward error within $n$ updates for an $n\times n$ matrix, together with a computable stopping test and specified exponent-range assumptions. We use polynomial estimates and numerical experiments to examine the trade-off between working precision and the number of CG iterations needed to achieve a prescribed backward error. We also compare results on inexact matrix-vector products and preconditioning with the frameworks of Paige and Greenbaum.
  For block Lanczos, we analyze Householder orthogonalization and singular-value truncation as the block size changes. Under componentwise and normwise error bounds, the computed coefficients satisfy a controlled local recurrence and an exact block Lanczos relation for a nearby symmetric problem in a larger space. A block form of Paige's identity bounds the overlap with a Ritz vector along its residual coordinate direction. An inter-block recurrence describes the evolution of overlap, and a Gram-matrix argument gives a count of additional nearby Ritz values.

</details>


### [296] [Third-order Halley-type iterative method with positive and bounded correction function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08110)
*Hirai Mukasa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种新的单点迭代方法，通过修改Halley方法求解非线性方程的简单根。修正函数在实数域上有界且为正，在简单根附近与Halley修正函数的局部展开匹配，避免了Halley校正函数的奇异性和符号反转，同时至少保持三阶局部收敛。数值实验和van der Waals方程应用验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Halley方法虽然具有三阶收敛性，但其校正函数在求解过程中可能出现奇异点（分母为零）和符号反转问题，导致迭代失败或不稳定。作者旨在设计一种修正的Halley型方法，保留其局部高收敛阶的同时消除奇异性和符号反转，并建立全局收敛的充分条件。

Method: 修改Halley方法的校正函数：构造一个在实数域上正值且有界的校正函数，使其在简单根附近的局部展开与Halley校正函数一致。该方法为单点迭代法，避免了Halley校正函数的分母奇异和符号反转问题，并在理论上证明至少保持三阶局部收敛性，同时建立全局收敛性的充分条件。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法在广泛的初始猜测范围内具有较高的收敛成功率和有利的迭代次数。在Halley校正函数奇异点附近的进一步实验展示了该方法稳健的收敛行为。对van der Waals方程的应用也证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过合理地改造Halley校正函数，该方法成功克服了经典Halley方法的缺陷，在保证三阶收敛精度的前提下扩展了收敛域并提升了鲁棒性，是求解非线性方程简单根的实用且可靠的迭代方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a new one-point iterative method for finding simple roots of nonlinear equations by modifying Halley's method. Its correction function is positive and bounded on $\mathbb{R}$ and matches the local expansion of Halley's correction function near a simple root. The method avoids the singularity and sign reversal of Halley's correction function while retaining at least third-order local convergence. We also establish sufficient conditions for global convergence. Numerical experiments show that the proposed method achieves high convergence success rates and favorable iteration counts over a wide range of initial guesses. Further experiments near the singularity of Halley's correction function demonstrate robust convergence behavior of the proposed method. An application to the van der Waals equation illustrates the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [297] [Finite difference methods for three kinds of reaction-diffusion equations with free boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08212)
*Caiyun Huang,Weizhi Liao,Weiping Bu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对三类带自由边界的反应扩散方程，采用前沿固定法将其转化为固定边界问题，再利用有限差分法构造系数矩阵为M-矩阵的数值格式，并建立了包括正性保持、单调性保持和稳定性在内的数值理论，最后通过数值算例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带自由边界的反应扩散方程在诸多领域有广泛应用，但数值求解存在挑战。现有工作在讨论数值理论时往往对时间步长施加严格限制，这限制了实际应用的灵活性。因此，本文旨在构造仅需温和限制的数值格式并建立相应的数值理论。

Method: 采用前沿固定法（front-fixing method）将自由边界问题转化为固定边界问题，随后应用有限差分法构造系数矩阵为M-矩阵的数值格式，并据此建立正性保持、单调性保持及稳定性的数值理论。

Result: 所提出的数值方案对时间步长仅需温和限制即可保证数值理论的有效性，数值算例验证了所开发格式的性能并证实了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文为三类带自由边界的反应扩散方程提供了一种仅需温和时间步长限制即可满足正性保持、单调性保持与稳定性等数值性质的有限差分数值格式，克服了已有方法中时间步长限制过于严格的问题。

Abstract: This work considers to numerically solve three kinds of reaction-diffusion equations with free boundaries. First, the popular front-fixing method is used to transform the considered free boundary problems into fixed boundary problems. Then, by employing the finite difference method, numerical schemes with $\mathrm{M}$-matrices as their coefficient matrices are developed for these transformed fixed-boundary problems. Next, for the developed numerical schemes, we establish numerical theory involving positivity preservation, monotonicity preservation, and stability. It is noteworthy that, unlike some existing works that impose tight restrictions on the time step-size to discuss the numerical theory, the proposed numerical schemes require only a mild restriction. Finally, numerical examples are provided to test the developed numerical schemes and to confirm the theoretical results.

</details>


### [298] [A discontinuous finite element method for the hydroelastic analysis of submerged structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08313)
*Zachary J. Wegert,Oriol Colomés,Michael H. Meylan,Ngamta Thamwattana,Vivien J. Challis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种用于水下弹性板水弹性建模中波-结构相互作用问题的有限元方法，适用于二维和三维通道。流体部分采用对称间断伽辽金方案，板部分采用连续/间断伽辽金方法处理四阶算子，并通过Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射思想构造非局部边界条件施加Sommerfeld辐射条件。方法被证明是适定的，并以Gridap/Julia开源工具实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水弹性建模中水下弹性板与波的相互作用问题需要精确高效的数值方法，而传统方法在处理四阶偏微分方程和辐射边界条件时面临挑战，需要开发一种既能处理板位移不连续又能正确处理无界域辐射条件的数值方案。

Method: 流体部分采用对称间断伽辽金（DG）格式来捕获势函数在板处的间断性；辐射边界条件借鉴Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射方法，利用半无界域的解析解构造非局部边界条件；板部分采用连续/间断伽辽金方法求解四阶算子，避免使用C1连续有限元；采用Gridap包在Julia中实现。

Result: 证明了方法的适定性，即sesquilinear形式有界且满足Gårding不等式，从而保证解的唯一性和稳定性。通过与超奇异边界积分方法对比验证，结果高度一致。成功模拟了二维斜板及三维几何（中心矩形板、偏移矩形板和环形板）配置。

Conclusion: 该方法可广泛应用于压电双晶片的波浪能转换建模，并可轻松扩展至固定或刚性结构的波散射问题以及可变底部地形问题，不仅限于水弹性应用。

Abstract: This paper develops a finite element method for wave-structure interaction problems arising in the hydroelastic modelling of submerged elastic plates. The approach is formulated for both two-dimensional settings and three-dimensional channels. For the fluid part of the problem, we use a symmetric discontinuous Galerkin scheme that captures the discontinuity in the potential across the plate. To impose the Sommerfeld radiation conditions, we take inspiration from the Dirichlet-to-Neumann map approach and use the analytic solution in the semi-infinite domains to construct appropriate non-local boundary conditions. For the plate, we use a continuous/discontinuous Galerkin method to resolve the 4$^{\rm th}$ order operator without using continuously differentiable finite elements. We prove well-posedness of the method, showing that the resulting sesquilinear form is bounded and satisfies the Gårding inequality, leading to a unique and stable solution. We implement our formulation as an open-source tool using the Gridap package in Julia. We validate our method against a hypersingular boundary integral method, finding excellent agreement. The flexibility of the methodology is demonstrated through simulations of a two-dimensional slanted plate and three-dimensional plate geometries including centred rectangular plates, offset rectangular plates, and annulus-shaped plates. The finite element method presented here can be readily applied to model wave energy conversion of piezoelectric bimorphs. Furthermore, the method could easily be extended to consider wave scattering from fixed or rigid structures or from variable bottom topography, and is not limited to hydroelastic applications.

</details>


### [299] [Compact implicit high-resolution numerical scheme for multicomponent transport problems with Langmuir sorption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08320)
*Dagmar Zakova,Peter Frolkovic*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于一维多组分非线性Langmuir吸附输运问题的紧凑隐式高分辨率有限体积格式。该方法将二阶紧凑隐式离散与WENO重构及时间限制器相结合，用于处理陡峭梯度和间断。数值实验表明，该方法对光滑解具有二阶收敛性，且可采用比显式方法大得多的Courant数，并在三组分置换色谱问题中成功应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分输运问题中的非线性Langmuir吸附会产生陡峭浓度前沿和间断，需要高分辨率数值方法。传统显式方法受限于稳定性条件（Courant数限制），导致计算成本高；隐式方法虽可放宽稳定性限制，但如何处理间断和陡峭梯度仍具挑战。

Method: 该方法结合了：(1) 二阶紧凑隐式离散；(2) WENO重构用于空间高阶精度和激波捕捉；(3) 时间限制器处理陡峭梯度和间断。特别地，限制器中采用的Courant数是基于逆延迟矩阵特征值的局部特征Courant数，而非全局Courant数。

Result: 数值实验表明：对光滑解达到二阶收敛精度；隐式方法可采用显著更大的Courant数（相比显式一阶和二阶方法）；该方法成功应用于具有尖锐浓度前沿的三组分置换色谱问题。

Conclusion: 紧凑隐式方法结合WENO重构和时间限制器是处理多组分非线性吸附输运问题的有效方案，兼顾了高分辨率和计算效率，能够在大Courant数下保持稳定性和精度。

Abstract: We present a compact implicit high-resolution finite volume scheme for one-dimensional multicomponent transport problems with nonlinear Langmuir sorption. The method combines a second order compact implicit discretization with WENO reconstruction and a time limiter for the treatment of steep gradients and discontinuities. A special attention is given to the Courant number used in the limiter, where local characteristic Courant numbers based on the eigenvalues of the inverse retardation matrix are considered. The compact implicit schemes are compared with analogous explicit first and second order methods. Numerical experiments show second order convergence for smooth solutions and demonstrate that the implicit approach can be used with significantly larger Courant numbers. The method is also applied to a three-component displacement chromatography problem with sharp concentration fronts.

</details>


### [300] [A Discontinuous Galerkin Method for the Intrinsic Beam Model with Kelvin-Voigt Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08397)
*Shivam Sundriyal,Christian Bleffert,Lukas Dreyer,Melven Röhrig-Zöllner,Gregor Gassner,Vadym Aizinger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对含Kelvin-Voigt阻尼的内禀梁方程，提出了一种能量稳定的间断伽辽金（DG）空间离散方法，并在Julia仿真框架Trixi.jl中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内禀梁方程在工程中广泛用于描述柔性结构的动力学行为，而Kelvin-Voigt阻尼的引入使其包含混合时空导数，给数值离散的稳定性分析带来挑战。现有方法难以在保持能量稳定性的同时有效处理这些混合导数项，因此需要一种稳健的数值方法。

Method: 将总截面合力（resultants）分解为弹性部分和黏性部分，从而将控制方程重构为不含混合时空导数的混合系统。在齐次悬臂边界条件下，连续系统满足能量耗散恒等式。DG离散采用特征迎风（characteristic upwinding）与交错辅助迹（alternating auxiliary traces）处理，使半离散系统在该齐次分裂边界闭合下达到能量稳定。方法在Trixi.jl框架中实现。

Result: 通过一个所有状态量和黏性分量均激活的物理相容的制造解（manufactured solution）验证了收敛性；旋转梁测试验证了解析常自旋分支及驱动能量平衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的DG离散格式在半离散层面具备能量稳定性，能正确收敛并准确再现解析参考解的动力学行为，为含阻尼内禀梁方程的高保真仿真提供了可靠的数值框架。

Abstract: We present an energy-stable discontinuous Galerkin (DG) spatial discretization of the intrinsic beam equations with Kelvin-Voigt damping. We reformulate the governing equations by splitting the total sectional resultants into elastic and viscous parts, thereby exposing a mixed system without mixed space-time derivatives. For homogeneous cantilever boundary data, the continuous system obeys an energy dissipation identity. With characteristic upwinding and alternating auxiliary traces the DG discretization is energy stable at the semidiscrete level for the stated homogeneous split boundary closure. The method is implemented in the Julia-based simulation framework Trixi.jl. A physically compatible manufactured solution with all state and viscous components active assesses convergence, while a rotating-beam test verifies the analytic constant-spin branch and the actuated energy balance.

</details>


### [301] [Selective boundary condition reduction via learned error gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08461)
*Daniel Fernández,Dominik Penk,Dominik Riedelbauch*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，用于学习何时可以用一种简化边界条件替换另一种更精确但计算成本更高的边界条件。通过配对解训练神经网络来预测替换后产生的域误差和边界误差，仅当两个预测误差都满足预设容差时才采用简化条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化偏微分方程（PDE）可配以不同精度的边界条件，高精度边界条件计算成本高，而简化边界条件可能在某些情况下足够准确。现有方法缺少系统性地判断何时可以安全地用简化边界条件替换精确边界条件的机制。

Method: 使用配对解（同时求解原边界条件和简化边界条件下的解）来训练神经网络，使其能够预测替换边界条件后产生的域误差和边界误差。当两个预测误差均低于预先设定的容差阈值时，才采用计算成本更低的简化边界条件。该方法聚焦于奇异极限应用场景，即刚性Robin边界条件或非线性边界定律被其极限Dirichlet形式替代的情况。

Result: 在电偶腐蚀问题以及其他非线性稳态和演化问题上评估了该方法的有效性，验证了所提出的边界条件替换学习框架能够可靠地判断何时可以使用简化边界条件。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种数据驱动的策略，在保证精度（通过误差预测和容差控制）的前提下，动态选择计算成本更低的边界条件，从而提升参数化PDE求解的计算效率。

Abstract: Parametric PDEs can admit different boundary conditions with different accuracy and computational cost. We introduce a framework for learning when one reduced boundary condition can replace another: paired solutions train a neural network to estimate the resulting domain and boundary errors, and the simpler condition is used only when both predicted errors meet prescribed tolerances. We focus on singular limits in applications, in which a stiff Robin or nonlinear boundary law is replaced by its limiting Dirichlet form. We evaluate the method on a galvanic corrosion problem and other nonlinear stationary and evolution problems.

</details>


### [302] [Cahn-Hilliard phase-field modeling of tumor growth via locally adaptive isogeometric analysis with THB-splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08479)
*Dhiraj S. Bombarde,Carlotta Giannelli,Alessandro Reali,Guillermo Lorenzo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Cahn-Hilliard (CH)方程的相场肿瘤生长模型，采用等几何分析(IGA)和局部自适应截断层次B样条(THB-splines)来解决四阶微分算子带来的高阶连续性要求，并在模型基准测试和基于MRI重建的患者特异性乳房几何模型上验证了模型预测肿瘤形态的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生理相关条件下利用数学模型预测肿瘤动力学仍然具有挑战性。CH方程引入了四阶微分算子，对逼近空间的高阶连续性要求使得传统有限元法的原始变分公式难以直接定义，因此需要新的数值方法来有效求解这一肿瘤生长模型。

Method: 采用Cahn-Hilliard方程的相场肿瘤生长模型，利用等几何分析(IGA)通过样条基函数天然满足高阶连续性要求，避免使用混合变量或辅助变量的方法；同时采用截断层次B样条(THB-splines)的局部自适应IGA方案以降低计算成本并保持精度。

Result: 模型在标准基准案例上得到验证，并成功应用于基于MRI数据重建的患者特异性乳房几何模型。结果表明模型能够再现已知的肿瘤形态（从球状到指状生长），并且不同模型参数选择产生了多样化的肿瘤动力学行为。

Conclusion: CH方程与局部自适应IGA框架结合所构建的相场肿瘤生长模型展现了良好的预测潜力，能够在器官尺度、患者特异性的复杂几何上有效模拟多样化的肿瘤生长形态。

Abstract: Predicting tumor dynamics in biological systems under physiologically relevant conditions using mathematical and computational models remains a challenging problem. Continuum models based on phase-field (diffuse-interface) formulations have proven to be an effective modeling strategy to govern tumor dynamics and interactions of multiple species. Within this framework, the present work investigates a tumor growth model based on the Cahn-Hilliard (CH) equation. The formulation involves a fourth-order differential operator that imposes higher continuity requirement on approximation spaces for a well-defined primal variational formulation. To address this challenge, we use isogeometric analysis (IGA), which inherently satisfies this requirement through spline-based basis functions and eliminates the need for mixed or auxiliary-variable approaches commonly used in standard finite element discretizations. Additionally, a locally adaptive IGA scheme with truncated hierarchical B-splines (THB-splines) is used to reduce computational cost while maintaining accuracy. The model is first evaluated on standard benchmark cases and then applied to an organ-scale, patient-specific geometric model of the breast reconstructed from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) data. Our results show that the model reproduces known tumor morphologies, ranging from a spheroidal pattern to fingered growth. A series of numerical experiments further shows the diversity of tumor dynamics produced by different model parameter choices. Our findings demonstrate the predictive potential of the CH-based phase-field tumor growth model integrated with a locally adaptive IGA framework.

</details>


### [303] [A Fully Dual Domain Decomposition Method for Extreme Learning Machines Using GenEO Spectral Coarse Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08630)
*Chang-Ock Lee,Byungeun Ryoo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的非重叠区域分解方法用于极限学习机（ELM），该方法同等对待边界连续性和通量条件的约束，并结合Additive Schwarz预处理和GenEO粗空间加速求解，在高达4096个子域上实现了弱可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ELM虽已成功应用于偏微分方程求解，但大规模最小二乘问题的计算成本限制其在大规模问题或高精度需求下的使用。已有的基于区域分解的ELM方法（包括含粗空间的方法）在可扩展性方面仍存在不足，需要提出新的方法解决这一问题。

Method: 提出一种新颖的非重叠区域分解方法用于ELM：1) 将子域间的连续性条件和通量条件作为等式约束同等对待并强制执行；2) 构建Additive Schwarz预处理子以加速收敛；3) 利用GenEO（重叠区域中的广义特征值问题）粗空间进一步提升方法的可扩展性。

Result: 数值实验表明，新方法在迭代次数和精度方面均优于已有的ELM区域分解方法，尤其在Stokes方程和波动方程上表现突出。当采用GenEO粗空间时，在多达4096个子域上观察到了弱可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建非重叠DDM并引入GenEO粗空间，有效解决了ELM在大规模PDE求解中最小二乘问题的可扩展性瓶颈，证明了同等对待连续性与通量约束的策略在迭代效率和精度上的优势。

Abstract: Extreme learning machines (ELMs) are a class of machine learning algorithms that randomly intialize and subsequently fix the hidden layer parameters, and solve for the last layer coefficients using a least squares method. ELMs have been successfully applied to the solution of various partial differential equations, but the cost of solving large least squares problems limits their use in large scale problems or when high accuracy is desired. To address this limitation, domain decomposition methods (DDMs) have been developed for ELMs, reducing training times via parallel computation. Yet these methods, even those incorporating a coarse space, have struggled with scalability. This paper introduces a novel nonoverlapping DDM for ELMs that treats and enforces continuity and flux conditions equally. Additive Schwarz preconditioners and GenEO (Generalized Eigenvalues problems in the Overlaps) coarse spaces are constructed to accelerate the method. Numerical experiments show the new method outperforms previous DDMs for ELMs in both iteration count and accuracy, especially for the Stokes and wave equations. Furthermore, weak scalability has been observed in up to 4,096 subdomains when employing GenEO coarse spaces.

</details>


### [304] [Off-Grid Point-Scatterer Localization from Sparse Limited-Aperture Data via Hankel Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08665)
*Jingzhi Li,Xiliang Lu,Juntao You*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究在稀疏采样、单次入射、单频率声学远场数据下，对有限接收孔径中s个平面点散射体进行定位的问题。作者提出一个基于广义孔径容许Hankel铅笔的统一框架，用于结构补全和离网格定位，建立了无噪声下的精确恢复和有界噪声下的稳定恢复理论，并提供定量准则用于选择样本高效的Hankel铅笔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限接收孔径下，稀疏采样的远场数据存在多个孔径容许的Hankel铅笔（具有不同的提升维度、采样多重性和Vandermonde条件数），不同选择会同时影响数据补全和后续定位的精度。现有方法缺乏统一框架来量化这些因素的影响，因此需要一种理论框架来分析铅笔几何与源配置条件的影响，并为实际选择提供可验证的准则。

Method: 作者基于一阶Born近似将相干数据建模为二维离网格指数和，并利用其低秩Hankel表示。引入统一的广义孔径容许Hankel铅笔框架用于结构化补全和离网格定位。理论上推导了无噪声精确恢复和有界噪声下稳定恢复的充分条件，将铅笔几何的贡献与源配置引致的条件数分离考虑，并提供显式的定位误差界（以补全误差表示）。同时给出可验证的MUSIC分辨条件。

Result: 证明了在无噪声情况下可实现精确恢复，在有界噪声下从线性于s的随机孔径样本中（受铅笔几何、源条件和对数因子影响）实现稳定恢复，并获得与补全误差相关的显式定位界。数值实验（连通与非连通孔径）展示了铅笔几何、采样和噪声的影响，验证了理论分析的定量准则。

Conclusion: 论文建立了有限孔径下声学散射体定位的统一理论框架，为在给定物理孔径下选择样本高效的Hankel铅笔提供了定量准则，揭示了铅笔几何与源配置条件在补全和定位中的独立贡献。

Abstract: We study the localization of $s$ distinct planar point scatterers from sparsely sampled single-incidence, single-frequency acoustic far-field data over a limited receiver aperture. Under the first Born approximation, the coherent data form a two-dimensional off-grid exponential sum and admit a low-rank Hankel representation. A limited aperture generally admits multiple aperture-admissible Hankel pencils with different lifted dimensions, sampling multiplicities, and Vandermonde conditioning. Hence, the choice of the Hankel pencil affects both data completion and subsequent localization.
  In this work, we introduce a unified framework for structured completion and off-grid localization based on general aperture admissible Hankel pencils. We establish exact recovery in the noiseless case and stable recovery under bounded noise from a number of random aperture samples that is linear in $s$, up to factors determined by the pencil geometry, source conditioning, and logarithmic terms, together with an explicit localization bound in terms of the completion error. The analysis separates the contribution of the pencil geometry from the conditioning induced by the source configuration and provides verifiable sufficient conditions for stable completion and MUSIC resolution. These results yield quantitative criteria for selecting sample efficient Hankel pencils under a prescribed physical aperture. Numerical experiments on connected and disconnected apertures demonstrate the effects of pencil geometry, sampling, and noise.

</details>


### [305] [Strong convergence rates of tamed exponential Euler schemes for superlinear hyperbolic SPDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08872)
*Katharina Klioba*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对半线性双曲型随机演化方程（具有超线性增长的非线性项和乘性噪声），证明了驯化指数Euler格式的逐点一致收敛率可达1/2阶。在局部Lipschitz、多项式增长、强制性和单调性条件下，建立了Hilbert空间上的路径一致强误差估计。结果从全局Lipschitz推广到局部Lipschitz非线性项，并应用于多个非线性随机方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前非抛物型SPDE的收敛性结果仅适用于全局Lipschitz非线性项，而现实中的许多物理模型（如随机输运、Airy、波型及耗散阻尼非线性薛定谔方程）涉及超线性增长的非线性项，需要将收敛性结论推广到局部Lipschitz情形。

Method: 采用驯化（tamed）指数Euler格式；对漂移和扩散均允许多项式增长的非线性项，在局部Lipschitz、多项式增长、强制性和单调性条件下进行误差分析。在随机Kato框架下建立局部与全局适定性及一致先验估计。根据非线性类型设计停止（stopped）与分数驯化（fractionally tamed）两类方案。

Result: 在Hilbert空间X上，对p∈[2,∞)，建立了形如(E max_{0≤j≤N}‖U(t_j)-U^j‖_X^p)^{1/p} ≲ √k的路径一致强误差估计，即收敛率可达1/2阶。同时建立了随机Kato框架下的局部/全局适定性和一致先验估计。应用覆盖非线性随机输运、Airy、波型及耗散阻尼非线性薛定谔方程；对带三次速度阻尼的Klein-Gordon方程，补充了此前仅适用于加性噪声的结果。

Conclusion: 本文将非抛物型SPDE的驯化指数Euler格式收敛性从全局Lipschitz非线性推广至局部Lipschitz情形，涵盖漂移和扩散均为多项式增长的场景，并通过多个具体应用验证了方法的普适性与有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove pathwise uniform convergence at rates up to $1/2$ for tamed exponential Euler schemes for semilinear hyperbolic stochastic evolution equations with superlinearly growing nonlinearities and multiplicative noise. We take the term hyperbolic to mean that the leading operator generates a contractive $C_0$-semigroup but no parabolic smoothing occurs. Under local Lipschitz, polynomial growth, coercivity, and monotonicity conditions on the nonlinearities, we establish pathwise uniform strong error estimates of the form \begin{equation*}
  \Big(\mathbb{E}\max_{0\le j \le N} \|U(t_j)-U^j\|_X^p\Big)^{1/p} \lesssim \sqrt{k} \end{equation*} on a Hilbert space $X$ for $p\in [2,\infty)$. Here, $U$ is the mild solution and $U^j$ is the tamed exponential Euler approximation at time $t_j=jk$ with step size $k>0$. This extends previous convergence results for non-parabolic SPDEs from globally to locally Lipschitz nonlinearities, allowing both drift and diffusion to grow polynomially. In a stochastic Kato framework, we further establish local and global well-posedness as well as uniform a priori estimates for the mild solution and its approximation. Applications to nonlinear stochastic transport, Airy, wave-type, and dissipatively damped nonlinear Schrödinger equations are included, covering different nonlinearities-stopped and fractionally tamed schemes. For the Klein-Gordon equation with cubic velocity damping, this complements previous results obtained for additive noise.

</details>


### [306] [High-Order Discontinuous Cut Finite Element Methods for Scalar Hyperbolic Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08880)
*Pei Fu,Gunilla Kreiss,Zelin Xin,Sara Zahedi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一族基于不连续伽辽金（DG）框架的高阶切割有限元方法，用于复杂域上的标量双曲守恒律。通过结合宏单元稳定化与通量限制，该方法在任意边界切割的背景网格上保持最大值原理并具有鲁棒性，同时通过ghost penalty稳定项避免小切割单元导致的严苛时间步限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在切割有限元（CutFEM）框架下求解复杂域上的标量双曲守恒律时，任意边界切割会产生极小的切割单元，导致严重的时间步长限制和数值不稳定性。现有方法在保持最大值原理、抑制非物理振荡和保证最优收敛阶之间难以兼顾，需要一种既能处理任意切割配置又保持DG方法精度和稳定性的高阶方案。

Method: 该方法构建在规则背景网格上的嵌入域求解框架中：(1) 在宏单元内部面上添加ghost penalty稳定项以消除极小切割单元带来的时间步限制；(2) 提出宏单元基的参数化通量限制器，并改编Zhang-Shu保界限制器和Barth-Jespersen斜率限制器到CutFEM框架中；(3) 通过宏单元上定义限制参数，将标准DG的限制技术适配到切割场景，实现最大值原理的保持和无振荡的激波捕捉。半离散格式在周期性和流入-流出边界条件下均推导了L^2稳定性结果。

Result: 二维和三维数值实验表明，该方法在包含极小切割单元交叉的挑战性切割配置下，仍能达到最优收敛阶、保持最大值原理，并实现无虚假振荡的精确激波捕捉。其稳定性与精度特性与拟合网格上的标准DG方法相当。

Conclusion: 宏单元稳定化与限制器技术的结合使得切割DG方法能够在复杂几何上保持DG方法的理想性质（稳定性、最优收敛阶、最大值原理），同时有效应对任意小切割单元带来的数值挑战，为切割有限元方法求解双曲问题提供了一套鲁棒且高阶的求解框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a family of high-order cut finite element methods based on the discontinuous Galerkin (DG) framework for scalar hyperbolic conservation laws on complex domains. Building on our previous work, we develop a multidimensional formulation that combines macro-element stabilization with flux limiting to obtain a scheme that preserves the maximum principle and remains robust with respect to arbitrary boundary cuts of the background mesh. The physical domain is embedded in a regular background mesh, which may produce arbitrarily small cut cells. To avoid the severe time step restrictions typically associated with such cells, ghost penalty stabilization terms are added on interior facets of macro-elements. The resulting method exhibits stability and accuracy properties similar to those of standard DG methods on fitted meshes. An $L^2$-stability result is derived for the semi-discrete scheme under both periodic and inflow-outflow boundary conditions. To enforce the maximum principle and suppress nonphysical oscillations, we adapt limiter techniques from standard DG methods to the CutFEM setting by defining limiting parameters on macro-elements. In particular, we present a macro-element-based parameterized flux limiter together with adaptations of the Zhang-Shu bound-preserving limiter and the Barth-Jespersen slope limiter. Numerical experiments in two and three spatial dimensions demonstrate optimal convergence orders, preservation of the maximum principle, and accurate shock capturing without spurious oscillations, even for challenging cut configurations involving very small-cut cell intersections.

</details>


### [307] [On the well-posedness and efficient approximation for the classical Melan equation in suspension bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08912)
*Jinxiang Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究经典Melan方程（用于模拟悬索桥），基于简化“低刚度”模型的解析解推导原始模型解的先验估计，并在显式荷载条件下证明其在向下活荷载下具有非负积分的唯一解，从而在工程意义上确立了挠度曲线的唯一性。同时提出一种高效迭代逼近方法（以简化模型解为首步迭代），证明其几何收敛并给出显式误差估计，通过两座实际桥梁的计算验证了方法的适用性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 悬索桥的经典Melan方程是描述其力学行为的重要数学模型，但其非线性非局部项使得解析求解和唯一性分析极具挑战。此前缺乏对原模型解的存在唯一性、先验估计以及高效数值求解方法的系统研究，工程实践中也无法明确简化模型与原模型之间的关系以及非线性项的实际影响。

Method: 1) 首先研究简化“低刚度”Melan模型解析解的显式表达式与全局性质；2) 基于此推导原经典Melan方程解的a priori估计；3) 在显式荷载条件下利用积分不等式等工具证明原模型存在唯一且具有非负积分的解；4) 提出以简化模型解为初值的迭代逼近算法，并证明其几何收敛速率与显式误差界；5) 通过两座实际桥梁数据进行数值计算验证。

Result: 在显式荷载条件下建立了原经典Melan方程解的唯一性（具有非负积分），提出了以简化模型解为首步迭代的高效迭代方法并证明了其几何收敛性；两座实际桥梁的数值计算验证了方法的适用性与计算效率，量化了非线性非局部项对解的影响，并厘清了简化模型与原模型的关系。

Conclusion: 研究从理论和数值两方面系统解决了经典Melan方程的解析性质、唯一性与高效求解问题，验证了解释了若干工程技术观察结果，同时提出了一些相关开放问题供后续研究。

Abstract: The classical Melan equation modeling suspension bridges is considered. We first study the explicit expression and global properties of the analytical solution for the simplified ``less stiff'' model, based on which we derive an a priori estimate for the original classical Melan equation and establish, under an explicit load condition, that it has a unique solution with nonnegative integral under downward live loads, thereby showing the uniqueness of the corresponding deflection curve in the engineering setting. We also develop an efficient iterative approximation method by taking the solution of the simplified ``less stiff'' model as the first iterate, and prove its geometric convergence with explicit error estimates. The applicability and computational efficiency of the method are demonstrated through calculations for two actual bridges, which also quantify the influence of the nonlinear nonlocal term on the solution and clarify the relationship between the simplified and original models. Several engineering observations are verified and explained, and some related open problems are suggested.

</details>


### [308] [On the sample complexity of the active subspace method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08940)
*Fabio Nobile,Matteo Raviola,Raúl Tempone*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究经验主动子空间方法在脊逼近投影误差度量下的样本复杂度。作者推导了由正则化逆Christoffel函数控制的非渐近准最优性界，证明了在光滑梯度假设下样本复杂度估计显著改进，并给出了包括秩、正则化尺度和样本量的完整先验样本复杂度刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动子空间方法在实践中使用有限的梯度蒙特卡洛估计，但经典基于算子范数协方差误差的分析给出的样本复杂度估计比实际计算所用的采样规则悲观得多，需要在脊逼近相关的投影误差度量下直接研究经验主动子空间方法以弥合理论与实践的差距。

Method: 在脊逼近的投影误差框架下直接分析经验主动子空间方法，推导由梯度场相关的正则化逆Christoffel函数控制的非渐近准最优性界；在有界梯度假设下改进样本复杂度；进一步假设梯度映射属于再生核希尔伯特空间以获得更锐利的coherence估计，并提出基于核对角测度的可行重要性采样；结合总体主动子空间尾部能量的先验衰减界，联合刻画秩、正则化尺度和样本量的先验样本复杂度。

Result: 得到了较算子范数协方差界更优的样本复杂度估计；通过RKHS光滑性假设获得更锐利的coherence界和重要性采样方法；样本复杂度在给定光滑性下可被完全先验刻画。抽象假设通过对数正态高斯和仿射均匀参数椭圆PDE的Hermite与Legendre级数加权可和性得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作从投影误差视角建立了经验主动子空间的严格非渐近理论，显著改进了样本复杂度的先验估计，并通过理论-计算匹配的采样策略和具体PDE案例验证了假设的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Active subspaces identify low-dimensional linear structure in high-dimensional parameter-to-output maps by estimating the dominant eigenspace of a gradient covariance operator. In practice this covariance is replaced by a Monte Carlo estimator built from a limited number of gradient evaluations. Classical analyses based on controlling the covariance error in operator norm lead to sample-complexity estimates that can be substantially more pessimistic than the sampling rules commonly used in computations. This paper studies the empirical active subspace method directly in the projection-error metric relevant for ridge approximation. We derive non-asymptotic quasi-optimality bounds governed by a regularized inverse Christoffel function associated with the gradient field. Under a bounded-gradient assumption, the resulting estimates already improve the sample-complexity estimates obtained from operator-norm covariance bounds. We then show that additional smoothness of the gradient map, expressed through membership in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space, yields sharper coherence estimates and motivates tractable importance sampling from kernel diagonal measures. Furthermore, the same smoothness assumption yields a priori decay bounds for the population active subspace tail energy, which can be combined with our finite-sample estimate to prescribe rank, regularization scale, and sample size, allowing to fully characterize the a priori sample complexity. The abstract assumptions are verified for lognormal Gaussian and affine uniform parametric elliptic PDEs using weighted summability of Hermite and Legendre series expansions.

</details>


### [309] [Sharp Computational Bounds for Spectral Types of Schrödinger Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08945)
*Matthew J. Colbrook,George Coote*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在Smale计算基础纲领的框架下，证明了Schrödinger算子谱型分解的尖锐界限：纯点谱与绝对连续谱一般无法通过任何单一极限过程恢复，奇异连续谱一般无法通过两个嵌套极限过程恢复；并给出了匹配的上界（两点/三点极限分别用于恢复纯点+绝对连续谱和奇异连续谱），形成了谱型的尖锐层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Smale关于计算基础的纲领精神下，作者希望从计算复杂性/可判定性的角度严格刻画Schrödinger算子各类谱型（纯点、绝对连续、奇异连续）的可恢复性极限，弥补仅关注理论谱结构而忽略"能否通过有限算法过程计算"这一根本问题的空白。

Method: 作者采用分层论证：(1) 构造性下界——利用Gordon型重复构造证明纯点谱的不可单极限恢复，高势垒构造证明绝对连续谱的不可单极限恢复，通过Riesz乘积、矩消去微扰和计算性Gelfand-Levitan方案的逆谱构造证明奇异连续谱需要至少两个嵌套极限；(2) 上界——基于小波的可认证计算，对系数为局部有界变差且具有定量局部变差控制的R^d上自伴微分算子给出证明确保的匹配算法。有限描述框架还推导出固定形式系统中的可证明性限制。

Result: (1) 纯点谱和绝对连续谱集合一般不能被任何单一极限过程恢复；(2) 奇异连续谱一般不能被两个嵌套极限过程恢复（更困难一阶）；(3) 对广泛的自伴微分算子类，两个极限足以恢复纯点和绝对连续部分，三个极限足以恢复奇异连续部分；(4) 有限描述框架还暗示了计算机辅助证明中固定形式系统可认证的对应限制。

Conclusion: 本文为Schrödinger算子谱型分解建立了一个完备且尖锐的计算层级：纯点与绝对连续谱需要两个极限，奇异连续谱需要三个极限，且上下界匹配，从而在计算理论意义上给出了谱类的精确"计算深度"。

Abstract: We prove sharp bounds for determining the spectral-type decomposition of Schrödinger operators in the spirit of Smale's program on the foundations of computation. For explicit one-dimensional self-adjoint Schrödinger operators $H=-{\mathrm d^2}/{\mathrm dx^2}+V$ on $L^2(\mathbb R)$, where $V\in C^\infty(\mathbb R;\mathbb R)$ is given by a finite description of all derivatives and derivative bounds, the pure point and absolutely continuous spectral sets cannot, in general, be recovered by any single limiting procedure. The singular continuous spectral set is strictly harder: it cannot, in general, be recovered by two nested limiting procedures. Analytic constructions of dichotomies realize the lower bounds: Gordon-type repetitions for pure point spectrum, high barriers for absolutely continuous spectrum, and an inverse spectral construction for singular continuous spectrum based on Riesz products, moment-killing perturbations, and a computational Gelfand--Levitan scheme. The finite-description framework also implies corresponding limitations on what can be certified in fixed formal systems (e.g., when used in computer-assisted proofs). Conversely, using wavelet-based certified computation, we prove matching upper bounds for broad classes of self-adjoint differential operators on $\mathbb R^d$ with coefficients of locally bounded variation and quantitative local variation control: two limits suffice for the pure point and absolutely continuous parts, and three for the singular continuous part. This provides a sharp hierarchy for spectral types.

</details>


### [310] [Quadratic Point Estimate Method for Uncertainty Quantification with Dependent Non-Gaussian Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09053)
*Minhyeok Ko,Konstantinos G. Papakonstantinou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对二次点估计法（QPEM）进行了增强，通过引入copula函数实现对输入变量联合概率密度函数的有效建模，从而在无需数值优化的前提下高效、准确地估计输出量的概率矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统点估计法（PEM）在n维空间中估计输出量概率矩时精度有限，其sigma点数量随问题维度线性增长。虽然QPEM已被提出以处理高阶输入矩，但其框架目前尚未支持对输入变量间复杂依赖结构的有效建模。

Method: 本文在QPEM框架中引入copula集成，通过分别估计边缘分布和随机变量间的依赖结构来建模联合输入概率密度函数，使QPEM能够处理具有复杂相关结构的随机输入，避免了传统方法中对独立或简单相关假设的依赖。

Result: 通过多种采样方法的对比，在两种实际场景下（可从数据推断联合依赖结构，以及仅已知边缘分布和相关矩阵）验证了基于copula的QPEM的有效性和高效性能。

Conclusion: copula增强的QPEM在保持计算成本竞争力的同时，显著提高了高维问题中输入相关结构建模的准确性，为不确定性量化中的概率矩估计提供了更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: As an extension of the Point Estimate Method (PEM) to evaluate probabilistic moments of quantities of interest (QoI) in general $n$-dimensional spaces, the Quadratic Point Estimate Method (QPEM) has been recently developed. This new method is defined to fully represent up to fifth-order input moments in the Gaussian space, providing general analytical expressions for sample locations and weights, without requiring any numerical optimization. The QPEM can significantly improve the estimation accuracy of the output QoI moments, in relation to PEM-based methods whose numbers of sigma points grow linearly with the problem dimension, while at the same time having an affordable and competitive computational cost up to a considerable number of dimensions. The QPEM is further enhanced in this work by enabling copula integration into the framework, which enables effective modeling of the joint input probability density function by estimating marginals and the dependence structure of the involved random variables. The validity and efficient performance of the copula-based QPEM are showcased against numerous other sampling methods in various examples considering two practical scenarios: (i) when the joint dependence structure can be inferred from data, and (ii) when only marginal distributions and correlation matrices are known.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [311] [Rollcast: Proper-Score Gated Rolling Anchors for Adaptive Probabilistic Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05561)
*Giancarlo Vercellino*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Rollcast是一种面向单变量时间序列的概率预测方法，通过整合一组紧凑的滚动统计锚点（而非依赖单一全局模型）来构建预测。它利用滚动均值、中位数、极值、回归端点和分位数定义候选预测位置及当前状态表征，结合状态依赖的softmax门控学习锚点概率，并从相似历史状态中检索残差分布以获得局部不确定性，最后通过递归模拟传播混合分布至多步预测视界。在涵盖八种数据生成过程的Monte Carlo研究中，与oracle模拟器生成的真实条件预测分布相比，Rollcast在整体覆盖率、区间宽度和CRPS指标上表现接近最优，但在局部趋势和机制转换场景下仍有改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率预测方法常依赖单一全局模型，可能难以适应时间序列的局部动态变化。作者试图探索：能否仅使用一组简单、可解释的滚动统计量（而非复杂模型）来构建竞争性的概率预测，从而在保持模型可解释性和简洁性的同时，获得接近理论最优（oracle）的预测性能。

Method: Rollcast方法包含以下关键组件：(1) 滚动统计锚点集：使用滚动均值、中位数、极值、回归端点和分位数作为候选预测位置并表征当前状态；(2) 状态依赖softmax门控：通过最小化负对数预测密度来学习各锚点的概率权重；(3) 局部不确定性估计：从与当前状态相似的历史状态中检索残差分布；(4) 递归模拟：将得到的混合分布传播到多个预测视界。评估采用Monte Carlo框架，覆盖自回归、随机游走、局部趋势、阈值、机制转换、随机波动、重尾和方差突变等八种数据生成过程。

Result: 在2,000个独立拟合序列上，Rollcast的名义90%和95%预测区间的整体经验覆盖率分别为86.2%和91.5%（均略低于名义水平，存在一定校准不足）；90%水平下预测区间平均比oracle宽13.6%，CRPS比oracle高14.4%。在自回归、阈值、随机波动、重尾和方差突变动力学下，Rollcast表现最接近oracle；而局部趋势和机制转换场景则更具挑战性，性能差距更大。

Conclusion: Rollcast证明了一组简单、可解释的局部统计摘要足以构建具有竞争力的概率预测，验证了"锚点+门控+局部残差"这一混合预测框架的可行性。但结果同时也揭示了该方法在校准方面存在系统性偏差（区间偏窄）以及递归不确定性传播的局限，尤其是在趋势性和状态切换场景下，为未来改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Rollcast is a probabilistic forecasting method for univariate time series that combines a compact set of rolling statistical anchors rather than relying on a single global model. Rolling means, medians, extrema, regression endpoints, and quantiles define candidate forecast locations and a representation of the current state. A state-dependent softmax gate learns anchor probabilities by minimizing negative log predictive density, while residual distributions retrieved from similar historical states provide local uncertainty. Recursive simulation propagates the resulting mixture over multiple forecast horizons. The method is evaluated in a Monte Carlo study covering eight data-generating processes, including autoregressive, random-walk, local-trend, threshold, regime-switching, stochastic-volatility, heavy-tailed, and variance-break dynamics. Across 2,000 independent fitted series, Rollcast is compared with the true conditional predictive distribution generated by an oracle simulator. Overall empirical coverage is 86.2% for nominal 90% intervals and 91.5% for nominal 95% intervals. Predictive intervals are on average 13.6% wider than the oracle at the 90% level, while CRPS is 14.4% higher than oracle CRPS. Performance is closest to the oracle under autoregressive, threshold, stochastic-volatility, heavy-tailed, and variance-break dynamics, while local trends and regime switching are more challenging. The results indicate that Rollcast can construct competitive probabilistic forecasts from simple, interpretable local summaries, while also identifying limitations in calibration and recursive uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [312] [Diagonal Attenuation: A Finite-Sample Correction for PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05796)
*Qiang Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了"对角线衰减"(diagonal attenuation)方法用于改进小样本下PCA的估计偏差。该方法在保留样本交叉协方差的同时降低各坐标的样本方差，可通过随机输入掩码下的线性全输出重建损失的均值精确推导，并可扩展到掩码范围之外。论文分离了样本方差误差对保留与省略总体方向间随机耦合的贡献，证明衰减可降低由此导致的方向旋转偏差，并在多个数据集和任务上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCA在协方差矩阵仅从有限数据估计时，其主方向会偏离总体目标，产生旋转偏差。现有方法缺乏对这种偏差的系统性校正手段，需要一种既能保留总体PCA子空间结构、又能缓解有限样本导致的方向旋转的新方法。

Method: 1) 引入"对角线衰减"方法：保留样本交叉协方差，缩减各坐标样本方差；2) 证明该方法可由在随机输入掩码上求平均的线性全输出重建损失精确揭示，并直接研究校正可超越掩码可达范围；3) 分离样本方差误差对保留与省略总体方向间随机耦合的贡献，展示衰减如何降低旋转；4) 在平衡边际方差假设下推导显式期望风险定理(对衰减路径一致成立)，并给出渐近风险最小化强度；5) 对一般协方差情形刻画衰减不改变总体PCA子空间的条件，并给出兼顾特征间隙变化和总体代价的风险定理；6) 在图像块、语音频谱和智能手机加速度等数据集上，在两种拟合样本预算下验证掩码导出和直接衰减方法的改进效果。

Result: 模拟实验追踪了从精确保持到PCA的权衡曲线。在局部图像块、语音频谱和智能手机加速度数据上，掩码导出和直接衰减方法在两种拟合样本预算下都改进了PCA，其中一种方法在每个数据-预算组合中都是七种方法里平均增益最大的。在63%-95%的子样本上，完整路径选择的衰减强度超出了掩码导出的边界。

Conclusion: 对角线衰减是一种有效且理论上可刻画的小样本PCA校正方法，能够降低有限样本导致的PCA方向旋转偏差，同时保留总体PCA子空间结构。该方法在多种真实数据下一致优于现有方法，且完整衰减路径提供超出掩码导出范围的选择灵活性。

Abstract: Principal component analysis (PCA) can rotate away from its population target when a covariance matrix is estimated from limited data. We introduce diagonal attenuation, which preserves sample cross-covariances while reducing coordinatewise sample variances. The method is revealed exactly by averaging a linear full-output reconstruction loss over random input masks; studying the correction directly extends it beyond the range attainable by masking. We isolate the part of the random coupling between retained and omitted population directions that is contributed by sample-variance errors, and show how attenuation can reduce the resulting rotation. Under balanced marginal variances, we derive an explicit expected-risk theorem, uniform over the attenuation path for all sufficiently large finite samples, and obtain the asymptotically risk-minimizing strength. For general covariances, we characterize when attenuation leaves the population PCA subspace unchanged and give a risk theorem that also accounts for changing eigengaps and the population cost when the target moves. Simulations track this tradeoff from exact preservation back to PCA. Across local image patches, speech spectra, and smartphone acceleration, both mask-derived and direct attenuation improve PCA under two fitting-sample budgets, and one of them has the largest mean gain among seven methods in every data--budget cell. The full path selects strengths beyond the mask-derived boundary on $63\%$--$95\%$ of the subsamples.

</details>


### [313] [Functional Attentive Interpretable Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05846)
*Haixu Wang,Tianyu Guan,Jiguo Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出功能注意力可解释回归(FAIR)方法，通过坐标特征和自注意力机制直接表示函数间回归中的系数曲面β(s,t)，学习效应自适应邻域，在局部和全局尺度上实现信息共享，并通过稀疏性和平滑性惩罚恢复复杂支撑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数间回归中的系数曲面β(s,t)可能表现出复杂的支撑结构（从局部块到全局模式，如不连通区域、带状或环状），其中效应相似性与欧氏距离不对齐。基于投影的固定基展开方法会模糊这种结构，而直接平滑方法可能过度平滑曲面及其边界。现有方法难以准确恢复这种复杂支撑几何结构。

Method: FAIR方法通过坐标特征直接表示系数曲面β(s,t)，利用自注意力机制学习效应自适应邻域，实现局部和全局尺度的信息共享；通过标量压缩网络将学习到的表示映射为系数曲面；对邻域施加稀疏性和平滑性惩罚以促进具有连贯边界的局部支撑。建立了与张量积样条空间的筛等价性，并推导了收敛速率。

Result: 在模拟实验以及海洋学和水文学数据应用中，FAIR在恢复支撑几何形状方面优于现有方法，同时在预测精度上表现更好，尤其在稀疏采样条件下优势更为明显。

Conclusion: FAIR提供了一种灵活有效的系数曲面表示方法，能在不依赖固定基的前提下自适应学习复杂的支撑结构，兼顾局部细节与全局模式，在支撑恢复和预测性能上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: In function-on-function regression, the coefficient surface $β(s,t)$ may exhibit complex support structure---from localized patches to global patterns such as disconnected regions, bands, or rings---where effect similarity does not align with Euclidean proximity. Projection-based methods that rely on fixed basis expansions can obscure such structure, while direct smoothing approaches risk oversmoothing the surface and its boundaries. We propose Functional Attentive Interpretable Regression (FAIR), which represents $β(s,t)$ directly through coordinate features and uses self-attention to learn effect-adaptive neighborhoods, enabling information sharing at both local and global scales. A scalar compression network maps these learned representations to the coefficient surface. Sparsity and smoothness penalties applied over these neighborhoods promote localized support with coherent boundaries. We establish a sieve equivalence to tensor-product spline spaces and derive convergence rates. Simulations and applications to oceanographic and hydrological data demonstrate that FAIR recovers support geometry more accurately than existing methods while achieving superior prediction, particularly under sparse sampling.

</details>


### [314] [Recovering linear images of sparse signals from indirect observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06182)
*Anatoli Juditsky,Arkadi Nemirovski*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种在凸紧致稀疏约束下，从间接含噪观测中恢复线性图像的方法，且不依赖任何关于感知矩阵的强假设（如零空间条件或受限等距性质），相关参数与风险上界可通过显式凸优化计算得出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的ℓ1最小化稀疏恢复方法通常依赖于对感知矩阵A施加难以验证的假设（如零空间条件、受限等距性等），这些条件在实际应用中往往无法确认或满足，限制了方法的应用范围。

Method: 提出的估计属于ℓ1最小化恢复族；不对感知矩阵A做任何特殊假设；其参数和风险上界不作为闭合解析形式给出，而是通过求解显式凸优化问题来高效计算。

Result: 方法在无特殊假设条件下仍能提供恢复线性图像Bx的估计，估计性能由显式凸优化问题的解来刻画。

Conclusion: 所提方法在无常规强假设的情况下实现了对稀疏信号线性图像的鲁棒恢复，同时利用凸优化保证了参数与风险的数值可计算性，为稀疏恢复提供了更具实际可操作性的途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop and analyze techniques for recovering a linear image $Bx$ of an unknown signal $x$ from indirect noisy observation $ω=Ax+ξ$. It is {\em a priori} known that $x\in \cX$, a given convex compact set, and that $x$ is $s$-sparse---has at most $s$ nonvanishing entries. The proposed estimates belong to a large family of recovery routines by $\ell_1$-minimization. However, unlike the classical result describing performance of such estimates, we do not make any special (and hard to check) assumptions about the sensing matrix $A$ such as nullspace or Restricted Isometry condition and the like. As a consequence, parameters of the estimates and the upper bounds on their risks are not available in a closed analytic form, but are delivered instead by efficient computation as solutions to explicit convex optimization problems.

</details>


### [315] [Robust conditional dimension reduction for dissimilarity data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06284)
*Xiao Ling,Anh Bui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出鲁棒条件多维缩放（rcMDS）方法，用Fair M-估计目标替代标准平方应力准则，并通过加权条件SMACOF算法优化，以解决条件多维缩放对污染相异性数据敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件多维缩放（cMDS）的标准平方应力公式对受污染的相异性数据（离群值）高度敏感，因为离群值会主导目标函数并扭曲学习的配置。

Method: 将平方应力准则替换为Fair M-估计目标，开发了加权条件SMACOF算法来优化该目标。该算法允许计算上易处理的更新，且其稳定化目标值单调递减并收敛到有限极限。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明该方法有效（原摘要截断，但可推断rcMDS在存在相异性污染的情况下优于标准cMDS）。

Conclusion: rcMDS通过鲁棒目标函数和单调收敛的加权算法，在存在离群值时实现了更稳健的条件维度约简学习。

Abstract: Conditional dimension reduction (cDR) learns low-dimensional latent coordinates while accounting for observed covariates that represent known sources of variation in the data. Conditional Multidimensional Scaling (cMDS) is a cDR technique that works directly with dissimilarity data. Its standard squared-stress formulation, however, is sensitive to contaminated dissimilarity, since outliers can dominate the objective and distort the learned configuration. We proposed Robust Conditional Multidimensional Scaling (rcMDS) by replacing the squared-stress criterion with a Fair M-estimation objective. We developed a reweighted conditional SMACOF algorithm to optimize this objective. The proposed algorithm admits computationally tractable updates, and its stabilized objective values decrease monotonically and converge to a finite limit. Experiments on synthetic and real data show that the pro

</details>


### [316] [Recovering Weak Signals with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06382)
*Sarod Yatawatta*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用归一化流模型来重建受校准影响的弱信号。在许多科学领域中，弱信号被比其强几个数量级的干扰信号所掩盖，校准过程中会不可避免地扭曲或部分抑制目标信号。论文利用目标信号的统计不变性并假设最小初始抑制，框架能够有效恢复丢失的信号成分，并通过模拟数据验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多科学学科中，感兴趣的弱信号被强度高出数个数量级的主导干扰信号所掩盖。恢复这些弱信号需要减去主导信号，但这一校准过程会固有地扭曲或部分抑制目标信号，导致信号失真和信息丢失。

Method: 提出使用归一化流（normalizing flow）模型来重建受校准影响的弱信号。核心思路是利用目标信号的统计不变性，并假设存在最小的初始抑制，从而有效恢复丢失的信号成分。

Result: 提供了基于归一化流的恢复方法的全面理论概述，并通过模拟数据证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 归一化流模型能够有效重建受校准影响的弱信号，为强干扰背景下的弱信号恢复提供了一种新的理论框架和实用方案。

Abstract: In many scientific disciplines, weak signals of interest are obscured by dominant nuisance signals that are several orders of magnitude stronger. Recovering these weak signals requires subtracting the dominant ones; however, this calibration process inherently distorts or partially suppresses the underlying signal of interest. To address this problem, we propose the use of normalizing flow models to reconstruct calibration-affected weak signals. By leveraging the statistical invariance of the target signals and assuming minimal initial suppression, our framework effectively recovers the lost signal components. We provide a comprehensive theoretical overview of this normalizing flow-based recovery method and demonstrate its efficacy using simulated data.

</details>


### [317] [Beyond Worst-Case Coreset Bounds for $k$-Clustering via Determinantal Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06394)
*Diptarka Chakraborty,Satyaki Mukherjee,Gaurav Vallabhdas Revankar,Hoang-Son Tran*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行列式点过程（DPP）的"行列式采样"框架，在温和且自然的数据分布假设下，为(k,z)-聚类问题构建比最坏情况理论下界更小的ε-coreset，首次在超越最坏情况假设下严格突破ε^{-2}的尺寸下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中的大规模数据集使得数据缩减成为核心挑战，尤其在聚类任务中需要紧凑但忠实的数据摘要。现有(k,z)-聚类问题的ε-coreset尺寸最坏情况下界已被证明是近乎紧的，但这类最坏情况实例通常不能代表真实世界数据，因此作者希望在温和自然的分布假设下实现显著更小的coreset。

Method: 作者引入一种新的相关采样框架，称为"行列式采样"（determinantal sampling），其核心是基于行列式点过程（DPP）的创新应用。该框架能够在d固定时，为(k,z)-聚类构建可高效构造的ε-coreset，其对1/ε的依赖指数严格小于2。

Result: 在超越最坏情况的假设下，作者获得了对1/ε依赖指数严格小于2（d固定时）的可高效构造的ε-coreset，改进了最坏情况下ε^{-2}的障碍。这是首个通过超越最坏情况假设严格突破这些下界的结果。实验表明该方法在合成和真实基准数据集上即便未显式执行分析中的假设，也始终比现有最先进方法获得更小的coreset。

Conclusion: 该工作证明在温和自然的数据分布假设下，聚类coreset可以比最坏情况理论下界显著更小，并通过行列式采样框架首次严格超越ε^{-2}障碍，理论和实验均验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Massive datasets in modern machine learning have made data reduction a central challenge, particularly for clustering tasks where memory and computational constraints demand compact yet faithful summaries. A standard approach is to construct an \textit{$ε$-coreset}: a small weighted subset that approximately preserves the clustering cost for every plausible choice of centers. For the \textit{$(k,z)$-clustering problem}, existing worst-case bounds on coreset size are essentially tight, ruling out substantially smaller coresets in general. However, such worst-case instances are often unrepresentative of real-world data. In this work, we show that significantly smaller coresets are possible under mild and natural assumptions on the underlying data distribution. We introduce a new correlated sampling framework, called \textit{determinantal sampling}, based on a novel application of determinantal point processes. Using this framework, we obtain an efficiently constructible $\varepsilon$-coreset for $(k,z)$-clustering in $\mathbb R^d$ whose dependence on $1/\varepsilon$ has exponent strictly smaller than $2$ when $d$ is fixed. This improves over the worst-case $\varepsilon^{-2}$ barrier under our beyond-worst-case assumptions. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first result that provably surpasses these lower bounds through beyond-worst-case assumptions. Finally, we validate our approach on synthetic and real-world benchmark datasets, where it consistently achieves smaller coresets than existing state-of-the-art methods, even without explicitly enforcing the assumptions used in the analysis.

</details>


### [318] [RoPE attention is an exact forward-pass gradient step with softmax intact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06685)
*Julie Huang,Maggie Chlon,Leon Chlon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文推导了RoPE-softmax注意力前向传播的精确梯度步表示。对任意确定性RoPE-softmax注意力头及任意仿射投影权重，构造了与查询相关的有效矩阵ΔM_i，使得y_i = μ_i + u_i^T ΔM_i，其中μ_i为被注意值的一致均值，u_i为增广查询输入。该构造使用经典指数差商ρ=φ_1精确保留softmax，其正系数给出查询条件下的二次目标上的单位梯度步表示。同一函数将RoPE生成器与精确位置有限差分联系起来。文章推导了复用某查询矩阵时误差的逐词公式，并证明了非恒定有限缓存注意力头无法拥有全局精确的仿射查询读出。在预训练的Qwen2.5-0.5B层上的重建检查和冻结复用校准验证了该表示并量化了复用单查询矩阵时所需的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解注意力机制（特别是RoPE-softmax）的内部计算结构，探索能否将复杂的前向传播过程表示为更简单的数学形式（如梯度步），从而为注意力机制的解析分析、缓存复用和推理加速提供理论工具。

Method: 利用经典指数差商ρ=φ_1来精确保持softmax的非线性，构造查询依赖的有效矩阵ΔM_i表示注意力输出；推导该表示在RoPE生成器与位置有限差分之间的联系；推导误差逐词公式以刻画复用查询矩阵的代价；通过预训练模型层的重建与冻结复用校准进行实证验证。

Result: 成功构造了精确的梯度步表示ΔM_i，其系数为正从而保证单位梯度步特性；证明非恒定有限缓存注意力头不存在全局精确的仿射查询读出；在Qwen2.5-0.5B层上重建检查与冻结复用校准均验证了表示的准确性，并量化了复用查询矩阵时所需的修正量。

Conclusion: RoPE-softmax注意力前向传播可被精确重构为查询条件下的梯度步形式，该表示具有正性保证和明确的操作解释；同时揭示了有限缓存注意力在查询读出上的内在局限性，为注意力缓存共享与压缩提供了理论框架和实用量化工具。

Abstract: We derive an exact gradient-step representation of the RoPE-softmax forward pass. For every deterministic RoPE-softmax attention head with arbitrary affine projection weights, we construct a query-dependent effective matrix $ΔM_i$ satisfying $y_i = μ_i + u_i^\top ΔM_i$, where $μ_i$ is the uniform mean of the attended values and $u_i$ is the augmented query input. The construction applies the classical exponential divided difference $ρ= φ_1$ to retain the softmax exactly. Its positive coefficients give a unit gradient-step representation on a query-conditioned quadratic objective. The same function connects the RoPE generator to exact positional finite differences. We derive a tokenwise formula for the error of reusing one query's matrix and prove that a nonconstant finite-cache head cannot admit a globally exact affine query readout. Reconstruction checks and frozen-reuse calibration on one pretrained Qwen2.5-0.5B layer verify the representation and quantify the correction required when one query's matrix is reused.

</details>


### [319] [Large Classification-Risk-Optional Label Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06873)
*F. Setoudehtanzangi,Geoffrey J. McLachlan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限标注预算下如何分配标签以最小化多类零一分类风险，提出基于Fisher信息与多类过量风险局部几何的采集准则，并开发了两阶段自适应程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主动学习/选择性标注场景中，特征可观测但标签需选择性获取。现有方法（如基于后验不确定性或全局参数信息）未必直接优化分类风险，本文旨在建立直接最小化多类零一分类风险的标签采集准则。

Method: 结合已获取标签提供的Fisher信息与多类过量风险的局部几何结构，推导最小化期望多类过量风险渐近系数的采集准则；刻画oracle采集设计、阈值结构及代价敏感扩展；提出两阶段自适应程序并在正则条件下达到oracle准则。

Result: 解析例子表明后验不确定性与分类价值可能产生不同甚至相反的采集排序；三分类QDA实验及六分类Statlog Landsat卫星数据实验表明分类风险采集与基于不确定性的方法存在实质性差异，自适应方法在多数标注预算下优于Fisher比较器。

Conclusion: 分类风险导向的标签价值取决于参数方向与活跃Bayes决策边界的对齐程度，而非后验不确定性或全局参数信息；该设计在预算增大时与其他方法差异缩小，并非在所有情况下都优于熵或边际采样。

Abstract: We study how a limited labeling budget should be allocated to minimize multiclass zero-one classification risk. We consider parametric classification problems in which features are observed for all sampling units while class labels can be acquired selectively. By combining the Fisher information supplied by an acquired label with the local geometry of multiclass excess risk, we derive an acquisition criterion that minimizes the leading asymptotic coefficient of expected multiclass excess risk. The resulting rule values a label according to how strongly its information is aligned with parameter directions that perturb the active Bayes decision boundary, rather than according to posterior uncertainty or global parameter information alone. We characterize the oracle acquisition design, establish its threshold structure, and derive face-specific and cost-sensitive extensions. An analytic example shows that posterior uncertainty and classification value can produce different, and even reversed, acquisition rankings. We further develop a two-stage adaptive procedure that attains the oracle leading-risk criterion under regularity conditions and provide explicit results for Gaussian discriminant analysis. Three-class QDA experiments illustrate the resulting acquisition geometry, while an application to the six-class Statlog Landsat Satellite data shows that classification-risk acquisition can differ materially from both uncertainty-based acquisition and the complete-classification-information comparator. The adaptive classification-risk design attains lower mean error than this Fisher comparator across the labeling budgets considered, although it does not uniformly outperform entropy or margin sampling and differences among the targeted strategies become small as the labeling budget increases.

</details>


### [320] [Distributed Lag Neural Additive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07381)
*Calle Helmersson,Shivang Pandey,Leonardo Olivetti,Elena Raffetti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式滞后神经加性模型（DLNAMs），作为分布式滞后非线性模型（DLNMs）的神经加性类比，用于学习跨滞后的非线性暴露-滞后响应效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DLNMs依赖预先指定的样条交叉基函数，需要选择基函数族、维度和节点位置，这些选择可能限制模型灵活性和拟合表现。

Method: DLNAMs用神经组件替代预指定的样条交叉基来学习暴露-滞后响应曲面，采用exp-centered输入层、光滑激活函数和学习到的子网络混合结构以产生光滑且局部自适应的表示；点级不确定性结合条件最后一层拉普拉斯近似与成员间集成变异。

Result: 在模拟中，DLNAMs在恢复已知响应函数方面普遍优于包括惩罚型和树型变体在内的DLNM比较方法，具有更低偏差、更强的边界恢复能力和更好校准的累积区间；对更复杂的函数增益最大。该架构在样本量、结果族、滞后窗口以及联合拟合的多暴露设置下表现一致，添加暴露时仍保持恢复性能。

Conclusion: DLNAMs避免了基函数族、维度和节点位置的选择，同时保持加性可解释性和常见的分布式滞后汇总指标，是一种有效的DLNM替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce Distributed Lag Neural Additive Models (DLNAMs), neural-additive analogues of Distributed Lag Non-linear Models (DLNMs) for learning nonlinear effects distributed over lags. DLNAMs replace a prespecified spline cross-basis with neural components that learn exposure--lag response surfaces, avoiding choices of basis family, dimension, and knot placement while preserving additive interpretability and familiar distributed-lag summaries. Exp-centered input layers, smooth activations, and learned subnetwork mixtures produce smooth, locally adaptive representations; pointwise uncertainty combines a conditional last-layer Laplace approximation with between-member ensemble variation.
