In simulations, DLNAMs generally outperformed DLNM comparators, including penalized and treed variants, in recovering known response functions, with lower bias, stronger boundary recovery, and better-calibrated cumulative intervals; gains were largest for more demanding functions. The architecture performed consistently across sample sizes, outcome families, lag horizons, and jointly fitted multi-exposure settings, retaining recovery performance as exposures were added; fit-specific changes were largely confined to optimization, and applications recovered established empirical patterns.

</details>


### [321] [SGD in Multiclass Logistic Regression: Sequential Learning and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07868)
*Konstantinos Christopher Tsiolis,Denny Wu,Christos Thrampoulidis,Murat A. Erdogdu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了多类逻辑回归在高斯混合模型上的训练动态，建立了交叉熵风险随梯度优化演化的精确标度律，并通过容量与优化的权衡推导出计算最优的模型缩放律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的理论缩放律主要针对线性回归，缺乏对多类分类问题的系统理论分析。作者希望将缩放律理论从回归扩展到分类任务，并连接大规模神经网络中观测到的经验缩放行为，为模型容量与训练时间的权衡提供理论基础。

Method: 以多类逻辑回归为研究对象，在高维高斯混合模型框架下分析梯度优化动力学。通过投影到主成分来限制有效维度，利用类先验服从幂律分布的假设，推导出风险随时间演化的精确标度律，并在固定计算预算下优化容量项与优化项之间的折中。

Result: 研究表明学习按类别频率从高到低顺序进行（顺序学习效应）；在幂律类先验下，风险动态呈现三个相：初始平台期、幂律衰减期和最终收敛期。在受限于有效维度时，风险分解为容量项（关于保留维度的幂律）与优化项（关于训练时间的幂律），从而得到了针对逻辑回归的计算最优缩放律及模型大小和训练时间的显式闭式解。

Conclusion: 该工作将缩放律理论从线性回归推广至多类分类，揭示了类别不平衡、顺序学习与计算最优之间的深层联系，为理解大规模神经网络的训练行为提供了新视角，并对实际中如何分配计算资源（模型规模vs训练时间）给出了理论指导。

Abstract: We study the training dynamics of multiclass logistic regression on high-dimensional Gaussian mixture models with a large number of classes and establish precise scaling laws governing the cross-entropy risk under gradient-based optimization. We show that learning proceeds sequentially across classes, from most to least frequent. When the class priors follow a power law distribution, the risk dynamics decompose into three phases: an initial plateau until the first class is learned, a power-law decay regime during which sequential learning occurs, and a final convergence regime.
  We then analyze how model capacity interacts with optimization under a fixed compute budget. When the effective dimension is restricted via projection onto leading principal components, the risk decomposes into a capacity term (a power law in the retained dimension) and an optimization term (a power law in training time). Optimizing this tradeoff yields a compute-optimal scaling law for logistic regression, with explicit prescriptions for model size and training time as functions of compute. These results extend theoretical scaling laws from linear regression to multiclass classification, while connecting to empirical scaling laws observed in large-scale neural networks.

</details>


### [322] [The Role of Uncertainty in Assessing the Fairness of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07959)
*Francesca Panero,Ernst C. Wit,Marco Scutari*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文超越点估计，系统讨论公平机器学习中的不确定性量化问题，涵盖频率学派和贝叶斯学派方法，并通过模拟与真实数据给出实践示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型广泛应用于临床、社交媒体、执法和关键基础设施等敏感领域，验证其输出是否对弱势群体产生偏见至关重要。然而现有文献大多仅关注识别单个在预测准确性与公平性之间取得平衡的模型，很少对公平性违规进行严格的风险评估和不确定性量化，这可能导致对模型公平性的判断缺乏统计可靠性。

Method: 本文跳出点估计框架，从频率学派和贝叶斯学派两个视角分别讨论公平机器学习中的不确定性量化方法，包括如何对公平性指标构建置信区间/可信区间、如何评估模型选择与估计过程中的不确定性，并通过模拟数据和真实数据提供实际案例和影响分析。

Result: 文章展示了在不同数据设定下，频率学派与贝叶斯方法在公平性不确定性量化上的表现差异及实际含义，说明了忽视不确定性可能导致对公平性风险的低估或错误判断。

Conclusion: 公平机器学习的不确定性量化是实现可靠公平风险评估的必要环节，研究应超越单一的公平性—准确性权衡模型选择，系统纳入模型估计与选择过程中的统计不确定性。该研究为公平机器学习领域提供了方法论层面的新视角和实践指导。

Abstract: Machine learning models are widely used in clinical applications, social media, law enforcement and critical infrastructure. Verifying whether their outputs are biased against disadvantaged groups or individuals is crucial to ensuring they are fair and allowing their use in such settings. A rigorous risk assessment of possible fairness violations requires quantifying the uncertainty associated with selecting and estimating such models. Yet, this is rarely done in the literature, which focuses on identifying a single model with a suitable trade-off between predictive accuracy and fairness. In this paper, we move beyond point estimation and discuss frequentist and Bayesian approaches to uncertainty quantification for fair machine learning, with practical examples and implications for simulated and real data.

</details>


### [323] [Distribution-free inference on the number of changepoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08234)
*Rohan Hore,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在数据分布发生K次未知位置变化的情况下，如何对变化次数K进行无分布推断。作者证明了关于K的无分布置信上界必然平凡无信息，随后利用conformal p值构建了名为CLOCC的有限样本有效的置信下界，并证明其在所给假设下具有唯一性和普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变点检测问题中，现有方法大多关注如何定位变点位置，而对变点总数K的统计推断（尤其是无需分布假设的推断）缺乏系统研究。作者希望在不依赖具体分布假设的情况下，对K提供有意义的置信界。

Method: 首先证明任何无分布的上置信界必然是平凡且无信息的（不可能结果）。然后基于conformal p值方法，在数据段内部可交换且段间相互独立的假设下，构造一个有限样本有效的K的下置信界CLOCC，并论证其普遍性（即给定假设下唯一可行的下界方案）。文章还提供了选择评分函数的实际指导以提升下界的效率与紧致性。

Result: 在多个合成和真实数据实验中，CLOCC 能够提供有信息量的K值的下界，展示了其实用适用性。

Conclusion: 无分布推断变点数量K时，可行且有意义的方向是构造下置信界而非上置信界；CLOCC在极弱的假设下（段内可交换、段间独立）提供了唯一可行的有限样本有效下界，具有理论和实践价值。

Abstract: Suppose we are given an ordered sequence of independent data whose distribution changes $K$ times at unknown locations, for some unknown $K \geq 0$. In this paper, we study the problem of performing distribution-free inference on $K$. First, we show an impossibility result: any distribution-free upper confidence bound on $K$ must be trivial and uninformative. Then, using conformal $p$-values, and under only the assumption that the data segments induced by the changepoints are exchangeable (within themselves) and mutually independent, we construct a finite-sample valid lower confidence bound on $K$, which we call the Conformal LOwer bound on Changepoint Count (CLOCC). We show that CLOCC is the only feasible way to provide a lower bound on $K$ under the stated assumptions, a property we refer to as its universality. We provide practical guidelines for choosing score functions that yield efficient and tight lower bounds. We evaluate CLOCC in several synthetic and real-data experiments, where it provides informative lower bounds on $K$, demonstrating its practical applicability.

</details>


### [324] [Non-Adaptive 1-Bit Mean Estimation: Minimax Rates and the Sample-Interval Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08564)
*Ivan Lau,Jonathan Scarlett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式一维均值估计在1比特通信约束下的样本复杂度问题。作者证明对于所有k>1，非自适应协议可以达到与自适应协议相同的1比特极小极大速率（即单轮交互并非必要）。此外，当每个1-集合被限制为至多s个区间的并集时，作者给出了非自适应1比特估计器的极小极大样本复杂度，并刻画了样本复杂度与区间复杂度之间的完整权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lau和Scarlett提出的两阶段最优协议使用第一批响应来选择第二批次查询，这引发了一个核心问题：单轮交互（适应性质询）是否是达到最优1比特极小极大速率所必需的？作者希望回答这一问题，并进一步理解对查询集施加区间结构约束时样本复杂度的影响。

Method: 作者通过构造非自适应协议来证明其与自适应协议达到相同速率。具体来说，对于任意k>1，非自适应1比特协议能够达到自适应1比特极小极大速率。对于区间约束场景，作者分析了当每个1-集合Q^{-1}(1)被限制为至多s个区间的并集时，极小极大样本复杂度相比无约束情况增加了一个约(λσ/(sε²))·log(1/δ)的项，并通过k相关常数因子刻画了完整的权衡关系。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对每个k>1，单轮交互并非必要——非自适应协议即可达到自适应1比特极小极大速率（与并行工作的结论一致但策略不同）；(2) 在区间复杂度约束下，极小极大样本复杂度比无约束情况增加(λσ/(sε²))·log(1/δ)量级的项，给出样本复杂度与区间复杂度的完整权衡；(3) 作为推论，确定了保持无约束1比特极小极大样本速率所需的最小区间预算（按阶）。

Conclusion: 作者从理论上否定了自适应交互在1比特分布式均值估计中的必要性，证明非自适应协议即可达到最优速率，同时完整刻画了区间结构约束对样本复杂度的影响，为实际系统中设计简单（非自适应、区间查询）且高效的1比特通信协议提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study distributed one-dimensional mean estimation under a 1-bit communication constraint. Each agent observes one sample, drawn independently from an unknown distribution, and returns a single bit in response to a query $Q: \mathbb{R}\to\{0,1\}$ chosen by a central learner. The distribution has mean in $[-λ,λ]$ and $k$-th central moment at most $σ^k$, for a fixed $k>1$. The order-optimal two-stage protocol of Lau and Scarlett uses responses from the first batch to choose the second-batch queries, motivating the question of whether this single round of interaction is necessary. We answer this negatively: for every $k>1$, a non-adaptive protocol attains the adaptive 1-bit minimax rate (and concurrent works reached the same conclusion via different strategies). We further determine the minimax sample complexity among non-adaptive 1-bit estimators when every one-set $Q^{-1}(1)$ is restricted to a union of at most $s$ intervals. Relative to unrestricted non-adaptive 1-bit querying, this constraint adds a term of order $(λσ/(s\varepsilon^2))\log(1/δ)$, giving the full tradeoff between sample complexity and interval complexity to within $k$-dependent constant factors. As a corollary, we identify, order-wise, the minimum interval budget needed to retain the unrestricted 1-bit minimax sample rate.

</details>


### [325] [Optimal estimation for Functional Linear Regression with Noisy Discretized Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08671)
*Sixtine Sphabmixay*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究标量对函数线性回归模型，该模型在规则网格上观测受加性噪声污染的函数型协变量。提出两步估计方法：先用基于傅里叶的投影方法重构潜在曲线，再用带数据驱动维数选择的惩罚最小二乘准则在有限维三角空间上估计斜率函数。建立了预测误差的oracle型不等式，并在正则性假设下推导出收敛速度，证明网格点数足够大时估计量达到极小极大最优率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中函数型协变量通常在规则网格上离散观测且混入噪声，而非直接观测到光滑的潜在曲线。现有文献多假设曲线已完全观测或先进行平滑，本文旨在更贴近实际的采样方案下，提出同时处理离散观测和噪声的函数型线性回归估计方法，并给出理论保障。

Method: 两步估计程序：(1) 利用基于傅里叶的投影方法从离散含噪观测中重构底层潜在曲线；(2) 在有限维三角函数空间上，通过带惩罚的最小二乘准则估计斜率函数，模型维数采用数据驱动方式选择。推导了相对于重构曲线和真实潜在曲线的预测误差oracle型不等式。

Result: 在斜率函数正则性假设及协变量特征值多项式衰减条件下，推导出预测误差的收敛速率；当网格点数足够大时，证明估计量达到极小极大最优率。此外，通过模拟数据和真实气象数据集验证了方法的效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的两步法在规则网格含噪观测的实际采样框架下具有理论最优性（网络格点充分密时达极小极大率），且在实际数据中表现良好，为函数型线性回归在更现实条件下的应用提供了理论和方法支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the scalar-on-function linear regression model under a realistic sampling scheme in which the functional covariates are observed on a regular grid and contaminated by additive noise. We propose a two-step estimation procedure: first, the underlying curves are reconstructed from the discrete noisy observations using a Fourier-based projection method; second, the slope function is estimated by a penalized least-squares criterion over finite-dimensional trigonometric spaces, with data-driven selection of the model dimension. We establish oracle-type inequalities for the prediction error, both with respect to the reconstructed curves and to the true latent curves. Under regularity assumptions on the slope function and polynomial decay of the eigenvalues of the covariate, we derive convergence rates for the prediction error and show that our estimator attains the minimax rate when the number of grid points is sufficiently large. Finally, the proposed method is illustrated on simulated data and on a real meteorological dataset.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [326] [Remarks on Fixed- and Variable-Order Fractional-Laplacian Closures for TurbulentDissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05490)
*José I. H López*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文对比了两种旨在用分数阶Laplacian刻画湍流耗散的独立模型：Chen算子（固定阶数算子的叠加，相对权重随尺度漂移）与自适应分数阶Navier-Stokes（AFNS，算子阶数随局部Reynolds数连续流动）。尽管两者收敛于同一算子阶数的固定点，但作者证明它们属于不同的普适类，并提出一种尺度分辨可观测量——有效谱阶数，其对数反常项可在原则上区分两种机制。进一步在二维涡量守恒极限下得出纯解析结论：一旦涡旋拉伸被运动学关闭，将稳定过程指数与经验对散布定律相匹配的生成机制无解。该二分法在真实准二维流动中是否留下残差特征留作开放实验挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两种相隔二十年的独立尝试——固定阶数模型与自适应分数阶Navier-Stokes——都在算子阶数上收敛，表明其均以充分发展的惯性区为特征。但作者质疑这种表面上的一致性是否掩盖了深层机制差异，因此需要判定这两种构造是否属于同一普适类，并寻求可用于区分它们的理论判据与可观测信号。

Method: 作者首先证明尽管两种模型共享同一固定点，但它们属于不同的普适类：Chen算子是两种固定阶核的叠加且相对权重随尺度漂移，而AFNS则假设单一算子的阶数随局部Reynolds数连续流动。作者据此推导出尺度分辨的可观测量——有效谱阶数，利用其对数反常项在原则上区分两种机制。随后转向二维涡量守恒极限，通过运动学关闭涡旋拉伸，检验稳定过程指数与经验对散布定律匹配的生成机制是否存在解析解。

Result: 作者得到了更锐利的纯解析判定：在二维涡量守恒极限中（涡旋拉伸被关闭），将稳定过程指数与经验对散布定律相匹配的生成机制不存在解，从而在理论层面排除了其中一种普适类的有效性。

Conclusion: 两种分数阶Laplacian湍流耗散模型虽收敛于同一算子阶数特征，但在机制上分属不同普适类。二维极限下的解析无解表明二者存在本质区分，但该二分法在真实准二维流动中能否留下可观测的残余特征，仍需实验来最终裁决。

Abstract: Two independent attempts, separated by two decades, to encode turbulent dissipation with a fractional Laplacian both converge on the operator order as the signature of the fully developedinertial range: the additive, fixed order model, and the dynamically deforming operator of the Adaptive Fractional Navier Stokes. We show that, despite sharing this fixed point, the two constructions belongto distinct universality classes: Chen operator is a superposition of two fixed order kernels whose relative weight drifts with scale, while AFNS posits a single operator whose order itself flows continuously with the local Reynolds number. We derive a scale resolved observable, the effective spectral order, whose logarithmic anomaly term discriminates between these two mechanisms in principle, and then obtain a sharper, purely analytic verdict from the two dimensional, enstrophy conserving limit: the generative mechanism matching a stable process index to the empirical pair dispersion law has no solution once vortex stretching is kinematically switched off. Whether this dichotomy leaves a residual signature in real quasi two dimensional flows is left as an open experimental challenge.

</details>


### [327] [A Gauge Theory of Turbulence:](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05544)
*V. E. R. Lemes*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文形式化地将不可压缩流体的Navier-Stokes方程规范化为类似电磁学中Coulomb规范的结构，构建了完整的Martin-Siggia-Rose作用量及其BRST对称性，通过Higgs机制使Gribov参数获得真空期望值产生质量尺度，并用Gribov启发的log-Poisson层级来描述湍流间歇性（精确满足ζ₃=1），为湍流研究开辟了量子场论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典流体力学中的Navier-Stokes方程与规范场论及自发对称破缺统一，从而引入量子场论的成熟方法（BRST对称性、Higgs机制、有效势）来研究湍流和间歇性这一长期未解的经典物理难题。

Method: 将∇·v=0解释为如同Coulomb规范一样的规范固定条件；构造MSR作用量及其完整BRST对称性；为Gribov参数γ和单值相θ引入BRST双重态；通过Higgs机制使γ获得真空期望值γ₀，产生涡量和鬼场的质量尺度；计算单圈有效势并分析真空稳定性；用Higgs场σ围绕凝聚态的涨落结合Gribov启发的log-Poisson层级描述间歇性指数ζₙ；在d=3中计算单圈反常维数Δ_σ≈0.50。

Result: 间歇性指数ζₙ通过log-Poisson层级精确满足ζ₃=1（Kolmogorov四分之五定律）；对DNS数据的拟合倾向于丝状涡量结构，单参数拟合给出D_f≈1，双参数拟合给出D_f≈2.1和Δ≈0.44；三维单圈反常维数Δ_σ≈0.50支持D_f=2Δ_σ的识别。

Conclusion: 该形式主义将经典流体力学与规范理论和自发对称破缺统一起来，为利用量子场论方法研究湍流和间歇性开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We formalize the gauge structure of the Navier--Stokes equation for incompressible fluids, interpreting $\nabla\cdot\mathbf{v}=0$ as a gauge fixing analogous to the Coulomb gauge in electromagnetism. We construct the Martin--Siggia--Rose action and its full BRST symmetry, introducing BRST doublets for the Gribov parameter $γ$ and the monodromy phase $θ$. Through a Higgs mechanism, $γ$ acquires a vacuum expectation value $γ_{0}$, generating a mass scale for the vorticity and the ghosts; we compute the one-loop effective potential and analyze vacuum stability. Intermittency --- measured by the exponents $ζ_{n}$ --- is described by fluctuations of the Higgs field $σ$ around the condensate, via a Gribov-inspired log-Poisson hierarchy that satisfies exactly $ζ_{3}=1$ (Kolmogorov's four-fifths law). Fits to DNS data favor filamentary vorticity structures, with $D_{f}\approx1$ (one parameter) or $D_{f}\approx2.1$, $Δ\approx0.44$ (two parameters). The one-loop anomalous dimension in $d=3$, $Δ_σ\approx0.50$, supports the identification $D_{f}=2Δ_σ$. The formalism unifies classical hydrodynamics with gauge theory and spontaneous symmetry breaking, opening the study of turbulence and intermittency to quantum field theory methods.

</details>


### [328] [Direct Numerical Simulation of Thermoacoustically Unstable Flames Via Non-Drifting Acoustic Delay Characteristic Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05656)
*Benjamin L. Cookman,Jack R. C. King,Raphaël C. Assier,Steven J. Lind*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过直接数值模拟（DNS）研究了装有声学封闭上游端和开放下游端的管道中理想化预混火焰的热声不稳定性。研究发现初级不稳定性在火焰变平时发生非线性饱和，并伴随非稳态瑞利-泰勒效应引起的火焰指状振荡。为降低计算成本，仅在火焰周围区域进行DNS，采用NSCBC方法（ADCBC）模拟声学在进出口的延迟再入，并引入新的APILR方法（在经典线性松弛基础上增加积分控制项以消除非零时均入流速度）以维持入流速度和出流压力的时间平均值。两种新方法在惰性和逆流火焰测试中均展现出良好性能，使燃烧不稳定性的数值模拟计算成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热声不稳定性是预混火焰中重要的工程问题，理解其非线性机制至关重要。然而由于数值模拟成本高昂，相关研究稀缺，因此需要开发低成本的数值方法来实现此类现象的模拟。

Method: 采用DNS仅模拟火焰周围区域，利用NSCBC方法（称为ADCBC）描述声学在流入和流出端的延迟再入；同时引入新的APILR方法，在经典线性松弛（CLR）基础上添加积分控制项以维持时均入流速度和出流压力，保证火焰位置可控。方法在惰性和逆流火焰测试用例中验证。

Result: 模拟结果展示了初级不稳定性的非线性饱和（伴随火焰变平）以及非稳态瑞利-泰勒效应引起的火焰指状振荡。APILR和ADCBC两种新方法均验证了其有效性，能大幅降低燃烧不稳定性数值模拟的计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提出的ADCBC和APILR方法使得在显著降低计算开销的条件下实现热声不稳定火焰的DNS成为可能，为低成本研究燃烧不稳定性提供了有效的数值工具。

Abstract: Thermoacoustic instability in premixed flames results from the coupling between the flame's heat release, as determined by combustion parameters, and surrounding acoustics, as determined by combustor geometry. A primary instability results in flame flattening as intrinsic flame instability modes are stabilised. Secondary thermoacoustic instability results in a parametric flame instability and drastic growth of acoustic amplitudes. Due to their relative expense, numerical simulations of these phenomena remain scarce. In this work, Direct Numerical Simulations (DNS) of thermoacoustically unstable idealised premixed flames in a tube with acoustically closed upstream and open downstream ends are presented. Results herein demonstrate nonlinear saturation of the primary instability as the flame flattens as well as an oscillating flame fingering characteristic of the unsteady Rayleigh-Taylor effect. To reduce computational cost, we perform DNS only on the region surrounding the flame. Acoustics at in- and outflows are described using the Navier-Stokes Characteristic Boundary Condition (NSCBC) method to model their delayed reentry into the domain in a formulation referred to as the Acoustic Delay Characteristic Boundary Condition (ADCBC) method. A new Averaged Proportional and Integral Linear Relaxation (APILR) method is also introduced, which modifies the Classic Linear Relaxation (CLR) method to maintain time-averaged values of inflow velocity and outflow pressure. Here, an integral control term is used to remove non-zero equilibrium time-averaged inflow velocities which impinge control over flame position. Both new methods demonstrate their capability in inert and counterflow flames test cases. These methods enable the numerical simulation of combustion instabilities at significantly reduced computational expense.

</details>


### [329] [Unsteady Thin-Airfoil Theory Revisited: An Approximate Analytical Solution and the Self-Similar Wagner Effect in Viscous Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05839)
*Tianshu Liu,Jianfeng Lin,Shizhao Wang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文从黏性流动视角推导了薄翼在一般非定常运动下的非定常升力的近似解析解，尾涡涡片强度以显式卷积形式给出（作为Wagner积分方程的近似解）。该解被验证应用于Wagner和Theodorsen问题，并以有限时间尺度起动流推广到广义Wagner问题，揭示了重归一化环量升力系数的自相似性及其与重归一化Wagner函数的等价性。更重要的是，在低雷诺数起动平板翼的数值模拟中发现，即使流动中等程度分离，仍存在自相似Wagner效应，该自相似性体现了雷诺数不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典非定常空气动力学理论（如Wagner和Theodorsen理论）通常基于势流假设，忽略了黏性效应和流动分离的影响。作者希望从黏性流视角出发，建立更一般的非定常升力解析模型，以在流动存在中等分离时仍能捕捉Wagner效应等核心物理特征，并探索其雷诺数不变性。

Method: 作者从黏性流视角推导薄翼非定常升力的近似解析解，其中尾涡涡片强度以显式卷积表达式给出，作为Wagner积分方程的近似解。将该解析解分别退化为Wagner问题、Theodorsen问题和广义Wagner问题（有限时间尺度起动流），并通过低雷诺数下起动平板翼的数值模拟进行验证，考察流动分离条件下的自相似性。

Result: 该解析解在退化为Wagner和Theodorsen问题时分别给出了Wagner函数和Theodorsen函数的显式积分形式。在广义Wagner问题中，重归一化环量升力系数表现出自相似性，并在有限时间域内与重归一化Wagner函数等价。数值模拟表明，即使在中等分离的低雷诺数起动平板翼流动中，自相似Wagner效应依然存在。

Conclusion: 自相似Wagner效应在包含中等分离的实际黏性流动中仍然成立，说明非定常升力的起动过程中存在雷诺数不变性，这为超越经典势流理论适用范围的非定常空气动力学建模提供了理论基础。

Abstract: An approximate analytical solution for the unsteady lift of a thin airfoil with a general unsteady motion is derived from a viscous-flow perspective, where the wake vortex-sheet strength is given in an explicit convolution-type expression as an approximate solution of the Wagner integral equation. For validation, this analytical solution is applied to the Wagner and Theodorsen problems, giving the explicit integral forms of the Wagner and Theodorsen functions as the reduced cases. Further, this analytical solution is applied to the starting flow with a finite timescale in the generalized Wagner problem, revealing the self-similarity of the re-normalized circulatory lift coefficient and its equivalence to the re-normalized Wagner function in a finite time domain. More importantly, the self-similar Wagner effect is found in numerical simulation of the flow over a starting flat-plate airfoil at low Reynolds numbers even when the flow is moderately separated. This self-similarity represents the Reynolds-number-invariance.

</details>


### [330] [Phase Reduction Analysis of Transonic Buffet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05967)
*Michael Candon,Earl Dowell*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了跨声速抖振的相位约化描述，将抖振视为受弱简谐结构运动扰动的稳定自治流体极限环，并结合气动功得出等效气动阻尼与刚度的闭式表达式，统一解释了相位锁定、相位滑移及流固系统的气动弹性稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨声速抖振与结构振动之间存在复杂的流固耦合，传统方法难以统一描述相位锁定、相位滑移及气动弹性失稳等非线性现象，缺乏对机理的深入理解。

Method: 采用相位约化方法将跨声速抖振建模为受弱谐波结构运动扰动的稳定极限环，结合气动功分析，推导等效气动阻尼和刚度的闭合解析表达式，并建立统一的相位动力学框架。

Result: 获得了气动阻尼图谱，揭示了零失谐（zero detuning）处的奇异行为，以及大范围的亚临界气动弹性失稳区域，为振荡流中气动弹性失稳的机理提供了新的见解。

Conclusion: 相位约化模型能够以统一的数学框架描述跨声速抖振下流固系统的相位锁定、相位滑移与气动弹性稳定性，为理解和预测振荡流中的气动弹性失稳提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper develops a phase-reduced description of transonic buffet, treating buffet as a stable autonomous fluid limit cycle that is weakly perturbed by harmonic structural motion. When combined with aerodynamic work, the phase reduction model yields closed-form expressions for the equivalent aerodynamic damping and stiffness, together with a unified description of phase locking, phase slipping and aeroelastic stability of the fluid-structural system. The resulting analysis provides new insight into the mechanisms governing aeroelastic instability in oscillatory flows, including aerodynamic damping maps, singular behaviour at zero detuning, and broad regions of subcritical aeroelastic instability.

</details>


### [331] [Coupled Analytical Model for the Internal Boundary Layer Height, Wall Shear Stress and Mean Velocity Behind a Surface Roughness Transition in Boundary-Layer Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06034)
*Kingshuk Mondal,Niranjan S. Ghaisas*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个耦合解析框架，用于预测中性大气边界层中表面粗糙度突变下游的内部边界层高度、壁面剪应力和平均速度剖面。新模型将三层解析速度公式与改进的扩散类比相结合用于IBL增长率预测，并针对12组数据集（涵盖风洞实验和大涡模拟）进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面粗糙度突变是大气边界层中常见的流动现象，准确预测其下游的IBL高度、壁面剪应力和速度剖面对于风能、空气污染扩散和城市气象等领域至关重要。此前已有模型存在物理不一致性：一是假设湍流扩散仅受上游表面条件控制，忽略了下游粗糙度的影响；二是假定由流线位移引起的特征流向速度差与距粗糙度突变点的下游距离无关。需要解决这些局限并提出更物理合理的模型。

Method: 新模型由两部分组成：(1) 三层解析速度公式用于描述粗糙度突变下游的平均速度剖面；(2) 改进的扩散类比用于IBL增长率预测。扩散类比将IBL增长率与湍流扩散和平均垂直对流联系起来。关键参数——湍流扩散系数和涡粘性增强系数——被建模为上下游空气动力学粗糙长度之比的函数，从而修正了先前模型中将湍流扩散仅归因于上游条件且特征速度差与下游距离无关的物理不一致性。

Result: 耦合模型与另外三个解析模型一起，在12组数据集上进行了测试，涵盖广泛的粗糙度比，包括风洞实验和大涡模拟结果。新模型在所有上游和下游粗糙度组合中准确预测了壁面剪应力、平均速度和IBL高度，并且对光滑到粗糙和粗糙到光滑两种过渡均表现良好。

Conclusion: 提出的耦合解析模型通过修正先前模型的物理缺陷（考虑下游条件对湍流扩散的影响，以及特征速度差随下游距离的变化），显著提升了对粗糙度突变下游流动参数的预测精度，并已在广泛的实验和数值模拟数据上得到验证。

Abstract: A coupled analytical framework is developed to predict the internal boundary layer (IBL) height, wall shear stress and the mean velocity profile downstream of a surface roughness transition in a neutral atmospheric boundary-layer. The new model combines a three-layer analytical velocity formulation with a modified diffusion analogy for the IBL growth rate. The diffusion analogy links the growth rate of the IBL to turbulent diffusion and to mean vertical advection. Our formulation rectifies physical inconsistencies in a previous model that assumed that the turbulent diffusion is controlled only by the upstream surface conditions and that the characteristic streamwise velocity difference caused by streamline displacement is independent of downstream distance from the roughness transition. Key model parameters, namely the turbulent diffusion coefficient and the eddy viscosity augmentation coefficient, are modelled as functions of the upstream-to-downstream aerodynamic roughness length ratio. The coupled model, along with three other analytical models, is tested against twelve datasets covering a wide range of roughness ratios, including wind-tunnel experiments and large-eddy simulations. The new model accurately predicts wall shear stress, mean velocity, and IBL height for all combinations of upstream and downstream roughness values evaluated, for both smooth-to-rough and rough-to-smooth transitions.

</details>


### [332] [Sloshing reduction in a swaying tank with porous baffles using scaled boundary finite element method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06141)
*Pramod,Aln,Choudhury,Aditi,Vijay,KG,Natarajan,S*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文采用线性化势流理论与比例边界有限元法（SBFEM），研究了部分充液晃荡水箱中多种构型的多孔薄挡板对晃荡的抑制作用，发现顶装式凸形挡板在所有晃荡模态下能实现约50%的抑制提升，是最有效的构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晃荡是远洋船舶不可避免的现象且会产生不利影响，因此需要研究有效的晃荡抑制措施，特别是通过多孔挡板的不同构型设计来系统评估其减晃效果。

Method: 在线性化势流理论框架下使用比例边界有限元法（SBFEM）求解边值问题；假设薄多孔挡板的流场遵循达西定律；将计算域划分为最少数量的子域以保证星凸性；沿各子域边界使用高阶多项式表示未知速度势场；系统研究挡板构型、孔隙率、水箱宽度、挡板浸没深度及相邻挡板间距等参数的影响。

Result: 与已有文献结果验证了数值模型的正确性；参数研究表明，考虑所有晃荡模态时，顶装式挡板较底装式垂直挡板可提升晃荡抑制效果约50%；顶装式凸形挡板构型在所有模态下实现了均衡的减晃效果，是最有效的构型。

Conclusion: 顶装式凸形多孔挡板构型是晃荡抑制的最佳方案，能够在所有晃荡模式下取得均衡且显著的减晃效果，为船舶晃荡控制提供了有价值的工程设计参考。

Abstract: Sloshing is an inevitable phenomenon in an ocean-going vessel that can have adverse effects. In this work, the mitigation of sloshing is investigated using multiple thin porous baffles of various configurations in a partially filled swaying tank. The boundary value problem is solved within the framework of a linearized potential flow theory using the scaled boundary finite element method (SBFEM). The flow through the thin porous baffles is assumed to follow Darcy's law. The computational domain is divided into a minimum number of subdomains due to the presence of porous baffles and to ensure star convexity. Higher-order polynomials are used along each subdomain edge to represent the unknown field, i.e., velocity potential. The developed numerical model is validated with the known results in the literature. Subsequently, various results, such as the amplification factor and the forces of the tank wall, are presented and discussed for the effect of configuration, porosity, slosh tank width, depth of baffle submergence and the space between adjacent baffles. From the parametric study, it is observed that top-mounted baffles enhance sloshing suppression by $50\%$ compared to bottom-mounted vertical baffles, considering all sloshing modes. Assessing the overall effectiveness, top-mounted convex baffle configuration emerges as the most efficient configuration for sloshing suppression, achieving a well-balanced reduction across all modes.

</details>


### [333] [Hydrodynamic mechanism and suppression of cavity breathing oscillations in a twin-cavity supersonic combustor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06142)
*Sumit Lonkar,Singeetham Pranaykumar,Pratikash P. Panda*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了对冲双腔超燃冲压燃烧室中的低频腔体"呼吸"振荡现象。在低焓条件下观察到约156 Hz的自持呼吸模态，通过实验与数值模拟揭示了腔体与自由来流之间周期性质量交换驱动压力-剪切层反馈回路的机制。提出了耦合剪切层位移坐标的两压力状态降阶模型，可复现频率与稳定性特征。发现上游氮气注入仅产生瞬态衰减，而乙烯注入点火可永久抑制振荡，使非线性吸引子坍缩至稳定平衡点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对冲双腔构型中低频腔体呼吸振荡会严重影响火焰稳定性和可操作性，但其水动力学起源与抑制机理尚未被充分理解。理解该振荡的产生与抑制机制对于超燃冲压燃烧室的稳定运行至关重要。

Method: 采用多手段联用方法：同步高速Schlieren成像、壁面压力测量、数值模拟、非线性相空间重构、希尔伯特变换分析以及降阶建模。提出一种耦合剪切层位移坐标的两压力状态降阶模型来描述腔体呼吸动力学，并通过对比实验测量中氮气与乙烯注入对振荡的影响来验证抑制机制。

Result: 低焓条件下观测到约156 Hz的自持呼吸模态，周期性的腔体-自由流质量交换驱动腔体泄压与再增压，形成压力-剪切层反馈回路。降阶模型准确复现了测量频率、压力相位关系和稳定性特征。氮气注入仅产生瞬态衰减；而乙烯点火注入永久抑制振荡，吸引子坍缩至稳定平衡。热释放通过重组腔体压力场并改变密度、可压缩性及压力响应时间尺度来削弱维持呼吸模态的反馈机制。

Conclusion: 对冲双腔超燃燃烧室的低频呼吸振荡由腔体腔压与再附着/激波脚压力状态共同控制，其反馈回路可通过热释放（乙烯点火燃烧）注入来永久打断从而实现有效抑制，而单纯的惰性气体注入不足以实现持久控制。

Abstract: Low-frequency cavity breathing oscillations can strongly affect flame stabilization and operability in cavity-stabilized supersonic combustors, yet their hydrodynamic origin and suppression remain insufficiently understood in opposed twin-cavity configurations. We investigate the breathing dynamics of an opposed twin-cavity supersonic combustor under low- and high-enthalpy conditions using synchronized high-speed Schlieren imaging, wall-pressure measurements, numerical simulations, nonlinear phase-space reconstruction, Hilbert-transform analysis, and reduced-order modelling. Under low-enthalpy conditions, a self-sustained breathing mode is observed at a dominant frequency of approximately 156 Hz, with large phase differences between cavity-floor and ramp pressures. Measurements and simulations show that periodic mass exchange between the cavity and freestream drives cyclic cavity depressurization and repressurization, establishing a pressure-shear-layer feedback loop that sustains the oscillation. A two-pressure-state reduced-order model, coupled with a shear-layer displacement coordinate, reproduces the measured frequency, pressure phase relationship, and stability characteristics, demonstrating that both bulk-cavity and reattachment/shock-foot pressure states are required to capture the dynamics. Upstream nitrogen injection produces only transient attenuation, with the oscillation recovering as the injected gas convects downstream. In contrast, ethylene injection followed by ignition permanently suppresses the oscillation, with the nonlinear attractor collapsing to a stable equilibrium. Heat release reorganizes the cavity pressure field and alters density, compressibility, and pressure-response timescales, thereby weakening the feedback mechanism responsible for sustaining the breathing mode.

</details>


### [334] [Maintenance of a columnar vortex by inertial waves in rotational turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06158)
*Nikolay A. Ivchenko,Sergey S. Vergeles,Daniil D. Tumachev,Sergey V. Filatov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种理论，用于确定在快速旋转的湍流流体中形成的相干地转涡旋的平均速度径向剖面。理论假设涡旋内的流动由来自涡旋外围的短波长惯性波吸收所维持，且波间的非线性相互作用可忽略。实验结果支持该模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解湍流快速旋转流体中相干地转涡旋的形成机制及其平均速度径向剖面的理论预测，是一个重要的流体动力学问题。现有理论尚不能充分解释实验观察到的涡旋速度剖面，需要建立新的理论框架。

Method: 建立一种理论模型，基于实验条件假设涡旋内部流动由外围到达的短波长惯性波的吸收所驱动，并假设波浪间的非线性相互作用在理论中可以忽略，从而推导出相干地转涡旋的平均速度径向剖面。

Result: 理论推导得到的平均速度径向剖面与实验数据吻合，支持了所提出模型的正确性和适用性。

Conclusion: 所发展的理论能够成功地预测湍流快速旋转流体中相干地转涡旋的平均速度径向剖面，验证了惯性波吸收作为涡旋维持机制这一假设的合理性。

Abstract: We develop a theory that determines the radial profile of the mean velocity in a coherent geostrophic vortex forming in a turbulent rapidly rotating fluid. Following the conditions of our experiments, we assume that the flow in the vortex is sustained by the absorption of short-wavelength inertial waves arriving from the vortex periphery. The nonlinear interaction between waves is assumed negligible in the theory. Comparison with experimental data supports the validity of the developed model.

</details>


### [335] [Turbulence Decay Intensifies Clustering of Bubbles and Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06389)
*Dante L. Naylor,Zhiyu Yang,Lei Yi,Rodolfo Ostilla Monico,Enrico Calzavarini,Varghese Mathai*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过实验与直接数值模拟研究自由衰减湍流中惯性粒子和气泡的动力学行为。研究发现粒子加速度随时间单调衰减，但团簇强度呈现先增强后减弱的非单调演化。通过动态重标度方法，可将衰减湍流中的行为映射到稳态湍流，映射在广泛密度比和两个数量级Stokes数范围内均适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有对湍流中惯性粒子动力学的理解主要基于统计稳态湍流，但自然界许多流动的能量输入是间歇性、循环性或可能突然终止的。为了真实理解自然流动中的粒子行为，需要研究自由衰减湍流中粒子和气泡的动力学。

Method: 采用互补的实验测量与直接数值模拟相结合的方法；利用湍流的演化长度尺度和时间尺度对数据进行动态重标度，将衰减湍流中的加速度和团簇行为映射到稳态湍流的对应结果；并推导了动态重标度成立的效度条件。

Result: 发现粒子加速度随时间单调衰减，而团簇演化是非单调的——先急剧增强后减弱。动态重标度在实验与数值数据中均有效，且适用于从轻到重的广泛密度比粒子以及跨越两个数量级范围的Stokes数。

Conclusion: 自由衰减湍流中的惯性粒子动力学可以通过动态重标度统一地映射到稳态湍流框架中理解，这为描述自然流动（如非稳态环境）中的粒子弥散和团簇提供了普适的理论工具。

Abstract: Our understanding of inertial particle dynamics in turbulence is mostly based on flows held in a statistically stationary state, a particular regime that differs from many natural flows where energy input can often be intermittent or cyclic, or may abruptly cease. Here we investigate inertial particle and bubble dynamics in freely decaying turbulence through complementary experiments and direct numerical simulations. While particle accelerations decay monotonically in time, we find evidence that the clustering can exhibit a non-monotonic evolution, intensifying sharply before subsequently weakening. We demonstrate that both the acceleration and clustering behaviors can be mapped onto their counterparts in stationary turbulence using a dynamic rescaling of the evolving length and time scales of the turbulence. Validity conditions for the dynamic rescaling, satisfied by both the experimental and numerical datasets, are derived. The proposed mappings remain applicable across a broad range of density ratios, from light to heavy particles, and particle sizes spanning two orders of magnitude in Stokes number.

</details>


### [336] [Transverse transport of microswimmers in oscillatory channel flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06393)
*Raghav Ram,Ashwin Ramachandran*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了振荡压力驱动通道流中细长微游泳体的横向输运，通过Langevin模拟与Fokker-Planck模型的互补验证，揭示了振荡流动如何根本性地改变经典稳态剪切滞留效应，并推导出了一系列解析解和普适传递定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动微生物广泛存在于生理和工程系统中的振荡流中，但流动非定常性对其剪切诱导的择优浓度分布的影响尚未被充分理解，这构成了本研究的核心动机。

Method: 采用Langevin模拟与一维和二维Fokker-Planck模型进行互补研究；在弱游泳极限下，推导了耦合方向矩的递推层次，实现任意时间谐波阶次的解析求解；利用Womersley数（Wo）和频率比（β，即转动扩散率与流动振荡频率之比）两个无量纲参数描述系统。

Result: (1) 粒子模拟与连续统模型在各控制参数下吻合，验证了Fokker-Planck公式的可靠性；(2) 增大Wo将振荡剪切限制在更薄的近壁区域，减小中心线贫化；(3) 增大β增强持续的取向各向异性并导致中心线贫化的饱和式增加；(4) 解析解建立普适传递定律：给定游泳体形状下，归一化首阶取向响应仅依赖于β；(5) 稳态流动弱剪切标度I_D∝Pe_f^2被恢复，振荡强迫以因子16β²/(1+16β²)衰减此响应。

Conclusion: 本文为预测振荡通道流中微游泳体的横向输运提供了完整的理论框架，建立了从取向动力学到浓度分布及贫化指数的封闭解析表达。

Abstract: Motile microorganisms inhabit oscillatory flows encountered in physiological and engineering systems, yet the influence of flow unsteadiness on their shear-induced preferential concentration remains less understood. We investigate transverse transport of elongated microswimmers in oscillatory pressure-driven channel flow using complementary Langevin simulations and two- and one-dimensional Fokker-Planck models. Agreement between particle-based and continuum descriptions across governing parameters establishes the fidelity of the Fokker-Planck formulation for swimmer transport in oscillatory shear flows. Our analysis demonstrates that oscillatory forcing fundamentally modifies classical steady-flow shear-trapping. We find that increasing Womersley number $Wo$ reduces centerline depletion by confining oscillatory shear to thinner near-wall regions. In contrast, increasing frequency ratio $β$, of swimmer rotational diffusion rate and flow oscillation frequency, promotes sustained orientational anisotropy, and leads to a saturating increase in centerline depletion. In the weak-swimming limit, we derive a hierarchy of coupled orientational moments, enabling analytical solutions at arbitrary temporal harmonic order. The asymptotic solutions establish a universal transfer law showing that, for a given swimmer shape, the normalized leading-order orientational response depends solely on $β$. Reconstruction of the orientational distribution yields closed-form expressions for the swimmer concentration profile and depletion index, $I_D$. Our asymptotic solution recovers the steady-flow weak-shear scaling of $I_D$ with flow Peclet number, $Pe_f$, of $I_D \propto Pe_f^2$, and reveals that oscillatory forcing attenuates this response by a factor $16β^2/(1+16β^2)$. These results provide a theoretical framework to predict transverse transport of microswimmers in oscillatory channel flows.

</details>


### [337] [Unification of The Navier-Stokes Equation for Fluid Mechanics and Aeroacoustics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06457)
*Tapan K. Sengupta*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了可压缩牛顿流体的纳维-斯托克斯方程，质疑了斯托克斯假设（即忽略体积粘度）的必要性，提出了一种新的广义运动粘度概念及方法论，可在不依赖斯托克斯假设的情况下估计第二粘性系数，从而为流体力学和气动声学提供统一的N-S方程表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于近期气动声学基础研究的结果，作者认为传统的斯托克斯假设（将体积粘度设为零）在实际应用中存在局限，有必要重新评估N-S方程的完整性，以弥合流体力学与气动声学之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 重新审视可压缩牛顿流体的N-S方程推导过程，批判性分析斯托克斯假设的由来与当前实践；提出一种新的广义运动粘度概念，并发展出能够估计第二粘性系数（体积粘度）的新方法论，从而完全避免对斯托克斯假设的依赖。

Result: 提出了一种新的广义运动粘度和新的估算方法，使得在不做出斯托克斯假设的条件下也能确定第二粘性系数，为全新的N-S方程表述开辟了道路，该方程可统一应用于流体力学与气动声学两个领域。

Conclusion: 斯托克斯假设应当被废弃，取而代之的是更一般的粘性理论框架；新的N-S方程形式有望实现流体力学与气动声学的统一理论基础。

Abstract: The Navier-Stokes equation for a compressible Newtonian fluid is reassessed, in view of recent investigation results on the foundations of aeroacoustics. In the process, the role and necessity of the so-called Stokes' hypothesis is reconsidered, which in its current practice omits the bulk viscosity. In contrast, the relevance of a new generalized kinematic viscosity is proposed, along with a new methodology that will enable estimation of second coefficient of viscosity completely obviating the necessity of making the Stokes' hypothesis. This opens up the vista of a new formulation for the Navier-Stokes equation applicable for both the subjects of fluid mechanics and aeroacoustics in a unified approach.

</details>


### [338] [A Hybrid POD-Autoencoder Framework for Reduced Order Modeling of Turbulent Flow via Strategic Field Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06992)
*Xianglong Li,Zeng Liu,Zhan Wang,Kai Wang,Shunxiang Cao,Guangyao Wang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种混合降阶模型框架，将湍流场按时间特征分解为POD保留场和POD截断场，分别用VAR模型和FK-β-VAE结合切换VAR模型进行预测，在Re_τ=110的槽道湍流中验证了良好的统计预测精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单一场建模的降阶模型在预测湍流的长期统计特性时往往精度不足或不够稳健。本文动机在于按时间尺度对湍流动力学进行分解并分别建模，以提升长时程统计预测的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: 首先将全场划分为由少量POD模态表示的子场（POD保留场）及残余子场（POD截断场）；采用频率信息POD策略基于模态能量和主频选择保留模态；用VAR模型预测时间尺度相近的保留POD系数动力学；POD截断场通过基于Fourier神经算子的Koopman β变分自编码器（FK-β-VAE）压缩到低维潜空间，并用切换VAR模型预测潜变量；将两个分量贡献组合恢复全场的湍流统计量。在摩擦雷诺数Re_τ=110的湍流槽道流中进行评估。

Result: 预测的雷诺应力分量、湍动能（TKE）和主波数谱与参考值良好吻合；相比不做场分解的全场建模框架，本框架提供了更准确和稳健的长期统计预测。

Conclusion: 基于时间特征分解的混合ROM框架能够有效改善湍流长期统计量预测的精度与鲁棒性，为该类流的仿真提供了一种可行的替代方案。

Abstract: This study proposes a hybrid reduced-order modeling (ROM) framework for the simulation of turbulent flow. The central idea is to decompose flow dynamics according to their temporal characteristics and predict the resulting components individually. The full field is first divided into a sub-field represented by a limited number of proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) modes (named as POD-retained field) and the corresponding residual sub-field (named as POD-truncated field). A frequency-informed POD strategy identifies the retained modes by considering both modal energy and dominant frequency. The evolution of retained POD coefficients, which feature similar temporal scales, is described using a vector autoregressive (VAR) model. In parallel, the POD-truncated field is compressed into a low-dimensional latent space using a Fourier-neural-operator-based Koopman $β$-variational autoencoder (FK-$β$-VAE), with the latent variables subsequently predicted by a switching-VAR model. Turbulent statistics of the full field are recovered by combining the contributions from the two components. The framework is assessed using turbulent channel flow at a friction Reynolds number of $110$. The predicted Reynolds-stress components, turbulent kinetic energy (TKE), and dominant wavenumber spectra show good agreement with the reference. Moreover, in comparison with an alternative framework of full-field modeling (i.e., without field decomposition), the proposed framework yields more accurate and robust long-term statistical predictions.

</details>


### [339] [A Universal Vortex-Formation Law for Insect Hovering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07073)
*David Greenblatt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究揭示昆虫悬停飞行时扑翼产生的前缘涡（LEV）的形成频率遵循经典的涡脱落普适Strouhal数范围（约0.15-0.17），将人工生成/自然产生的LEV与经典分离流统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑动昆虫翅膀产生的前缘涡（LEV）是悬停升力的主要来源，但其形成频率与经典涡脱落之间的关系此前尚不清楚。作者希望找到LEV形成的统一时间尺度，并将其与经典的钝体尾流涡脱落联系起来。

Method: 结合Roshko钝体尾流标度和Sigurdson对壁面受限分离流的扩展，确立约0.15-0.17的普适涡形成Strouhal数范围。首先对静止平板翼发展近似标度（通过周期性前缘扰动产生离散LEV），并检验最大升力出现的Strouhal范围；随后将该框架应用于已发表的昆虫悬停飞行数据，其中每个平移半行程形成一个LEV。

Result: 静止平板翼最大升力出现在普适Strouhal范围内；悬停昆虫数据尽管在形态、运动学和Reynolds数上差异巨大，仍恢复出相同的Strouhal范围（约0.15-0.17）。

Conclusion: 存在一个共同涡形成时间尺度，将经典分离流、人工生成的前缘涡以及维持昆虫悬停飞行的自然LEV连接起来，表明LEV形成本质上是经典的流动控制现象。

Abstract: Flapping insect wings generate leading-edge vortices (LEVs) that produce much of the lift required for hovering, yet the frequency governing their formation has not been connected to classical vortex shedding. Roshko scaling for bluff-body wakes, together with the Sigurdson extension to wall-bounded separated flows, identifies a universal vortex-formation Strouhal-number range of approximately 0.15 to 0.17. Here we show that this same range governs LEV formation on both stationary wings and hovering insect wings. We first develop an approximate scaling for stationary flat-plate wings in which discrete LEVs are generated by periodic leading-edge perturbations, and show that maximum lift consistently occurs within the universal Strouhal range. In hovering insects, one LEV forms during each translational half-stroke, allowing the same framework to be applied to published flight data. Despite large differences in morphology, kinematics and Reynolds number, the insect data recover the same Strouhal range. These results reveal a common vortex-formation timescale linking classical separated flows, artificially generated LEVs and the natural LEVs that sustain insect hovering.

</details>


### [340] [Micro-convection mass transfer following bubble coalescence on a solid wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07232)
*Zahra Saadat,Michele Cattani,Amin Soltani,Pourya Forooghi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用Basilisk求解器中的VOF方法，研究了气泡在固体基底上聚结诱导跳跃后的质量传递。研究发现，合并界面快速运动引起的低浓度液体向下夹带导致了Sherwood数在核心区域的局部增强，且增强程度高度依赖于聚结时质量传递边界层的状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解气泡聚结诱导跳跃后对壁面传质的强化机制，特别是在不同气泡尺寸和Schmidt数条件下的传质行为，为工业应用中微气泡传质强化提供理论依据。

Method: 采用Basilisk流求解器中的Volume-of-Fluid（VOF）方法进行数值模拟，考察了4种组合工况（气泡半径150和25微米，氢气-水物性，Schmidt数为210和1），分析气泡跳跃后界面动力学与传质场的耦合特征。

Result: 在聚结后约0.5-1个惯性-毛细时间单位内，界面快速运动诱导低浓度液体向下夹带。Sc=210时，Sherwood数在合并气泡南极下方约R_m/2半径的核心区域内出现高度局部增强，且由于高Sc下扩散缓慢该增强持续到气泡脱离后很久。Sherwood数增强因子强烈依赖于聚结时刻传质边界层的状态，边界层越薄增强越大。跳跃后气泡速度迅速衰减并接近自由上升末端速度，微对流的单独传质增强效果虽略强于文献报道的纯浮力驱动情形，但仍不显著。

Conclusion: 气泡聚结诱导跳跃通过界面快速运动引发的微对流可在核心区域显著增强壁面传质，尤其在边界层较薄时效果更突出；但跳跃后微对流对整体壁面传质系数的增强效果有限。

Abstract: The Volume-of-Fluid implementation in the \href{basilisk.fr}{Basilisk} flow solver is employed to study mass transfer after coalescence-induced jump-off of bubbles on solid substrates at four combinations of bubble radius ($R_m=150$ and 25 $μ$m, hydrogen-water properties) and Schmidt number ($\text{Sc}=210$ and 1). The results show a relatively strong downward entrainment of low-concentration liquid, induced by a rapid movement of the bubble interface at approximately 0.5 to 1 inertio-capillary time units after the moment of coalescence. At $\text{Sc}=210$, this leads to a highly local increase of the Sherwood number at a core region (roughly an area of radius $R_m/2$) below the south pole of the merged bubble, which persists long after the bubble departure due to the slow diffusion at large $\text{Sc}$. The enhancement factor of the Sherwood number is highly dependent on the state of the mass-transfer boundary layer at the moment of coalescence, increasing with smaller boundary layer thicknesses. Shortly after the jump-off, the velocity of the bubble is considerably damped and rapidly approaches its free-rise terminal velocity. The effect of micro-convection on the wall mass transfer coefficient at this stage is insignificant when isolated, similar to what is reported in the literature for purely buoyancy-driven bubble rise -- but slightly stronger.

</details>


### [341] [Weakly nonlinear dynamics of a follower-force active filament in simple shear flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07248)
*Ory Schnitzer*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过弱非线性理论研究了外部简单剪切流对无惯性主动丝状体在斯托克斯流中自发动力学起始的影响，推导出广义振幅方程，发现剪切流会诱导稳态偏转并抑制丝状体内在振荡，打破无剪切时的简并性，驱动从圆形涡旋到椭圆涡旋、横向拍动直至稳态偏转的一系列动力学转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解外部剪切流如何影响主动丝状体（如鞭毛、纤毛等生物及仿生系统）在粘性流体中的自发运动起始，是对此前无剪切情况下弱非线性分析的自然延伸，具有重要的基础流体力学和生物物理意义。

Method: 采用弱非线性理论，将前作（J. Fluid Mech., 1007 A65, 2025）中无剪切情形的弱非线性分析推广到存在弱简单剪切流的场景，推导出描述近临界动力学的广义振幅方程，并对方程进行定性与动态分析。

Result: 研究表明剪切流在诱导稳态偏转的同时会阻尼丝状体的内在振荡，阻尼源于剪切与内在振荡之间微妙的非线性共振，其随剪切率呈二次方标度，且具有各向异性——沿流动方向强于垂直方向。剪切打破了无剪切情形下圆形涡旋与不稳定平面拍动共存的简并性，驱动系统经历由圆形涡旋到椭圆涡旋、再到横向（垂直于剪切方向）拍动、最终到稳态偏转的动力学转变序列。

Conclusion: 外部简单剪切流可显著改变主动丝状体的近临界动力学行为，通过剪切频率与内在振荡之间的非线性共振产生各向异性阻尼，从而控制丝状体的运动模式转变，为主动丝状体在剪切环境中的集体行为与运动控制提供理论框架。

Abstract: We employ weakly nonlinear theory to investigate how an externally imposed simple shear flow affects the onset of spontaneous dynamics of an inertialess active filament deforming in Stokes flow. The filament, clamped to a wall at one end, is subjected to a compressive "follower force" applied tangentially at its free end. By extending my earlier weakly nonlinear analysis of the shear-free case (J. Fluid Mech., 1007 A65, 2025), we derive a generalized amplitude equation governing the near-onset dynamics under weak shear. Analysis of the amplitude equation shows that, besides inducing a steady deflection, the shear damps the filament's intrinsic oscillations. This damping arises from a subtle nonlinear resonance between the shear and the intrinsic oscillations, scales quadratically with the shear rate, and is anisotropic---stronger in the flow direction than normal to it. Without shear, stable whirling states, where the filament tip traces a circular orbit in a plane parallel to the wall, and unstable planar-beating states are known to simultaneously emerge at a critical follower-force value, with circular whirling typically observed beyond this threshold. Shear breaks this degeneracy, driving a sequence of dynamical transitions: from circular to elliptical whirling, then to transverse beating (normal to the shear), and ultimately to steady deflection.

</details>


### [342] [A Comparative Study of Finite-Volume-based Coupled and Segregated Reduced-Order Models for Incompressible Flows in Parametrized Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07472)
*Andrea Buffolini,Davide Oberto,Gianluigi Rozza*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对几何参数化域内不可压缩流体动力学的降阶模型（ROMs）进行了比较分析，研究了两种降阶求解策略： monolithic 耦合求解器和基于 SIMPLE 的分裂式算法。两者在两个二维稳态基准案例（方腔驱动流和圆柱绕流）上进行性能评估，发现耦合方法在超压缩子增强后收敛更快但自由度更多，而 SIMPLE 分裂算法在低维约化空间中重建精度更高但收敛较慢，体现了精度与计算效率之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何参数化域中应用降阶模型求解不可压缩流体动力学问题时，存在多种降阶求解策略（如 monolithic 耦合与 SIMPLE 分裂式），但目前缺乏对这两类方法的系统性比较研究，因此需要评估它们在精度与计算效率上的差异，以指导实际应用中的算法选择。

Method: 本文对两种降阶求解策略进行了对比：(1) monolithic 耦合求解器，并辅以超压缩子（supremizers）增强以处理速度-压力稳定性约束；(2) 基于 SIMPLE 的分裂式算法。两者均在两个二维稳态基准问题（方腔驱动流、圆柱绕流）上测试，比较指标包括流场重建精度和气动系数预测能力。

Result: 实验结果表明：耦合方法在适当的超压缩子增强后表现出更快的收敛速度，但需要更大规模的自由度；而 SIMPLE 分裂算法在低维约化空间中展现出更优的重建精度，代价是收敛速度较慢。两者在精度-效率权衡上呈现出互补的特性。

Conclusion: 两种降阶算法各有优劣：如果优先追求收敛速度和计算效率，可选用超压缩子增强的 monolithic 耦合方法；如果降低约化空间维度但需要更高重建精度，SIMPLE 分裂算法是更好的选择。该研究为几何参数化不可压缩流体问题中降阶模型的算法选型提供了有价值的参考依据。

Abstract: This work presents a comparative analysis of Reduced-Order Models (ROMs) applied to incompressible fluid dynamics within geometrically parametrized domains. Two distinct reduced-order solution strategies are investigated and compared: a monolithic coupled solver and a segregated SIMPLE-based algorithm. Their performance is assessed on two steady, two-dimensional benchmark cases: a lid-driven cavity flow and a flow past a cylindrical obstacle. The two algorithms are compared in terms of fields evaluation and aerodynamic coefficients prediction. The computational results highlight a fundamental trade-off between accuracy and numerical efficiency. On the one hand, after an opportune supremizers enrichment, the coupled approach guarantees a faster convergence, despite the need of a larger number of degrees of freedom. On the other hand, the segregated SIMPLE algorithm yields superior reconstruction accuracy, particularly for lower-dimensional reduced spaces, at the cost of a slower convergence rate.

</details>


### [343] [Physics-informed neural networks for viscoelastic fluid flows around a cylinder in a two-dimensional channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07861)
*Midhuna Suresh,Akanksha Gupta*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出Cholesky-PINN框架用于求解高弹性Oldroyd-B流体的粘弹性流动问题。通过Cholesky分解构造张量保证应力场正定性，缓解高Weissenberg数问题导致的训练发散；结合稀疏CFD数据同化和迁移学习克服深度学习谱偏置，成功在单圆柱和多圆柱阵列等复杂几何上准确捕捉粘弹性尾流结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于网格的流体动力学求解器面临网格生成繁琐和逆问题不适定等挑战；而将物理信息神经网络（PINNs）应用于高弹性Oldroyd-B流体时频繁遭遇训练失败。高Weissenberg数问题（HWNP）源于停滞点附近应力的指数增长，导致标准PINN优化器发散；同时深度模型的谱偏置限制了网络对高弹性尾流结构的精确捕捉。

Method: (1) 对构象张量施加Cholesky分解作为数学约束，确保应力场正定性，稳定梯度并防止网络崩溃；(2) 引入稀疏数据同化，将物理损失与目标CFD数据点锚定，迫使模型朝向真实物理解；(3) 采用迁移学习加速训练，帮助网络跳出非物理局部最小值；(4) 采用构造实体几何（CSG）方法处理复杂几何，避免传统CFD繁琐的重网格化过程。

Result: 在单圆柱绕流几何上验证了Re=5-25的适用性，并将松弛时间λ从0.1增至0.5以测试网络稳定性；进一步将该框架扩展到复杂的3圆柱阵列。实验结果表明该框架能够准确捕捉尖锐的粘弹性尾流，提供一种稳定可靠的计算工具用于复杂流变学建模。

Conclusion: Cholesky分解约束结合数据同化与迁移学习的组合策略，有效解决了PINN在高弹性流体模拟中的HWNP和谱偏置问题，构建了无需网格化即可处理复杂几何的稳定粘弹性流体数值求解框架。

Abstract: Grid-based fluid dynamics solvers routinely struggle with exhaustive meshing demands and ill-posed inverse problems. A practical mesh-free alternative is given by Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) but, applying them to highly elastic Oldroyd-B fluids results in many training failures. The High Weissenberg Number Problem (HWNP), driven by the exponential stress growth near stagnation points is a major issue, which causes standard PINN optimizers to diverge. To prevent the network from crashing, we apply a Cholesky decomposition to the conformation tensor. This mathematical constraint stabilizes the gradients by guaranteeing a positive-definite stress field. Beyond mathematical stability, the inherent spectral bias of deep learning models can hinder the network from accurately capturing the highly elastic wake structures. Therefore, we used sparse data assimilation to force the model toward the actual physical solution. By anchoring the physics loss with targeted CFD data points and accelerating training via transfer learning, we successfully pushed the network past non-physical local minima. We validated this Cholesky-PINN approach on flow past cylindrical geometries for Reynolds numbers (Re) between 5-25, for the single-cylinder setup. In addition, the relaxation time (λ) is increased from 0.1 to 0.5 to test the stability of the network. Finally, we scale the framework to a complex 3-cylinder array which proved our constructive solid geometry approach completely bypasses the tedious re-meshing steps of traditional CFD. The combined framework accurately captured sharp viscoelastic wakes, providing a stable computational tool for complex rheological modeling.

</details>


### [344] [Marangoni flow induced by crystallization in saline droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07543)
*Théophile Gaichies,Javier Martínez-Puig,Javier Rodríguez Rodríguez*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了氯化钠在固定液滴中结晶所诱导的流动，测得流速约1 cm/s，比先前研究高两个数量级。差异源于早期实验中的表面污染物。数值模拟表明，干净情况下流动由Marangoni应力驱动，污染情况下则由密度驱动，液滴清洁度显著影响流动并进而决定晶体生长速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结晶过程中由流动诱导的传质直接影响晶体的成核与生长，但此前关于氯化钠结晶液滴中流速的报道存在量级上的不一致（相差两个数量级），需要澄清这一矛盾的根本原因，以理解液滴结晶的真实物理机制。

Method: 对固定液滴中氯化钠结晶过程进行实验流速测量，结合数值模拟对比干净与受污染（含表面活性剂）两种液滴体系下的流动驱动力（Marangoni应力 vs 密度驱动）及其流场特征。

Result: 干净液滴结晶时流速约1 cm/s，是文献报道值的百倍；先前实验中的表面污染物抑制了Marangoni流动，导致流速被低估。干净条件下流动由Marangoni应力主导，污染条件下则转为密度驱动流。

Conclusion: 液滴表面清洁度对结晶诱导的流动起着决定性作用：它不仅改变了流动的驱动机理，还通过改变流速和流场结构影响晶体生长速率。这提示在相关实验和工艺中必须严格控制液滴表面污染状态。

Abstract: In this article, we study the flow induced by crystallization of sodium chloride in sessile droplets. We measure flow velocities on the order of 1 $\mathrm{cm.s^{-1}} during crystallization, two orders of magnitude higher than those reported in previous studies. We show that this discrepancy is due to surface contaminants in earlier experiments. Numerical simulations reveal that in the clean case, the flow is driven by Marangoni stresses, whereas it is density- driven in the contaminated case. Consequently, droplet cleanliness significantly influences the flow which, in turn, determines the growth rate of crystals.

</details>


### [345] [Propeller-wing interactions of a lift + cruise eVTOL configuration during transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07665)
*Oliver Westcott,Swathi Krishna,Robert Entwistle*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过风洞实验探讨了升力+巡航飞行器在过渡阶段（悬停转向前飞）垂直升力旋翼与机翼之间轴向与纵向间距变化对气动性能的影响。实验在雷诺数52,000至377,000范围内进行，发现早期过渡阶段（前旋翼前进比0-0.25）对构型变化尤为敏感：后电机朝上且纵向间距最短时，升阻比提升约4个单位；后电机倒置时则降低约4个单位。缩短纵向间距会放大后旋翼的升力贡献及前旋翼的不利影响，但在前旋翼朝上且靠近机翼的构型下，翼与旋翼尾涡的有利相互作用可改善前进比0.11-0.25范围内的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 升力+巡航飞行器在从悬停过渡到前飞的阶段，垂直升力旋翼尾流会与机翼发生复杂干扰。理解旋翼与机翼之间轴向及纵向间距的几何变化如何影响这一关键过渡阶段的气动性能，是优化此类飞行器构型设计的重要基础。

Method: 将电机以朝上或倒置两种轴向方式安装于相对机翼的两个纵向位置，形成不同的升力+巡航机翼构型；在雷诺数52,000至377,000范围内开展风洞实验，利用流场显示（tufts）、压力测点（pressure taps）和载荷传感器（load cells）综合测量气动特性。

Result: 早期过渡阶段（前旋翼前进比0至0.25）对构型变化最为敏感。后电机朝上且纵向间距最小时，主动升力旋翼使升阻比提升约4个单位；后电机倒置时则导致升阻比降低约4个单位。缩短纵向间距会同时放大后旋翼的有利升力贡献与前旋翼的不利影响；但在前电机朝上且靠近机翼时，前进比0.11至0.25范围内性能得到改善，源于机翼与前旋翼尾涡间的有利相互作用。

Conclusion: 旋翼轴向取向（朝上/倒置）与相对机翼的纵向间距是影响升力+巡航飞行器过渡阶段气动性能的关键设计参数；后电机朝上配合最短纵向间距可获得最优升阻比收益，而旋翼与机翼间尾涡相互作用可在特定前进比区间形成有利干扰，为过渡飞行构型的优化设计提供依据。

Abstract: The wakes of a lift + cruise aircraft's vertical lift propellers interact with the wing during the transition from hover to forward flight. This study investigates how variations in axial and longitudinal separations between the vertical lift propellers and the wing affect aerodynamic performance during this critical phase. Axial variations were introduced by orienting the motors either upright or inverted at two longitudinal positions relative to the wing. Wind tunnel tests were conducted on the resulting lift + cruise wing configurations over a Reynolds number range of 52,000 to 377,000, using tufts, pressure taps, and load cells to capture aerodynamic behaviour. Results show that the early transition phase (front propeller advance ratios between 0 and 0.25) is particularly sensitive to configuration changes. Rear motor orientation had a pronounced effect: when oriented upright at the shortest longitudinal separation, the active lift propellers increased the lift-drag ratio of the design by approximately 4 units, whereas in the rear motor inverted case the design suffered a 4-point reduction in the lift-drag ratio. Decreasing the longitudinal separation amplified both the beneficial lift contribution of the rear propeller and the generally adverse effects of the front propeller. However, placing the front propeller closer to the wing in the upright orientation enhanced performance at advance ratios between 0.11 and 0.25, due to favourable interactions between the wing and the front propeller's trailing vortices.

</details>


### [346] [Coupled thermoacoustic resolvent analysis of a model two-stream coaxial combustor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08096)
*Lu Chen,Zheng Qiao,Yu Lv*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文推导了一种解析算子(resolvent operator)来分析两股同轴燃烧室中的火焰-声学耦合效应，通过耦合热声解析算子的方法识別不同机制的最优强迫及其响应，揭示了纯水动力系统與耦合系统中的本质差异，并提出了一种可抵消特定扰动强迫的新型控制策略设计思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火焰与声学的耦合效应对燃烧不稳定性的激发和维持至关重要，但传统分析通常将火焰水动力效应与腔体声学效应分开处理，缺乏能够同时容纳两者及其耦合的统一建模框架。为了更准确地理解耦合热声系统中的强迫-响应特性，并识别对火焰-声学耦合最敏感的模式，从而指导热声控制策略的制定，需要建立一种全新的解析分析工具。

Method: 本文推导并应用一种耦合热声解析算子(coupled thermoacoustic resolvent)，该理论分析同时包含活性火焰的水动力效应、燃烧室声学效应以及二者的耦合效应。通过该算子可以识别不同物理机制的最优强迫(input forcing)及其对应的最优响应(output response)。此外，还提出了一种新颖的敏感性分析方法来识别对火焰-声学耦合效应最敏感的模式。理论工具被应用于两股同轴模型燃烧室。

Result: 应用该耦合解析算子于两股同轴模型燃烧室后发现：耦合系统中的强迫-响应特性与纯水动力线性系统存在显著差异；观察到指示共振的声学能量峰；声学强迫不具有秩一(rank-one)性质；通过提出的敏感性分析方法可识别对火焰-声学耦合最敏感的模式；更重要的是，利用耦合热声解析算子可以设计特定强迫来抵消或中和另一属性强迫引起的扰动。

Conclusion: 耦合热声解析算子是一种有效的分析工具，能够揭示纯水动力分析中无法观测到的共振现象和耦合效应特征。该研究不仅深化了对火焰-声学耦合机制的理解，还为热声不稳定性的主动控制提供了理论依据——即通过设计反相强迫来抵消不稳定扰动，有望推动有效的热声控制策略的开发。

Abstract: We derived a resolvent operator to analyze the coupled flame-acoustic effect in a model two-stream coaxial combustor. The theoretical analysis accommodates both the hydrodynamic effect of the active flame and chamber acoustic effect, as well as their coupling effect. Utilizing the coupled thermoacoustic resolvent, we are able to identify the optimal forcing of different mechanisms and their corresponding optimal responses. When this new modeling tool is applied to a two-stream coaxial model combustor, it reveals distinctly different forcing-response characteristics in the coupled system compared to those in a purely hydrodynamic linear system. Acoustic energy peaks, indicative of resonance, are observed. Additionally, it has been found that the acoustic forcing does not maintain a rank-one property. Furthermore, the modes most receptive to the flame-acoustic coupling effect can be identified using a novel sensitivity analysis approach. Interestingly, by employing the coupled thermoacoustic resolvent, we demonstrate that forcing can be designed to negate or counteract perturbations caused by the forcing of another attribute, potentially aiding in the development of effective thermoacoustic control strategies.

</details>


### [347] [Closed-Form Solution for Oscillatory Flow and Wall Shear Stress in Axisymmetric Corrugated Tubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08651)
*Frederik Keil,Paolo Malgaretti,Othmane Aouane,Jens Harting*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将Womersley经典解从刚性圆管推广到半径缓慢变化的刚性轴对称波纹管，在润滑近似下推导出轴向速度、体积流量、相位滞后和壁面剪切应力的闭式表达式，并通过三维格子玻尔兹曼模拟验证其适用范围。研究发现脉动流特征在宽窄截面间呈现plug-like到parabolic的转变，脉动性增强可减弱波纹对流量衰减的影响，其缩放规律从准稳态的⟨R⁻⁴⟩⁻¹过渡到高频的⟨R⁻²⟩⁻¹。壁面剪切应力在瓶颈处最大但随Womersley数增加而减小，且瓶颈剪切应力对波纹幅度呈非单调依赖。文章还给出了时均壁面剪切应力与振荡剪切指数的闭式解，与标准血流动力学指标建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在血流动力学和微流控中，脉冲流动常通过波纹管（几何收缩区域），而经典Womersley解仅限于半径均匀的刚性圆管，无法刻画几何波纹与振荡驱动共同作用下的输运和壁面载荷问题，因此需要将理论扩展到变截面管几何。

Method: 在润滑近似的框架下，将Womersley振荡流解推广到半径缓慢变化的轴对称刚性波纹管，推导出速度剖面、流量、相位滞后和壁面剪切应力的闭式解析表达式；同时利用三维格子玻尔兹曼（Lattice Boltzmann）数值模拟评估理论解的适用范围。对于正弦波纹，进一步分析了波纹参数对瓶颈壁面剪切应力的影响，并推导时均壁面剪切应力和振荡剪切指数的闭式公式。

Result: 理论分析表明：局部速度剖面沿管轴变化显著，宽截面呈plug-like而窄瓶颈附近接近parabolic；周期峰值流量随波纹增强而降低，但脉动性越强则这种衰减越弱，反映从准稳态⟨R⁻⁴⟩⁻¹到高频⟨R⁻²⟩⁻¹的交叉过渡；壁面剪切应力在瓶颈处最大并随Womersley数增大而减小；对正弦波纹，瓶颈剪切应力因局部剪切放大与全局水力阻力间的竞争而对波纹幅度呈非单调变化。三维LBM模拟验证了理论的有效范围。

Conclusion: 本文建立的扩展Womersley理论为波纹刚性管内的脉动流提供了系统的解析框架，揭示了几何效应与振荡效应对输运和壁面载荷的耦合机制，并通过闭式的时均剪切应力与振荡剪切指数公式与标准血流动力学指标直接衔接，为相关血管和微流控应用提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Pulsatile flow in corrugated tubes arises in hemodynamics and microfluidics, where oscillatory forcing and geometric constrictions jointly determine transport and wall loading. We extend Womersley's classical solution for oscillatory flow in a rigid circular tube to rigid axisymmetric tubes of slowly varying radius. Within the lubrication approximation, we derive closed-form expressions for the axial velocity profile, volumetric flow rate, phase lag, and wall shear stress at an arbitrary Womersley number. Three-dimensional lattice Boltzmann simulations are used to assess the regime of validity of the theory. The analysis shows that the local velocity profile varies strongly along the tube, ranging from plug-like in wide sections to more parabolic near bottlenecks. The cycle-maximum flow rate decreases with increasing corrugation, but this reduction weakens as pulsatility increases, reflecting a crossover from the quasi-steady scaling $\langle R^{-4}\rangle^{-1}$ to the high-frequency scaling $\langle R^{-2}\rangle^{-1}$. The wall shear stress is maximal at the bottleneck and decreases with Womersley number. For sinusoidal corrugations, the bottleneck wall shear stress depends non-monotonically on the corrugation because of the competition between local shear amplification and global hydraulic resistance. Closed-form expressions for the time-averaged wall shear stress and oscillatory shear index further connect the theory to standard hemodynamic metrics.

</details>


### [348] [ONE CYLinder: A Benchmark for Graph-Based Surrogate Modeling of Unsteady Bluff-Body Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08947)
*Théodore Michel,Antoine Campos,Alban Dujardin,Henry Areiza,Philippe Meliga,Elie Hachem*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了ONECYL基准数据集，包含450个高保真VMS有限元模拟（270,000个快照），涵盖圆柱绕流在层流、过渡和高雷诺数三个流态下的非定常流动，并提供统一的评估框架（全场轨迹误差、虚拟探针、阻力/升力预测）。同时开发了一个Graph Transformer基线模型用于非结构化网格上的自回归速度和压力场预测，实验表明显式编码圆柱几何（水平集表示）能提升长程预测精度和泛化能力，而基于散度的正则化随流动复杂度增加愈发有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的替代模型有望加速非结构化网格上的CFD仿真，但其发展受限于缺少覆盖多流态的标准基准数据集以及缺乏长程自回归预测的标准化评估协议。已有基准多聚焦单一流动状态，缺乏统一的评估机制来全面衡量数值精度和物理保真度。

Method: 1) 构建ONECYL基准：450个随机化圆柱几何的高保真变分多尺度有限元模拟，覆盖层流、过渡和高雷诺数三个流态，提供时间分辨的速度/压力场、网格连接性、几何描述符与气动积分量；2) 建立统一评估框架：结合全场rollout误差、虚拟探针和阻力/升力预测；3) 开发Graph Transformer参考基线，在非结构化网格上自回归预测速度与压力场；4) 对比不同几何表示方法（水平集显式编码vs隐式表示）及物理正则化策略（散度正则化）在三类流态下的表现。

Result: 显式通过水平集表示编码圆柱几何一致地提升长程预测精度与对未见几何的泛化能力；基于散度的正则化在流动复杂度增加时（如高雷诺数过渡流）作用愈发显著。ONECYL成功区分了不同几何表示与正则化策略在多个流态下的优劣。

Conclusion: ONECYL提供了首个覆盖多流态的圆柱绕流图替代模型基准，配以分层评估框架和Graph Transformer基线，为未来非定常钝体绕流长程预测研究提供了可复现的基础平台。

Abstract: Graph-based surrogate models offer a promising route to accelerate computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations on unstructured meshes. However, their development is limited by the scarcity of benchmark datasets spanning multiple flow regimes and standardized protocols for long-horizon autoregressive prediction. We introduce ONECYL (ONE CYLinder), a new benchmark for unsteady flow past a circular cylinder across laminar, transitional, and high-Reynolds-number regimes. The benchmark comprises 450 high-fidelity Variational Multiscale finite-element simulations (270,000 flow snapshots) with randomized cylinder geometries, providing time-resolved velocity and pressure fields together with mesh connectivity, geometric descriptors, Reynolds numbers, and integrated aerodynamic quantities. Beyond the dataset, ONECYL establishes a unified evaluation framework combining full-field rollout errors, virtual probes, and drag and lift predictions to assess numerical accuracy and physical fidelity. To accompany the benchmark, we develop a Graph Transformer as a reference baseline predicting velocity and pressure fields autoregressively on unstructured meshes. Using ONECYL, we investigate geometric representations and physics-based regularization across the three Reynolds-number regimes. The results show that explicitly encoding the cylinder geometry through a level-set representation consistently improves long-horizon prediction accuracy and generalization to unseen geometries, while divergence-based regularization becomes increasingly beneficial as flow complexity increases. The ONECYL benchmark and its Graph Transformer baseline provide a reproducible framework for evaluating graph-based surrogate models and establish a foundation for future research on long-horizon prediction of unsteady bluff-body flows.

</details>


### [349] [Fish navigate a hydrodynamic maze via yaw-mediated lateral migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09024)
*Michael A. Calicchia,Rui Ni*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究设计了一个具有空间变化平均流和湍流的水动力迷宫，发现鱼类通过将身体偏转（yaw）相对于水流方向来逃离能量不利的区域。这种偏转姿态可能通过升力促进横向迁移并提高对局部流动变化的敏感性，且鱼类将身体保持在接近但低于预测失速条件的角度，以利用升力而不显著增加阻力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解鱼类如何在复杂、异质的水动力环境中感知和响应微妙的水动力线索并以能量高效的方式导航，是揭示鱼类识别和利用有利水流条件机制的核心，对自然和工程流动系统都有潜在意义。

Method: 设计了一个具有空间变化的平均流速和湍流的水动力迷宫，构建了可控的异质流动景观，用于观察和分析鱼类在不同流动条件下的运动策略和身体姿态。

Result: 发现鱼类通过将身体偏转以逃离能量不利的区域；偏转姿态通过升力促进横向迁移并可能增强对局部流动的感知；鱼类维持在接近但低于预测失速条件的偏转角度，以在无显著阻力增加的情况下利用升力。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了鱼类在异质水动力环境中导航的运动策略机制，为理解鱼类在不同流动环境中的行为提供了新见解，并对自然和工程流动系统的设计具有潜在参考价值。

Abstract: In aquatic environments, gradients in flow velocity and turbulence define a constantly shifting landscape that sets the physical constraints on propulsion and energy expenditure in fish. Successfully navigating these complex flows depends on the ability to sense and respond to subtle hydrodynamic cues in an energy-efficient manner. Revealing these mechanisms is central to understanding how fish identify and exploit favorable flow conditions. To this end, we designed a hydrodynamic maze with spatially varying mean flow and turbulence, creating a controlled heterogeneous flow landscape for understanding fish movement strategies. We found that fish escape energetically unfavorable regions by yawing their bodies relative to the flow. These yawed orientations appear to facilitate lateral migration through lift forces and may improve sensitivity to local flow variations. Notably, fish maintain body orientations near, but below, predicted stall conditions to exploit lift without a significant increase in drag. These findings provide insight into how fish navigate heterogeneous hydrodynamic environments, with potential relevance for both natural and engineered flow systems.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [350] [Physical-Field Reconstruction from Sparse Observations: When Are Diffusion Models Preferable to Deterministic Regression?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06135)
*Hao Zhou,Rui Zhang,Qi Wang,Hao Sun*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文对确定性U-Net、条件扩散模型和先验引导扩散模型在稀疏物理场重建中的表现进行了公平对比（涵盖2D泊松方程、2D Navier-Stokes流动和1D Kuramoto-Sivashinsky动力学），发现扩散模型在精度上并无系统性优势，但其价值体现在不确定性估计、样本保真度与鲁棒性等具体场景中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏观测重建物理场本质上是病态逆问题而非简单插值。尽管已有大量确定性和生成式方法，但对于何时应该使用单点估计（确定性方法）、何时应使用分布化重建（生成式方法），目前缺乏明确的共识。这种分歧阻碍了方法选择与工程落地。

Method: 在匹配的实验设置下，对三种代表性方法进行公平对比：(1) 确定性U-Net（单点估计）；(2) 条件扩散模型；(3) 先验引导扩散模型。测试涵盖了三个物理系统（2D Poisson方程、2D Navier-Stokes流动、1D Kuramoto-Sivashinsky动力学），评估了在不同复杂度和稀疏度下的重建精度与不确定性质量。

Result: 三项关键发现：(1) 重建精度依赖于具体物理场和流动状态，扩散方法在高复杂度或更稀疏观测下并无系统性精度优势；(2) 集成均值可提升相位对齐精度，而单个样本能更好地保留变异性和所选状态下的高波数能量；(3) 条件扩散在更低成本下提供更可靠的不确定性估计，先验引导扩散对掩码分布偏移更鲁棒，但推理成本更高且需要引导参数调优。

Conclusion: 该研究厘清了生成式重建的适用场景，为改进稀疏场重建中的不确定性估计、细尺度样本保真度、鲁棒性和计算效率提供了明确的方法选择指导。

Abstract: Reconstructing physical fields from sparse observations is central to system identification, forecasting, and control, yet sparse measurements generally underdetermine the full field. This makes reconstruction an ill-posed inverse problem rather than simple interpolation. Although many deterministic and generative methods have been developed, there is still no clear consensus on when a single point estimate is sufficient and when a distribution of plausible reconstructions is more useful. We conduct a fair comparison of a deterministic U-Net, conditional diffusion, and prior-guided diffusion under matched experimental settings, including 2D Poisson equation, 2D Navier-Stokes flow, and 1D Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamics. Through this comparison, we make three observations. First, accuracy is field- and regime-dependent, with no systematic advantage for diffusion under higher complexity or sparser observations. Second, ensemble means improve phase-aligned accuracy, whereas individual samples better preserve variability and can retain high-wavenumber power in selected regimes. Third, conditional diffusion provides more reliable uncertainty estimates at lower cost, while prior-guided diffusion is more robust to mask-distribution shifts but requires substantially higher inference cost and guidance tuning. These results clarify when generative reconstruction is useful and provide guidance for improving uncertainty estimation, fine-scale sample fidelity, robustness, and computational efficiency in sparse field reconstruction.

</details>


### [351] [A Sparsity-Promoting Electromagnetic Inversion Method Regularized by $\ell_1 / \ell_2$-Norm of the Model Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06524)
*Lingqi Gao,Hakan Bagci*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以模型梯度的ℓ₁/ℓ₂范数作为正则化的非线性电磁反演方法，该范数的尺度不变特性比传统ℓ₁范数能更准确地刻画解的稀疏性。通过交替方向乘子法（ADMM）将非凸非光滑的逆问题重构为五个子步骤的高效优化算法，其中第一步用Gauss-Newton方法线性化非线性反演。数值实验（合成与实验数据）表明该方法在重建介电常数剖面、保持尖锐边缘、抑制伪影方面优于Tikhonov和全变差（TV）正则化方法，且计算开销不增加，对测量噪声鲁棒性强，对正则化权重选择不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ℓ₁范数虽常用于稀疏正则化，但其对幅度的线性惩罚导致尺度相关，不能充分表征稀疏性。ℓ₁/ℓ₂范数作为两者之比具有尺度不变的优良性质，能更准确地度量稀疏性，但其商结构带来的非凸性和非光滑性使得优化求解具有挑战性，作者旨在提出一种高效可行的求解框架。

Method: 采用模型梯度上的ℓ₁/ℓ₂范数作为正则项；利用比率形式下的非凸非平滑特性，借助交替方向乘子法（ADMM）将原始逆问题分解为五个相互交替的子步骤求解；其中第一个子步骤引入Gauss-Newton方法对非线性电磁反演方程进行线性化处理，从而在保持计算效率的同时处理非线性。整个算法由ADMM框架统一，各子步骤交替迭代直至收敛。

Result: 在大量合成数据和实验数据的数值算例中，与Tikhonov正则化和全变差（TV）正则化做了系统对比。结果显示，所提方法在介电常数剖面重建方面表现更优，尤其在边缘保持（sharp edge preservation）和伪影抑制方面效果显著，且没有增加额外计算成本；此外对测量噪声有强鲁棒性，并对正则化权重参数的选择表现出较低敏感性。

Conclusion: ℓ₁/ℓ₂范数正则化可作为传统TV或ℓ₁正则化的强有力替代，在非线性电磁反演问题中实现了更高的稀疏表征精度与更好的重建质量，同时兼顾了计算效率与参数鲁棒性，具有实际工程应用潜力。

Abstract: A nonlinear EM inversion method regularized by $\ell_1 / \ell_2$-norm of the model gradient is proposed. The $\ell_1 / \ell_2$-norm, defined as the ratio of $\ell_1$-norm to $\ell_2$-norm, exhibits a scale-invariant property that enables it to more accurately characterize sparsity in the solution compared with the conventional $\ell_1$-norm. To address the nonconvexity and nonsmoothness introduced by the quotient structure of $\ell_1 / \ell_2$-norm, the inversion problem is reformulated and solved using the alternating direction method of multipliers, resulting in an efficient optimization algorithm comprising five sub-steps. The Gauss--Newton method is incorporated into the first sub-step to linearize the nonlinear inversion. The proposed method is validated by a series of numerical examples using both synthetic and experimental datasets, and is compared against standard regularization methods such as Tikhonov and total variation. The results demonstrate that the proposed method yields superior reconstructions of permittivity profiles, particularly in preserving sharp edges and suppressing artifacts, without incurring additional computational cost. Furthermore, the method exhibits strong robustness to measurement noise and reduced sensitivity to the choice of the regularization weight.

</details>


### [352] [A HIP-Compatible Accelerator Backend for Fourier-Bessel Particle-in-Cell Simulations on CPU/DCU Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06680)
*Jingliang Fan,Ruiqing He,Yang Wan,Jiandong Shang,Hengliang Guo,Qiang Chen*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个兼容HIP的加速器后端，使FBPIC（基于Fourier-Bessel粒子网格的等离子体与加速器物理仿真代码）能够在DCU平台上高效运行，同时保留Python用户接口和高层仿真工作流。在LWFA工作负载上，相比原始FBPIC实现，该后端在NVIDIA V100 GPU上取得1.32-1.54x加速，并在DCU平台上实现高效执行。多DCU实验展示了1.88x强扩展加速和约68%弱扩展效率下的2.72x聚合吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FBPIC原始实现依赖Numba CUDA后端，无法直接部署在使用HIP编程环境的加速器（如国产DCU加速器）上，限制了其在异构加速平台上的可移植性和部署范围。

Method: 开发一个与HIP兼容的加速器后端，替代原有的Numba CUDA后端，使FBPIC能在DCU平台上运行，同时保持其Python用户接口和原有仿真工作流不变。研究还包括多DCU并行通信分析以及移植经验总结。

Result: 在LWFA测试负载上，新后端在NVIDIA V100上实现1.32-1.54x的加速比；在DCU平台上实现高效执行。多DCU实验中，四加速器强扩展加速比为1.88x，约68%弱扩展效率下聚合吞吐量提升2.72x；通信分析表明节点间通信与同步是主要扩展性瓶颈。

Conclusion: 提出的HIP兼容后端方案成功实现了FBPIC在DCU平台上的高效移植，不仅提供了性能提升和可扩展性验证，还为其他基于Python开发并运行在异构加速器平台上的科学计算应用提供了可参考的移植与优化实践。

Abstract: FBPIC (Fourier-Bessel particle-in-cell) is a high-performance simulation code for relativistic plasma and accelerator physics. Its original accelerator backend relies on Numba CUDA, which limits its direct deployment on accelerators using the HIP (Heterogeneous-Compute Interface for Portability) programming environment, such as DCU (Deep Computing Unit) accelerators. In this work, we develop an accelerator backend compatible with HIP that enables FBPIC to run efficiently on DCU platforms while preserving its Python user interface and high level simulation workflow. For the evaluated LWFA (laser-wakefield acceleration) workloads, the proposed backend achieves 1.32-1.54x speedups over the original FBPIC implementation on an NVIDIA V100 GPU and enables efficient execution on the DCU platform. We also summarize the key lessons learned from porting FBPIC to the DCU platform. Multi-DCU experiments achieve a 1.88x strong-scaling speedup on four accelerators and a 2.72x increase in aggregate throughput at approximately 68\% weak-scaling efficiency, with communication analysis identifying inter-node communication and synchronization as the main scalability limitations. Beyond FBPIC, the proposed approach provides a practical reference for porting and optimizing other scientific computing applications developed with Python on heterogeneous accelerator platforms.

</details>


### [353] [Beyond Residuals: Energy based solutions of partial differential equations using scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08239)
*Timon Rabczuk,Yizheng Wang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统性地重新审视了Deep Energy Method (DEM)，将其置于物理信息学习和变分建模的更广泛背景下，阐明了DEM与PINNs、VPINNs的关系，并重点展示了增量变分公式如何使DEM适用于非线性、历史依赖和时间依赖问题（如相场断裂和耗散系统），通过数值算例论证了DEM在变分问题上是残差基方法的一种有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差基物理信息神经网络（PINNs）及其弱形式变体通过损失最小化来强制求解强式或弱式方程，往往需要残差加权和高阶导数，且在一些问题上存在稳定性和鲁棒性方面的不足。而能量方法基于变分原理提供了物理上一致且自然的框架，但DEM与其对应方法之间的关系以及其适用问题类别尚未被系统地阐明。作者希望厘清DEM与现有物理信息学习框架的异同，并识别能量最小化在稳定性、鲁棒性和可解释性方面具有内在优势的问题类别。

Method: 本文采用系统性的理论分析框架，将DEM置于物理信息学习的统一视角下进行回访与比较。主要包括：(1) 从变分原理出发，阐明DEM通过最小化能量或增量势泛函直接求解，而非通过残差加权；(2) 厘清DEM与PINN、VPINN之间的数学关系与区别；(3) 重点发展增量变分公式，使DEM能够扩展到非线性、历史依赖和时间相关问题，包括相场断裂和耗散系统；(4) 将变分结构用于优化和反问题，以能量泛函作为基于物理的约束而非残差惩罚项；(5) 通过一系列数值算例实证验证DEM在各类问题上的表现与局限性。

Result: 研究表明，DEM消除了残差加权需求，避免了高阶导数，并能通过变分结构直接施加物理约束。在增量变分公式下，DEM可成功应用于非线性、历史依赖和时间依赖问题（如相场断裂与耗散系统）。数值算例证实DEM在变分问题上比残差基方法更具原理性和有效性，同时明确了其优势与局限性。

Conclusion: DEM为变分问题提供了基于原理的、有效的残差基方法替代方案。其变分结构在稳定性、鲁棒性和可解释性方面具有内在优势，尤其适用于力学和流体力学的变分问题以及反问题与优化问题。但该方法也有其适用范围限制，作者通过系统比较揭示了其与PINN/VPINN方法的互补关系。

Abstract: Energy-based approaches provide a natural and physically consistent framework for a large class of partial differential equations arising in solid and fluid mechanics, where the governing equations follow from variational principles. In contrast to residual-based physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and their weak-form variants, which enforce the strong or weak form of the equations through loss minimization, the Deep Energy Method (DEM) directly computes the solution as the minimizer of an energy or incremental potential functional. This eliminates the need for residual weighting, avoids high-order derivatives, and enables the direct enforcement of physical constraints through the variational structure.In this work, we systematically revisit the Deep Energy Method, placing it in the broader context of physics-informed learning and variational modeling. We clarify the relationship between DEM, PINNs, and VPINNs, and identify the class of problems for which energy minimization provides intrinsic advantages in terms of stability, robustness and interpretability. Particular emphasis is placed on incremental variational formulations, which allow DEM to be applied to nonlinear, history-dependent and time-dependent problems, including phase-field fracture and dissipative systems. The variational structure underlying DEM further provides a natural foundation for optimization and inverse problems, where the energy functional acts as a physics-based constraint rather than a residual penalty. Through a series of numerical examples, we demonstrate that DEM offers a principled and effective alternative to residual-based methods for variational problems, highlighting its strengths and limitations relative to existing physics-informed approaches.

</details>


### [354] [A finite-strain logarithmic viscoelastic model for Antarctic ice shelves based on an additive split](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08451)
*Maxime Nutte,Sebastian Skatulla,Carlo Sansour*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对数应变空间（Hencky应变）的有限应变Maxwell粘弹性模型用于冰架/冰舌的断裂前沿应力场模拟。模型将应变率加性分解为弹性和粘性部分，弹性部分为各向同性Hencky弹性，粘性部分为基于对数应变率及其功共轭应力的Glen型幂律本构。该模型通过中点评估和后退欧拉修正实现有限元数值集成，并应用于理想化冰舌算例，探讨了前沿形态（前脚、基底切削）及粘弹性对终端的控制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰架主要通过崩解（calving）过程损失质量，而崩解由前沿附近的应力场控制，其时间尺度跨越弹性弯曲和粘性蠕变。现有模型难以同时准确刻画这两种机制作用下的前沿应力状态，因此需要建立能统一描述弹性和粘性行为的有限应变本构模型。

Method: 构建有限应变Maxwell模型：在固定参考构型下将Hencky（对数）应变在率层次上加性分解为弹性和粘性部分。弹簧采用各向同性Hencky弹性，阻尼器采用以对数应变率及其功共轭应力表示的Glen型幂律。数值求解采用中点评估结合对试验对偶量做后退欧拉修正，在有限元框架中实现。首先进行粘弹性柱体基准验证，随后应用于理想化冰舌算例，考虑深度变化的密度与弹性模量、温度相关流体性和含前脚或基底切削的悬崖几何。

Result: 无穷小应变下该阻尼器退化为经典Glen流动律；本文所考察冰架构型中的弹性应变保持在该准则范围内。基准测试通过后，理想化冰舌算例揭示了粘弹性响应与前沿形态如何共同控制终端的张应力分布。结果显示前沿几何特征（前脚和基底切削）与粘弹性效应显著影响终止线附近的拉应力场。

Conclusion: 该有限应变粘弹性本构框架为冰架崩解前沿应力场的模拟提供了更完整的力学基础，能够统一描述弹性挠曲与粘性蠕变在终端应力控制中的耦合作用。未来可推广至更真实的冰架前沿稳定性分析。

Abstract: Ice shelves lose mass primarily by calving, a process controlled by the near-front stress field on timescales that span elastic flexure and viscous creep. We formulate a finite-strain Maxwell model for glacier ice in logarithmic strain space. The Hencky strain of a fixed reference configuration is split additively at the level of rates into elastic and viscous parts; the spring is isotropic Hencky elasticity and the dashpot is a Glen-type power law written on the logarithmic strain rate and its work-conjugate stress. At infinitesimal strain the dashpot coincides with Glen's flow law; the elastic strains in the ice-shelf configurations of this paper remain in that regime. The model is integrated with a midpoint evaluation and a backward-Euler correction of the trial dual, and implemented in a finite-element setting. After a viscoelastic column benchmark, the formulation is applied to an idealised ice tongue, including depth-dependent density and moduli, temperature-dependent fluidity, and cliff geometries with a frontal foot or basal undercutting. The resulting stress fields show how viscoelasticity and front morphology control tension near the terminus.

</details>


### [355] [Structure-Informed Bayesian Inference of Anomalous Transport and Hidden Molecular Trapping in Amorphous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08780)
*Andrey Ananev,Maria Potapova,Nikolay Kondratyuk,Timur Vostroknutov,Aleksey Khlyupin*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于离散Morse理论与贝叶斯正则化的新方法，用于从单粒子追踪轨迹中提取波动无定形/高分子介质中的局域束缚态。该方法利用结构信息构建物理先验，通过两阶段概率精化进行轨迹分段，消除了传统几何算法在热波动导致孔隙重构时的系统性偏差，并以O(N)线性复杂度实现了高精度束缚参数提取，已应用于I型干酪根中氢气和甲烷的受限扩散解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子扩散在波动无定形和高分子介质中主导着软物质物理、能量存储和生物膜中的关键输运过程。然而，从单粒子追踪轨迹中提取局域束缚态仍是一大挑战——由于热结构呼吸不断重构孔隙边界，传统几何算法存在严重系统偏差，会在分子循环返回时错误地合并不同的局域态。因此亟需一种克服动态结构波动影响的新方法。

Method: 本文采用从局部几何循环到结构信息贝叶斯正则化的范式转变：(1) 利用离散Morse理论提取波动宿主矩阵的时间不变拓扑骨架，构建鲁棒且气体特异的物理先验（考虑个体分子尺寸）；(2) 通过两阶段概率精化（two-stage probabilistic refinement）对轨迹步骤进行顺序划分，动态适应输运景观；(3) 在合成粒子探索真实互连矩阵图的严苛基准环境下验证其消除系统性偏差的能力。

Result: 该方法在严格基准环境中消除了系统性偏差，在最优化O(N)线性计算复杂度下将宏观束缚参数偏差限制在仅几个百分点以内。应用于I型干酪根基质中氢气和甲烷的输运（作为高度迂曲、柔性高分子网络的原型），成功解码了受限扩散的隐藏微观机制，并公开了文档化的开源代码和数据。

Conclusion: 该结构信息贝叶斯正则化方法为波动介质中的单粒子轨迹分析提供了一种可靠、高效且可推广的新策略，其应用范围可扩展到从电池聚合物中的离子输运到细胞环境中的蛋白质运输等广泛追踪现象，具有重要的跨学科影响力。

Abstract: Molecular diffusion in fluctuating amorphous and macromolecular media governs key transport processes across soft-matter physics, energy storage, and biological membranes. Extracting localized trapping states from single-particle tracking trajectories remains a fundamental challenge; because thermal structural breathing continuously reconfigures pore boundaries, conventional geometric algorithms suffer from severe systematic biases, erroneously merging distinct localized states during cyclic molecular returns. Here, we address this deadlock by shifting the paradigm from local geometric recurrence to a structure-informed Bayesian regularization. Leveraging discrete Morse theory, we extract the time-invariant topological skeleton of the fluctuating host matrix to construct robust, gas-specific physical priors that account for individual molecular dimensions. Trajectory steps are sequentially partitioned via a two-stage probabilistic refinement that dynamically adapts to the transport landscape. Benchmarked against a rigorous environment where synthetic particles explore the actual interconnected matrix graph, our approach eliminates systemic biases, restricting macroscopic trapping parameter deviations to just a few percent under optimal linear $O(N)$ computational scaling. Applied to hydrogen and methane transport within a type-I kerogen matrix, serving as a prototype for highly tortuous, flexible macromolecular networks, the method successfully decodes the hidden microscopic mechanisms of confined diffusion. To ensure immediate broad impact, the documented open-source code and data are made publicly available, offering an accessible strategy readily adaptable to a broad spectrum of tracking phenomena, from ion transport in battery polymers to protein trafficking within cellular environments.

</details>
